### [1] [GRIT: Gradient Masking for Visually Misgrounded Tokens in Text-Rich Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41629)
*Chanseul Cho,Noori Bae,Dongheon Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出RLVR应用于视觉语言模型时存在"正确但视觉错位推理"(CVMR)现象：模型在中间步骤误读视觉信息仍得到正确答案，而仅奖励最终答案正确性的RLVR无法区分。作者据此提出GRIT训练框架，将视觉落地引入RLVR，并构建GRIT-Bench基准，实验表明其同时提升推理准确率与落地保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽已扩展到VLM，但在文本密集的推理任务上效果有限。作者归因于一个关键因素：模型常在中间步骤误感知视觉信息却仍得出正确最终答案(CVMR)。由于标准RLVR只奖励最终答案正确性，无法区分"落地正确"与"落地错误"的推理，二者被同等强化，从而阻碍了VLM在文本密集任务上的性能提升。

Method: 1) 定义并形式化CVMR现象；2) 通过token级分析发现CVMR token具有与良好落地token系统性不同的注意力模式，作为检测/干预依据；3) 提出GRIT训练框架，在RLVR中融入视觉落地信号以缓解CVMR；4) 构建GRIT-Bench基准，支持跨文本长度与推理深度的系统化评测。

Result: 与现有RLVR方法相比，GRIT显著提升了推理准确率与视觉落地保真度(grounding fidelity)，并在所构建的GRIT-Bench上完成跨文本长度与推理深度的系统评估。

Conclusion: 仅依赖最终答案正确性的RLVR奖励对VLM文本密集推理不足；显式建模并惩罚视觉错位推理(CVMR)是关键改进方向。GRIT通过将视觉落地纳入强化学习，兼顾准确性与可解释的落地质量，且配套基准为后续研究提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently been extended to vision-language models (VLMs), yet its effectiveness remains limited for text-rich reasoning tasks. We highlight a key factor contributing to this limitation: models frequently arrive at correct final answers despite misperceiving visual information during intermediate steps. We define this phenomenon as Correct-but-Visually-Misgrounded Reasoning (CVMR). Because standard RLVR rewards only final-answer correctness, it fails to differentiate between properly grounded and misgrounded reasoning, reinforcing both equally. Through token-level analysis, we reveal that CVMR tokens exhibit distinct attention patterns that differ systematically from well-grounded ones. Based on this observation, we propose GRIT, a training framework that incorporates visual grounding into RLVR to mitigate the impact of CVMR. Furthermore, to address the lack of text-rich reasoning benchmarks that allow for systematic evaluation across text length and reasoning depth, we introduce GRIT-Bench. Experiments demonstrate that GRIT significantly improves both reasoning accuracy and grounding fidelity over existing RLVR methods.

</details>


### [2] [Latent Reasoning in Continuous Space for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42149)
*Byungwoo Jeon,Yoonwoo Jeong,Hyunseok Lee,Minsu Cho,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续空间潜在推理框架LARC，通过课程SFT与信息增益强化学习两阶段将token级推理转化为连续隐状态推理，缓解模态不匹配，在文本到图像生成上刷新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）难以有效利用语言链式思维推理来辅助图像生成，无法继承大语言模型中观察到的测试时扩展与细粒度可控性。作者将这一失败归因于根本性的模态不匹配：推理发生在离散token空间，而图像生成在连续空间进行，形成token级瓶颈。

Method: LARC（Latent Reasoning in Continuous space）在连续隐状态上进行推理。训练分两阶段：(i) 课程式监督微调（SFT），逐步将token级推理转换为潜在推理；(ii) 信息增益强化学习（RL），在无真值监督下自演化潜在推理。

Result: 在多个文本到图像生成基准上，LARC持续提升生成质量、提示词遵循度与组合保真度。在GenEval上达到SOTA，较BAGEL提升7个百分点，并在并行与串行两种测试时扩展策略下均优于token-based生成，表现出明显的测试时扩展行为。

Conclusion: 以连续空间潜在推理替代离散token推理可有效弥合推理与生成之间的模态鸿沟，使推理增强的UMMs获得测试时扩展能力与更强的生成表现，为多模态推理与生成统一提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) achieve remarkable success on diverse multimodal understanding and generation tasks, yet they still struggle to effectively leverage language-based chain-of-thought reasoning for image generation. As a result, existing reasoning-augmented UMMs often fail to inherit the test-time scaling and fine-grained controllability observed in large language models. We attribute this failure to a fundamental modality mismatch: reasoning is performed in a discrete token space, whereas image generation operates in a continuous space. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Latent Reasoning framework in Continuous space (LARC), which alleviates the token-level bottleneck by reasoning over continuous hidden states. Specifically, the training framework consists of two stages: (i) curriculum supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and (ii) information-gain reinforcement learning (RL). First, the curriculum SFT stage gradually converts token-level reasoning into latent reasoning. Subsequently, the information-gain RL stage self-evolves latent reasoning without ground truth supervision. Experiments across diverse text-to-image generation benchmarks show that LARC consistently improves generation quality, prompt following, and compositional fidelity. Notably, LARC achieves state-of-the-art performance on GenEval with a 7%p gain over BAGEL and demonstrates clear test-time scaling behavior, outperforming token-based generation under both parallel and sequential test-time scaling strategies.

</details>


### [3] [Open-Weight MLLMs Cannot Compare What They Cannot Name](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41841)
*Wenxin Su,Anna Kreshuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在合成蚂蚁图像上进行身体部位尺寸比较的简单基线任务中，当前开源权重MLLM对部分身体部位失败、对另一些成功；即使视觉上对它们都极其简单。作者提出"识别驱动的接地失败"假说：模型无法识别的结构会被错误关联到某个可识别的语义概念，从而同时损害接地与比较；视觉编码器分析亦支持细粒度视觉结构在缺乏语义支持时保持不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物显微成像数据规模与多样性持续增长，已超出人工视觉分析可行范围，因此希望用MLLM实现复杂表型的视觉比较自动化。为此先考察一个最简基线：比较合成蚂蚁图像中身体部位的尺寸，以判断当前模型是否具备可靠的基础视觉比较能力。

Method: 在合成蚂蚁图像上执行身体部位尺寸比较的简单基线任务；评测多种当前最优的开源权重MLLM；比较不同上下文学习（in-context learning）策略的效果；在相关目标识别任务上评估性能以定位失败环节；并对视觉编码器的表征进行分析，检验细粒度视觉结构在无语义支撑下的保持情况。

Result: 模型在某些身体部位上失败、在另一些上成功，而任务本身视觉上极为简单；上下文学习策略与识别任务表现揭示性能与可识别性相关联；视觉编码器分析结果与"细粒度视觉结构在缺乏语义支持时保持不佳"的假设一致，共同指向识别驱动的接地失败而非比较能力本身的缺失。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM存在一个出人意料的局限：语义识别会成为简单视觉比较的前置门槛，即便该比较本质上并不需要语义理解；不能命名的结构便无法被正确接地与比较。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly capable of understanding images and performing complex visual reasoning, suggesting the possibility of using them to automate biological image analysis. This need is becoming increasingly urgent as advances in technology continue to generate ever larger and more diverse microscopy datasets, already exceeding the scale at which manual visual analysis is feasible. With the final aim to use MLLMs for visual comparison of complex phenotypes, we investigate a very simple baseline: comparison of the size of body parts in synthetic images of ants. We find that state-of-the-art open-weight MLLMs fail for some body parts, but not for others, even though the comparison itself is visually trivial for all of them. We evaluate different in-context learning strategies and analyze performance on the related object recognition task. Our results suggest a recognition-driven grounding failure: structures that the models cannot recognize are instead associated with a recognizable semantic concept, impairing the grounding and comparison of both. Further analysis of the vision encoder is consistent with the hypothesis that fine-grained visual structure may be less well preserved without semantic support. Together, our results reveal a surprising limitation of current MLLMs: semantic recognition can gate simple visual comparisons that do not intrinsically require semantic understanding. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/kreshuklab/Open-Weight-MLLMs-Cannot-Compare-What-They-Cannot-Name

</details>


### [4] [Different Models, Shared Failures: Auditing Frozen-Encoder Pools for Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41790)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CoFailMed，审计医学分割预训练编码器池中的相关失效：平均精度与模型数量掩盖了模型是否在同一批病例上失败，需补充依赖性与阈值相关的有效池大小指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均分割精度和模型数量无法反映模型失败是否集中在相同病例上。若池内模型高度相关，名义规模大于实际有效规模，集成/多数投票的收益被高估；因此需系统审计编码器池的失效相关性。

Method: 在5个2D医学分割任务上，用冻结特征加单独训练的1×1读出层（每编码器4个随机种子），评估11个公开预训练编码器（受控样本而非全量普查）；另做17编码器扩展分析比较同族与跨族相关性；以33百分位Dice为失败阈值，用失败相关性估计池的有效规模（均值1.56–2.92）；并考察更丰富读出层与微调对相关性与有效规模的影响。

Result: 共享检查点对的逐病例Dice相关性最高；同一编码器家族内不同检查点仍比跨家族对更相关。来自不同编码器的四成员池平均有效规模仅1.56–2.92。更丰富读出层与微调缩小了共享检查点对（或DINOv2-B/S同发布对）与其他对的Dice相关性差距，但不同编码器间依赖可上升，微调在各训练配方重算的33百分位阈值下并不稳定提升有效池规模；组间差距变小可与依赖增强并存。

Conclusion: 池评估应同时报告绝对依赖性与阈值特定的有效规模，而非仅报告精度与名义池大小；CoFailMed发布对齐的逐病例轨迹、分析脚本与研究代码以复现核心审计结果。

Abstract: Average segmentation accuracy and model count do not reveal whether models fail on the same cases. We present CoFailMed, an audit of correlated failures in pretrained-encoder pools for medical segmentation. Across five 2D tasks, we evaluate 11 public pretrained encoders (a controlled sample, not a census) using frozen features and separately trained $1\times 1$ readouts with four seeds per encoder. Shared-checkpoint pairs have the highest mean per-case Dice correlation. An expanded 17-encoder analysis shows that distinct checkpoints within the same encoder family remain more correlated than cross-family pairs. At the pooled 33rd-percentile Dice threshold, four-member pools drawn from distinct encoders have mean effective sizes of 1.56-2.92, estimated from failure correlations in pools with enough failures and passes per member. In the primary pool, richer readouts and fine-tuning narrow the Dice-correlation gap between pairs sharing a checkpoint (or the DINOv2-B/S release) and all other pairs. Yet dependence between different encoders can increase, and fine-tuning does not consistently increase effective pool size at 33rd-percentile thresholds recomputed for each training recipe. A smaller between-group gap can coexist with greater dependence. Pool evaluation should therefore report absolute dependence and threshold-specific effective size alongside accuracy and nominal pool size. The CoFailMed release provides aligned per-case traces, analysis scripts and study code to reproduce the central audit results.

</details>


### [5] [OT-CAL: Optimal-Transport-Guided Adaptive Sample Selection for Efficient Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41677)
*Suryansh Talukdar,Jin Soung Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OT-CAL，一种自适应数据选择方法，在训练中自动调整采样偏好，用五维信息性特征+课程向量+最优传输匹配选样，无需手工调度，节选50%反传样本即可提升精度并加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mini-batch训练中并非所有样本对学习贡献相同，可在信息性样本上做反传以降低计算量；但样本信息性随训练阶段变化（早期难样本、后期提升样本、末期类别多样性），现有方法依赖固定选择规则或昂贵的逐样本梯度计算，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 将每个样本表示为五个信息性特征（含难度、可学习性、不确定性等）；用一个课程向量刻画当前偏好的样本特性；基于最优传输的匹配按样本与目标的接近程度分配选择权重；再用被选样本更新课程向量，形成无需人工调度的反馈闭环。

Result: 在三个标准视觉学习基准上，每个mini-batch仅选50%样本时，OT-CAL较AdaSelection提升1.3–2.1个百分点，且相比全数据训练达到1.18–1.24倍加速。

Conclusion: OT-CAL通过特征化表示、课程向量与最优传输匹配的自适应反馈机制，实现了随训练动态变化的数据选择，在精度和效率上均优于基线，且免除手工设计调度。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for image recognition are typically trained using mini-batches, with a forward pass to compute predictions and losses followed by a more computationally expensive backward pass to update model parameters. However, not all training samples contribute equally to learning, suggesting that computation can be reduced by performing backward passes only on informative samples. The challenge is that sample informativeness changes throughout training: difficult samples may be valuable early on, improving samples become important later, and class diversity becomes increasingly relevant toward the end. Existing methods often rely on fixed selection rules or computationally expensive per-sample gradient calculations. We propose OT-CAL, an adaptive data selection method that automatically adjusts its sampling preferences during training. OT-CAL represents each sample using five informativeness features, including difficulty, learnability, and uncertainty. A curriculum vector describes the currently preferred sample characteristics, and optimal-transport-based matching assigns selection weights according to how closely samples match this target. The curriculum vector is then updated using the selected samples, forming a feedback loop that adapts without a manually defined schedule. Experiments on three standard visual learning benchmarks show that, when selecting 50% of each mini-batch, OT-CAL improves performance by 1.3--2.1 points over AdaSelection while achieving 1.18--1.24 times faster training than full-data training.

</details>


### [6] [Coarse3D: Controllable and Faithful Sparse Structure Generation for Image-to-3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41231)
*Gaige Wang,Bowen Pan,Hao Ren,Jiangjing Lyu,Chengfei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Coarse3D，一种从任意无位姿图像生成朝向可控、结构忠实体素几何的稀疏结构生成模型，可直接替换现有粗到细3D生成流程的第一阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到细的3D原生生成高度依赖第一阶段稀疏结构，但现有粗生成器存在两点缺陷：语义规范化模糊导致朝向难以控制；2D-3D grounding 薄弱导致结构与输入图像不一致。

Method: 三项设计：(1) 首帧锚定的规范空间，使生成与第一输入视角对齐同时保留物体中心归一化；(2) 双塔DiT，将生成与辅助前馈重建耦合以学习空间 grounded 特征；(3) 空间先验引导注意力，用像素到体素对应关系正则化 cross-attention。

Result: 实验表明显著提升稀疏结构质量，并持续改善最终3D生成，取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Coarse3D作为可插拔的粗阶段模块，通过朝向控制与2D-3D空间对齐两方面改进，为粗到细3D生成提供更强的结构先验。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine 3D-native generation relies heavily on the first-stage sparse structure, which determines the global orientation and overall geometry for subsequent refinement. However, existing coarse generators suffer from two key limitations: their orientation is difficult to control due to ambiguous semantic canonicalization, and their structures are often inconsistent with input images due to weak 2D-3D grounding. We propose Coarse3D, a sparse-structure generation model that produces orientation-controllable and structurally faithful voxel geometry from arbitrary unposed images. Coarse3D introduces three designs: a first-frame anchored canonical space that aligns generation with the first input view while preserving object-centric normalization; a dual-tower DiT that couples generation with auxiliary feed-forward reconstruction to learn spatially grounded features; and spatial-prior guided attention that regularizes cross-attention with pixel-to-voxel correspondence. Coarse3D can directly replace the coarse stage of existing pipelines. Experiments show that it substantially improves sparse structure quality and consistently enhances final 3D generation, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [7] [GenDSR: Transferring Representations from Video Generation Models for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41314)
*Ke Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenDSR 框架，将视频生成模型（VGM）的中间表征迁移到视觉语言模型（VLM）中，以增强动态空间推理能力，在 DSR-Bench 上达到 67.2% 的新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态空间推理是连接视觉智能与物理世界的关键能力，但 VLM 在此方面仍有困难：它需要既保持场景状态、又跟踪空间关系随时间的变化。与此同时，为合成时间连贯世界动态而训练的视频生成模型蕴含丰富的时空表征，可作为可利用的先验来源。

Method: GenDSR 不把 VGM 特征当作单一整体先验，而是构建两条互补分支：raw 分支保留整体生成上下文，delta 分支强调时间变化；随后用任务条件化的逐 token 门控（task-conditioned tokenwise gates）融合两路信号，并将融合结果注入 VLM 原生的视觉表征中。

Result: 在 DSR-Bench 上取得 67.2% 的成绩，超过此前最佳结果，建立新的 state of the art。

Conclusion: 通过双分支分解与任务条件门控融合的方式迁移 VGM 时空表征，可有效提升 VLM 的动态空间推理性能，验证了生成模型表征作为空间推理先验的价值。

Abstract: Dynamic spatial reasoning is essential for connecting visual intelligence to thephysical world, yet remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs), which must preserve scene state while tracking how spatial relations evolve over time. Meanwhile, video generation models (VGMs), trained to synthesize temporally coherent world dynamics, offer a rich source of spatiotemporal representations. We present GenDSR, a framework that transfers intermediate representations from VGM into VLM for dynamic spatial reasoning. Rather than treating a VGM feature as a monolithic prior, GenDSR constructs two complementary branches: a raw branch preserving holistic generative context and a delta branch emphasizing temporal variation. Task-conditioned tokenwise gates then fuse both signals and inject the result into the native visual representation. On DSR-Bench, GenDSR achieves 67.2%, surpassing the previous best result and establishing a new state of the art.

</details>


### [8] [From Evaluation to Evolution: Benchmarking Image Editing and Evolving Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41210)
*Jun He,Canmiao Fu,Ying Zhang,Zhipeng Huang,Chenyu Mu,Yazhen Xie,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Weijia Li,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出细粒度图像编辑基准WeGenEditBench（2,740实例、148任务类型）与面向递归自我改进的多智能体进化框架WeGenEvoStudio，实现从原子需求级诊断到定向数据构建与再评估的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像编辑需同时满足多重变换、内容保持与参考约束，而聚合分数易掩盖局部未达标项；有效评估须定位局部失败并转化为模型改进优先级。

Method: WeGenEditBench覆盖单图、文本密集与多参考编辑；以跨模型共享的预生成检查清单将请求分解为原子需求，先做条目级扣分与视觉证据标注，再汇总为编辑执行、内容保持、视觉质量与任务特定正确性四维分数。配套WeGenEvoStudio统一多智能体框架，整合模型条件化失败诊断、检索支撑的数据构建、监督适配与再评估。

Result: 15个配置中，GPT-Image-2单图编辑Overall 4.076领先，Seedream-5.0-Pro文本密集编辑4.291领先；四个基准上的进化实验中，FLUX.2 [dev]在其Top-5单图目标上Overall由1.083升至3.517，Qwen-Image-Edit-2511提升13.2%。

Conclusion: 可解释的条目级评估与反馈驱动学习可形成闭环，为构建更可靠的图像生成/编辑模型提供基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Real-world image editing requires coordinating multiple visual transformations with content preservation and reference constraints, yet aggregate scores can readily obscure unmet local requirements. Effective evaluation must therefore identify local failures and translate them into priorities for subsequent model improvement. We introduce WeGenEditBench, comprising 2,740 evaluation instances and 148 task types across single-image, text-intensive, and multi-reference editing. Pre-generated checklists shared across models decompose each request into atomic requirements; item-level deductions and visual evidence are established first and then summarized into scores for edit execution, preservation, visual quality, and task-specific correctness. To support the experience-acquisition component of recursive self-improvement (RSI), we develop WeGenEvoStudio, a unified multi-agent harness for RSI-oriented learning that integrates model-conditioned failure diagnosis, retrieval-grounded data construction, supervised adaptation, and re-evaluation. Among 15 evaluated configurations, GPT-Image-2 leads single-image editing with an Overall score of 4.076, while Seedream-5.0-Pro leads text-intensive editing at 4.291. Evolution experiments across four generation and editing benchmarks demonstrate substantial targeted gains: on their report-selected Top-5 single-image targets, FLUX.2 [dev] improves Overall from 1.083 to 3.517, while Qwen-Image-Edit-2511 gains 13.2%. Together, our benchmark and harness provide a foundation for developing more reliable image models through interpretable evaluation and feedback-driven learning.

</details>


### [9] [Beyond CLIP: Calibrated (Few-Shot) Defect Detection With System One Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41199)
*Fadel M. Megahed,Ying-Ju Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究在极低样本（k=2 参考图像/类）条件下的制造视觉检测，提出 Jev 与 Clef-Flash 的 k-shot 学习框架、文本描述智能体自动精炼方法及缺陷概率校准方法，在五个制造案例中验证其优于 CLIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉检测训练需大量良品与缺陷图像；即便 CLIP 类 ViT 可降至每类 50–100 张，高良率或小批量制造中仍难以采集足够缺陷样本（如 50 张）。因此需探索仅用极少参考图（少至每类 2 张）即可完成分类/检测的方案。

Method: 评估两种结构化决策模型（Jev、Clef-Flash）；提出三项贡献：(a) 面向图像分类/检测的 k-shot 学习框架；(b) 智能体（agentic）方法自动精炼引导分类的文本描述；(c) 对输出缺陷概率进行校准以提升检测决策可靠性。在五个制造案例中验证，并开源复现代码、R 包 "decisions" 及无代码 Web 应用。

Result: 每类仅用 2 张参考图时，Jev（0.75–1.00）与 Clef-Flash（0.71–0.99）测试准确率均高于同条件 CLIP（0.67–0.84）；智能体文本精炼在 8 个模型-应用组合中的 6 个显著提升准确率；校准方法在 15 个模型-应用配置中的 13 个降低 Brier 分数。

Conclusion: 在极低样本（k=2）条件下，结构化决策模型结合文本精炼与概率校准可有效支持制造视觉检测，并具备可复现性与实践可及性（开源包与 Web 应用）。

Abstract: Cameras and vision sensors are widely used for product and process inspection in manufacturing environments. Traditionally, this process required thousands of images of both nominal and defective products to train such models. Recent research shows that vision transformer (ViT) models, such as CLIP, can reduce this requirement to 50-100 images per nominal and defective class. However, collecting even 50 defective images can be difficult in high-yield or low-volume manufacturing processes. In this paper, we examine the utility of two recently proposed structured decision models (Jev and Clef-Flash) in applications using as few as k=2 reference images per class at inference. In the process, we provide three main contributions: (a) a framework for k-shot learning of these models for image classification/inspection applications; (b) an agentic approach for automatically refining the text descriptions that guide image classification in these decision models; and (c) an approach to calibrate the resulting defect probabilities to improve their reliability for inspection decisions. We test these contributions in five manufacturing case studies. Using two reference images per class, both Jev and Clef-Flash outperform CLIP's test accuracies with the same references (0.75-1.00 [Jev] and 0.71-0.99 [Clef-Flash] vs 0.67-0.84 [CLIP]). Furthermore, we show that our agentic refinement of text descriptions significantly improves accuracy across 6 of 8 model-application combinations, and our calibration approach reduces the Brier score in 13 of the 15 model-application configurations. To support the reproducibility of our work, we provide our replication code and have created the R "decisions" package for evaluating additional models. We also provide a publicly available web app that allows practitioners to inspect their own images with no code.

</details>


### [10] [Dreams Are Cheaper Than Flights: Curated World-Model Data Improves Aerial Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41030)
*Rugved Katole,Vedant Patil,Jenna Kline,Christopher Stewart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 用7B视频世界模型以真实无人机野生动物视频为种子生成合成帧，可将训练集翻倍以上；在留出真实视频上mAP@50-95提升4.3%（与真实数据混合）和10.3%（替换真实数据），城区基准最高提升33.5%，而成本仅为GPU约\$1,100，低于野外采集人力\$3,200。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知日益依赖数据，但野外实地数据采集速度慢、成本高且有时具有破坏性；作者希望验证视频世界模型生成的合成数据能否在实用价值上媲美真实野外数据，并检验其所训练检测器在真实场景中的表现，从而把数据成本从“田野劳动”转移到“计算”。

Method: 以野生动物无人机视频的帧作为种子，驱动一个70亿参数视频世界模型，在部署所用传感器、飞行高度与环境条件下将已录场景外延为新视频；经人工筛选整理出合成帧，将训练集扩充一倍以上；随后在同一批无人机留出视频及城区基准上评估检测器（mAP@50-95），并核算GPU时间成本与野外采集人力成本。同时报告专家对生成片段的拒收比例。

Result: 在来自同一批无人机的留出视频上，mAP@50-95在合成帧加入真实数据时提升4.3%，在替代真实数据时提升10.3%；城区基准上最高提升33.5%。尽管专家估计拒收约87.5%的生成片段，可用数据仅耗约\$1,100 GPU时间，而一个效用更低的真实训练集仅野外人力就超过\$3,200。

Conclusion: 视频世界模型生成的合成数据可在效用上匹配甚至超越等量真实野外数据，所训检测器在真实部署视频上具备有效性；合成数据实质上是把数据成本由野外作业转移至计算资源，为野外机器人感知提供了一条更具成本效益的数据扩展路径，但其高拒收率也说明生成数据仍需严格的筛选与质量控制。

Abstract: Robot perception is increasingly data driven, yet field data is slow, costly, and sometimes disruptive to collect. Video world models offer a cheaper source: seeded with real frames, they extend a recorded scene into new footage with the deployment's sensor, altitude, and environment. We ask whether such synthetic data matches the utility of real field data and whether detectors trained with it perform in the field. Seeding a 7B-parameter world model with wildlife drone video, we more than double the training set with curated frames. On held-out videos from the same drones, mAP@50--95 rises 4.3\% when these frames are added to real data and 10.3\% when they replace it, and up to 33.5\% on an urban benchmark. Although an expert rejects an estimated 87.5\% of generated clips, the data costs about \$1,100 of GPU time, against more than \$3,200 of field labor alone for a less useful real training set. Synthetic data thus shifts data costs from fieldwork to compute.

</details>


### [11] [WebOverlay: High-Throughput Vision-Only Distillation for Extreme Aspect-Ratio Web Overlay Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41037)
*Jiannan Huang,Jerry Ding,Li Yu,Zhenting Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WebOverlay：一种仅用视觉、通过从前沿视觉语言教师模型蒸馏得到的高吞吐网页遮挡元素检测器；采用动态切块+边界仲裁处理极端纵横比页面渲染，性能超越DOM启发式并逼近专有MLLM，吞吐量与成本显著优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页覆盖层（Cookie同意横幅、订阅提示、年龄门槛等）会阻碍自动化爬虫并劣化下游文档表示。专有多模态大模型虽能准确识别但延迟与推理成本高，无法支撑网页级规模化摄取；轻量视觉模型在极端纵向纵横比的整页渲染上因各向同性缩放丢失关键文本特征而产生大量误报。

Method: 提出WebOverlay，从前沿视觉语言教师模型蒸馏训练纯视觉检测器。核心设计：(1) 动态切块策略，将极端纵横比网页渲染划分为保持纵横比、带空间重叠的patch；(2) 纵横比感知的边界仲裁机制，消除切块边界伪影；(3) 学生模型仅依赖视觉线索，绕开DOM树解析，消除跨模态延迟开销。

Result: 显著优于基于DOM的启发式方法；在精确率与召回率上与重量级专有MLLM基线具有竞争力，同时吞吐量高出数个数量级并显著降低部署成本。

Conclusion: WebOverlay表明，通过蒸馏结合动态切块与边界仲裁，纯视觉紧凑模型可在网页级规模化场景下替代专有MLLM完成遮挡元素检测，兼顾精度、吞吐与成本。

Abstract: Web overlays, such as cookie consent banners, newsletter prompts, and age gates, obstruct automated web crawlers and degrade downstream document representations. While proprietary multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can accurately identify overlays, their latency and inference cost preclude web-scale ingestion. Conversely, lightweight vision models struggle with full-page renderings characterized by extreme vertical aspect ratios, where standard isotropic resizing collapses critical text features and causes false positives. In this work, we present WebOverlay, a high-throughput vision-only detector trained via distillation from a frontier vision-language teacher. We establish a dynamic tiling strategy that partitions extreme aspect-ratio web renders into aspect-preserving patches with spatial overlap, coupled with an aspect-ratio-aware boundary adjudication mechanism to eliminate boundary artifacts. To eliminate cross-modal latency overheads, our student model relies purely on visual cues, bypassing DOM tree parsing. WebOverlay significantly outperforms DOM-based heuristics and achieves competitive precision and recall against heavy proprietary MLLM baselines while delivering multiple orders of magnitude higher throughput and substantially reduced deployment costs.

</details>


### [12] [Attention Maps Know When to Think: Training-Free Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41618)
*Chanseul Cho,Jaesung Jun,Dongheon Lee,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的自适应框架DA-CoT，用注意力图熵判断样本难度，按需触发CoT提示，并加入双向反思机制，在推理分割任务上兼顾SOTA性能与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT已成为多模态大模型的标准推理策略，但无差别使用会引入不必要推理（"过度思考"），在推理分割中导致查询意图与视觉定位失配、性能下降。因此需要按输入内在难度选择性触发CoT。

Method: 发现注意力图熵能可靠反映任务难度（低熵=置信且定位集中，高熵=不确定、可从CoT受益），据此用熵信号决定是否施加CoT；并引入双向反思（Bidirectional Rethinking）机制，在初次输出失败时自动翻转推理决策，以从过度思考/思考不足导致的失败中恢复。整体为免训练框架。

Result: 在推理分割基准上取得SOTA性能，同时显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 无差别CoT并非最优；基于注意力熵的难度感知自适应触发与双向反思，可有效缓解过度思考与思考不足，在性能与效率间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard reasoning strategy in Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs), showing strong performance in complex vision-language tasks such as reasoning segmentation. However, our analysis reveals that indiscriminate CoT usage can lead to degraded performance by inducing unnecessary reasoning, a phenomenon we term overthinking. In reasoning segmentation, overthinking often leads to misalignment between the query's intent and visual grounding. To address this, we propose Difficulty-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT), a training-free adaptive framework that selectively triggers CoT based on the input's intrinsic difficulty. We find that the entropy of the model's attention maps reliably reflects task difficulty: low entropy indicates confident, localized grounding, while high entropy suggests uncertainty and potential benefit from CoT. DA-CoT uses this entropy signal to determine whether to apply CoT prompting. Additionally, we introduce a Bidirectional Rethinking mechanism that enables the model to automatically flip its reasoning decision when the initial output fails, enhancing robustness by recovering from failures induced by overthinking and underthinking. DA-CoT demonstrates its effectiveness by achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Reasoning Segmentation benchmarks while significantly improving inference efficiency.

</details>


### [13] [Learning Clean Generative Processes from Noisy Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42036)
*Fabian Kruse,Lorenzo Baldassari,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅用独立噪声观测，通过 Noise2Noise 回归学习噪声水平以上的后验预测，并以一致性约束推广到干净图像分布，从而在泊松、二项、Gamma、负二项噪声下实现生成式建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散及非高斯噪声推广的生成模型需要在每个噪声水平上获得后验预测，但实际只能获取带噪声观测，无法直接得到干净训练数据。

Method: 先用 Noise2Noise 回归从独立噪声视图学习后验预测（仅在训练噪声水平及以上有效）；再引入一致性关系——预测应等于继续采样过程后的期望——推导其唯一确定干净图像分布续延的条件，并在 Poisson、binomial、Gamma、negative-binomial 测量上验证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、光子计数显微成像和 SAR 上，所需后验预测可仅由噪声视图学得；一致性关系修复了低于数据噪声水平的续延，而无一致性时该续延严重退化。

Conclusion: Noise2Noise 与一致性约束结合，可在仅有噪声观测的非高斯噪声设定下学习生成模型，并恢复干净图像分布。

Abstract: We study how to learn a generative model of clean images using only noisy observations. Diffusion processes and their generalizations to non-Gaussian noise require posterior predictions at every noise level. We first show that it is possible to learn these predictions from independent noisy views using Noise2Noise regression, but only at or above the noise level of the training data. To learn the predictions below that level, needed to generate clean images, we impose consistency: a prediction should equal its expectation after continuing the sampling process. We derive conditions under which consistency uniquely determines the continuation to the clean image distribution and verify them for Poisson, binomial, Gamma, and negative-binomial measurements. Experiments on CIFAR-10, photon-counting microscopy, and synthetic aperture radar show that the required posterior predictions can be learned from noisy views alone and that the consistency relation repairs the continuation beyond the noise level of the data, which severely deteriorates without consistency.

</details>


### [14] [PGGT-Loc: Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer for Feed-Forward Camera Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41833)
*Hanqiao Ye,Yuzhou Liu,Mengqi Rong,Jian Liu,Yangdong Liu,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出“图元接地定位”新概念，并用 Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer（PGGT）在平面图元上实现单图前馈 6-DoF 相机定位，在稠密表面地图与极简布局地图上均刷新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机定位多为多阶段流水线，依赖专门且复杂的专用地图，构建、存储与长期维护成本高；而结构良好、以紧凑且富表达力图元组织场景的地图往往易于获得，蕴含人类可解读的丰富结构与语义线索，但因图元与视觉观测之间存在显著模态鸿沟，其直接用于定位的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出以“查询观测接地到地图图元”来估计相机位姿的新定位范式；在平面图元场景下实例化为 PGGT 网络：输入单张查询图像与一组平面地图图元，训练网络预测多个定位目标，实现前馈 6-DoF 相机定位，并可泛化至未见环境；架构简洁且可扩展。

Result: 在稠密表面地图与极简布局模型两类地图上，均将定位性能推进至当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 验证了直接以结构化地图图元为定位依据的可行性与优越性，表明简洁可扩展的图元接地 Transformer 架构可跨地图表征与未见环境取得领先定位精度；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Camera localization methods are mostly multi-stage pipelines relying on dedicated and complex maps that are costly to construct, store, and maintain over time. Alternatively, well-structured maps that organize scenes with compact yet expressive primitives are often readily available, providing rich structural and semantic cues that humans can interpret. However, their potential for direct camera localization remains largely unexplored due to the significant modality gap between map primitives and visual observations. This motivates a new concept for localization, which estimates camera poses by grounding query observations to map primitives. In this paper, we instantiate the concept in the context of planar primitives with PGGT, the Primitive-Grounded Geometry Transformer network. Given a single query image and a set of planar map primitives, the network is trained to predict multiple localization targets for feed-forward 6-DoF camera localization that generalizes to unseen environments. With this straightforward and scalable architecture, our method pushes the state-of-the-art localization performance on both dense surface maps and minimal layout models. The code and models are available at https://github.com/3dv-casia/PGGT-Loc

</details>


### [15] [GazeRIB: Systematic Evaluation of Multi-Reader Gaze-Guided Chest X-ray Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42152)
*Nattakorn Kittisut,Roy M. Gabriel,Marly van Assen,Mohammadreza Zandehshahvar,Carlo N. De Cecco,Elizabeth A. Krupinski,Ali Adibi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估GazeRIB方法中读者小组构成对眼动监督价值的影响：多读者小组显著优于无眼动基线，三读者即可捕获大部分收益，但迁移性依赖外部队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生的视觉注意力可用于监督医学图像学习，但读者小组（panel）构成如何影响其监督价值尚未被检验。

Method: 提出GazeRIB（Gaze-guided Radiologist-Informed Blackout）：训练时遮蔽放射科医生未注视区域，推理时使用未遮蔽图像。8名专家重复阅读同一80张胸片，比较个体读者与3、5、8人小组，覆盖7种架构与10个随机种子，共910个模型，在独立2,897张胸片队列上评估四级严重程度预测。

Result: 所有小组较无眼动基线宏F1提升5.33–7.47个百分点（Bonferroni校正p<0.007），个体读者未达显著；3人小组可捕获8人小组在宏F1、ROC-AUC和AU-PRC上73–80%的增益。无重训练外部验证中，四个小组在SIIM-FISABIO-RSNA上ROC-AUC提升+1.93至+4.24 pp（p≤0.004）；在MIMIC-Eye上无小组显著优于基线，而单读者平均下降1.40 pp（p=0.008）。

Conclusion: 读者构成会影响模型性能，小组级眼动监督优于个体读者，但迁移效果取决于目标队列。

Abstract: Radiologists' visual attention can supervise medical image learning, but how reader panel composition affects its value remains untested. We evaluated GazeRIB (Gaze-guided Radiologist-Informed Blackout), which masks unviewed regions during training and uses unmasked images at inference. Eight experts repeatedly read the same 80 chest X-rays (CXRs). We compared individual readers with three-, five- and eight-reader panels across seven architectures and 10 seeds, evaluating 910 models on a separate 2,897-CXR cohort for four-class severity prediction. Every panel improved macro F1 over the no-gaze baseline by 5.33-7.47 percentage points (pp; Bonferroni-adjusted p < 0.007); no individual reader reached significance on this endpoint. Three readers captured 73-80% of the eight-reader gain in macro F1, the area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) and the area under the precision-recall curve (AU-PRC). On external validation without retraining, all four panels improved ROC-AUC on SIIM-FISABIO-RSNA by +1.93 to +4.24 pp (p <= 0.004); on MIMIC-Eye no panel differed significantly from baseline, whereas the single-reader average declined (-1.40 pp, p = 0.008). Reader composition therefore influenced performance, although transfer depended on the cohort.

</details>


### [16] [ReLift: Dense 3D Tracking as Lifted Trajectory Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42136)
*Assaf Singer,Maroun Khriesh,Noam Rotstein,Yoni Kasten,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReLift，将稠密 3D 跟踪重构为几何轨迹的修复与补全，仅用几何输入训练轨迹校正器，可即插即用地提升任意 2D 跟踪器与几何模型组合的 lifted 轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体式联合学习 3D 跟踪依赖稀缺的 4D 监督，训练目标过重；而预训练 2D 跟踪器 + 3D 几何模型的 lifted 轨迹已可与专用 3D 跟踪器相当，但继承对应与深度误差，遮挡时深度来自遮挡物而非被跟踪点。

Method: 前馈框架 ReLift：核心为轨迹校正器，沿轨迹方向与跨轨迹方向交替注意力，保留可靠观测、修复受损位置、补全缺失运动；仅以几何输入（轨迹、深度等）为条件，并用模拟 lifting 误差的 3D 轨迹直接训练，训练与图像、具体跟踪器/几何模型解耦。

Result: 在 WorldTrack 上取得最优平均精度，全点 APD 较最强基线提升 2.1、动态点提升 3.0；快速配置与领先 3D 跟踪器相当且快于 4RC；对多种跟踪器与几何提供者零重训即稳定提升。

Conclusion: 把 4D 监督集中于轨迹精炼可获得可复用模型，随 2D 跟踪与 3D 几何组件的进步直接受益，是解耦式 3D 跟踪的有效范式。

Abstract: Dense 3D tracking from monocular video requires establishing point correspondences over time, estimating their depth, and inferring their trajectories during occlusion. Existing methods typically learn these tasks jointly, a demanding objective given the scarcity of 4D supervision. We show that strong pretrained 2D trackers and 3D geometry models provide an effective foundation: lifted trajectories, with occluded segments simply interpolated, already rival dedicated 3D trackers using the same geometry. However, lifted trajectories inherit correspondence and depth errors, and under occlusion, their depth comes from the occluder rather than the tracked point. We introduce ReLift, an efficient feed-forward framework that recasts dense 3D tracking as geometric trajectory repair and completion. At its core, a trajectory corrector alternates attention along and across trajectories to preserve reliable observations, repair corrupted positions, and complete missing motion. Using only geometric inputs allows us to train directly on 3D trajectories with simulated lifting errors, decoupling training from images and from any specific tracker or geometry model. Concentrating 4D supervision on trajectory refinement yields a reusable model that consistently improves lifted trajectories across trackers and geometry providers without retraining, so it benefits directly from future advances in either component. On WorldTrack, ReLift achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy, improving on the strongest baselines by 2.1 APD on all points and 3.0 on dynamic points, with faster configurations remaining competitive with leading 3D trackers while running faster than 4RC. Visit our supplementry page for more examples and code.

</details>


### [17] [Think in 360°: Benchmarking and Bootstrapping Panoramic Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42082)
*Lidong Lu,Lidong Zou,Xinyi Mao,Jiaxin Lv,Zhenquan Li,Liangrui Dong,Tong Lu,Guo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个全人工标注的全景视频理解基准PanoVideoBench（1000题、5类15型），并构建首个基于真实全景视频的推理数据集PanoVideoCoT（13,465条QA-CoT），通过全景CoT SFT与证据优先的在线策略自蒸馏训练PanoVideoReasoner，在基准上达55.7%，较骨干提升12.5分、超前SOTA 2.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景视频涵盖相机周围所有方向，关键证据散布于不同视角与时刻，要求模型跨视球与时间整合观测。现有基准与推理数据缺乏对跨视角追踪与球-时证据整合能力的系统诊断与训练支撑，难以暴露和提升相应短板。

Method: 1) 构建PanoVideoBench：1000题、5大类15种题型，含目标搜索与全景能力的诊断性标注；2) 评估11个专有、开源及全景专用模型；3) 通过球-时接地合成（sphere-time-grounded synthesis），将经验证的全景观测与成功的智能体探索转换为13,465条QA-CoT对，构建PanoVideoCoT；4) 以通用视频MLLM为起点，经全景CoT监督微调与Evidence-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation（Vision-OPD的OPSD在全景场景下的实例化，教师用已验证透视视图引导学生自生成轨迹）训练得到PanoVideoReasoner。

Result: PanoVideoReasoner在PanoVideoBench上取得55.7%，较骨干模型提升12.5个百分点，较此前SOTA提升2.6个百分点；增益集中于基准所揭示的空间、跨视角与时间能力维度。11个模型的评测显示跨视角追踪与球-时证据整合是最弱能力。

Conclusion: 球-时接地的监督信号可使通用视频MLLM获得全景推理能力；基准诊断与推理数据、训练方法形成闭环，短板定位与能力提升相互印证。

Abstract: A panoramic video captures all directions around the camera, but relevant evidence can appear in different directions and at different times. Understanding it requires models to connect observations across the viewing sphere and time. We introduce PanoVideoBench, the first manually annotated benchmark for comprehensive panoramic video understanding. Its 1,000 questions span five categories and 15 question types, with diagnostic annotations for target search and panoramic capabilities. Evaluation of 11 proprietary, open-source, and panoramic-specialized models identifies cross-view tracking and sphere-time evidence integration as their weakest capabilities. To strengthen these capabilities, we construct PanoVideoCoT, the first reasoning dataset grounded in real panoramic video, through sphere-time-grounded synthesis that converts verified panoramic observations and successful agentic explorations into 13,465 QA-CoT pairs. Starting from a general video MLLM, PanoVideoReasoner learns from this corpus through Panoramic CoT SFT and Evidence-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation, a panoramic instantiation of Vision-OPD's OPSD in which the teacher uses verified perspective views to guide student-generated trajectories. It reaches 55.7% on PanoVideoBench, improving the backbone by 12.5 points and the previous SOTA by 2.6. The gains concentrate on the same spatial, cross-view, and temporal capabilities exposed by the benchmark, showing that grounded sphere-time supervision can equip a general video MLLM with panoramic reasoning.

</details>


### [18] [Any Forcing: Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42031)
*Jiahua Xu,Yijie Fang,Nannan Wang,Xunhao Lin,Li Cao,Lei Hao,Mingrui Zhu,Fei Gao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Any Forcing统一框架，实现自回归视频生成的任意步数通用性与单步稳定性，单卡H100上短/长视频分别达20.9/24.7 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成方法受限于固定采样步数，难以灵活权衡质量与效率；而单步生成存在视觉质量下降与长序列误差累积问题，导致内容漂移与生成崩溃。

Method: 1) 统一对抗判别器，联合执行真实分布对齐、步间一致性对齐与首帧质量增强，兼顾灵活采样与单步质量；2) 免训练的自适应长期记忆选择机制，基于全局语义相关性与局部价值贡献筛选历史状态，并重映射其时间位置以保留记忆缓存中的时序关系。

Result: 实现高质量且稳定的单步自回归视频生成，在单张H100 GPU上短视频与长视频生成分别达到20.9 FPS和24.7 FPS。

Conclusion: 统一的对抗判别与自适应记忆选择可同时解决任意步采样的灵活性与单步长视频生成的稳定性问题，为高效自回归视频生成提供可行方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation enables efficient inference through streaming prediction. However, existing methods are typically restricted to fixed sampling steps, limiting the flexibility to balance generation quality and efficiency, while one-step generation suffers from degraded visual quality and error accumulation over long sequences, leading to content drift and generation collapse. To address these issues, we propose Any Forcing, a unified framework for achieving Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability. Specifically, we introduce a unified adversarial discriminator that jointly performs real-distribution alignment, inter-step consistency alignment, and first-frame quality enhancement, enabling flexible sampling while preserving one-step generation quality. Furthermore, we propose a training-free adaptive long-term memory selection mechanism that selects historical states based on global semantic relevance and local value contribution, while remapping their temporal positions to preserve temporal relationships within the memory cache, thereby enabling stable one-step long-video generation. Experiments demonstrate that Any Forcing achieves high-quality and stable one-step autoregressive video generation, achieving 20.9 FPS and 24.7 FPS for short and long video generation on a single H100 GPU, respectively.

</details>


### [19] [MATS: A Multi-Agent Framework for Controllable and Reflective Tumor Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41159)
*Zhongnan Shi,Keyang Zhou,Siyi Li,Qichao Ying,Xinchen Ma,Shuyi Gong,Wei Tang,Sheng Li,Zhenxing Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MATS：一个多智能体框架，实现文本可控、跨器官的肿瘤合成，可对属性级生成进行闭环质量校验，并在单源数据训练下提升跨域下游分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像肿瘤合成可缓解临床AI的数据稀缺问题，但现有扩散方法存在三点不足：缺乏细粒度属性级可控性、缺乏闭环质量验证、且不了解目标分布中哪些形态模式被欠表达。

Method: 给定目标疾病，先在其细粒度属性空间（覆盖肿瘤几何与外观）上刻画形态分布，构建放大欠表达子类、保持常见子类主导的生成计划；随后定位目标器官并提出解剖学合理的放置位置；执行两阶段扩散，分别以对应属性为条件生成肿瘤掩膜与渲染纹理；最后在合成全过程的三个检查点用闭环反思机制校验属性对齐，检测到错位时给出针对性反馈以引导重生成。

Result: 仅在单源胰腺神经内分泌肿瘤数据集上训练，MATS在同域及LiTS、KiTS、MSD-Pancreas等基准上的下游分割均有稳定提升，而多数先前的生成式增强方法甚至低于仅用真实数据训练。

Conclusion: 属性可控的规划式生成加闭环质量校验，可有效提升合成肿瘤的可用性，并具备跨器官泛化能力，为医学图像数据增强提供了新范式。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic tumors on medical images helps address data scarcity for clinical AI. Existing diffusion-based methods, however, lack fine-grained attribute-level controllability, closed-loop quality verification, and awareness of which morphological patterns are underrepresented in the target distribution. We present MATS, a multi-agent framework for text-controllable, cross-organ tumor synthesis. Given a target disease, MATS first profiles its morphological distribution over a fine-grained attribute space covering tumor geometry and appearance, and constructs a generation plan that amplifies underrepresented subtypes while keeping common ones dominant. It then localizes the target organ, proposes anatomically valid placement, and executes two-stage diffusion to produce the tumor mask and render texture, each conditioned on the corresponding attributes. A closed-loop reflective mechanism verifies attribute alignment at three checkpoints throughout synthesis and provides targeted feedback to guide re-generation when misalignment is detected. Trained exclusively on a single-source pancreatic neuroendocrine tumor dataset, MATS consistently improves downstream segmentation both in-domain and across LiTS, KiTS, and MSD-Pancreas benchmarks, whereas most prior generative augmentation methods fall below real-only training.

</details>


### [20] [An Empirical Study on Learning Rooftop Solar Potential from Satellite Imagery Directly Without Explicit Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41111)
*Joshua Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRSP：端到端直接从卫星影像与区域日照信息预测屋顶级太阳能输出，无需LiDAR/DSM等几何中间表示与几何监督；并构建约6万（美国中西部）与8.7万（德州）屋顶的两个数据集，系统分析地理分布、模型深度、分辨率、日照条件化与热力图回归目标的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有屋顶光伏潜力估计依赖LiDAR、DSM，或需先显式重建屋顶几何再估计太阳能，流程复杂、成本高、难以大规模推广。作者希望验证能否跳过几何中间表示，直接从2D影像学习应用级太阳能信息。

Method: 端到端公式化：输入卫星影像＋粗粒度区域日照信息，直接输出可用屋顶掩膜与空间太阳能目标（热力图回归）；不预测朝向、坡度、高度或DSM，也无几何中间监督。构建程序化数据策管流程，将Google Solar API的逐板信息转换为屋顶掩膜与空间能量目标，形成两个地理上独立的数据集。围绕地理训练分布、模型深度、影像分辨率、区域日照条件化及热力图回归损失进行系统消融分析。

Result: 在约6万（中西部）与8.7万（德州）屋顶数据上验证了DRSP的可行性，并量化了各设计因素（训练地理分布、深度、分辨率、日照条件化、回归目标）对性能的影响。

Conclusion: 无需显式几何中间表示，即可从2D影像中学到有用的屋顶尺度太阳能信息，为大规模太阳能评估提供了更简洁的路径；代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Rooftop solar potential estimation often relies on LiDAR, digital surface models (DSMs), or pipelines that explicitly reconstruct roof geometry before estimating solar potential. We propose Direct Rooftop Solar Prediction (DRSP), an end-to-end formulation that maps satellite imagery and coarse regional sunlight information directly to application-level solar outputs. DRSP neither requires explicit geometric representations as input nor predicts roof orientation, slope, height, DSMs, or other geometric quantities as intermediate outputs, and therefore requires no geometric intermediate supervision. To enable large-scale study of this formulation, we develop a programmatic data-curation pipeline that converts per-panel information from the Google Solar API into usable-rooftop masks and spatial solar-energy targets, yielding two geographically distinct datasets containing approximately 60K rooftops from the U.S. Midwest and 87K from Texas. We demonstrate the feasibility of DRSP and systematically characterize the effects of geographic training distribution, model depth, image resolution, regional sunlight conditioning, and heatmap regression objectives. Overall, our results suggest that useful rooftop-scale solar information can be learned directly from 2D imagery without explicit geometric intermediate representations, offering a simpler path toward large-scale solar assessment. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/joshualee124/direct_rooftop_solar_prediction.

</details>


### [21] [Drone-PUR: From Visual Clue Discovery to Understanding and Reasoning in UAV Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40801)
*Jiahe Wu,Bing Cao,Xingyue Guo,Ruipu Zhao,Beiming Yu,Jiekang Feng,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Drone-PUR数据集与基准，围绕视觉线索的发现、解释与整合对应感知、理解、推理三个能力维度，并基于Qwen3-VL分阶段微调得到UAV-PUR模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用领域视觉-语言模型的推理能力受限于不完整或不准确的视觉观察；在无人机场景中，小目标、细微属性和视角变化进一步加剧感知与理解难度，缺乏针对性评测与训练资源。

Method: 构建Drone-PUR数据集与基准，按感知/理解/推理三维度设计需多线索联合的推理样本；共11项任务、175k训练样本与20k测试样本（经人工审核），覆盖单图、图像序列、跨模态图像与多视角。评测22个通用VLM，并进行单任务微调以分析能力迁移；再以Qwen3-VL为基础，通过带辅助监督的分阶段微调构建UAV-PUR（4B/8B）。

Result: 22个通用VLM在空间判断、跨模态与跨视角推理上持续存在短板；跨模型结果显示更强的感知与更好的推理相关联，单任务微调揭示感知与推理间存在选择性的双向迁移；UAV-PUR 4B与8B在多项选择准确率上分别较预训练基线提升18.33与17.52个百分点。

Conclusion: 无人机场景下的视觉推理需以线索发现—解释—整合为主线强化感知与推理的耦合；所提基准可有效诊断通用VLM缺陷，分阶段微调能显著提升域内性能，数据集与项目资源已公开。

Abstract: The reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) can be limited by incomplete or inaccurate visual observations in the general domain. In unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) scenes, small targets, subtle attributes, and changing viewpoints further complicate perception and understanding. Therefore, we introduce Drone-PUR, a dataset and benchmark organized around visual clue discovery, interpretation, and integration, corresponding to the three capability dimensions of perception, understanding, and reasoning. Reasoning samples are designed to require models to combine multiple visual clues to derive visually grounded conclusions. Its 11 tasks provide 175k training and 20k test samples spanning single images, image sequences, cross-modal images, and multiple viewpoints. All test samples undergo human review. Evaluating 22 general-purpose VLMs reveals persistent limitations in spatial judgment and cross-modal and cross-view reasoning. Across models, stronger perception is associated with better reasoning, while single-task fine-tuning further reveals selective bidirectional transfer between these capabilities. To examine domain adaptation, we develop UAV-PUR from Qwen3-VL through staged fine-tuning with auxiliary supervision. Its 4B and 8B variants improve overall multiple-choice accuracy by 18.33 and 17.52 percentage points over their pretrained counterparts, respectively. The dataset and project resources are available at https://drone-pur.github.io.

</details>


### [22] [Hard Sample-Conditioned Decorrelated Attention for Robust Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41789)
*Tahar Chettaoui,Guray Ozgur,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViT-HarDis，将注意力头解耦正则化与样本难度挂钩：对与负类中心余弦相似度高的难样本施加更强的头去相关惩罚，从而在人脸识别中以无架构改动、无额外推理成本的方式提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT多头注意力在缺乏显式正则时易学到冗余而非互补的注意力模式；现有基于相关性的头解耦惩罚对所有样本统一施加，忽视了人脸识别中难样本（嵌入接近竞争身份）对优化与泛化的关键作用。

Method: ViT-HarDis以样本难度为条件调节注意力头解耦强度：对与负类中心余弦相似度高的样本施加强的头部去相关正则，在身份歧义最大处强制头特化；并发现仅对最后若干Transformer层施加解耦优于全网络正则。

Result: 在多数基准上优于基线与均匀解耦方法；分析表明其降低了难样本与竞争类中心的相似度并降低头间注意力相似度。WebFace4M训练下在IJB-B、IJB-C、TinyFace Rank-1上取得ViT类方法SOTA；MS1MV3训练下取得最佳IJB-B与TinyFace结果。

Conclusion: 样本难度条件化的注意力头解耦是一种无需架构修改与额外推理开销即可提升ViT人脸识别的有效正则策略，且局部（后层）施加更为高效。

Abstract: Multi-head attention in Vision Transformers (ViTs) allows independent attention patterns across heads, but without explicit regularization, heads may learn redundant patterns rather than complementary roles. Correlation-based penalties encourage head disentanglement, yet apply uniform regularization across all training samples. In face recognition (FR), hard samples whose embeddings lie close to competing identities are critical for optimization and generalization. Motivated by this, we propose ViT-HarDis, which conditions attention head disentanglement on sample difficulty: samples with high cosine similarity to negative class centers receive stronger head decorrelation, enforcing specialization where identity ambiguity is highest. Experiments show improvements over both the baseline and uniform disentanglement on most benchmarks. Our analysis shows that ViT-HarDis reduces the similarity of hard samples to competing class centers while decreasing inter-head attention similarity, and that applying disentanglement only to the final transformer layers outperforms regularizing the entire network. Trained on WebFace4M, ViT-HarDis achieves SOTA results among ViT-based methods on IJB-B, IJB-C, and TinyFace Rank-1, while achieving the best IJB-B and TinyFace results when trained on MS1MV3, improving FR without architectural modifications or additional inference cost. Code: https://github.com/TaharChettaoui/Hard-Sample-Conditioned-Attention-for-FR.

</details>


### [23] [WorkIng: A Real-World and Synthetic Highway Work-Zone Benchmark Dataset for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41772)
*Xujun Xie,Dominik Rößle,Adithya Mohan,Daniel Cremers,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorkIng双重域基准：WorkIng-Highway为360°多模态真实公路作业区数据集（128线LiDAR、6相机、RTK-GPS，32 km，12,000+帧，227,399个3D框），WorkIng-CARLA为覆盖24个法规感知场景、6种拓扑与6种国际交通标准的程序化仿真。实验表明真实数据是性能主要驱动，场景分层合成增强可提升2D识别、3D检测与轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公路作业区是自动驾驶的关键边缘场景，兼具非典型几何与属地化法规，现有基准无法覆盖：真实数据集缺乏360°多模态全场景3D作业区标注，且单一采集点位无法涵盖可泛化模型所需的布局与法规变体。

Method: 构建双域资源：真实侧WorkIng-Highway提供同步128线LiDAR、六路RGB相机与RTK-GPS的360°多模态标注，覆盖32 km、12,000+标注帧、227,399个跟踪3D边界框；仿真侧WorkIng-CARLA以程序化生成构建24个法规感知场景，涵盖6种典型拓扑与6种国际交通标准，并使用100+自建3D资产。

Result: 对代表性3D检测与端到端规划架构的基准评测显示：真实数据仍是性能的首要驱动因素；场景分层的合成数据增强在2D识别、3D检测与轨迹规划三类任务上均带来性能提升。

Conclusion: WorkIng为作业区场景提供了真实—仿真互补的基准与训练资源，验证了仿真增强对多层级感知与规划任务的有效性，并公开于 https://github.com/cvims/WorkIng。

Abstract: Highway work zones are critical edge cases for autonomous vehicles (AVs), combining atypical geometries and jurisdiction-specific regulations that existing benchmarks fail to capture: real-world datasets lack 360° multi-modal, full-scene 3D work zone annotation, and no single recorded site can span the layouts and regulatory variants needed for generalizable models. We present WorkIng, a dual-domain benchmark and training resource. WorkIng-Highway provides a 360° multi-modal work zone benchmark with synchronized 128-channel LiDAR, six RGB cameras, and RTK-GPS across 32 km, yielding 12,000+ annotated frames and 227,399 tracked 3D bounding boxes. WorkIng-CARLA complements this with procedural simulation spanning 24 regulation-aware scenarios across six canonical topologies and six international traffic standards, populated by 100+ custom 3D assets. Benchmarking representative 3D detection and end-to-end planning architectures shows that real-world data remains the primary performance driver, and that scenario-stratified synthetic augmentation improves architectures across 2D recognition, 3D detection, and trajectory planning. Resources: https://github.com/cvims/WorkIng

</details>


### [24] [Measuring What Matters: A Causal Reliance Score for Histopathology Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41763)
*Samuel Thomas,Daniel Tweneboah Anyimadu,Ahmed Karam Eldaly,Somaia Elsheikh,Mohammed M. Abdelsamea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出因果启发的干预式评估框架与因果依赖分数（CRS），用于量化医学图像分割模型对前景组织与背景/域变化的相对敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的病理分割要求模型依赖病理相关结构而非虚假的背景相关性；作者认为仅改进架构不足，需有方法审计模型行为。

Method: 基于受控干预构建评估框架，显式探测模型对前景组织扰动与背景/域变化的敏感性；提出CRS指标；设计作为隐式反事实干预的增强策略，在训练中提升对无关因素的不变性并保留形态；以自监督Transformer（CAUSE-ViT）为实例验证。

Result: 干预揭示了模型间标准指标（Dice、IoU）无法捕捉的行为差异，说明常规指标对依赖性的评估不足。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是原则性的干预方法与评估指标而非新架构；通过反事实干预评估模型行为对开发可靠、临床稳健的分割系统至关重要。

Abstract: Reliable histopathology segmentation requires models that rely on pathology-relevant structures rather than spurious background correlations. In this work, we argue that improving architectures alone is insufficient if models continue to rely on spurious correlations rather than pathology-relevant features. We introduce a causality-inspired evaluation framework based on controlled interventions that explicitly probes model sensitivity to foreground tissue structure and background/domain variation. Building on this, we propose the Causal Reliance Score (CRS), a metric that quantifies the relative sensitivity of model predictions to foreground perturbation versus background/domain variation under controlled interventions. To support this analysis, we design augmentation strategies that act as implicit counterfactual interventions during training, encouraging invariance to nuisance factors while preserving morphology. We demonstrate how these interventions reveal behavioural differences between models that are not captured by standard metrics such as Dice or IoU. While we instantiate our framework using a self-supervised transformer (CAUSE-ViT), the primary contribution of this work is not a new architecture, but a principled intervention-based methodology and evaluation metric for auditing model behaviour in medical image segmentation. Our results suggest that evaluating model behaviour through counterfactual interventions is essential for developing reliable and clinically robust medical image segmentation systems.

</details>


### [25] [A Self-Supervised Pose Estimation Approach for Construction Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41673)
*Alaa Alshubbak,Daniel Görges*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对比学习的自监督三阶段模型，用于在无标注数据下估计重型工程机械（如挖掘机）的骨架姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有姿态估计研究多依赖监督学习，需大量标注数据；而重型工程机械标注成本高，作者希望减少对标注图像的依赖。

Method: 三阶段结构：第一阶段用SimCLR对比学习在无标注的ACID数据集上预训练；第二阶段为迁移学习的微调；第三阶段为下游任务微调，并可将知识迁移到仅为ACID 3.5%规模的小数据集，同时在NVIDIA Isaac与MATLAB Simscape仿真器生成的小数据集上学习。骨干网络为ResNet-50与HRNet-W32。

Result: 在真实图像上的重型工程机械姿态估计精度，相比普通自监督方法分别提升11%（ResNet-50）和13%（HRNet-W32）。

Conclusion: 自监督对比学习结合多阶段微调可有效降低对标注数据的依赖，并显著提升工程机械姿态估计精度，具备向仿真到真实迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Pose estimation is a computer vision task used to estimate a skeleton of dynamic systems to predict future movements. Most of the research in this direction is based on a supervised learning approach which requires a massive amount of labeled datasets. In this paper, a self-supervised three-stage model based on a contrastive learning approach is introduced for estimating a skeleton of dynamic construction machines; such as excavators without using any labeled images for the first stage. The whole model structure is divided into three stages: the pre-train stage using the SimCLR contrastive approach, and two fine-tuning stages for the transfer learning and the downstream task. The model can leverage the features and learn from a huge unlabeled dataset called ACID to two small datasets generated from NVIDIA Isaac and MATLAB Simscape simulators as well as transfer the knowledge to a smaller dataset with a ratio of 3.5% from the original ACID dataset. The results show that the proposed approach can improve the accuracy of pose estimation for heavy construction machines in real images by 11% and 13% in comparison to the normal self-supervised approach with two backbones ResNet-50 and HRNet-W32, respectively.

</details>


### [26] [Beyond Trial-and-Error: A Geospatial-Guided Active Reasoning Framework for Robust Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41446)
*Jiawei Ge,Wenkang Gong,Jiuxin Cao,Bo Liu,Lei Cui,Haoran Wang,Xuelin Zhu,Biwei Cao,Chang Liu,Chen Feng,Ioannis Patras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GeoReasoner 框架，将空中视觉语言导航（Aerial VLN）从“反应式试错”转变为“地理空间引导的主动推理”，在 CityNav 零样本设置下取得新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aerial VLN 中目标在高空视角下往往尺寸小、视觉歧义大。现有方法沿用室内 VLN 的反应式范式，仅依赖局部观测反复更新导航状态，未充分利用导航过程中稳定存在的地理空间结构；视觉相似干扰物会使基于被动累积证据的反应式搜索不断强化局部看似合理的错误目标假设，导致定位不可靠。

Method: GeoReasoner 包含三个环节：(1) 预搜索地理空间推理，推导区域级先验以约束目标假设空间；(2) 假设驱动的主动探索，主动获取判别性证据并细化相互竞争的目标假设；(3) 一致性感知的目标验证，在停止前对抗性地评估互补证据源，降低过早或错误的目标准认。

Result: 在真实世界 CityNav 基准上，GeoReasoner 在零样本 Aerial VLN 方法中达到新的 SOTA，并与有监督方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用稳定地理空间结构并进行主动证据获取与一致性验证，可有效缓解反应式范式下的干扰物误定位问题，为 Aerial VLN 提供更鲁棒的范式。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires UAVs to locate language-specified goals in large-scale urban environments, where objects often appear small and visually ambiguous from high-altitude viewpoints. Existing methods largely follow a reactive paradigm common in indoor VLN, repeatedly updating navigation states from local observations while underutilizing the geospatial structure that remains stable during navigation. As a result, visually similar distractors can cause a reactive goal search to repeatedly reinforce locally plausible goal hypotheses based on passively accumulated evidence, leading to unreliable localization. To address this, we propose GeoReasoner, a robust framework that shifts the paradigm of Aerial VLN from reactive trial-and-error to geospatial-guided active reasoning. Specifically, GeoReasoner first performs pre-search geospatial reasoning to derive region-level priors and constrain the goal hypothesis space. Building on these, it further conducts hypothesis-driven exploration to actively acquire discriminative evidence and refine competing goal hypotheses. Finally, consistency-aware goal verification adversarially assesses complementary evidence sources before stopping, hence reducing premature or erroneous goal confirmations. Experiments on the real-world CityNav benchmark demonstrate that GeoReasoner sets a new SOTA among zero-shot Aerial VLN methods while competing favorably with supervised approaches.

</details>


### [27] [Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Transformer Encoders for Audio-Image-Symbolic Music Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41512)
*Eliseo Fuentes-Martinez,Antonio Hidalgo-Centeno,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza,Antonio Javier Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了参数高效微调（PEFT）在音乐多模态检索中的作用，发现LoRA/QLoRA在大多模态-目标组合中可媲美甚至优于全量微调，但单模态判别力的提升并不必然转化为多模态表征的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于分数的多模态音乐检索要求表征既在各模态内具有判别性，又能在音频、乐谱图像与符号乐谱之间保持兼容。作者希望探究PEFT用于适配预训练音乐编码器的有效性，并检验单模态层面的改进能否迁移为更好的多模态表征。

Method: 在TriScore与Musical Theme Dataset两个数据集、九种预训练音频/图像/符号乐谱编码器上，对比全量微调、LoRA与QLoRA。先用冻结编码器线性探针作参照，通过有监督单模态分类评估并筛选编码器；随后设计2×2受控实验，交叉“编码器适配”与“任务专用共享投影”，比较投影前后的冻结与适配表征。

Result: LoRA/QLoRA在18个模态-目标组合中的14个被选用，常可匹敌或超过全量微调且参数量显著更少。但单模态增益未均匀迁移至多模态分类：适配与投影对TriScore作曲家分类互补，对TriScore乐器及MTD乐器/合奏任务基本冗余，对MTD作曲家分类部分相互干扰。最终“适配+投影”的标题表征在TriScore上实现曲目级检索，平均Top-5/Top-10准确率为55.32/64.68；跨模态排除与邻域分析显示投影空间中存在较强的模态特异局部结构。

Conclusion: PEFT是全量微调的有效且计算高效的替代方案，但仅靠单模态判别力的提升并不能保证更好的多模态组织；适配与投影的交互效果依赖具体任务，需按任务设计。

Abstract: Score-based multimodal music retrieval requires representations that are both discriminative within each modality and compatible across audio recordings, sheet music images, and symbolic scores. This paper investigates the effectiveness of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for adapting pretrained music encoders and examines whether modality-specific improvements translate into better multimodal representations. We compare full fine-tuning, LoRA, and QLoRA across nine pretrained audio, image, and symbolic score encoders on TriScore and the Musical Theme Dataset. Encoders are first evaluated and selected through supervised unimodal classification, using frozen-encoder linear probing as a reference. We then introduce a controlled 2x2 design crossing encoder adaptation with task-specific shared projection, comparing frozen and adapted representations before and after projection. LoRA or QLoRA is selected in 14 of 18 modality-target combinations, frequently matching or outperforming full fine-tuning while optimizing substantially fewer parameters. However, these unimodal gains do not transfer uniformly to multimodal classification. Adaptation and projection are complementary for composer classification on TriScore, largely redundant for TriScore instrument and the Musical Theme Dataset instrument and ensemble tasks, and partially interfering for composer classification on the Musical Theme Dataset. The final adapted-and-projected title representation on TriScore supports piece-level retrieval, reaching average Top-5 and Top-10 accuracies of 55.32 and 64.68, respectively. Cross-modal exclusion and neighborhood analyses further reveal strong modality-specific local structure in the projected spaces. Overall, PEFT provides an effective and computationally efficient alternative to full fine-tuning, but improved unimodal discrimination alone does not guarantee better multimodal organization.

</details>


### [28] [VoxLution: Evolving Voxels for High-Fidelity 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41501)
*Daoyi Gao,Xinyi Zhang,Alexey Bokhovkin,Lei Li,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxLution：基于多分辨率稀疏体素序列建模的由粗到细高保真3D室内场景生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式模型生成的室内场景几何过于平滑，缺乏细节，难以满足VR、空间计算与机器人仿真等对高分辨率3D场景的需求。

Method: 采用由粗到细的Transformer框架：先生成低分辨率空间布局，再依据该结构信息分块局部渐进细化几何；将3D超分辨率建模为多分辨率稀疏体素网格上的序列建模问题；提出免训练、分辨率无关的tokenization方案，将8个卦限的几何信息紧凑编码为单个十进制数；序列中融合多个分辨率以让粗布局引导细部生成，并直接嵌入相邻块边界信息以保证跨块一致性。

Result: 在比现有基线更细的体素分辨率下实现场景生成，在标准基准上达到SOTA：3D-FRONT上FPD降低59.6%、FID降低51.1%；Scannet++上FPD降低70.5%、FID降低73.8%。

Conclusion: 将3D超分辨率转化为多分辨率稀疏体素序列建模，配合分辨率无关tokenization与边界感知序列设计，可有效生成细节丰富且局部连贯的高分辨率室内场景。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D indoor scene generation is crucial for immersive applications such as virtual reality, spatial computing, and robotics simulation. We introduce VoxLution, a coarse-to-fine transformer-based approach that generates high-fidelity indoor scenes with rich details, overcoming the overly smooth geometry typical of existing generative models. First, we generate a low-resolution spatial layout; then, based on this structural information, we progressively refine the scene geometry locally in chunks. Key to our approach is formulating 3D super-resolution as a sequence modeling problem over multi-resolution sparse voxel grids. To achieve this, we introduce a training-free, resolution-invariant tokenization scheme that compactly encodes the geometric information of eight octants into a single decimal number. We formulate the transformer sequence to include multiple resolutions, allowing the coarse layout to guide the generation of fine local details. To ensure coherence across local chunks, we incorporate adjacent chunk boundary information directly into the transformer sequencing. VoxLution achieves scene generation at a finer voxel resolution than existing baselines, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks with 59.6% lower FPD and 51.1% lower FID on 3D-FRONT, and 70.5% lower FPD and 73.8% lower FID on Scannet++.

</details>


### [29] [FoAViS: Analyzing Learned Fixation Strategies in a Foveated Active Vision System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41987)
*Valentin Hassler,Jonathan Henrich,Yohaï-Eliel Berreby,Haoze Zhu,Alexander Ecker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FoAViS系统：采用对数极坐标（log-polar）视野采样+2D空间记忆累积信息，配合与任务联合训练的注视策略，在分类与语义分割上验证了学习到的注视策略优于随机/启发式策略，且扫描路径呈现类人特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉具有中央凹化（foveated）与主动注视特性。构建同构机器系统可在组件与目标已知的可控条件下研究注视行为，并与生物视觉理解相互印证。既有模型难以同时满足中央凹采样、跨任务（分类到稠密预测）、以及与注视策略联合训练三项要求。

Method: 构建FoAViS系统：视觉输入以既定log-polar中央凹方式采样；采样信息写入二维空间记忆；因记忆为2D结构，同一记忆可全局读出用于分类，亦可逐点读出用于语义分割，无需改动架构；注视点由2D注视图上的策略网络产生，该策略与任务端到端同时训练。

Result: 学习到的注视策略选定的位置比非学习的启发式更具信息量且更任务相关；学习策略还以有益方式塑造任务表征——分类与分割中，联合训练策略的模型均优于使用随机位置训练的模型，即便后者获得学习到的扫描路径亦不例外；此外生成的扫描路径捕捉到经验扫描路径的部分特性，表现出与人类注视行为的相似性。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从标准被动、单遍架构迈向具有生物学基础的视觉模型的重要一步，为目标导向注视行为与视觉系统信息表征的研究开辟了路径。

Abstract: Human vision is foveated and active: resolution is highest at the fovea and falls off towards the periphery, and fixations are actively directed to locations of interest. Building a machine system that processes information in the same way lets us study gaze behavior in a controlled setting where the system's components and objectives are known. The emergence of gaze characteristics can therefore be examined systematically, which may in turn inform our understanding of biological vision. This requires a model that can sample like the fovea, handle various tasks from classification to dense prediction, and be trained concurrently with a fixation policy. In our study, we present such a system and study its capabilities and the fixation policies that emerge from it. Our system, FoAViS, samples visual input by adopting the established log-polar foveation and accumulates information in a 2D spatial memory. Due to its 2D structure, the same memory can be read out globally for classification or densely for semantic segmentation, without any architectural change. Fixations are drawn from a 2D map by a policy trained concurrently with the task. We find that learned fixation policies select more informative, task-specific locations than non-learned heuristics. These policies also shape the task representations in a beneficial way: for both classification and segmentation, models trained concurrently with a policy outperform models trained with random locations, even when the latter are provided with learned scanpaths. Moreover, these scanpaths exhibit similarities to human gaze behavior by capturing aspects of empirical scanpath characteristics. Our work marks an important step away from standard passive, single-pass architectures, towards biologically grounded models of vision. It opens a path for studying goal-directed gaze behavior and information representation in the visual system.

</details>


### [30] [RADI3N: GeometRic ImAge EDiting via ParametrIc 3D CoNtrol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40973)
*Abdul Wasi,Akhil Gorugantu,Mahesh Bhosale,Anjan Dutta,Junsong Yuan,Sauradip Nag,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RADI3N框架，实现语义 grounding 的部件级参数化3D控制物体编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散编辑方法在部件级参数化3D控制上不足：或对整体物体操作需手工绑定，或退化为2D控制点；且在大幅变换下难以建模外观与纹理，几何密集编辑产生不真实结果。

Method: 1) CS-ARAP形变模块：通过邻域协方差平滑抑制部件边界处旋转场不连续，实现结构一致的部件级形变；2) 免反演的选择性纹理迁移机制：在跨图像注意力中抑制高频位置偏置、放大低频语义线索，消除严格空间偏置并支持语义匹配，使源外观随编辑部件迁移到新几何。

Result: 实验表明RADI3N在几何保真度、视角一致性与可控性上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: RADI3N通过CS-ARAP形变与选择性纹理迁移，实现语义一致、结构稳定的部件级参数化3D编辑，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based methods have substantially advanced image editing, yet parametric 3D control at the part level remains challenging. Existing approaches either operate on whole objects with manual rigging, or revert to 2D handles at the part level. They also struggle to model appearance and texture under large transformations, leading to unrealistic outputs in geometry-intensive edits. To address these limitations, we introduce RADI3N, a framework for semantically-grounded part-aware object manipulation with parametric 3D control. Our CS-ARAP deformation module suppresses rotation-field discontinuities at part boundaries via neighborhood covariance smoothing, producing structurally consistent deformations under complex part-level edits. We further introduce an inversion-free selective texture transfer mechanism that suppresses high-frequency positional biases and scales up low-frequency semantic cues within cross-image attention. This eliminates strict spatial bias and supports semantic matching, allowing source appearance to follow the edited part onto its new geometry. Experiments demonstrate that RADI3N achieves state-of-the-art performance in geometric fidelity, viewpoint consistency, and controllability.

</details>


### [31] [Sparse Autoencoders for Expert-Assisted Interpretation of Histopathology Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40905)
*Šimon Šmída,Wanda Benesova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）能否将病理基础模型嵌入分解为可供专家辅助解读的稀疏潜在特征，并在NCT-CRC上验证其保真度、稀疏性、一致性、标签富集与下游信号保留。结果表明SAE潜在特征比原始维度与PCA更具一致性和标签集中性，且行为不限于单一模型（UNI2-h可复现），支持将其用于专家辅助分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型嵌入表征内部结构难以检视；而病理学家依赖反复出现的形态与组织模式（组织结构、细胞形态、染色变异、伪影）进行推理，因此概念级可解释性在计算病理中尤为关键。作者希望检验SAE能否提供适合专家辅助解读的稀疏概念级分解。

Method: 在NCT-CRC嵌入上训练vanilla与Batch-TopK两种SAE变体，主模型为CONCH，并以UNI2-h作稳健性评估；从重建保真度、稀疏性、一致性（coherence）、标签富集、下游类别信号保留五个维度刻画潜在特征；结合病理专家讨论提出WSI级潜在激活可视化流程，将特征激活置于更大组织情境中。

Result: 稀疏表征保留了大部分下游组织类别信息；其潜在特征检索到的patch组在一致性与标签集中度上优于原始嵌入维度与PCA成分；UNI2-h上的额外评估表明该行为并非CONCH特有。

Conclusion: SAE可用于病理基础模型嵌入的专家辅助分析，具有一定跨模型可迁移性；但潜在特征应被视为需病理学评估的“候选概念”，而非自动验证的病理学概念。

Abstract: Histopathology foundation models learn rich embedding representations, but their internal structure remains difficult to inspect. Concept-level interpretability is particularly relevant in computational pathology because pathologists reason through recurring morphological and tissue-level patterns, including tissue architecture, cellular morphology, staining variation, and artifacts. In this work, we investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can decompose embeddings from histopathology foundation models into sparse latent features suitable for expert-assisted interpretation. We train vanilla and Batch-TopK SAE variants on NCT-CRC embeddings, using CONCH as the primary foundation model and UNI2-h as an additional robustness evaluation, and characterize the resulting latent features through reconstruction fidelity, sparsity, coherence, label enrichment, and downstream class-signal preservation. The learned sparse representations preserve most downstream tissue-class information, and their latent features retrieve more coherent and label-concentrated patch groups than raw embedding dimensions or PCA components. Discussions with pathology experts further motivated a WSI-level latent visualization workflow that places feature activations into larger tissue context. Overall, the additional UNI2-h evaluation suggests that the observed behavior is not specific to CONCH, supporting the use of sparse autoencoders for expert-assisted analysis of histopathology foundation-model embeddings, while emphasizing that latent features should be treated as candidate concepts requiring pathological assessment rather than automatically validated histopathological concepts.

</details>


### [32] [Shifting Focus by Reliability: Geometric Fusion Modulation for Robust RGB-T Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41204)
*Liuyin Shi,Chengqi Li,Wenbo He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PG-FM（Prompt-Guided Feature Modulation），根据估计的每种模态腐败类型与严重程度对RGB-T检测器做逐输入的自适应调制，无需复原输入即可显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-T检测器在任一模态信号受损时精度显著下降；基于复原的方法延迟高且不能稳定提升下游精度，故转而研究"不复原输入、而是让检测器高效适应变化信号质量"这一问题。常规PEFT虽高效，但其与输入无关的修正无法跟踪多样的测试时腐败。

Method: 受控腐败扫描实验发现：轻度腐败下各类型影响相近，随严重度增加而分化，从而支持腐败感知的逐输入自适应。据此设计PG-FM：先估计每种模态的腐败类型与严重程度，再通过提示（prompt）引导特征调制，对RGB-T检测器进行自适应。

Result: 在受控RGB-T腐败模拟下，PG-FM保持干净集性能，FLIR上mAP50最高提升20.8点、KAIST上提升19.9点，并可无需重训练迁移至未修改的自然采集外部RGB-T数据集。

Conclusion: 腐败感知的逐输入特征调制是提升RGB-T检测鲁棒性的高效替代方案，优于依赖输入复原的路线，且具备良好的跨数据集迁移性。

Abstract: RGB-thermal (RGB-T) object detectors combine visible and thermal imagery for robust detection, but signal corruption in either stream can substantially degrade accuracy. Restoration-based methods incur significant latency and do not consistently improve downstream accuracy, raising a more direct question: rather than restoring corrupted inputs, can we efficiently adapt the detector to their varying signal quality? Conventional PEFT offers an efficient route, but its input-independent corrections cannot track diverse test-time corruptions. Our controlled corruption sweeps show that corruption types have similar effects near clean conditions but diverge increasingly with severity, motivating corruption-aware, per-input adaptation. We therefore propose PG-FM (Prompt-Guided Feature Modulation), which adapts RGB-T object detectors to the estimated corruption type and severity of each modality. Under controlled RGB-T corruption simulations, PG-FM preserves clean-set performance, improves mAP50 by up to 20.8 points on FLIR and 19.9 points on KAIST, and transfers without retraining to unmodified, naturally acquired external RGB-T datasets.

</details>


### [33] [Grounding Image Composition with Label-Conditioned Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41080)
*Armin Dadras,Tingyu Lin,Robert Sablatnig,Franziska Proksa,Markus Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 KU-PCPE，一个面向摄影构图的标签条件区域证据基准：给定图像与已判定适用的构图标签，分割出能解释该标签为何适用的可见区域。基准含 4,093 张单标注者训练掩码，以及 1,327 个任务的专家测试集（每个图文标签任务 3 个掩码），并附有"区域 vs 语义引导线"的探索性评分。结果显示专家间 Dice 达 .867，最强可部署模型仅 .664，提议级 oracle 为 .672，最佳 VLM 提议选择器为 .395。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有构图数据集（如 KU-PCP）只提供图像级标签和类别特定的几何要素，未揭示人类将哪些可见区域与九个构图类别相关联，即缺少"标签为何适用"的区域级证据标注。

Method: 构建标签条件分割任务与基准数据：单标注者训练掩码 + 三专家标注的测试任务；设计逐图像-标签的区域证据分割评测，并以 Dice 衡量；同时采集探索性 segment-versus-line 评分，判断区域证据或语义引导线更能解释标签；对比可部署模型、提议 oracle 与 VLM 提议选择器。

Result: 留出专家对同批专家达 .867 Dice；最强可部署模型 .664，提议 oracle .672，最佳 VLM 提议选择器仅 .395，均显著低于人类一致性。

Conclusion: KU-PCPE 揭示了"识别构图标签"与"定位支撑该标签的人类对齐视觉证据"之间的显著差距，为该方向的构图证据接地研究提供评测基础。

Abstract: Image composition labels such as center, diagonal, or rule of thirds describe visual structure rather than object categories. KU-PCP provides image-level labels and class-specific geometric elements, but does not show which visible regions people associate with each of its nine classes. We introduce KU-PCP Elements (KU-PCPE), a label-conditioned region-evidence benchmark for photographic composition. Given an image and a label already judged applicable, the task is to segment the visible regions that explain why the label applies. KU-PCPE contains 4,093 single-annotator training masks and a 1,327-task expert test split with three masks per image-label task. The annotations also include exploratory segment-versus-line ratings that describe whether region or semantic-guideline evidence better explains the label. A held-out expert reaches .867 Dice against the other experts, while the strongest deployable model reaches .664, a proposal oracle .672, and the best vision-language model (VLM) proposal selector .395. KU-PCPE exposes the gap between recognizing a composition label and grounding the human-aligned visual evidence that makes it plausible in a particular image.

</details>


### [34] [Single-Annotation, Multi-Hypothesis Segmentation with Semantic-Spatial Residual Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41069)
*Armin Dadras,Robert Sablatnig,Franziska Proksa,Markus Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究单标注多假设分割：训练时每张图仅一个掩码，测试时需输出有限个掩码覆盖多位人类标注的多样性。提出语义-空间残差掩码库（SSRB），冻结用平衡分配训练的语义库并为每个假设学习图像残差，在五个医学数据集上提升留出掩码覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标注分割数据集表明一张图可存在多个有效掩码，但多数训练集每图仅有一个标注。作者希望在此单标注约束下训练出能覆盖人类标注多样性的有限掩码库，并探究何种表示更有利于该任务。

Method: 实验揭示表示权衡：直接从图像学习的掩码库能恢复局部边界与范围变化但丢失结构假设；冻结语义特征能保留结构分组但空间变化表达不足。据此提出SSRB：冻结以平衡分配训练的语义掩码库，并为每个假设头从图像学习残差，叠加到语义库上。

Result: 在相同单掩码约束下，K=8 时SSRB在五个医学终点上相对Probabilistic U-Net提升留出掩码覆盖率；在LIDC与LIDC-hard上产生明显更多互异且有用的掩码；在BSDS上保持语义库的区域分组性能；返回掩码数增加时优势扩大。

Conclusion: 为对应假设头学习的残差可在保留区域分组方式的同时改善对局部标注变化的覆盖，说明语义库加图像残差的分解是单标注多假设分割的有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-rater segmentation datasets show that a single image can admit several valid masks, yet most training sets expose only one annotation per image. We study single-annotation multi-hypothesis segmentation: each training image exposes one fixed mask, while the model must return a finite bank that covers held-out human annotations at test time. Our experiments reveal a representation tradeoff. Banks learned directly from images recover variations in local boundaries and extent but lose structural hypotheses, whereas frozen semantic features preserve structural grouping but underrepresent spatial variation. We introduce the Semantic-Spatial Residual Bank (SSRB), which freezes a semantic bank trained with balanced assignments and learns a residual from the image for each hypothesis. Across five medical endpoints, SSRB improves coverage of held-out masks at $K=8$ over a Probabilistic U-Net under the same one-mask constraint. On LIDC and LIDC-hard, it also produces substantially more distinct useful masks. On BSDS, it preserves the semantic bank's performance in grouping regions. Its advantage grows as more masks are returned, showing that residuals learned for corresponding heads improve coverage of local annotation variation while preserving how regions are grouped.

</details>


### [35] [Rethinking Asymmetry in Self-Supervised 3D Geometry Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42092)
*Gyeongjin Kang,Freeman Cheng,Botao Ye,Weijie Lyu,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Eunbyung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将自监督的"不对称性"放到3D表征空间而非输入空间，并利用视角密度一致性作为几何训练信号，从而在无位姿图像上显著提升深度与位姿估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从无位姿图像学习3D几何的自监督方法以渲染质量为目标，通过在输入空间施加不对称（如分离分支、掩码注意力）来避免平凡解，但这种设计限制了视角间信息交互，损害了几何精度。

Method: 1）将不对称性转移到3D表征空间：共享主干网络使用全部视角建立对应关系，仅在重建场景时排除留出视角；2）视角密度对齐：将稀疏上下文特征与稠密上下文特征对齐，作为渲染之外的几何训练信号；3）进一步与冻结的视觉基础模型特征对齐；全部信号均无需几何标注。

Result: 相较输入空间设计，深度与位姿估计均有提升；密度对齐与基础模型对齐带来进一步增益；整体几何估计优于以往自监督方法；受控实验分离了各组件的贡献；自监督预训练还能提升后续监督训练效果。

Conclusion: 将不对称性施加于3D表征空间并结合视角密度一致性，是一种无需几何标注、可有效替代输入空间掩码设计的自监督几何学习范式，且可作为有益预训练。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning relies on asymmetry between what a model observes and what it must explain. Recent methods learn 3D geometry from unposed images by predicting cameras and scene structure, then rendering held-out views. To avoid trivial solutions, they impose this asymmetry in the input space, restricting how views interact through separate branches or masked attention. We revisit this design with geometric accuracy, rather than rendering quality, as the main objective. When a held-out view lies between context views, the context already constrains the geometry it observes. We therefore impose the asymmetry in the 3D representation space, excluding held-out views only from the reconstructed scene while a shared backbone uses all views for correspondence. This improves depth and pose estimation over input-space designs. We further exploit view density: sparse and dense observations depict the same scene but provide different amounts of correspondence. Aligning sparse-context features with dense-context features supplies a geometric training signal alongside rendering, without a pretrained geometric teacher. We additionally align features with a frozen vision foundation model, which brings further gains. None of these signals requires geometric annotation, and the resulting model improves geometry estimation over prior self-supervised methods. Controlled experiments isolate the contribution of each component, and self-supervised pretraining also improves subsequent supervised training.

</details>


### [36] [DyCraft: An Agentic System for Long-Horizon 4D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40754)
*Jeff Tan,Hang Dai,Zeqian Long,Yanzhe Lyu,R. Kenny Jones,Shangzhe Wu,Chen Geng,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DyCraft，将长时程3D动画生成分解为"规划—静态场景—关键姿态3D分镜—局部运动创作"的多步流程，使编码智能体可逐步验证与调试，显著减少物理违规并提升动画质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体能生成高质量3D场景与关节化资产，但在长时程动画上表现不佳：通用框架下常出现物体互相穿插、缺乏物理支撑、运动不自然等物理不合理输出。其根源在于智能体倾向一次性脚本化生成整段动画，难以验证成果、难以解耦复杂的时空约束。

Method: 借鉴人类动画师工作流程，DyCraft先将用户提示扩展为详细计划并构建静态场景；再生成关键动画姿态的3D分镜（每个分镜帧为可独立构建与调试的静态3D场景）；分镜将问题分解为若干已验证的起止状态对，智能体借助一组运动创作工具在相邻状态间编写局部运动。

Result: 在24个长时程、多镜头动画任务上评测（涵盖人形机器人、人与动物角色及物理机构）；与Codex中的原生GPT-6智能体及已有智能体基线相比，DyCraft显著减少物理违规，生成的动画3D场景质量更高，更受人类评分者偏好。

Conclusion: 通过分镜式问题分解与逐步验证，可将长时程动画生成转化为编码智能体可可靠求解的子问题，是提升智能体3D动画生成物理合理性与质量的有效范式。

Abstract: While frontier coding agents can generate high-quality 3D scenes and articulated assets, they struggle to create coherent long-horizon animations.
