When equipped with general-purpose harnesses, they frequently produce physically implausible outputs, generating objects that interpenetrate, lack physical support, or move unnaturally. Such bare agents struggle to repair these errors because they default to scripting the full animation in one pass, making it difficult to verify their work and disentangle complex spatiotemporal constraints. Drawing on the practice of human animators, we introduce DyCraft, a system for animated 3D scene generation that breaks animation into steps that coding agents can more reliably solve. DyCraft first expands the user's prompt into a detailed plan and builds a static scene. It then creates a 3D storyboard of key animation poses, where each frame is a static 3D scene that the agent can build and debug in isolation. Storyboarding decomposes the problem into pairs of verified start and end states, allowing the agent to author local motion between them with a collection of motion authoring tools. We evaluate DyCraft on 24 long-horizon, multi-shot animation tasks covering humanoid robots, human and animal characters, and physical mechanisms. Compared with a native GPT-6 agent in Codex and prior agentic baselines, DyCraft substantially reduces physical violations and generates higher-quality animated 3D scenes that are more often preferred by human raters.

</details>


### [37] [PathoJev: Efficient Pathology Decisions from Shared Visual Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40752)
*Chengyang Zhang,Wenchuan Zhang,Bo Li,Xinyu Liu,Jiaming Yang,Mengran Li,Jie Chen,Wei Ju,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出病理模型 PathoJev，基于 System One 范式对整张玻片只编码一次，批量评估独立问题后缀并直接输出答案概率，在整片与 ROI 基准上兼顾精度与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理工作流需对同一张切片做多项视觉判断（肿瘤类型、分级、浸润等），逐题生成并解析独立文本响应会在视觉证据与可用决策之间引入延迟，计算随问题数量线性增长。

Method: 基于 System One 范式，一次编码整张切片，在共享视觉上下文上批量评估独立问题后缀，直接预测答案概率而非生成文本；复用混合骨干网络的循环状态，以限制随切片上下文长度增长的计算量。

Result: 三个整片基准上 9B/27B 平均准确率 77.2%/78.1%，较 SlideChat 高 5.4/6.3 个百分点；五个 ROI 基准保持竞争力；WSI-Bench 上 9B 较 SlideChat 快 9.1 倍；共享上下文对 8 问题工作负载加速 6.9x/5.9x，64 问题时达 11.2x/8.9x。

Conclusion: 为整片与局部组织评估中的多视觉判断组合提供了可复用的概率接口，在精度与延迟上均优于逐题生成式基线。

Abstract: Pathology workflows require multiple visual judgments about the same slide, from identifying tumor type to assessing grade and invasion. Generating and parsing a separate textual response for each structured question adds latency between visual evidence and usable decisions. Building on the System One paradigm, we introduce PathoJev, a pathology model that directly predicts answer probabilities for whole-slide and region-level assessment. PathoJev encodes each slide once and evaluates independent question suffixes in batches over the shared visual context. Reusing the hybrid backbone's recurrent states further limits the computation that grows with slide-context length for each additional question. Across three whole-slide benchmarks, the 9B and 27B models achieve mean accuracies of 77.2% and 78.1%, exceeding SlideChat by 5.4 and 6.3 percentage points. Both scales also maintain competitive accuracy on five ROI benchmarks. On WSI-Bench, the 9B model runs 9.1x faster than SlideChat. Shared context accelerates eight-question workloads by 6.9x and 5.9x over separate prefix encoding, rising to 11.2x and 8.9x for 64 questions. These results provide a reusable probabilistic interface for combining multiple visual judgments in whole-slide and local tissue assessment. Code: https://github.com/ChyaZhang/PathoJev.

</details>


### [38] [Queryable Gaussian View Index: Text Retrieval and Selective Camera Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41373)
*Tianyu Xiong,Rui Li,Jiaqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出QGVI（Queryable Gaussian View Index），为现有高斯场景资产及其标定参考数据提供可查询索引；其中QGVI-T将高斯渲染视图绑定结构化描述与已知相机位姿，实现文本-视图检索与相机定位，QGVI-I则基于LoGS几何估计实现图像路线的选择性RGB相机定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯场景资产缺乏面向查询与定位的统一接口：文本检索场景位置通常依赖在线渲染、VLM调用或场景专用位姿网络，成本高且泛化性差；图像定位则常需光度精修。作者希望在资产坐标系内直接、低成本地支持文本-视图检索与选择性相机定位。

Method: 构建QGVI索引。QGVI-T：将高斯渲染视图转为可语言检索的场景位置索引，把结构化captions绑定到已知相机位姿与地点标识，通过语义与词法检索在离线条件下选取不同地点的视图，无需在线渲染、VLM调用或场景专用位姿网络。QGVI-I：以LoGS几何估计为基础，利用不同内点查询关键点进行过滤，省略光度精修并保留被接受的位姿坐标。评估在九个开发场景上进行，采用模型判定的语义成功与源相机恢复分开报告，几何评估使用源验证或估计尺度。

Result: 九场景上QGVI-T取得58.3% Top-1、78.3% Top-5的联合相关性与有效性成功率，对比CityLoC重实现（含精修）的26.1%与34.6%；QGVI-I在1 m/2°阈值下场景平均位姿成功率达97.3%，合并误接受率0.707%、误拒绝率1.142%；配对缓存评估中全部1,775个初始化位姿在该阈值下保持正确；文本查询中位延迟0.020 s。对照实验显示BM25检索具竞争力，阈值化完整LoGS略降误接受；网格增强对由新视图生成的描述有小幅提升。

Conclusion: QGVI以离线索引方式高效地统一了高斯资产的文本检索与相机定位，在精度、延迟与误接受/拒绝率上均具优势，为高斯场景资产的可查询化提供了可复用的基准与评估协议。

Abstract: We present Queryable Gaussian View Index (QGVI) for querying existing Gaussian scene assets and their associated calibrated reference data. Text-view retrieval and selective RGB camera localization return poses in the asset's coordinate frame. QGVI-T turns Gaussian-rendered views into a language-searchable index of scene locations, binding structured captions to known camera poses and place identifiers. Semantic and lexical retrieval select views from distinct places without online rendering, VLM calls, or a scene-specific pose network. The image route, QGVI-I, filters LoGS-based geometric estimates using distinct inlier query keypoints. It omits photometric refinement and preserves accepted pose coordinates. On nine development scenes, QGVI-T achieves 58.3% Top-1 and 78.3% Top-5 joint relevance-and-validity success, compared with 26.1% and 34.6% for our CityLoC reimplementation with refinement. QGVI-I achieves 97.3% scene-mean pose success at 1 m/2 degrees, with pooled false-acceptance and false-rejection rates of 0.707% and 1.142%. A paired cached evaluation retains all 1,775 initializer poses correct at this threshold. Recorded median text query latency is 0.020 s. Matched controls show competitive BM25 retrieval and slightly lower false acceptance for thresholded full LoGS; grid augmentation suggests modest gains on novel-view-derived descriptions. We report model-judged semantic success separately from source-camera recovery, with source-verified or estimated scales for geometric evaluation.

</details>


### [39] [Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion for Flow-Based Inverse Problem Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41695)
*Hansam Cho,Seoung Bum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将"初始噪声选择"作为基于流模型的逆问题求解中的独立设计维度，提出检索增强+双引导反演的初始化方法RA-DGI，以更低计算开销取得优于随机初始化与推理时扩展的重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流模型的逆问题求解器普遍采用随机高斯噪声初始化，而在图像生成领域已证明初始噪声显著影响生成结果；初始化在流式逆问题求解中的作用尚未被系统研究。

Method: 固定下游流式逆问题求解器不变，系统评估多种噪声初始化策略；提出RA-DGI：检索代理干净图像作为先验，经双引导反演构建改进的初始噪声。

Result: 在多个逆问题上的实验表明初始噪声质量显著影响重建保真度与感知质量；RA-DGI优于标准随机初始化，并优于推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）策略，且计算开销更低。

Conclusion: 初始化是基于流模型逆问题求解中被长期忽视的关键因素，值得作为独立设计选择加以研究；检索增强与引导反演是一种高效有效的实现途径。

Abstract: Flow models have recently emerged as powerful generative priors for solving inverse problems. Despite their success, most existing flow-based inverse problem solvers initialize the reconstruction process with random Gaussian noise. However, recent studies in image generation have shown that the choice of initial noise can significantly influence generation outcomes. The role of initialization as a distinct design choice in flow-based inverse problem solving remains comparatively underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate noise initialization strategies while holding the downstream flow-based inverse solver fixed. We propose Retrieval-Augmented Initialization with Dual-Guided Inversion (RA-DGI), a method that constructs improved initial noise by leveraging retrieved proxy clean images and dual-guided inversion. Extensive experiments across multiple inverse problems demonstrate that the quality of the initial noise substantially affects reconstruction fidelity and perceptual performance. RA-DGI outperforms standard random initialization and achieves superior results compared to inference-time scaling strategies, while requiring less computational overhead. These findings highlight initialization as a critical yet previously overlooked factor in flow-based inverse problem solvers.

</details>


### [40] [TADP: Dual-Branch Temporally Adaptive Differential Privacy for Expression-Aware Video Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41642)
*Feng Xu,Xun Li,Lars Petersson,Yulei Sui,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Dadong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TADP：面向推理期隐私保护视频生成的时间自适应差分隐私框架，在固定长度片段替换邻接关系下同时私有化身份与表情双分支，并给出片段级DP保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理期视频生成（如人脸表情驱动）存在身份泄漏风险；而现有DP机制多采用统一噪声，未考虑片段内时间依赖与双分支联合释出的隐私预算分配，隐私—效用权衡不佳。

Method: 双分支结构分别提取身份与表情表示；两支均用时间自适应高斯机制加噪，噪声尺度仅依赖同一片段内先前已私有化的释出，且以历史均匀校准的下界约束；联合释出通过自适应Rényi DP组合记账；片段级RDP保证转换为(ε,δ)-DP；软量化与后续时序处理仅在私有化之后进行，作为DP后处理。

Result: 在人脸表情数据集上，所评估攻击下身份泄漏降低，感知质量与时序稳定性可用，人脸表情识别效用高于所对比的隐私保护基线；形式化满足片段级DP。

Conclusion: TADP在已评估的威胁模型与数据集上实现了有利的隐私—效用权衡，兼具形式化片段级DP保证与实用生成质量。

Abstract: We present TADP, a temporally adaptive differential privacy (DP) framework for inference-time privacy-preserving video generation under fixed-length clip-replacement adjacency. TADP uses a dual-branch design to extract identity and expression representations, clips and privatizes both branches with temporally adaptive Gaussian mechanisms whose noise scales depend only on preceding privatized releases within the same clip and are bounded below by history-uniform calibrated minima, and accounts for their joint release via adaptive Rényi Differential Privacy (RDP) composition. The clip-level RDP guarantee is converted to $(\varepsilon,δ)$-differential privacy, while soft quantization and subsequent temporal processing are applied only after privatization as DP post-processing. Experiments on facial expression datasets show reduced identity leakage under evaluated attacks, usable perceptual quality and temporal stability, and high facial expression recognition utility relative to the evaluated privacy-preserving baselines. TADP formally satisfies clip-level DP and empirically provides a favorable privacy--utility trade-off under the evaluated threat models and datasets.

</details>


### [41] [ObjectGuidedMap: An Object-Aware Model for Robust Bird's Eye View Map Prediction in High-Occlusion Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41834)
*Christian Bohn,Harsh Yadav,Klaus Friedrichs,Hasan Tercan,Tobias Meisen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种联合预测BEV地图与3D目标的多任务架构，在BEV特征层与实例查询层引入跨任务特征融合，显著提升遮挡场景下的地图预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV地图预测模型（如Mask2Map）虽能直接从多视角图像预测矢量化实例级地图元素，但基本独立于目标级场景理解；而交通参与者常遮挡道路几何或隐含其信息。作者希望显式建立地图与目标两任务间的信息交换，以提升地图预测在遮挡下的鲁棒性。

Method: 以Mask2Map为基础扩展出3D目标检测分支，构建多任务架构；在BEV特征层通过可变形注意力、在实例查询层通过交叉注意力实现跨任务特征融合，使地图实例能利用目标上下文并对交通参与者造成的遮挡保持鲁棒。

Result: 在nuScenes与Argoverse 2数据集上，BEV地图预测精度得到一致提升；在目标遮挡严重的场景中，mAP提升达21%。

Conclusion: 联合建模BEV地图与3D目标并实现跨任务特征交互，可有效利用目标上下文补偿遮挡信息缺失，是一种提升矢量化BEV地图预测性能的有效多任务范式。

Abstract: Accurate BEV map prediction is a critical component of autonomous driving systems, enabling downstream planning and control. Recent approaches such as Mask2Map achieve strong performance by predicting vectorized, instance-level map elements directly from multi-view images. However, existing BEV map prediction models operate largely independently of object-level scene understanding, despite the fact that traffic participants frequently occlude or implicitly encode information about road geometry. In this work, we propose a multitask architecture that jointly predicts BEV maps and 3D objects, explicitly enabling information exchange between the two tasks. Building upon Mask2Map, we introduce an object detection branch and incorporate cross-task feature fusion at both the BEV feature level and the instance-query level via deformable attention and cross-attention mechanisms. This design allows map instances to leverage object context while remaining robust to occlusions caused by traffic participants. Experimental results on the nuScenes and Argoverse 2 datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently improves BEV map prediction accuracy, with particularly significant gains of 21% mAP in scenes containing heavy object occlusion.

</details>


### [42] [DrawSplat: Atlanta World-Guided Implicit Gaussian SLAM for Indoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41602)
*Fan Zhu,Zhisong Xu,Ziyu Chen,Zhenjun Zhao,Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DrawSplat：一种Atlanta World引导的隐式高斯SLAM框架，先由点-线显式几何“勾勒”结构布局，再由隐式神经高斯“填充”外观与几何细节，并通过结构平面正则与自适应采样实现稳健跟踪、高保真渲染与几何一致重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯SLAM主要依赖光度、深度或局部帧到模型约束优化高斯基元，在弱纹理、大平面结构、深度噪声大、长轨迹的室内场景中不足，导致位姿漂移、表面厚度伪影、法向噪声与结构边界模糊。

Method: 点-线联合跟踪估计相机位姿、稀疏几何与重力方向；基于Atlanta World先验的共享重力分量从跟踪线推断结构平面，并转化为对高斯中心与法向的可微正则项；采用锚点式隐式神经高斯表示结合自适应结构采样，将高斯容量分配给结构信息丰富区域。

Result: 在多个数据集上，DrawSplat在高斯类SLAM系统中实现稳健跟踪、高保真渲染与较强几何重建能力，并在质量、效率与模型规模之间保持实用平衡。

Conclusion: 将显式点-线结构先验与隐式神经高斯表示相结合，并以Atlanta World结构正则与自适应采样加以约束，是提升室内高斯SLAM跟踪鲁棒性、渲染质量与几何一致性的有效途径。

Abstract: RGB-D SLAM requires accurate online tracking, high-quality rendering, and geometrically consistent reconstruction. Existing Gaussian SLAM methods mainly optimize Gaussian primitives with photometric, depth, or local frame-to-model constraints, which are often insufficient in indoor scenes with weak textures, large planar structures, noisy depth, and long trajectories. The resulting maps may suffer from pose drift, surface thickness artifacts, noisy normals, and blurred structural boundaries. We propose DrawSplat, an Atlanta World-guided implicit Gaussian SLAM framework for indoor reconstruction. DrawSplat follows a drawing-like process: explicit point-line geometry first sketches the structural layout, and implicit neural Gaussians then fill in high-fidelity appearance and continuous geometric details. It estimates camera poses, sparse geometry, and gravity direction through point-line joint tracking, infers structural planes from tracked lines using the shared-gravity component of the Atlanta World prior, and converts them into differentiable regularization terms on Gaussian centers and normals. To improve compactness and efficiency, DrawSplat further combines an anchor-based implicit neural Gaussian representation with adaptive structural sampling, which allocates Gaussian capacity to structurally informative regions. Experiments on multiple datasets show that DrawSplat achieves robust tracking, high-fidelity rendering, and strong geometric reconstruction among Gaussian-based SLAM systems, while maintaining a practical balance among quality, efficiency, and model size.

</details>


### [43] [BDNet: Fast-slow thinking is all you need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41485)
*Chenzhi Zheng,Zhuoran Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BDNet 是一个以"快速图像特征 + 缓慢统计参考"为核心的分层图像分类骨干网络，通过低秩投影与可学习通道缩放对特征相对参考的偏差进行变换，实现线性复杂度的鲁棒分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望用"缓慢参考"提升紧凑表示的鲁棒性，同时保持简单的张量实现；并将其与依赖输入的自注意力机制明确区分，凸显训练的统计机制与线性推理行为。

Method: 提出基线变形算子（BDO）：对特征相对阶段特定参考的偏差做低秩投影与学习到的通道缩放，直接作用于基线相对残差而非成对 token 相似度，复杂度随 token 数线性增长；四个参考缓冲在训练中累积分离（detached）的阶段统计量，推理时冻结。网络为四阶段金字塔，深度 (2,2,6,2)、宽度 (96,192,384,768)，224×224 输入下分类器 20.70M 参数。

Result: 围绕 ImageNet-1K 组织研究：干净分类、八项受控消融，以及高斯噪声、对比度、亮度三类扰动实验。

Conclusion: 慢速统计参考可在保持简单张量实现的前提下改善紧凑表示的鲁棒性；该机制属训练期统计方法，区别于输入相关的注意力，推理复杂度为线性。

Abstract: We present BDNet, a hierarchical image-classification backbone built around fast image features and slow statistical references. Its baseline deformation operator (BDO) transforms the deviation of a feature from a stage-specific reference through a low-rank projection and a learned channel scale. Rather than computing pairwise token similarity, the branch operates directly on baseline-relative residuals with complexity linear in the token count. Each of four reference buffers accumulates detached stage statistics during training and remains frozen at inference. A four-stage pyramid with depths $(2,2,6,2)$ and widths $(96,192,384,768)$ yields a 20.70M-parameter classifier at $224\times224$ resolution. We organize an ImageNet-1K study around clean classification, eight controlled ablations, and Gaussian-noise, contrast, and brightness perturbations. The central hypothesis is that a slow reference can improve the robustness of a compact representation while retaining a simple tensor implementation. We distinguish this training-time statistical mechanism from input-dependent attention and make its linear inference behavior explicit.

</details>


### [44] [FlexReader: Towards Adaptive Decoding Paths for Efficient Arbitrary-Length Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41426)
*Zhibin Ma,Pengwen Dai,Zhigang Yao,Xugong Qin,Wenqi Ren,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FlexReader，一个支持多种解码步长的场景文本识别模型，单模型可在不同数据集上实现最优的精度-效率权衡，并提出自适应前缀解码以动态选择解码步长，最高相较自回归解码提速 6 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 STR 模型在训练范式上与其特定解码策略强耦合，导致部署时难以灵活适配多样化识别场景，缺乏精度与效率之间的可调性。

Method: 1) 子串感知模块（Sub-string Perception）从视觉特征中感知目标子串；2) 多个并行解码块分别独立解码子串中每个字符，使模型可在感知子串范围内以任意步长解码；3) 自适应前缀解码（Adaptive Prefix Decoding）根据已解码字符的置信度逐步自适应选择解码步长，为每个输入寻找最优解码路径。

Result: 在包括长文本、弯曲文本和遮挡场景在内的多种基准上表现稳健；识别精度持续优于现有解码策略，且相较自回归解码最高实现 6 倍解码加速。

Conclusion: 通过解耦训练范式与单一解码策略，FlexReader 以单模型支持多步长解码并结合自适应前缀解码，在保持/提升精度的同时显著提高效率，为跨场景部署提供了灵活的精度-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Existing Scene Text Recognition (STR) models are inherently coupled with a specific decoding strategy by their training paradigms, limiting their flexibility in deployment across diverse recognition scenarios. In this paper, we propose FlexReader, an STR model that supports multiple decoding strides, enabling a single model to achieve an optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off across different datasets. Specifically, we first introduce a Sub-string Perception module to perceive the target sub-string from visual features, followed by multiple parallel decoding blocks that independently decode each character in the sub-string. During inference, FlexReader supports decoding with arbitrary strides within the perceived sub-string range. Furthermore, we propose Adaptive Prefix Decoding, which adaptively selects the decoding stride at each step based on the model's confidence in the decoded characters, thereby identifying the optimal decoding path for each input. Experimental results demonstrate that FlexReader exhibits robust recognition capability across a diverse range of benchmarks, including those featuring long text, curved text, and occlusions. It consistently outperforms existing decoding strategies in recognition accuracy while achieving up to 6-flod decoding speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [45] [Parse Before You Execute: Efficient Spatial Reasoning with Executable Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41389)
*Xiwei Liu,Yulong Li,Xinling Zhuang,Xuhui Li,Yichen Li,Haolin Yang,Zhixiang Lu,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PEPS 是一种免训练智能体范式，通过"先解析后执行"把空间查询编译为 FESM 类型化模式下的可执行原语，从而提升 VLM 空间推理的准确性与执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成式空间推理管线常将查询编译为代码、任务约束、工具计划或证据集合等开放式产物，执行前须解决的内容欠明确，导致不必要的工具获取与多轮精化，并能在空间承诺不完整时仍生成看似合理的推理轨迹。作者将此失效模式命名为 under-committed query compilation（欠承诺查询编译）。

Method: 提出 PEPS——免训练、智能体式范式，基于 Frame、Entity、State、Metric 的 FESM 类型化模式，遵循 parse-before-execute 原则：Parser 将每条查询承诺为一组紧凑的可执行需求；Executor 通过固定几何 API 与确定性计算有选择地接地所需原语；Verifier 检验执行充分性，支持提前终止或定向修复。

Result: 在多个高难度空间推理基准上，PEPS 稳定提升推理准确率，同时相较强工具集成基线显著减少精化轮数、API 开销与端到端时延。

Conclusion: 显式的需求承诺可使工具集成式空间推理不仅更可靠，也更具备执行效率。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) has increasingly benefited from tool-integrated pipelines that ground computation in explicit geometry. However, existing systems often compile queries into relatively open-ended artifacts, such as code, task constraints, tool plans, or gathered evidence, leaving what must be resolved before execution under-specified. This can lead to unnecessary tool acquisition and iterative refinement, while still producing plausible reasoning traces from incomplete spatial commitments. We identify this failure mode as under-committed query compilation. To address it, we propose PEPS, a training-free agentic paradigm Parsing Executable Primitives for Spatial reasoning under a typed FESM schema over Frame, Entity, State, and Metric. PEPS follows a parse-before-execute principle: the Parser commits each query to a compact set of executable requirements; the Executor selectively grounds the required primitives through fixed geometric APIs and deterministic computation; and the Verifier checks execution sufficiency, enabling early termination or targeted repair. Extensive experiments across challenging spatial reasoning benchmarks show that PEPS consistently improves reasoning accuracy while substantially reducing refinement rounds, API cost, and end-to-end latency over strong tool-integrated baselines. These results demonstrate that explicit requirement commitment can make tool-integrated spatial reasoning not only more reliable, but also more execution-efficient.

</details>


### [46] [Task-Performance Routing for Multi-Teacher Distillation in CT Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41362)
*Jiaye Yang,Yao Liu,Die Dai,Hanwen Zhang,Peiyuan Jiang,Qiao Liu,Yutong Xie,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Task-Performance Routing（TPR），用区域级分割误差动态确定多教师蒸馏权重，实现CT器官分割中教师优势的细粒度融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型在CT器官分割上各有优势，但优势随器官与空间区域变化；现有多教师蒸馏的全局或实例级教师权重忽略了3D体数据内部的空间异质性。

Method: TPR基于区域级分割误差在训练中确定教师权重，无需可学习路由参数：困难区域偏向低误差教师，简单区域采用均匀权重融合知识；并采用分层空间划分，浅层编码器阶段细粒度路由、深层阶段粗粒度路由。

Result: 在BTCV、MSD-Liver、MSD-Pancreas上用两组教师对与两种骨干验证，Dice均高于单教师、自适应集成及所评估的多教师蒸馏基线；MSD-Pancreas上SwinUNETR达65.40%、U-Net达68.25%，对应自适应集成为64.38%与67.45%。

Conclusion: 区域级误差驱动的教师路由可有效整合互补优势；推理仅用学生模型，无路由与教师开销，部署成本低。

Abstract: Pre-trained models offer complementary strengths in CT organ segmentation, but their relative accuracy varies across organs and spatial regions. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation can combine these strengths in a single student, yet global or instance-level teacher weights overlook variation within a 3D volume. We propose Task-Performance Routing (TPR), which uses region-wise segmentation errors to determine teacher weights during training without learned routing parameters. In hard regions, TPR favors teachers with lower prediction errors; in easy regions, it uses uniform weights to combine their knowledge. A hierarchical spatial partition provides fine-grained routing in shallow encoder stages and coarse-grained routing in deep stages. Experiments on BTCV, MSD-Liver, and MSD-Pancreas with two teacher pairs and two backbone architectures show higher Dice scores than the individual teachers, adaptive ensembles, and evaluated multi-teacher distillation baselines. On MSD-Pancreas, TPR achieves Dice scores of 65.40% with SwinUNETR and 68.25% with U-Net, compared with 64.38% and 67.45% for the corresponding adaptive ensembles. Inference uses the student alone, with no routing or teacher overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/EdvinCecilia/TPR_MultiTeacher.

</details>


### [47] [SketchDirector: Complex Scene Sketching via Agentic Layout Reasoning and Detail Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41260)
*Yiqi Liang,Xiaodong Cun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SketchDirector——一个模仿人类绘画流程的智能体框架，实现文本驱动的多概念复杂场景草图自动生成，并构建 MCSketch8K 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草图生成方法依赖图像引导，且多局限于单体物体或简化场景，文本引导的复杂场景草图生成研究不足。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 用 LLM 将场景级文本提示分解为多个以 Bézier 曲线表示的草稿素材；2) 基于自演化记忆的智能体推理循环，迭代规划并执行素材的空间布局；3) 细节精修模块，利用扩散先验在保持几何结构的前提下提升草图质量。同时构建 MCSketch8K 数据集（可编辑矢量素材、构图布局、物体级操作标注）。

Result: 大量实验表明，SketchDirector 在语义与构图质量上优于当前最优基线，并在精修过程中有效保持整体轮廓组织。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 分解、智能体布局推理与扩散先验精修相结合，是文本驱动多概念场景草图生成的有效范式；配套数据集为后续研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Existing sketch generation methods often rely on image guidance and are largely limited to individual objects or simplified scenes, leaving text-guided complex scene sketching underexplored. We present SketchDirector, an agentic framework that mimics the human sketching workflow for automatic multi-concept scene sketching. Given a scene-level text prompt, SketchDirector first employs a large language model~(LLM) to decompose the scene into multiple draft assets, where each is represented by a set of Bézier curves. Then, an agentic reasoning loop iteratively plans and executes their spatial arrangement, guided by self-evolving memory. Finally, a detail refinement module leverages diffusion priors to enhance sketch quality while preserving geometric structure. To support future research and applications in multi-concept scene sketch generation, we further introduce the \textit{MCSketch8K} dataset, comprising editable vector assets, compositional layouts, and object-level manipulation annotations. Extensive experiments show that SketchDirector outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in semantic and compositional quality while effectively preserving overall contour organization during refinement. The project page is: \href{https://yiki77.github.io/SketchDirector/}{https://yiki77.github.io/SketchDirector/}.

</details>


### [48] [CompoSET: A Controlled Single-Edit Benchmark for Vision-Language Compositionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41118)
*Laure Delisle,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CompoSET基准数据集，通过"单编辑对照(foil)"构造近乎相同但仅改变单一绑定的图文对，以控制现有图像-文本匹配基准中的全局混淆，并发现22个双编码器VLM在其上均达或低于随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言组合性基准采用图文匹配协议，但配对图像之间在场景、布局、内容上均不同（平均97%像素变化），结果被全局图像差异混淆，无法真正隔离组合绑定能力，导致此前进展可能是基准设计缺陷产生的伪象。

Method: 构建CompoSET数据集：每个基础图文对配有一个foil，保持场景不变、只做单一受控编辑（颜色、数量、空间关系、物体状态等），像素变化仅约8%；每个基础场景配约22个foil，以分离场景难度与编辑难度；共1,776个实例、80个自然场景、4大类16种编辑类型、字幕3种详细度。并在其上评测22个双编码器VLM。

Result: 所有22个双编码器VLM在CompoSET上表现均处于或低于随机水平，而它们在已有基准上得分良好。

Conclusion: 先前图文匹配协议下的表面进展可能是基准设计混淆造成的伪象，双编码器VLM的组合性理解远未解决；CompoSET提供了混淆更少、控制更严的基准，可用于衡量未来进展。

Abstract: Vision-language compositionality is the ability to identify which properties, relations, quantities, and states bind to which entities in both an image and a caption, and to match those bindings across modalities. Existing benchmarks evaluate this ability through image-text matching: a model ranks candidate image-caption match similarities and succeeds when the correct match comes on top. In such datasets, paired images differ by more than a single binding: scene, layout, and content all differ, with 97% of pixels changing on average. Such pairs do not isolate compositionality: results are confounded by global image differences. We introduce Compositional Single-EdiT (CompoSET), a benchmark dataset in which each base image-caption pair has an edited counterpart, or foil, that preserves the scene but differs by a single controlled edit, such as color, count, spatial relation, or object state. Base and foil images preserve naturalistic scene complexity yet are visually near-identical: only 8% of pixels change on average. Our dataset has many foils per base scene (~22) in order to separate scene difficulty from edit difficulty, a distinction one-foil-per-base benchmarks cannot make. CompoSET contains 1,776 instances across 80 naturalistic scenes, spanning 16 edit types in 4 semantic categories, with captions at 3 verbosity levels. We test 22 dual-encoder VLMs and find that all models perform at or below chance level on CompoSET, despite scoring well on prior benchmarks. This suggests that apparent progress under prior image-text matching protocols may be an artifact arising from confounds in benchmark design, and that compositional understanding in dual-encoder VLMs is far from solved. CompoSET addresses the confounds and offers a better controlled benchmark to measure future progress.

</details>


### [49] [AVID: A Weakly Aligned RGB-Infrared Dataset and Perturbation Benchmark for Tiny Aerial Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40949)
*Dharini Raghavan,Vishnu Rao,Sethu Selvi S*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AVID 基准（32,040 对 RGB-红外对齐帧、35,952 条鸟/无人机标注，320×256），并构建 AVID-C 退化数据集；引入目标相对对齐比与对齐负担曲线刻画跨模态几何难度，用固定特征分析显示小目标下 RGB+IR 融合可显著降低类间重叠（Bhattacharyya 界 0.148/0.198 vs 0.046）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离小无人机检测因与鸟类视觉相似、地基相机细节有限而困难；RGB 与红外互补性受目标尺寸、跨模态对齐和图像质量制约，但缺乏系统性研究这些因素的基准。

Method: 基于 Drone Detection Dataset 构建 ICIP 2025 VIP Cup 用的真实 RGB-IR 鸟-无人机监控基准 AVID（115 视频、32,040 帧对、35,952 标注）；提出目标相对对齐比和对齐负担曲线量化跨模态几何错位（抽样 300 对中 32.3% 全局平移>5 px，264 对匹配标注中 16.3% 超过一个中位目标边长）；构建 AVID-C，在 RGB/IR/双模态独立施加 7 类退化×5 级严重度；在固定特征与密度模型下拟合鸟-无人机类重叠。

Result: 目标尺寸分布极窄：68.1% 的几何平均框边长为 8–16 px，99.0% 满足 COCO 小目标标准。12–16 px 尺度下 Bhattacharyya 误差上界为 RGB 0.148、红外 0.198、融合特征 0.046。

Conclusion: AVID 与 AVID-C 支持尺度感知、对齐感知与传感器特异的小型空中目标研究；上述数值仅描述指定特征与密度模型下的可分性，而非检测器精度。

Abstract: Long-range detection of small drones is complicated by their visual similarity to birds and by the limited detail available from ground-based cameras. Combining visible and infrared observations offers complementary information, but their usefulness depends on target size, cross-modal alignment, and image quality. We present AVID, a benchmark for studying these factors using real RGB-infrared bird-drone surveillance footage originating from the Drone Detection Dataset and curated for the IEEE ICIP 2025 Video and Image Processing Cup. AVID comprises 32,040 corresponding frame pairs from 115 videos, with 35,952 existing bird and drone annotations at $320\times256$ resolution. Targets occupy an unusually narrow size range: 68.1\% have a geometric-mean bounding-box side of 8--16 pixels, and 99.0\% meet the COCO small-object criterion. To characterize the geometric difficulty of combining the modalities, we introduce an object-relative alignment ratio and an alignment burden curve. In a sample of 300 frame pairs, 32.3\% have estimated global translations greater than 5 pixels; among the 264 pairs with matching annotations, 16.3\% exceed one median target side. We further introduce AVID-C, which simulates seven families of image degradation at five severity levels independently in RGB, infrared, or both. A fixed-feature analysis examines how scale, degradation, and added cross-modal shifts affect fitted bird-drone class overlap. At 12--16 pixels, the Bhattacharyya error bounds are 0.148 for RGB, 0.198 for infrared, and 0.046 for their combined features. These values describe separation in the specified feature and density models, not detector accuracy. Together, AVID and AVID-C support scale-aware, alignment-aware, and sensor-specific studies of tiny aerial targets.

</details>


### [50] [UFMF: Radar-Guided Kinematic Priors for Real-Time 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40947)
*Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Robert Laganière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UFMF 超快多模态预测器，在无注意力的对角状态空间时序核心中注入运动学与几何先验，实现实时 4D 占据预测，单卡 122 FPS 并在 nuScenes 取得 0.45 快速位移 mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时 4D 占据预测需在严格延迟预算下可靠外推自车与周围动作者运动。现有占据世界模型借助生成式解码器与 3D 体素建模提升长时序保真度，但算力开销大难以实时；轻量 BEV 预测器虽满足延迟，却在长时域上模糊或抑制快速运动目标。

Method: 核心为无注意力的对角状态空间时序主干。引入雷达引导的速度感知动态先验（VADP），将未来位移分解为线性外推加可学习的时域相关残差，生成逐单元流场，在 rollout 中输运紧凑的动态隐子空间。另加两项辅助训练约束：世界坐标系动态监督以保证跨时域轨迹一致性；类别条件垂直剖面监督以正则化 BEV-to-3D 抬升。

Result: 在单张 RTX 4090 上达 122 FPS，nuScenes 上快速位移 mIoU 为 0.45，以远低算力超过近期占据世界模型。

Conclusion: 通过在轻量时序主干中有针对性地注入运动学与几何结构，可在实时约束下兼顾快速运动目标的预测精度与长时域保真度。

Abstract: Real-time 4D occupancy forecasting requires reliably extrapolating both ego motion and surrounding actor motion under strict latency budgets. Recent occupancy world models improve long-horizon fidelity through generative decoders and 3D volumetric modeling, yet their computational cost makes real-time deployment challenging. Light-weight BEV forecasters meet latency requirements but often blur or suppress fast-moving actors at longer horizons. We present UFMF (Ultra-Fast Multi-modal Forecaster), a real-time forecaster that keeps an attention-free diagonal state-space temporal core while injecting targeted kinematic and geometric structure where lightweight models typically fail. A radar-guided velocity-aware dynamic prior (VADP) decomposes future displacement into a linear extrapolation plus a learned horizon-dependent residual. The resulting per-cell flow fields transport a compact dynamic latent subspace during rollout. Two auxiliary training constraints complement this branch: world-frame dynamic supervision enforces inter-horizon trajectory coherence, and class-conditioned vertical-profile supervision regularizes BEV-to-3D lifting. UFMF operates at 122 FPS on a single RTX 4090 and reaches 0.45 fast-displacement mIoU on nuScenes, outperforming recent occupancy world models at a fraction of their computational cost.

</details>


### [51] [A Better Critic Matters for Distribution Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42135)
*Zongze Wu,Xianbang Wang,Zhengyang Geng,Mingdeng Cao,Zhoutong Zhang,Richard Zhang,Eli Shechtman,Ruiming Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究DMD蒸馏中critic所需的能力与信息，提出critic与teacher联合缩放（joint scaling）以及步感知critic（STAC），在图像、文本到图像、文本到视频任务上稳定提升少步蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD依赖teacher score与在线critic对student分布score估计之差来训练少步学生模型，但critic在容量与信息层面的需求尚不明确，小critic配大teacher可能导致质量下降甚至训练崩溃。

Method: 容量方面，在ImageNet上对七种SiT模型规模做控制实验，对比固定小critic与随teacher同步扩大critic（joint scaling）；信息方面，将原先建模与步数无关的student输出混合分布的critic，改为以student生成步为条件的步感知critic（STAC），估计步特定score。

Result: 固定小critic时增大teacher会降低student质量并导致训练崩溃；joint scaling可避免崩溃并使student从更大teacher中获益；joint scaling与STAC结合在类条件ImageNet生成、SDXL与FLUX.2文生图、Wan2.1文生视频上均稳定提升少步蒸馏效果。

Conclusion: DMD的有效性取决于critic的容量是否与teacher匹配以及是否获得步数信息；联合缩放critic并采用步感知critic是提升少步蒸馏鲁棒性与质量的关键设计。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) trains a few-step student using the difference between a teacher's score and an online critic's estimate of the student distribution's score. We investigate what the critic needs, in capacity and in information, to provide a reliable training signal. On the capacity side, controlled experiments across seven SiT model sizes on ImageNet show that increasing teacher size while keeping a small critic fixed can degrade student quality and cause training collapse. Scaling the critic alongside the teacher, a strategy we call joint scaling, prevents collapse in our experiments and allows the student to benefit from larger teachers. On the information side, rather than forcing the critic to model a step-agnostic mixture of student outputs, we introduce a step-aware critic (STAC) that conditions on the student's generation step to estimate step-specific scores. Together, joint scaling and STAC consistently improve few-step distillation across class-conditional ImageNet generation, text-to-image generation with SDXL and FLUX.2, and text-to-video generation with Wan2.1.

</details>


### [52] [GeoFlip: Benchmarking Geometry-Sensitive Physics in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42002)
*Yuqi Li,Junming Liu,Bowen Hu,Ruize Niu,Huiran Duan,Yingli Tian,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFlip：以几何干预诱导物理结果翻转的视频生成基准，含482对场景/964段参考视频、6类物理类别，评测16个I2V模型；全模型公共442条件下事件正确率最高仅27.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能合成逼真运动，但能否正确响应初始几何条件的变化（如支撑范围、物体位置、机构构型）尚不清楚；需要可控、可验证的基准来检验模型是否将首帧几何差异转化为正确的物理演化。

Method: 构建成对场景：固定物理设置与文本提示，仅对支撑范围、物体放置与机构构型施加受控干预，形成结果相反（如保持稳定 vs 倾倒/坠落）的配对参考事件；参考结果由程序化场景构建、物理仿真与物理事件的数值校验确认。评测16个图像到视频模型，沿事件正确性、结构完整性、与参考视频相似度三个维度打分，并用独立视觉语言评判器检验评估一致性。

Result: 在全部模型共享的442个条件上，跨任务平均事件正确率最高为27.98%；不同模型在各类物理机制上表现各异，三个评测维度反映相异能力；典型失败模式包括忽略几何变化、生成错误物体运动、刚性物体弯曲、引入额外部件；评判器间秩相关在事件正确性上达0.906，结构完整性上达0.929。

Conclusion: GeoFlip通过受控几何干预、可验证物理参考与多维度评估相结合，揭示当前视频生成模型在几何条件下的物理一致性仍严重不足，为从视觉真实感迈向物理一致性提供了可量化基准。

Abstract: Video generation models synthesize realistic motion, but can they respond correctly to changes in initial geometry? We introduce GeoFlip, a video generation benchmark centered on physical outcome flips induced by geometry. With shared physical settings and textual prompts held fixed, GeoFlip applies controlled interventions to support extent, object placement, and mechanism configuration, constructing paired scenes with contrasting reference events, such as remaining stable versus falling or tipping. These pairs test whether models translate geometric differences in the first frame into correct physical evolution. The benchmark comprises 482 scene pairs and 964 reference videos across six physical categories, with reference outcomes established through procedural scene construction, physics simulation, and numerical validation and verification of physical events. We evaluate 16 models for image to video generation along three dimensions: event correctness, structural integrity, and similarity to reference videos. We examine evaluation consistency with independent vision language judges. On the 442 conditions shared by all models, the highest event correctness averaged across tasks is 27.98%, revealing substantial limitations in physical generation conditioned on geometry. Model strengths vary across physical mechanisms, and the three evaluation dimensions capture distinct capabilities. Typical failures include ignoring geometric changes, generating incorrect object motion, bending rigid objects, and introducing additional components. Rank correlations between judges reach 0.906 for event correctness and 0.929 for structural integrity. By combining controlled geometric interventions, verifiable physical references, and multidimensional evaluation, GeoFlip assesses progress from visual realism toward physical consistency conditioned on geometry.

</details>


### [53] [TwinPast: Benchmarking Historical Evidence Use in Streaming Video Understanding with Evidence Contrastive Video Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41983)
*Haotian Yang,Yong Chen,Cheng Peng,Haobo Wang,Zhongle Xie,Xuanru Li,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TwinPast 基准，用350对人工审核视频对（固定近期上下文与题目、仅改变关键历史证据及正确答案）检验流式视频模型是否真正依据视觉历史作答；34种配置中最佳流式系统仅49.14% PairAcc，显著低于通用模型66.57–91.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频基准的高精度不能证明模型使用了视觉历史：抽样发现32%的历史类问题仅靠近期帧即可作答，且仅凭题目与选项模型也能答对不少问题，说明基准存在捷径与混淆因素。

Method: 构建 TwinPast：350对人工审核视频对，覆盖5类历史任务；每对保持近期视频上下文、问题与选项顺序不变，仅改变关键历史证据及正确答案；每条视频独立评估，采用 PairAcc（两段历史均答对才算正确）；共评测34种模型与输入配置，并进行案例分析。

Result: Qwen3-VL-8B 与 DeepSeek-Flash 在 TwinPast 上历史输入带来性能提升，逆转了其在现有基准上的"近期帧优势"；最强流式系统 PairAcc 为49.14%，通用模型为66.57–91.43%，差距在五类任务上一致存在；案例显示模型即使保留相关证据，仍会选取错误时间点的状态或违背事件顺序。

Conclusion: 历史记忆与检索必须与推理相衔接，使模型依据问题的时间指代与事件关系使用证据；仅靠现有基准精度无法表征真实的历史理解能力。

Abstract: High accuracy on streaming video benchmarks does not establish whether a model uses visual history. Our review of OVO-Bench and StreamingBench finds that recent frames suffice for 32% of sampled historical questions, while models also answer many questions correctly from the question and options alone. We introduce TwinPast to test whether answers correctly follow changes in history, using 350 human-reviewed video pairs spanning five historical tasks. Each pair holds the recent video context, question, and ordered options fixed while changing critical historical evidence and the correct answer. Each video is evaluated independently, and PairAcc requires correct answers for both histories. We evaluate 34 model and input configurations. For Qwen3-VL-8B and DeepSeek-Flash, historical inputs improve performance on TwinPast, reversing the recent-frame advantage on existing benchmarks. The strongest evaluated streaming system reaches 49.14% PairAcc, compared with 66.57-91.43% for the evaluated general-purpose models. This gap persists across all five tasks. Case studies further show that models select states from the wrong time or contradict recorded event order despite retaining the relevant evidence. These findings highlight the need to connect historical memory and retrieval with reasoning that uses evidence according to the question's temporal references and event relations.

</details>


### [54] [Nereus: A Large-Scale Underwater Dataset for Fine-Grained Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41705)
*Jingtao Deng,Zihao Jin,Yuehe Chen,Feixiang He,Shuiwang Li,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Nereus，一个面向水下细粒度感知的大规模数据集，同时支持细粒度物体属性理解与带定位的计数感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下数据集多只提供粗类别标签、泛化描述或总计数监督，难以支撑生物个体级分析与生态推理。

Method: 构建包含 88K 图像、2.68M 问答对的数据集，标注 136K 海洋生物目标，覆盖 2,690 个物种与 1,515 个属，并提供 65K 点标注与 67K 带定位计数问答对；用代表性多模态模型做实验验证。

Result: 实验表明 Nereus 相比现有水下领域数据能更有效地提升下游适配性能。

Conclusion: Nereus 为细粒度海洋感知与生态推理研究提供了数据基础。

Abstract: Underwater imagery records rich ecological information and supports applications such as science communication, fishery monitoring, ecological conservation, and oceanographic research. Beyond general scene understanding, these applications require models to recognize fine-grained biological traits and reason over spatially grounded evidence. However, existing underwater datasets often provide coarse category labels, generic captions, or total-count supervision, which limits their use for organism-level analysis and ecological reasoning. We introduce Nereus, a large-scale dataset for Underwater Fine-grained Perception that jointly supports Fine-Grained Object Attribute Understanding and Grounded Counting Perception. Nereus contains 88K images and 2.68M question-answer pairs, including annotations for 136K marine organism objects across 2,690 species and 1,515 genera, as well as 65K point annotations and 67K grounded counting QA pairs. Experiments with representative multimodal models show that Nereus improves downstream adaptation over existing underwater-domain data and provides a foundation for future research on fine-grained marine perception.

</details>


### [55] [ReVEAL: Eliciting Role-Aware Latent Visual Evidence from MLLMs for Reasoning Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41257)
*Zhongyuan Bao,Cunjie He,Lejun Zhang,Zenglin Xu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReVEAL：一种解耦的"视频中思考"框架，从MLLM推理中提取角色感知的潜在视觉证据以直接引导冻结SAM2完成推理视频目标分割（ReVOS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的ReVOS方法需昂贵的跨模型对齐，且MLLM训练与下游分割模型强绑定，灵活性差、成本高。

Method: 引入Visual Evidence Tokens（VETs）关联目标/背景/干扰物角色；用Dynamic Attention Layer Router跨层自适应聚合VET到视频的注意力，形成角色特定grounding证据；构建ReVEAL-47K数据集训练MLLM进行VET增强推理，并以Contrastive Attention Grounding Loss作辅助监督约束VET注意力、优化层路由；推理时融合角色证据引导冻结SAM2输出掩码。

Result: 五个基准上优于无对齐基线，并与基于对齐的方法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过角色感知潜在视觉证据实现MLLM与分割模型的解耦，可在无需跨模型对齐下取得有竞争力的ReVOS性能。

Abstract: Reasoning video object segmentation (ReVOS) requires segmenting the target specified by an implicit query throughout a video. Existing MLLM-based methods typically require costly cross-model alignment, tying MLLM training to a downstream segmentation model. We present ReVEAL, a decoupled thinking-with-video framework for ReVOS that elicits role-aware latent visual evidence from MLLM reasoning to directly guide segmentation. ReVEAL introduces Visual Evidence Tokens (VETs) into video reasoning to associate necessary visual regions or entities with target, background, or distractor roles, while a Dynamic Attention Layer Router adaptively aggregates VET-to-video attention across layers into role-specific grounding evidence. We train the MLLM to perform VET-augmented reasoning on our constructed ReVEAL-47K dataset, with a Contrastive Attention Grounding Loss as auxiliary supervision to apply role-specific constraints to VET attention and optimize layer routing for reliable latent grounding. At inference, ReVEAL identifies the target through this thinking-with-video process and fuses the resulting role-specific evidence to guide a frozen SAM2 in predicting target masks. Experiments on five benchmarks show that ReVEAL outperforms alignment-free baselines and remains competitive with alignment-based methods.

</details>


### [56] [Hawkeye: Reading Illegible Text with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41254)
*Brian Tang,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建了针对模糊/低分辨率场景文本的基准 BSTRB（8,398 张噪声图像、来自 1,699 段真实视频），并提出微调 VLM "Hawkeye"，借助上下文先验与像素级对齐读取不可辨认文本。结果显示其在 BSTRB 上整体比肩更大模型，且 ≤20 像素文本上更优，单次推理适合实时部署，并刻画了现有 VLM 的分辨率失效阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 VLM 在通用场景文本识别上表现优异，但对受运动模糊或低分辨率影响的长尾文本持续失败；在智能眼镜等第一视角应用中，噪声环境下的文本识别问题尤为突出，缺乏相应评测基准与针对性模型。

Method: 1) 构建 BSTRB 基准：8,398 个噪声图像样本，标注高保真，源自 1,699 段真实视频序列；2) 提出 Hawkeye，在 BSTRB 上微调的 VLM，利用上下文先验与像素级对齐进行读取与推理；3) 单次前向推理，面向实时部署。

Result: Hawkeye 在 BSTRB 上整体性能与更大规模 VLM 相当，在像素高度 ≤20 的文本上超过后者，且以单次推理完成，适配实时场景；同时刻画了当前 VLM 开始失效的分辨率区间。

Conclusion: 针对长尾退化文本，专用基准与轻量微调 VLM 可有效替代大模型，兼具精度与效率；分辨率是当前 VLM 场景文本识别的关键瓶颈之一。

Abstract: While modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have been remarkably successful in general scene text recognition, they consistently struggle with the long-tail distribution of text that is degraded by motion blur or low resolution. These limitations are particularly apparent in egocentric applications like smart glasses, where text is often captured in noisy environments. In this paper, we introduce the \textbf{BSTRB} (Blurry Scene Text Recognition Benchmark), a novel benchmark of 8,398 noisy image samples with high-fidelity annotations derived from 1,699 real-world video sequences. To support this, we present \textbf{Hawkeye}, a VLM fine-tuned on BSTRB to read and reason about illegible text by leveraging contextual priors and pixel-level alignment. Our results show that \name matches much larger VLMs on BSTRB overall and exceeds them on text at or below 20 pixels, in a single pass suited to real-time deployment. We characterize the resolution at which current VLMs begin to fail.

</details>


### [57] [MotorSense: A Video-EMG Dataset of Motoric Representations for Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41127)
*Eadom Dessalene,Michael Maynord,Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh,Botao He,Yasamin Tabatabaee,Ruoshi Liu,Ruohan Gao,Paul Pu Liang,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MotorSense——首个大规模配对的自我中心视频与表面肌电(EMG)数据集,并通过视频-EMG跨模态预训练学习感觉运动对齐,在四类以动作为中心的视觉任务与八个视频数据集上均超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉模型只能从视觉结果中学习手部动作,无法获取内在的具身运动信号;而人工标注成本高、主观性强,且在遮挡下不可靠。

Method: 构建 MotorSense 数据集:双手 EMG 臂带与自我中心视频配对,含100小时无脚本操作数据,同步采集视频、双手EMG、IMU与音频。以 EMG 作为稠密、无遮挡、无标注偏见的信号,通过视频与EMG的跨模态预训练实现感觉运动对齐,建立跨视觉与运动空间的共享动作组织;并将所得视觉模型迁移至动作识别、3D手部重建、3D接触重建,以及以EMG条件化视频扩散模型生成细粒度手部操作视频。

Result: 在四类以动作为中心的计算机视觉任务和八个主流视频数据集上,感觉运动对齐均优于传统视频自监督学习方法,在所有任务与数据集上超越全部基线。

Conclusion: EMG 是可扩展的标注替代信号;感觉运动对齐为视觉表征学习提供了比传统视频自监督更强的基座,并可拓展至生成式应用。

Abstract: Computer vision models learn hand action only from its visual consequences, without access to the underlying embodied motor signals. We address this absence with MotorSense, the first large-scale paired egocentric video and surface electromyography (EMG) dataset. MotorSense pairs bimanual EMG bands with egocentric video and yields synchronized video, bimanual EMG and IMU, and audio across 100 hours of unscripted manipulation. EMG offers a powerful scalable alternative to manual annotation: it captures finger movement, hand configuration, and the forces exerted between hand and environment as a dense signal free of occlusion and labeler bias, whereas manual annotation is costly, subjective, and unreliable under occlusion. We use MotorSense to learn sensorimotor alignment, producing an organization of action that is shared across the visual and motoric spaces, through cross-modal pre-training between video and EMG. The resultant vision models are then transferred to downstream tasks that benefit from sensorimotor alignment: in this case, action recognition, 3D hand reconstruction, and 3D contact reconstruction. We further demonstrate a generative application in which EMG motor signals are used to condition a video diffusion model, yielding videos of fine-grained hand manipulation. Across four action-centric computer vision tasks and eight popular video datasets, we demonstrate that sensorimotor alignment provides a stronger foundation for visual representation learning than conventional self-supervised learning methods over video, outperforming every baseline across all tasks and datasets evaluated.

</details>


### [58] [When Depth Lies: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Mirror-Induced RGB-D Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41651)
*Hao Yin,Tianchen Guo,Heming Du,Yan Ke,Yanbin Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MIRA-Bench——一个以镜子/反射场景为核心的 RGB-D 数据集与基准，用于评测视觉语言模型在反射导致的深度失真与虚实难辨情形下的空间推理能力，发现现有模型的系统性失败，且引入深度数据反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 空间推理工作普遍默认深度数据忠实反映物理距离，而镜子打破这一假设：反射会同时扭曲深度读数并复制物体外观，使倒影与真实物体难以区分。已有基准极少涉及反射场景，模型在此类情况下的表现几乎空白。

Method: 构建镜像中心的 RGB-D 数据集：采集 525 个真实场景的 1,011 张 RGB-D 图像，覆盖多样室内外环境、6 类反射表面、昼夜两种条件；标注反射表面区域掩码，以及直接观测目标与镜面反射目标的掩码。在此基础上构建 MIRA-Bench（Mirror-Induced Reasoning Ambiguity Benchmark），含 14,140 个 QA 对、8 个子任务，按三个认知层次组织：反射感知（Reflection-aware Perception）、空间推理（Spatial Reasoning）、场景级决策（Scene-level Decision-making）。随后对 SOTA VLM 进行评测。

Result: 各任务上模型表现出一致的失败：无法区分物体是直接观测还是反射成像；无法将反射实例与其真实对应物匹配；产生错误的空间推断。值得注意的是，提供深度数据反而使推理性能下降，说明 VLM 无法识别镜面场景中失真的深度。

Conclusion: 镜子反射是 RGB-D 空间推理中被忽视的关键失效模式，现有 VLM 对反射引起的虚实歧义与深度不可靠性缺乏鲁棒性；MIRA-Bench 为该问题提供了标准化评测，揭示"深度即可信"的假设在反射场景下不成立。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made significant progress in spatial reasoning, particularly by integrating RGB images with depth data, which is often assumed to faithfully reflect physical distances. However, mirrors violate this assumption: they distort depth readings while reproducing object appearances, making reflections difficult to distinguish from real objects. As existing benchmarks rarely consider such reflective scenarios, VLM performance in mirror scenes remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce a mirror-centric RGB-D dataset from reflective scenes to evaluate the spatial reasoning capabilities of state-of-the-art VLMs in these scenarios. Specifically, we collect 1,011 RGB-D images from 525 real-world scenes, spanning diverse indoor and outdoor environments, six reflective surface types, and both daytime and nighttime conditions. All images are annotated with reflective surface region masks and mask annotations for both directly observed and mirror-reflected objects. Then, we curate a benchmark named MIRA-Bench (Mirror-Induced Reasoning Ambiguity Benchmark), comprising 14,140 QA pairs across eight sub-tasks organized into three cognitive levels: Reflection-aware Perception, Spatial Reasoning, and Scene-level Decision-making. We evaluate state-of-the-art VLMs on MIRA-Bench and find consistent failures across tasks: models misclassify whether objects are directly observed or reflected, fail to match reflected instances to their real-world counterparts, and produce erroneous spatial inferences. Remarkably, providing depth data actually degrades reasoning performance, implying that VLMs cannot identify incorrect depth in mirror scenes. The code and data are available at https://github.com/EvaYin-cloud/MIRA-Bench

</details>


### [59] [Scout-Refine-Correct: Teaching Flow Models to Refine Their Own Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41598)
*Sergey Karpukhin,Aibek Alanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRC（Scout-Refine-Correct）是一种流匹配生成模型的训练配方，通过在采样过程中引入"侦察展开—自我条件—校正损失"机制，赋予生成器真正的推理时精炼能力，在ImageNet-256上将LightningDiT-B的FID从14.5降至7.26（NFE~550），且无需外部验证器或奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流匹配模型的生成质量在训练预算确定后基本固化；推理阶段唯一可调的是采样步数（NFE），且增益存在饱和。因此作者希望让模型在推理时具备可迭代的精炼能力，突破"训练预算决定质量上限"的限制，并避免依赖需oracle验证器的最优N选（best-of-N）方案。

Method: 核心为三步式训练流程：①Scout（侦察）：在每个采样步，令模型沿自身轨迹向前展开K步，外推得到全样本估计；②Refine（精炼）：将该估计作为自我条件信号回馈给模型，再进行真实采样步，可重复R轮；③Correct（校正）：设计两个校正损失，且这些损失施加于模型自身rollout实际到达的状态（而非仅真值轨迹上），以训练模型利用该信号探索新样本、避免坍缩到次优解。方法不引入外部验证器与奖励信号，消融后参数增量极小。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，SRC使LightningDiT-B在NFE~550时FID由14.5降至7.26，持平于使用oracle FID验证器进行样本筛选的privileged best-of-N基线；与best-of-N结合后进一步在NFE~4400达到FID~3.81，表明两条轴互补。消融验证了侦察信号的架构通路、侦察时域K、各校正损失及训练调度等关键设计组件。

Conclusion: SRC通过让生成器利用自身轨迹的自我条件信号并配合针对rollout状态的校正损失，实现了无需外部验证器的推理时精炼能力，显著改善低NFE下的生成质量，且与best-of-N正交可叠加，为突破采样步数的收益饱和提供了一条有效的训练侧路径。

Abstract: The quality of image generation models trained with diffusion or flow matching is largely fixed by the training budget: at inference, the only available lever is the number of sampling steps, and additional steps improve quality only up to a point. We present a training recipe, Scout-Refine-Correct (SRC), that instead gives a flow-matching generator a genuine inference-time refinement capability. At each sampling step the model takes a scout rollout -- $K$ steps ahead along its own trajectory -- extrapolates a full-sample estimate, and feeds that estimate back to itself as a self-conditioning signal before taking the real step. This can be repeated for $R$ refinement rounds. Two correction losses, applied during training at states the model's own rollouts actually reach rather than only on the ground-truth path, teach the model to use this signal to reach new samples instead of collapsing to a suboptimal one. On ImageNet-256, SRC improves LightningDiT-B from FID~14.5 to FID~7.26 at NFE~550, matching a privileged best-of-$N$ baseline that uses an oracle FID verifier to choose among samples. SRC uses no external verifier, no reward signal, and, after ablation, almost no extra parameters over the base architecture. Combining SRC with best-of-$N$ improves the result further, to FID~3.81 at NFE~4400, showing that the two axes are complementary. We ablate the main design choices -- the architectural pathway for the scout signal, the scout horizon, each correction loss, and the training schedule to demonstrate key working components of training.

</details>


### [60] [Prior-Guided Single-Exposure HDR Novel View Synthesis via Base-Gain Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41011)
*Armin Shafiee Sarvestani,Sheyang Tang,Jialu Xu,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PriorGS：用伪HDR监督+闭环约束+可学习先验，并以"3D基础-增益"分解提升单曝光HDR新视角合成的鲁棒性与可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单曝光HDR NVS仅有单曝光LDR视图作监督，缺少HDR真值，导致HDR重建欠约束、难以忠实恢复；同时单曝光LDR仅能在有限范围内约束CRF，超出范围的重曝光不可靠。故需引入学习到的LDR→HDR重建先验与CRF先验。

Method: 提出PriorGS（3DGS框架）：(1) 以2D单曝光HDR重建方法生成的伪HDR作为监督引导HDR恢复，并通过闭环重建回路将结果锚定回输入LDR视图；(2) 用保证单调性、促进合理响应的可学习先验正则化CRF建模；(3) 提出3D base-gain HDR表示，将HDR辐照度分解为LDR基色与叠加的3D增益场，以缓解独立生成伪HDR目标跨视图不一致的问题，并支持3D HDR外观编辑。

Result: 大量实验表明PriorGS显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过伪HDR监督+闭环锚定、CRF可学习先验以及base-gain分解表示，PriorGS在单曝光HDR新视角合成中实现更鲁棒的HDR重建与可靠的CRF/重曝光建模，并额外获得3D HDR外观编辑能力。

Abstract: Single-exposure high dynamic range (HDR) novel view synthesis (NVS) recovers HDR scenes from low dynamic range (LDR) views captured at a single exposure and estimates camera response function (CRF) for LDR re-exposure. Without HDR ground-truth, using single-exposure LDR views as the sole source of direct supervision underconstrains HDR reconstruction and cannot ensure its faithful recovery, motivating the need for learned LDR-to-HDR reconstruction priors. Moreover, single-exposure LDR views supervise CRF only over a limited range, necessitating a learnable prior for reliable re-exposure beyond it. We propose PriorGS, a 3D Gaussian Splatting framework that addresses both challenges by (1) guiding HDR recovery with pseudo-HDR supervision from 2D single-exposure HDR reconstruction methods while anchoring it to input LDR views through a closed reconstruction loop; and (2) regularizing CRF modeling with a learnable prior that guarantees monotonicity and promotes plausible responses. Yet cross-view inconsistencies in independently generated pseudo-HDR targets impair scene reconstruction when HDR is encoded directly as Gaussian's color attribute. We therefore introduce a 3D base-gain HDR representation that factors HDR radiance into an LDR base and an overlaid 3D gain field, promoting robustness to such inconsistencies and enabling 3D HDR appearance editing. Extensive experiments demonstrate PriorGS's substantial superiority over baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Let LiDAR Decide Where to Look: Geometry-Measured Token Selection for Efficient Sparse Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40935)
*Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Robert Laganière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何感知Token选择（GATS），利用LiDAR/雷达点投影在视觉骨干运行前筛选少量图像token，并以3D锚点驱动自监督潜在世界模型，实现最高18倍FLOPs削减且精度超过稠密基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉自动驾驶模型每帧编码数千图像token，其中天空、建筑、空路面等与规划无关，且稀释规划器注意力；实验显示随机丢弃75% token即可优于稠密模型，说明token冗余严重，需要更优的选择机制。

Method: GATS在骨干网络前用LiDAR与雷达点云投影做选择器，因基于实测3D几何而非视觉特征，骨干只编码被选中的patch；每个保留token获得来自LiDAR点的3D锚点，并输入自监督潜在世界模型预测未来特征与3D位移，无需框标注。

Result: 25%预算下nuScenes 3秒L2位移误差0.520 m、NAVSIM EPDMS 88.9，优于稠密基线（0.573 m/85.9），总FLOPs减少6.6倍；10%预算（24 GFLOPs，18倍削减）仍达0.537 m与87.9；739个关键场景中覆盖95.8%危险车辆，碰撞率0.09%，比稠密模型（0.34%）低73.5%。

Conclusion: 基于几何先验的token选择可大幅压缩视觉token而不损精度，精度提升来自注意力集中于驾驶关键内容，而非单纯算力节省；该框架兼顾效率与安全性。

Abstract: Vision-centric autonomous driving models encode thousands of image tokens per frame, yet most depict sky, buildings, or empty road---content irrelevant to planning. Worse, these tokens dilute the planner's attention: we show that randomly discarding 75% of tokens already outperforms the dense model, because the planner can concentrate on the remaining driving-critical content. Building on this finding, we present Geometry-Aware Token Selection (GATS), which uses LiDAR and radar point projections to select a small fraction of image tokens before the vision backbone runs. Because the selector operates on measured 3D geometry rather than visual features, the backbone encodes only the selected patches, cutting end-to-end FLOPs by up to $18\times$. Each kept token receives a 3D anchor from its LiDAR point and is fed to a self-supervised latent world model that predicts future features and 3D displacements without box annotations. At 25% budget, GATS achieves a 3-sec L2 displacement error of 0.520 m on nuScenes and an EPDMS of 88.9 on NAVSIM---surpassing the dense baseline (0.573 m / 85.9) at $6.6\times$ fewer total FLOPs. At an extreme 10% budget (24 GFLOPs, $18\times$ reduction), it still beats the dense baseline on both nuScenes (0.537 m vs. 0.573 m L2) and NAVSIM (87.9 vs. 85.9 EPDMS). On 739 intent-critical scenarios, GATS covers 95.8% of hazardous vehicles and achieves a 0.09% collision rate---73.5% lower than the dense model (0.34%).

</details>


### [62] [Coupled or Independent? Fault Coupling in Agentic Medical Vision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40913)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Md Mostafijur Rahman,Jamil Fayyad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验"多通道可检验性带来安全收益"这一假设在传感器读数本身不可信时是否成立：当视觉工具通道与推理通道共享同一视觉故障时，交叉比对会产生"联合错误一致"的盲区，硬标签通道比对无法检出联合失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为，配备工具的视觉语言智能体暴露中间输出，便于与模型推理比对，从而比单次推理模型更安全。但该假设隐含前提是各通道故障相互独立；本文要考察当工具输入（传感器图像）本身不可信、两通道共享同一视觉故障时，这种安全收益是否还存在。

Method: 构建基于分割、对比度检索与迭代视觉语言推理器的皮肤科智能体；提出耦合分类法（taxonomy），区分单通道错误与联合错误（一致/不一致）；在PAD-UFES-20上比较排版式注入（typographic injection）与物理损坏两类扰动，并对比裁剪优先检索与全图检索两种策略；以硬标签通道比对衡量不一致召回率，并在独立HIBA测试集上验证顺序效应方向。

Result: 在PAD-UFES-20上，排版式注入产生可检测的方向性检索劫持，物理损坏则不能；裁剪优先检索保留载荷的可读尺度，产生55%的联合错误一致（占故障样本71%），全图检索为35%（占43%）；二元决策下若两通道均错则预测必然一致，硬标签通道比对无法检出联合失效，不一致召回率仅为0.29（裁剪优先）与0.57（全图）；顺序效应的方向在HIBA测试集上得到复现。

Conclusion: 当工具与推理通道共享视觉故障时，可检验性带来的安全收益显著削弱：联合错误一致使通道间比对失效，且检索粒度（裁剪优先 vs 全图）影响联合错误率与不一致召回。需要超越硬标签比对的一致性感知检测机制，并在多个数据集上验证效应的稳健性。

Abstract: Vision-language agents equipped with tools expose intermediate outputs for inspection and comparison with the model's reasoning, which may offer safety benefits over single-shot models. This paper tests that potential benefit when the sensor reading itself is untrusted, so that the tool and reasoning channels share a common visual fault. We consider a dermatology agent based on segmentation, contrastive retrieval, and an iterative vision-language reasoner and define a coupling taxonomy that distinguishes single-channel errors from joint errors that agree or disagree. On PAD-UFES-20, we show that typographic injection produces detectable directional retrieval capture, whereas physical corruption does not. Crop-first retrieval preserves the payload at a readable scale and produces 55% joint wrong agreement across all cases (71% of faulty cases); full-image retrieval produces 35% (43%). Under a binary decision, if both channels are wrong, their predictions must agree, so hard-label channel comparison cannot detect joint failures, limiting disagreement recall to 0.29 (crop-first) versus 0.57 (full-image). The direction of the order effect is reproduced on the independent HIBA test set.

</details>


### [63] [AquaProbe: Efficient Adaptation of Frozen Vision Models for Underwater Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40900)
*Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Nishi Kanta Paul,Trung Dong,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AquaProbe，量化水下域差异随水深类型与成像距离在编码器各层的分布，并据此按比例分配LoRA秩；在相同0.59M参数预算下，mIoU达70.1，比均匀分配高1.9；三路条件路由器在SUIM上达70.6 mIoU、held-out退化下AUDRC 63.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陆上预训练视觉模型在水下因浑浊、后向散射与波长相关衰减而性能下降；现有适配方法或将适配器均匀分布于编码器各深度，或用两固定域间的散度选层，均未刻画域差异位置随水体类型与成像距离的变化。

Method: AquaProbe：用成对的清晰图像与物理渲染图像，将编码器新引入的域差异与经冻结编码器累积的差异分离，测量各层域差异剖面；再按该剖面比例分配固定的LoRA秩预算；并引入以估计条件簇为条件的四？三路路由器。

Result: 按测量剖面比例分配LoRA秩的分割mIoU为70.1，在0.59M适配参数预算下比均匀分配提升1.9 mIoU；条件三路路由器在SUIM标准划分上达70.6 mIoU，在held-out退化下AUDRC为63.4。

Conclusion: 域差异在水下编码器中的位置随水体类型和成像距离变化，基于层间差异剖面的比例化LoRA分配与条件路由可提升水下分割的可靠性与泛化性。

Abstract: Reliable visual perception is essential for safe autonomous underwater exploration. Yet vision models pretrained on terrestrial images become less reliable underwater, where turbidity, backscatter, and wavelength-dependent attenuation alter scene appearance. Existing adaptation methods either spread adapters equally across the encoder depth or select layers using the divergence between two fixed domains. In this paper, we introduce AquaProbe for measuring how the location of the domain gap changes with water type and imaging range. AquaProbe separates newly introduced divergence from that accumulated through a frozen vision encoder using paired clean and physically rendered images and allocates a fixed LoRA budget according to the measured profile. The segmentation mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) for the proportional LoRA rank allocation, based on the measured profile, is 70.1; this is a better result than uniform LoRA rank allocation by 1.9 mIoU points while having the same parameter budget of 0.59 million adapter parameters. The three-way router, conditioned on estimated condition clusters, achieves 70.6 mIoU on the standard SUIM split and 63.4 AUDRC under held-out degradation.

</details>


### [64] [EgoHSI3R: Online 4D Hand-Scene Interaction Reconstruction from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40832)
*Mingzhi Pei,Zhangchen Ye,Ruoqu Chen,Liu Cao,Mengdi Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoHSI3R，一个前馈、在线、因果的4D第一人称手-场景交互重建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只做相机坐标系下的手部重建或世界坐标系下的手部轨迹，未联合恢复场景点图与交互信息；从这类专门化预测构建完整重建通常需要逐序列优化。

Method: 维护随时间累积几何证据的循环场景表示，提供共享世界空间与尺度；引入以手为中心的提示，融合预训练手先验与局部场景上下文；用MANO顶点查询关注场景特征以预测逐顶点稠密接触；单一网络联合估计相机运动、稠密场景几何、世界空间双侧MANO手部及逐顶点接触。

Result: 在评测方法中，EgoHSI3R在ARCTIC上取得最低的世界坐标系手部关节误差；推理速度快于现有世界空间手部重建流程；相机与场景重建精度具竞争力。

Conclusion: EgoHSI3R 以因果、在线、前馈方式统一重建相机、场景与手部交互，避免逐序列优化。

Abstract: Egocentric videos offer an abundant source of hand-scene interactions for embodied learning, which requires recovering hands, camera, and scene consistently in a shared 3D world. Existing methods focus on either camera-frame hands or world-space hand trajectories, without jointly recovering the scene pointmaps or interaction information. Building a complete reconstruction from such specialized predictions therefore typically requires per-sequence optimization. We propose EgoHSI3R, a unified feed-forward framework for online 4D hand-scene interaction reconstruction from egocentric monocular video. To jointly recover these components, it maintains a recurrent scene representation that accumulates geometric evidence over time and provides a shared world space and scale. We introduce hand-centric prompts that fuse pretrained hand priors and local scene context, while MANO vertex queries attend to scene features to predict dense per-vertex contact. These designs enable a single network to jointly estimate camera motion, dense scene geometry, world-space bilateral MANO hands, and per-vertex hand contact. Despite operating causally, EgoHSI3R achieves the lowest world-space hand joint error on ARCTIC among the evaluated methods, while offering faster inference than existing world-space hand reconstruction pipelines and competitive camera and scene reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [65] [TACO: Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation for Memorization Localization and Mitigation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41520)
*Ruxi Gu,Zilei Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轨迹感知曲率观测（TACO）统一框架，通过聚合晚期去噪轨迹上的曲率响应实现扩散模型局部记忆的精确检测与针对性缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型会在局部图像区域复现记忆化的训练内容，需要精确定位以便针对性缓解；但单一步去噪状态的曲率含瞬态响应，且空间均匀的缓解会破坏与提示相关的内容。

Method: 经验分析发现记忆相关曲率在去噪后期显著，并在相邻状态间保持空间位置一致。据此，TACO沿晚期去噪轨迹聚合提示依赖的曲率响应，并用潜变量引导的空间细化提升空间连贯性；所得记忆图既作定位信号，也作缓解的空间控制信号，通过空间重加权与局部引导修正将现有推理时干预导向记忆相关区域。

Result: 大量实验表明TACO在定位性能上持续优于对比方法，并改善了记忆抑制、语义对齐与感知质量之间的帕累托权衡。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的空间分析是连接记忆定位与针对性缓解的有效纽带。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can reproduce memorized training content within localized image regions, making precise localization essential for targeted mitigation. However, curvature observations at a single denoising state can contain transient responses, while spatially uniform mitigation may disrupt prompt-relevant content. We present Trajectory-Aware Curvature Observation (TACO), a unified framework for local memorization detection and mitigation in diffusion models. Our empirical analysis reveals that memorization-related curvature becomes pronounced during late-stage denoising and persists at consistent spatial locations across neighboring states. Motivated by these observations, TACO aggregates prompt-dependent curvature responses along the late denoising trajectory and applies latent-guided spatial refinement to improve spatial coherence. The resulting memorization map serves both as a localization signal and as a spatial control signal for mitigation. Specifically, TACO uses this map to steer existing inference-time interventions toward memorization-related regions through spatial reweighting and localized guidance correction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TACO consistently outperforms competing localization methods, and improves the Pareto trade-off between memorization suppression, semantic alignment, and perceptual quality. These results establish trajectory-aware spatial analysis as an effective link between memorization localization and targeted mitigation.

</details>


### [66] [VariaFlow: Uncertainty-Aware Flow Matching for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41494)
*Arnela Hadzic,Martin Urschler,Cosmin I. Bercea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VariaFlow：用无条件流匹配先验下掩码引导修复的集成估计逐位置预测方差，并用其归一化残差，解决重建式异常检测中病理与健康解剖变异混杂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建式异常检测的瓶颈已从“重建质量”转移到“残差打分”。残差同时包含真实病灶与个体健康解剖差异两种不可区分来源，单重建打分把两者都判为异常，导致解剖变异大的区域假阳性升高，而小/细微病灶淹没在重建误差中。作者指出缺失的物理量是预测方差，即健康重建在每个位置可以变动的幅度。

Method: VariaFlow 在无条件 flow matching 先验下，对同一输入进行掩码引导修复，以不同噪声初始化构成集成，从集成分歧估计逐位置标准差，再据此归一化残差；采用少步 flow matching 保证集成计算可行，并在两个空间尺度上修复以覆盖不同尺寸病灶；仅在健康解剖上训练一次。

Result: 在覆盖卒中与肿瘤的三个脑 MRI 数据集上，单次训练即跨域泛化，AUROC 分别达 0.94、0.80、0.78，均为最高；在两个跨域数据集上 AUPRC 最高，分别超过最强基线 37% 与 50%。域偏移下该基线 AUPRC 最多下降 52%，VariaFlow 更稳定，并在三个数据集上均取得最佳小病灶 Dice。

Conclusion: 将残差打分显式建模为方差归一化问题，可有效抑制健康解剖变异带来的假阳性并保留小病灶敏感性；基于流匹配集成的预测方差估计在跨域、小病灶场景下优于单重建基线。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based anomaly detection no longer fails at reconstruction. Generative models produce high-quality pseudo-healthy reconstructions, and the remaining difficulty is the step of scoring residuals. The residual between a scan and its reconstruction has two indistinguishable sources, genuine pathology and the anatomical variation that separates one healthy brain from another. Scoring against a single reconstruction treats both as anomalous, inflating false positives where healthy anatomy varies most while small or subtle lesions are lost in the surrounding reconstruction error. We identify the missing quantity as the predictive variance, how much a healthy reconstruction can vary at each location. We introduce VariaFlow, which estimates this variance from an ensemble of mask-guided restorations under an unconditional flow matching prior, each started from a different noise initialization, and which normalizes the residual by the resulting standard deviation. Few-step flow matching keeps the ensemble computationally affordable, and restoring at two spatial scales captures lesions of different size. Across three brain MRI datasets spanning stroke and tumor, VariaFlow is trained once on healthy anatomy and generalizes across all three, attaining the highest AUROC (0.94, 0.80, 0.78) on each and the highest AUPRC on both cross-domain datasets, exceeding the strongest baseline by 37% and 50%. Under domain shift, the same baseline drops by up to 52% in AUPRC, whereas VariaFlow stays more stable and achieves the best small-lesion Dice on all three datasets.

</details>


### [67] [SACQ: Structure-Aware Codebook Quantization for Ultra-Low-Bit Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41408)
*Haesung Lee,Insu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SACQ is an ultra-low-bit weight-only vector-codebook quantization framework for Vision-Language-Action models, achieving 1.5-bit language/action weights with 93.85% average success on LIBERO and 4.83x memory reduction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA models have large memory footprints that hinder deployment on resource-constrained robots. Existing low-bit PTQ methods inherited from LLMs do not explicitly model downstream operation structure and allocate precision without considering the impact on policy actions, degrading at ultra-low precision.

Method: SACQ combines three components: (1) structure-aware codebook fitting with role-specific quadratic metrics reflecting input statistics and downstream sensitivity; (2) action-aware mixed-bit allocation based on the increase in final-action error when individual layers are compressed; (3) two-stage post-quantization refinement that reassigns codeword indices with second-order error compensation and calibrates codebooks and row-group scales against the decoded action.

Result: On GR00T N1.7, SACQ reaches 93.85% average success across four LIBERO suites at 1.5 bits per language/action weight with 4.83x memory reduction. With INT8 activation quantization and low-precision attention, it retains 93.55% success and runs 1.51x faster than BF16. On SimplER at 2 bits, it achieves 63.76% and 52.27% success under Visual Matching and Variant Aggregation, versus 58.90% and 53.45% for BF16.

Conclusion: SACQ demonstrates that structure-aware, action-aware ultra-low-bit weight-only vector-codebook quantization can substantially compress VLA models while preserving or even improving task success, enabling efficient deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable capable robot behavior, but their large memory footprint limits deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. Weight quantization is a natural remedy, yet post-training quantization (PTQ) recipes inherited from large language models degrade when applied to VLAs at ultra-low precision. Existing low-bit PTQ methods often fail to explicitly account for the structure of downstream operations and allocate precision without considering its effect on the policy actions. We introduce SACQ, an ultra- low-bit weight-only vector-codebook quantization framework for VLAs. SACQ combines (1) structure-aware codebook fitting with role-specific quadratic metrics that reflect input statistics and downstream sensitivity; (2) action-aware mixed-bit allocation based on the increase in final-action error as individual layers are compressed; and (3) two-stage post-quantization refinement that reassigns codeword indices with second-order error compensation and calibrates codebooks and row-group scales against the decoded action. Using GR00T N1.7, SACQ achieves 93.85% average success across four LIBERO suites at 1.5 bits per language/action weight while reducing model memory by 4.83x. With INT8 activation quantization and low-precision attention, it retains 93.55% success while running 1.51x faster than BF16. On SimplER, SACQ further achieves 63.76% and 52.27% success under Visual Matching and Variant Aggregation at 2 bits per language/action weight, compared to 58.90% and 53.45% for BF16, respectively.

</details>


### [68] [MoETok: Sparse Expert Routing for 1D Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41992)
*Jiahao Guo,Bao Tang,Haoxiang Cao,Jingfeng Yao,Bo Li,Wenyu Liu,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoETok：一种基于稀疏专家（MoE）路由的1D视觉tokenizer，依据语义监督下自发生成的"Content查询/Detail查询"功能分化，对token做角色专门化处理，在匹配监督与激活计算下同时提升重建、语义表示与自回归生成，并在统一理解-生成模型中达到1D离散模型SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询式1D视觉tokenizer虽能编码为灵活潜序列，但可学习查询如何收集与编码图像信息缺乏理解；作者发现语义监督下查询会自发分化出少数负责高层语义的Content查询与多数负责细粒度细节的Detail查询，而稠密统一处理忽视了这种异质性。

Method: 先分析查询角色分化现象；据此设计MoETok：将token经稀疏激活的专家网络路由，路由器在重建与语义目标联合优化下，为不同角色的token学习专门化处理路径；并在统一自回归模型中用next-token交叉熵联合训练理解与生成，验证可扩展性。

Result: 在多种模型规模与token预算下，匹配监督和激活计算时一致优于稠密tokenizer的重建、语义表示与自回归生成；ImageNet上兼顾强重建与语义表示，并在1D tokenizer族内取得SOTA类别条件生成；统一模型中DPG-Bench 87.61、GenEval2 40.04 GM、WISE 0.49，匹敌或超过BAGEL、Qwen-Image等扩散模型。

Conclusion: 查询式1D tokenizer的查询角色分化是可被显式利用的结构先验；以MoE稀疏路由实现角色专门化，可在不增加激活计算的前提下统一提升重建与语义能力，并支撑统一离散自回归理解-生成范式达到SOTA水平。

Abstract: Modern visual tokenizers aim to preserve fine-grained visual details while learning high-level semantic representations for downstream generation and understanding. Query-based 1D visual tokenizers encode images into flexible latent sequences, yet how individual learnable queries gather and encode image information remains poorly understood. We reveal that these queries spontaneously develop complementary roles under semantic supervision: a small set of Content queries captures high-level semantic information, while a larger set of Detail queries preserves fine-grained visual details for reconstruction. To adapt token processing to these heterogeneous roles, we introduce MoETok, a 1D visual tokenizer that routes tokens through sparsely activated experts. Jointly optimized with reconstruction and semantic objectives, the router learns role-specialized processing paths adapted to each token's representation. Across model scales and token budgets, MoETok consistently improves reconstruction, semantic representation, and autoregressive generation over dense tokenizers under matched supervision and active computation. On ImageNet, it combines strong reconstruction and semantic representation with state-of-the-art class-conditional generation within the 1D tokenizer family. We further validate MoETok at scale in a unified autoregressive model jointly trained for understanding and generation with a next-token cross-entropy objective. The resulting model combines competitive visual understanding with state-of-the-art generation among unified discrete models, matching or surpassing diffusion-based models such as BAGEL and Qwen-Image on benchmarks including DPG-Bench (87.61), GenEval2 (40.04 GM), and WISE (0.49).

</details>


### [69] [DUGO: Dual-Scale Gaussian Memory for Online Temporal Action Localization in Egocentric Procedural Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41402)
*Mohammed Aman Bhuiyan,Md. Sazzad Hossain Adib,Samiul Basir Bhuiyan,Mir Faiyaz Hossain,Syed Mohammed Shamsul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain,Shafin Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在线时序动作定位框架DUGO，用双尺度时序编码器与高斯记忆库同时建模局部运动与长程时序依赖，在EGTEA、EPIC-Kitchens-100及Muses上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线时序动作定位（OnTAL）无法访问未来帧且不可回修预测，实时约束与窄时间窗使任务更难。现有方法多依赖短时锚点上下文或单尺度的历史帧记忆，难以捕捉跨多时间尺度展开、需要长程推理的动作（尤其在程序性视频中）。

Method: DUGO框架包含两部分：(1) 双尺度时序编码器（Dual-Scale Temporal Encoder），将深度可分离的局部表示与全局自注意力并联，兼顾细粒度时序模式与更广的上下文线索；(2) 高斯记忆库（Gaussian Memory Bank），将动作记忆表示为随输入条件化的随机高斯原型，并在编码器阶段与锚点查询阶段均通过门控交叉注意力进行记忆读取。

Result: 在程序性第一人称数据集EGTEA与EPIC-Kitchens-100上达到SOTA，并在非第一人称基准Muses上也有显著提升。

Conclusion: 双尺度编码结合高斯概率化记忆可有效缓解在线场景下的多尺度与长程建模瓶颈，且具备跨数据集（第一人称/非第一人称）的泛化性。

Abstract: Online Temporal Action Localization (OnTAL) aims to detect and localize action instances from untrimmed streaming videos in real time. Unlike offline approaches, OnTAL models have no access to future frames and cannot go back and refine earlier predictions, which makes the task considerably harder given the strict real-time constraints and narrow temporal window available. Most existing OnTAL methods lean on either brief anchor-based context or a single-scale memory of past frames, which limits how well they can capture actions unfolding across multiple time scales or requiring long-range reasoning in Procedural Videos. To address these gaps, we present DUGO (Dual-Scale Encoding and Gaussian Memory Online), a new OnTAL framework that jointly models local motion dynamics and long-range temporal dependencies. At its core, DUGO uses a Dual-Scale Temporal Encoder, pairing depth-wise local representations with global self-attention so it can pick up both fine-grained temporal patterns and broader contextual cues. We also introduce a Gaussian Memory Bank, which represents action memory as stochastic, input-conditioned Gaussian prototypes, allowing for richer temporal memory through gated cross-attention applied at both the encoder and anchor-query stages. Extensive experiments on procedural egocentric datasets (EGTEA, EPIC-Kitchens-100) demonstrate that DUGO achieves state-of-the-art performance while also providing substantial improvements on non-egocentric benchmarks such as Muses. The project page is available at: https://aman554-eq.github.io/DUGO

</details>


### [70] [STAR-FER: Adaptive Selective Restoration for Edge-Based Low-Resolution Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41310)
*ZhongYang Wu,ZhenFeng Gan,Di Zhao,XiaoJun Zhu,HouDe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR-FER：一种尺度与任务自适应的选择性修复框架，通过图像尺度条件化的轻量 Gate 联合预测像素级空间排序与连续修复预算，经精确支撑投影生成自由形式掩码，仅在所选支撑上做稀疏修复，在低分辨率人脸表情识别中兼顾性能与算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端情感交互中，变长交互距离导致人脸分辨率持续下降，低分辨率 FER 在有限算力下困难；全图/全特征修复开销大，而轻量识别流程又易丢失退化后仍具判别性的局部线索。

Method: 轻量级图像尺度条件化 Gate 同时预测逐像素空间排序与连续修复预算；用精确支撑投影（exact-support projection）得到输入相关的自由形式掩码；掩码条件化修复器在保持完整人脸上下文的同时，将稀疏修复与残差插入集中于选定支撑区域。

Result: 在完整 RAF-DB 测试集 x2–x8 七种退化尺度下，平均准确率 80.92%、Macro-F1 71.64%，较低分辨率 ResNet-18 基线分别提升 2.25 与 2.71 个百分点；相比 SR-FER 准确率高 1.04 点，且仅需 8.84 GFLOPs（降低 54.29%），平均掩码覆盖率 28.01%；在未见过的非整数尺度上仍保持有效识别。

Conclusion: 空间排序与连续预算驱动的选择性修复可在降低空间修改量与计算量的同时维持低分辨率 FER 性能，为资源受限的情感交互提供高效路径。

Abstract: Variable interaction distances continuously degrade facial resolution in edge-based affective interaction, making low-resolution facial expression recognition (FER) difficult under limited computation. Full-face image or feature restoration incurs substantial overhead, whereas lightweight recognition pipelines may miss local cues that remain discriminative after degradation. We propose STAR-FER, a scale-and-task-adaptive selective restoration framework that balances recognition performance and resource constraints. A lightweight image-scale conditioned Gate jointly predicts a pixel-wise spatial ranking and a continuous restoration budget, then applies exact-support projection to obtain an input-dependent free-form mask. The mask-conditioned restorer retains complete facial context while concentrating sparse restoration and residual insertion on the selected support. Across seven degradation scales from x2 to x8 on the complete RAF-DB test set, STAR-FER achieves 80.92% mean accuracy and 71.64% Macro-F1, improving the low-resolution ResNet-18 baseline by 2.25 and 2.71 percentage points, respectively.
