It surpasses SR-FER by 1.04 accuracy points while requiring 8.84 GFLOPs, a 54.29% reduction, with 28.01% mean mask coverage, and maintains effective recognition at unseen non-integer scales. These results show that spatial-ranking and continuous-budget driven selective restoration reduces spatial modification and computation while maintaining low-resolution FER performance, offering an efficient path to resource-constrained affective interaction.

</details>


### [71] [FlowMoDE: Coarse-to-fine Flow Matching for Detail-aware Sim-to-Real Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41190)
*Liangjing Shao,Wanhao Liu,Jinsong Lin,Zhiwei Fang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlowMoDE——基于flow matching的由粗到细单目深度估计框架，兼顾高效推理与结构保真，并具备强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判别式方法性能强但泛化与细节重建有限；生成式扩散模型细节与泛化更好，但多步采样导致推理效率低，而单步扩散又难以恢复高保真深度图。因此需要一个既高效又结构感知的生成式深度估计方案。

Method: 采用flow matching的由粗到细框架：先以预训练语义编码器提取的扩散特征为条件进行粗级解码，再通过扩展解码层恢复细粒度结构模式，实现单步高效生成。

Result: 仅在单一合成室内数据集训练，却在5个真实室内/外数据集上超越已有方法：相对误差平均降低14.1%，点云重建Chamfer距离降低9.9%；并在真实机器人场景中与SOTA方法做了定性对比验证泛化性。

Conclusion: FlowMoDE证明了flow matching结合由粗到细解码可在保证推理效率的同时获得结构感知的高保真深度估计，并展现显著的sim-to-real与跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation is a fundamental capability for 3D spatial perception in robotic and intelligent systems. While data-driven discriminative methods have achieved compelling performance for monocular depth estimation, generative approaches can reconstruct fine structural details with stronger generalization. However, most existing generative diffusion models suffer from low inference efficiency caused by multi-step sampling, whereas single-step diffusion models struggle to recover high-fidelity depth maps. To mitigate these drawbacks, this paper presents FlowMoDE, a coarse-to-fine flow-matching-based framework for efficient, structure-aware monocular depth estimation supporting sim-to-real and cross-scene generalization. The core architecture first conducts coarse-level decoding conditioned on diffused features extracted from a pre-trained semantic encoder, and subsequently restores fine-grained structural patterns via expanded decoding layers. Trained exclusively on a single synthetic indoor dataset, our proposed method outperforms prior methods across five real-world indoor and outdoor datasets, yielding an average 14.1% reduction in relative error against state-of-the-art works as well as 9.9\% reduction in chamfer distance for point cloud reconstruction. We further validate the generalization capability of FlowMoDE on realistic robotic scenes, with qualitative comparisons against state-of-the-art methods. The project page is https://flowmode-shao.netlify.app/

</details>


### [72] [Segment Anything Pancreas: A Unified MRI Segmentation Model for Pancreatic Cancer Radiotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41063)
*Zach Eidex,Mojtaba Safari,Kundan Thind,Parag J. Parikh,Zhen Tian,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一模型 Pancreas-MedNeXt，用共享 MedNeXt 编码器-解码器与三输出头，在部分标注 MRI 数据上同时分割全胰、胰亚区、肿瘤及危及器官（OAR），在 PANTHER、Cyst-X、AMOS 及外部 T1 数据上多数指标优于 nnU-Net、MedNeXt、VISTA3D、Swin UNETR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI 引导放疗需要统一分割胰腺肿瘤与 OAR，但现有数据集肿瘤标注有限、标签不完整（各数据集仅标注部分结构），难以训练统一模型。

Method: 共享 MedNeXt 编解码器；先做全胰与亚区监督预训练，再多任务微调；三个输出头分别预测胰亚区（头/体/尾）、肿瘤与 OAR（胃、十二指肠、肾）；采用 annotation-aware 损失，使未标注结构不被当作背景；评测含三折交叉验证（142 例 PANTHER，92 诊断 + 50 MR-Linac）、Cyst-X 与 AMOS 测试集、33 例外部 T1 TotalSegmentator 扫描，指标为 DSC 及配对 bootstrap 95% CI。

Result: 诊断与 MR-Linac MRI 上胰腺/肿瘤 DSC 最高：肿瘤 58.41%、42.55%，较任务专用 MedNeXt 分别提升 4.94（95% CI 0.28–9.45）与 5.89（2.37–9.72）个百分点；全胰 DSC 85.73% 与 70.66%；预训练使头、体、尾 DSC 较从零训练提升 2.01、3.00、3.78 个百分点；AMOS 上胰腺、十二指肠、右肾 DSC 最高，外部 MRI 上五个器官均最优，领先次优方法 4.92–8.85 个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一 Pancreas-MedNeXt 模型可在诊断级与 MR-Linac MRI 上同时分割肿瘤、胰亚区与 OAR，有望简化胰腺癌放疗勾画流程。

Abstract: Background: Limited tumor annotations and incomplete labels across MRI datasets hinder unified segmentation of pancreatic tumors and organs at risk (OARs) for MRI-guided radiotherapy. Purpose: We propose Pancreas-MedNeXt, a unified model that segments the whole pancreas, its subregions, tumors, and surrounding OARs from one MRI volume. Methods: The model uses a shared MedNeXt encoder-decoder with supervised whole-pancreas and subregion pretraining, followed by multitask fine-tuning. Three output heads predict the pancreatic subregions, tumor, and OARs (stomach, duodenum, kidneys). An annotation-aware loss allows training on partially labeled data without treating unannotated structures as background. Pancreas and tumor were evaluated by three-fold cross-validation on 142 PANTHER patients (92 diagnostic, 50 MR-Linac), subregions and OARs on Cyst-X and AMOS test sets, and pancreas and OARs on 33 external T1-weighted TotalSegmentator scans. Comparisons with nnU-Net, MedNeXt, VISTA3D, and Swin UNETR used the Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) with paired bootstrap 95% CIs. Results: Pancreas-MedNeXt achieved the highest mean pancreas and tumor DSC on diagnostic and MR-Linac MRI. Tumor DSC was 58.41% and 42.55%, exceeding task-specific MedNeXt by 4.94 (95% CI: 0.28-9.45) and 5.89 (95% CI: 2.37-9.72) percentage points, respectively. Whole-pancreas DSC was 85.73% and 70.66%. Pretraining improved head, body, and tail DSC by 2.01, 3.00, and 3.78 percentage points over training from scratch. It also achieved the highest DSC for the pancreas, duodenum, and right kidney on AMOS, and for all five organs on external MRI, exceeding the next-best method by 4.92-8.85 percentage points.Conclusion: A single Pancreas-MedNeXt model segments tumors, pancreatic subregions, and OARs on diagnostic and MR-Linac MRI, which could streamline contouring for pancreatic cancer radiotherapy.

</details>


### [73] [SAR-TS-BERT: Physics-Guided Self-Supervised Pre-Training With Polarimetric Features for SAR Time-Series Crop Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41728)
*Jieqiu Wang,Yuhan Zhang,Bo Zheng,Shaoming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAR-TS-BERT——物理引导的BERT式SAR时序自监督预训练框架，将后向散射强度与双极化Cloude-Pottier分解参数融合为六波段输入，用于农作物分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多云雨地区需依赖多时相SAR进行作物物候监测，但高精度分类依赖大量标注样本；现有自监督预训练框架多针对光学影像或通用SAR空间场景，未充分利用时序后向散射动态与极化散射机理。

Method: 六波段输入（VV、VH、VV/VH及H/α/A分解参数）；两项核心设计：1）SpanPhysics物理引导跨段掩码策略，依据时序后向散射变化率自适应选择连续掩码跨段，优先覆盖关键物候转折；2）双分支跨模态融合编码器，用独立Transformer分支分别建模后向散射与极化分解特征，并通过双向交叉注意力融合。

Result: 爱沙尼亚EuroCropsML与加州CDL独立测试集OA分别为96.78%、87.29%；崇明岛月均OA达99.37%。爱沙尼亚基准上，全监督预训练较从头训练OA/Macro F1提升1.17/1.59个百分点；仅用1%标注数据时提升2.58/5.19个百分点。

Conclusion: 将SAR散射物理先验融入时序自监督预训练，可有效提升作物分类精度、地块级制图一致性与小样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Multitemporal synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imagery provides continuous, cloud-free observations of crop phenology for agricultural monitoring in cloudy and rainy regions, yet high-accuracy classification typically requires extensive labeled samples. Although self-supervised pre-training mitigates label dependency in remote sensing, existing frameworks primarily target optical imagery or generic spatial SAR scenes, underutilizing temporal backscatter dynamics and polarimetric scattering mechanisms. We propose SAR-TS-BERT, a physics-guided BERT-style self-supervised pre-training framework for SAR time-series crop classification. SAR-TS-BERT integrates backscatter intensities and polarimetric ratio features (VV, VH, and VV/VH) with dual-polarimetric Cloude-Pottier decomposition parameters ($H/α/A$) into a six-band input representation through two core designs: 1) a physics-guided span masking strategy (SpanPhysics) that adaptively selects contiguous masked spans based on temporal backscatter change rates to prioritize key phenological transitions; and 2) a dual-branch cross-modal fusion encoder that models backscatter and polarimetric decomposition features through separate Transformer branches followed by bidirectional cross-attention fusion. On independent test sets in Estonia (EuroCropsML) and the USA (California CDL), SAR-TS-BERT achieves overall accuracies (OAs) of 96.78% and 87.29%, respectively, and attains a mean monthly OA of 99.37% on Chongming Island, Shanghai. On the Estonia benchmark, pre-training improves OA and Macro F1 by 1.17 and 1.59 percentage points over training from scratch under full supervision, and by 2.58 and 5.19 percentage points using only 1% labeled data. Incorporating SAR scattering physics into temporal self-supervised pre-training effectively enhances crop classification accuracy, parcel-level mapping consistency, and few-shot transferability.

</details>


### [74] [FieldGuideRAG: Image-Grounded GraphRAG for Fine-Grained Recognition with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40971)
*Jiacheng Ding,Yanliang Qi,Cong Guo,Peihua Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FieldGuideRAG，一种无需训练的 GraphRAG 方法，利用冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）将查询与知识图谱节点上存储的真实物种照片比对以进行细粒度物种识别，并融合分布范围边与生态事实边完成问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 GraphRAG 以文本描述实体，文本难以承载区分相似物种所需的视觉细节，导致细粒度识别效果受限。

Method: 在知识图谱的每个物种节点上存储一张真实照片，并将物种与其近亲、地理分布范围和生态事实相连；使用冻结的 VLM 对查询与候选节点照片逐一核验，将核验分数与范围边融合，并从已验证物种的事实边回答问题；全过程无需训练。

Result: 在 CUB-200、一个 Wikidata 图谱及四个 iNaturalist 域上，节点照片相较物种名称在所有图谱上均提升核验效果，而文本描述增益甚微；候选池核验较 SigLIP 检索器最高高 16 分；范围边在 iNaturalist 上带来 7–19 分增益；基于事实边回答在 28,820 个生态问题上正确率达 89.5%；核验分数还能检测图中缺失物种的查询。

Conclusion: 以节点照片替代文本描述作为视觉核验依据，配合范围与事实边，可有效支撑细粒度物种识别与生态问答，并为图谱缺失物种的检测提供信号；图谱、提示词与代码将公开。

Abstract: We present FieldGuideRAG, a training-free GraphRAG method in which a vision-language model, or VLM, recognizes fine-grained species by comparing the query with real photographs stored on the nodes of a knowledge graph. Multimodal GraphRAG systems describe each entity in text, and text conveys little of the visual detail that separates look-alike species. Our graph stores one photograph on every species node and links each species to its relatives, its geographic range and its ecological facts. A frozen VLM verifies the query against the photograph of each candidate node. FieldGuideRAG fuses the verification scores with the range edge and answers questions about the organism from the fact edges of the verified species. On CUB-200, a Wikidata graph and four iNaturalist domains, the node photograph improves verification over the species name on every graph, while text descriptions add little. Verification of the candidate pool exceeds a SigLIP retriever by up to 16 points, the range edge adds 7 to 19 points on iNaturalist, and answering from fact edges is correct on up to 89.5% of 28,820 ecological questions. The verification scores also detect queries whose species is missing from the graph. Graphs, prompts and code will be released.

</details>


### [75] [Spatial Atlas-Informed Loss Ensembles for Whole-Body PSMA PET/CT Metastasis Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40834)
*Grant N. Chau,Bashirul A. Biswas,Biratal Wagle,Matthew E. Maeder,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将群体转移图谱统计量嵌入nnU-Net的交叉熵损失（空间图谱知情损失，SAILS集成），在推理阶段无需图谱或配准，即可提升全身PSMA PET/CT前列腺癌转移灶检测灵敏度，尤其在高风险骨区，但假阳性体积增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身PSMA PET/CT转移灶分割方法仅依赖图像本身，未显式利用"转移灶倾向于在何处发生"这一先验空间分布信息，作者探究将群体转移图谱整合进模型训练能否提升病灶检测性能。

Method: 基于375例专家标注训练扫描构建体素级均值与标准差图谱，用以加权nnU-Net的交叉熵损失；对漏检与假阳性的额外惩罚取决于空间图谱统计量，并随预测置信正确地减弱。验证集比较7种配置，其中3种经多数投票集成为SAILS。在AutoPET III（110例阳性病例）与Deep-PSMA（100例外部队列）上与基线nnU-Net及推理时图谱融合对比；推理阶段无需图谱或配准。

Result: AutoPET III上全身Dice提升（0.531 vs 0.520, p=0.006），漏检病灶体积降低（9.5 vs 12.1 cc/例, p<0.01），汇总病灶灵敏度提升（0.857 vs 0.843, p=0.004）；Deep-PSMA上额外检出126个参考病灶（灵敏度0.769 vs 0.751, p<0.001），全身Dice无显著变化（-0.009, p=0.36）。两队列高风险骨Dice均改善（0.576 vs 0.552；0.667 vs 0.653，均p<0.01）。假阳性体积分别增加2.3与18.2 cc/例（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 图谱加权训练可在无需测试时图谱配准的情况下提升病灶检测（尤其高风险骨区），但以假阳性增加为代价。

Abstract: Existing whole-body PSMA PET/CT prostate metastasis segmentation methods generally rely on images alone, without explicitly incorporating where metastases tend to occur. We investigate whether a population metastasis atlas can improve lesion detection when integrated into model training. We constructed voxel-wise mean and standard deviation maps from 375 expert-annotated training scans and used them to weight the cross-entropy loss of an nnU-Net. The additional penalties for missed lesions and false positives depend on spatial atlas statistics and diminish as predictions become confidently correct. Seven configurations were compared on validation data; three were combined by majority voting into SAILS (Spatial Atlas-Informed Loss enSemble). We compared SAILS with a baseline nnU-Net and inference-time atlas fusion on AutoPET~III (110 disease-positive cases) and Deep-PSMA (100 external cases). No atlas or registration is needed at inference. Results: On AutoPET~III, SAILS improved whole-body Dice (0.531 vs. 0.520; p=0.006), reduced missed-lesion volume (9.5 vs. 12.1 cc/case; p<0.01), and increased pooled lesion sensitivity (0.857 vs. 0.843; p=0.004). On Deep-PSMA, SAILS detected 126 additional reference lesions (sensitivity 0.769 vs. 0.751; p<0.001), without a significant whole-body Dice change (-0.009; p=0.36). High-risk bone Dice improved in both cohorts (0.576 vs. 0.552 and 0.667 vs. 0.653; both p<0.01). False-positive volume increased by 2.3 and 18.2 cc/case, respectively (p<0.001). Atlas-weighted training improves lesion detection, especially in high-risk bone, without test-time atlas registration, but increases false positives.

</details>


### [76] [A Patch is More Than a Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41814)
*Zixiong Wang,Haiping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PMTT框架，将ViT的单一规范patch token扩展为"表征状态族"，并构建patch级局部特征空间，证明冻结骨干网络中蕴含比标准单token接口更丰富的patch信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT通常用单一条件下的规范token表示每个图像patch，该token虽是有力接口，但仅为patch的一个已实现状态，可能丢失同一patch在其他观测条件下的信息。作者希望探究预训练视觉骨干是否包含超越单token接口的patch级信息。

Method: 提出PMTT，包含四部分：①Patch信息获取与稠密轨道视图（Dense Orbital Views）揭示合法的同patch状态；②可见到隐状态的预测任务检验观测状态是否揭示自身之外的结构；③内在性分析发现低维、能量集中、patch特异、可预测的几何；④表征组织将"获取更多信息"与"组织信息"分离并提出解析Manifold作为中心结构化表征。全部在冻结的DINOv3 S/B/L/H+上实现，不做表示层面的神经后训练。

Result: 两组独立4096锚点队列中，Manifold解释70.8–79.6%的Hidden变化，直接Observed为55.0–72.3%，且在每一规模上超过95%锚点占优；有效秩从约2.5降至2.0，主能量集中度随骨干规模上升。六个视觉能力×四规模共24条件下，Observed超Center 21次、Manifold超Center 23次、Manifold超Observed 16次；物理对应任务（MegaDepth）在每一规模均满足Center<Observed<Manifold。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉骨干含有比标准单token接口所直接暴露的更丰富的patch信息；将patch表示扩展为状态族并经Manifold组织，可在多个视觉能力上稳定带来增益，尤其在物理对应任务上表现突出。

Abstract: Vision Transformers represent each image patch through a canonical token produced under one observation condition. That token is a powerful interface, but it is still one realized state of the patch. We introduce PMTT, which establishes two foundations: a patch representation object can extend from one canonical state to a canonical-centered Representation State Family, and identity-preserving variation opens Patch-Level Local Feature Space. We develop four parts of this representation program. Patch Information Acquisition and Dense Orbital Views expose lawful same-patch states. Visible-to-Hidden prediction tests whether observed states reveal structure beyond themselves. Intrinsic analysis identifies low-dimensional, energy-concentrated, patch-specific, predictive geometry. Representation Organization separates acquiring more information from organizing that information, with an analytic Manifold as the central structured representation. We realize PMTT on frozen DINOv3 S/B/L/H+ backbones without neural post-training of the representation. Across two independent 4,096-anchor cohorts, Manifold explains 70.8-79.6% of Hidden variation, compared with 55.0-72.3% for directly Observed states, and wins on more than 95% of anchors at every scale. Effective rank falls from about 2.5 to 2.0 while dominant energy concentration rises with backbone scale. Across six visual capabilities and four scales, Observed exceeds the canonical Center in 21 of 24 conditions, Manifold exceeds Center in 23, and Manifold exceeds Observed in 16. The strongest current consequence appears in physical correspondence, where MegaDepth satisfies Center < Observed < Manifold at every scale. These results show that pretrained visual backbones contain richer patch information than the standard single-token interface directly exposes. Code and experimental assets are available at https://github.com/zixiongwang-ai/PMTT.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Asynchronous Flow Map Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42018)
*Chanhyuk Lee,Wonjung Kim,Patrick Pynadath,Jaehoon Yoo,Satoshi Hayakawa,Ruqi Zhang,Jinwoo Kim,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出异步流映射语言模型（aFMLM），通过异步流的流映射把token预测顺序内化到一步并行的生成中，在数独求解与GSM8K代码生成上取得一步生成的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: token预测顺序显著影响生成序列质量，自回归与掩码扩散模型可跨生成步利用顺序信息，但如何在一步并行语言建模中利用顺序尚不明确。

Method: 学习异步流的流映射以将预测顺序纳入一步建模：异步流通过逐token时间调度控制各token相对去噪进度从而确定顺序；给出允许预设式与学习式（状态自适应）调度的通用异步流类，证明其定义合法流映射，并提出相应的流映射蒸馏新方法。

Result: 模型通过把流中编码的顺序内化到隐式计算，可在单次前向中生成全部token；在数独求解与GSM8K数学应用题的代码生成任务上，aFMLM刷新了一步生成的最优性能。

Conclusion: 将预测顺序经异步流映射蒸馏进一步并行模型是可行且有效的，为高效高质量的一步语言生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: The order in which tokens are predicted in language modeling has a profound impact on the quality of generated sequences. Autoregressive and masked diffusion models iteratively fill in tokens and can exploit such orderings across generation steps, but how to leverage an ordering for one-step parallel language modeling is unclear. We introduce asynchronous flow map language models (aFMLMs), which incorporate prediction order into one-step modeling by learning the flow maps of asynchronous flows. These flows specify an ordering through a tokenwise time schedule that controls the relative denoising progress of tokens. We formulate a general class of asynchronous flows that admit both prescribed and learned, state-adaptive schedules, show that they define valid flow maps, and develop new methods for their flow map distillation. The resulting models can generate all tokens in a single forward pass by internalizing the ordering encoded in the flow into their hidden computations. On Sudoku solving and code generation for GSM8K math word problems, aFMLM establishes new state-of-the-art one-step performance.

</details>


### [78] [Better Retrievers, Worse Labels: How Incomplete Relevance Judgments Distort Abstention Evaluation in Conversational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40966)
*Ivan Kochergin,Vasily Konovalov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文证明将"上下文不含gold文档"当作"不可回答"是会话式RAG评测中的错误捷径：检索越强，该捷径越失效（精度从.68降至.10），应改用基于内容的人工/模型标签来评测弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话式RAG基准（MTRAG、RETECO）采用端到端评测，弃答是否合理要看每个检索上下文本身是否充分。现有做法存在一个便捷捷径：直接由相关性判定推出充分性，把不含gold文档的上下文判为不可回答。作者怀疑这一捷径在强检索器下会系统性失效，需实证检验。

Method: 严格复现官方基线，在RECOR（RETECO track 2）上使用4种检索器（nDCG@10跨0.18–0.59）和3个Qwen3阅读器；用2个开放LLM评审员加4个小模型小组按上下文内容打标签；另用不同家族模型对250个上下文盲审并做仲裁裁决；读者侧用Qwen3-4B实测回答率；并在标注者判定的MTRAG上复验；同时评估回答性AUROC与充分性检测器。

Result: 检索器越强，无gold上下文中实际含答案的比例从28%升至72%；捷径精度从.68降至.10（审计下.70→.21）。Qwen3-4B对"假漏"上下文答对率62–67%，而对被判定不充分的无gold上下文仅23–30%，无文档时1%。假漏答案多来自同题难负例、其他轮的gold或gold页邻近块。MTRAG上（标注者判定）精度由.72降至.50。捷径下回答性AUROC最多低估0.19，最佳充分性检测器竟是不读文档的检索分数。

Conclusion: "无gold即不可答"的捷径不可靠且随检索质量提升而恶化，会显著低估可回答性。弃答应以基于内容的充分性标签为评判依据，而非gold文档是否存在；作者开源代码、运行结果与标签并给出该建议。

Abstract: Conversational RAG benchmarks such as MTRAG and RETECO evaluate retrieval and generation end to end: each system answers from its own retrieval, so abstention must be scored against whether each retrieved context is sufficient. A convenient shortcut reads sufficiency off the relevance judgments and treats a context without gold documents as unanswerable. We re-run the official baselines exactly and test this shortcut on RECOR (RETECO track~2) with four retrievers (nDCG@10 from .18 to .59) and three Qwen3 readers. Two open LLM judges and a panel of four small open models label each context by content, and a model of another family audits 250 contexts blind, with adjudication. The stronger the retriever, the larger the share of no-gold contexts that still contain the answer (28\% to 72\%) and the lower the shortcut's precision (.68 to .10; .70 to .21 under the audit). A reader agrees: Qwen3-4B answers 62--67\% of these false misses correctly, against 23--30\% of no-gold contexts judged insufficient and 1\% with no documents. In most false misses, the answer comes from a hard negative mined for the same question, another turn's gold or a neighbouring chunk of the gold page. On MTRAG, whose judgments come from annotators, precision drops from .72 to .50. Under the shortcut, answerability AUROC is up to .19 too low, and the best sufficiency detector appears to be a retrieval score that never reads the documents. We release code, runs and labels, and recommend scoring refusals against content labels, not the presence of gold.

</details>


### [79] [Precision--Recall Trade-off on the Inference Level for LLMs in Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41698)
*Ryszard Staruch,Filip Graliński*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大语言模型在语法错误纠正（GEC）中的解码策略，发现重复惩罚参数可在推理时调节精确率—召回率权衡，无需改动模型架构、训练或提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为概率序列模型暴露了可直接影响token生成的推理时参数，但这些参数对GEC特有性能权衡的可控性尚未被系统研究；已有如Align-and-Predict Decoding（APD）等方法虽能调节精确率—召回率平衡，但较为复杂。

Method: 在标准GEC基准上进行实验，保持模型架构、训练过程与提示不变，仅调节推理时的重复惩罚（repetition penalty）参数，观察其对解码行为与精确率—召回率的影响。

Result: 实验表明，基于重复惩罚的解码可在推理时实现对精确率—召回率平衡的可控调节，其效果在概念上与APD等针对seq2seq GEC模型提出的方法相关。

Conclusion: 重复惩罚提供了一种简单、通用且无需修改模型或提示的GEC推理时控制手段，适用于所有暴露推理时解码惩罚的现有LLM，为GEC的可控解码提供了轻量替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adopted for Grammatical Error Correction (GEC), achieving strong performance via both full fine-tuning and prompt-based approaches. As probabilistic sequence models, LLMs expose inference-time parameters that directly modify token generation behavior; however, their potential for controlling GEC-specific trade-offs has not been systematically studied. In this work, we investigate decoding strategies for grammatical error correction. We show that the repetition penalty parameter enables controllable adjustment of the precision--recall trade-off at inference time, without modifying the model architecture, training procedure, or prompt. Through experiments on standard GEC benchmarks, we demonstrate that repetition-penalty-based decoding enables inference-time control of the precision--recall balance, conceptually related to methods previously proposed for sequence-to-sequence GEC models, such as Align-and-Predict Decoding (APD), while remaining simple and broadly applicable to existing LLMs that expose inference-time decoding penalties.

</details>


### [80] [When Steering Shifts Discourse: Identifying and Intervening on Discourse-Level Changes due to Activation Steering in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41661)
*Shivam Adarsh,Hetianyu Huang,Lau Safin,Maria Maistro,Christina Lioma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 激活引导会系统性地改变生成文本的话语维度（正式程度、证据使用、模糊性），可通过叠加第二个针对受影响话语维度的互补向量来抵消，恢复约78%的非预期变化并保留约93%的预期引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导是无须重训权重或修改提示即可控制LLM行为的推理期技术，但它会带来非预期的文本变化；已有工作仅用困惑度等粗粒度指标衡量这些变化，认识不足。

Method: 先系统刻画激活引导对生成文本话语维度（正式程度、证据使用、模糊性）的影响；再引入第二个互补向量，专门指向受影响的话语维度进行干预。

Result: 该干预恢复了约78%的非预期话语变化，同时在约93%的生成中保留了预期的引导行为。

Conclusion: 激活引导的副作用体现在可识别的话语维度上，且可通过互补向量进行针对性校正，实现副作用抑制与目标行为保持的较好权衡。

Abstract: Activation steering is an inference-time technique used to control or modify the behavior, tone, or safety properties of a large language model (LLM) without retraining the model's weights or editing its input prompt, but simply by adding a vector to its residual-stream activations. However, activation steering also changes the generated text in unintended ways, unrelated to intended behavior change, that are not well understood. Prior work has measured these changes only through coarse signals such as perplexity. In this work, we show that steering systematically produces significant changes in discourse dimensions of the generated text, such as its formality, use of evidence, and ambiguity. We present a way of countering this by adding a second, complementary vector that targets the affected discourse dimension. This intervention restores about 78% of the unintended discourse changes while preserving the intended steering behavior in about 93% of the generations.

</details>


### [81] [Li$^{2}$SAO: Lifting Light Singl-Rollout Asynchronous RL Optimization via Group-Relative Actor-Critic Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40726)
*Zichen Bai,Yanli Wang,Yang Liu,Junhao Shi,Xueqin Liu,Jinguo Sun,Muye Li,Shaoqiang Zheng,Xuefei Diao,Zhuwei Zeng,Nan Duan,Peng Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在单轮推理与多轮智能体任务上对比GRPO与轻量复现的LiSAO，指出LiSAO因价值模型冷启动而在训练早期落后，提出Li²SAO：critic未成熟时用组相对优势训练actor、组中心回报训练critic，之后切换为单轨迹GAE，从而在早期追平或超过GRPO并抬高后期性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步RL通过rollout到达即更新提升LLM后训练效率。已有工作认为GRPO的组采样因rollout长度长尾分布不适配异步训练，并提出SAO；但SAO依赖由价值预训练扩展得到的闭源价值模型，难以复现与使用。作者希望用无需预训练价值模型的轻量方案（LiSAO）来厘清GRPO与单轨迹异步优化在不同任务上的真实差异。

Method: 去掉预训练价值模型，轻量复现SAO得到LiSAO，并在单轮推理与多轮智能体任务上系统对比GRPO与LiSAO。针对LiSAO价值模型训练不足导致早期滞后的问题，提出Li²SAO：critic冷启动阶段，actor采用组相对优势（GRPO式）更新，critic采用组中心回报（group-centered return）作为目标；critic成熟后切换为标准单轨迹GAE的actor-critic训练。

Result: GRPO在推理任务上优于LiSAO，而LiSAO在智能体任务上更优；由于LiSAO价值模型训练不足，其在早期训练步显著落后GRPO。Li²SAO在训练早期即追平或超过GRPO，并进一步提升后期性能上限。

Conclusion: 单轨迹异步优化在智能体任务上具有结构性优势，但其收益受限于价值模型的冷启动问题；通过组相对actor-critic自举的预热设计可有效弥补早期差距，兼顾异步效率与最终性能。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) is becoming a mainstream paradigm for post-training large language models (LLMs), as it improves training efficiency by updating the model as rollouts arrive. Recent work argues that group-wise sampling in GRPO does not naturally fit asynchronous RL training because rollout lengths follow a long-tailed distribution, and proposes Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization (SAO) to improve stability and off-policy behavior, especially on agentic tasks. However, SAO relies on a closed-source value model obtained from scaling value pretraining. In this paper, we analyze GRPO and LiSAO, our lightweight reproduction of SAO without the pretrained value model, on single-turn reasoning and multi-turn agentic tasks. We find that GRPO outperforms LiSAO on reasoning tasks, while the reverse holds on agentic tasks. Since the value model of LiSAO is not yet well trained , LiSAO lags behind GRPO substantially in early training steps. To address this issue, we propose Li$^{2}$SAO, which Lifts Light Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization with group-relative actor-critic bootstrapping: while the critic is cold, a group-relative advantage trains the actor and a group-centered return trains the critic, after which training switches to single-rollout GAE. Experiments show that Li$^{2}$SAO catches up with or surpasses GRPO in early training and further raises the later performance ceiling. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-cc-coder/Li2SAO.

</details>


### [82] [KEEPER: Learning Control-Sufficient Textual Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41482)
*Jinkyeong Choi,Chaebin Jeong,Junsung Jeon,Donghyeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文定义"自一致性偏差"（记忆中原有错误主张的持续留存）并提出KEEPER框架，在统一递归接口下沿信息含量与更新敏感性两轴比较四种辅助监督目标，主张除任务表现外还应评估错误持续性与有效信息保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归文本记忆会把早期错误带入后续更新，而仅凭任务性能无法判定记忆可靠性，需要专门刻画并度量错误的持续留存。

Method: 定义自一致性偏差为已进入记忆的错误主张的持续性；构建KEEPER，在共享递归记忆接口下，以信息含量和更新敏感性两个维度对四种辅助目标（含奖励相关预测、联合监督等）进行对照分析；并针对某基线轨迹做主张级（claim-level）分析。

Result: 在评测的各条件中，奖励相关预测在离线强化学习前后均取得最高任务表现，优于联合监督；主张级分析显示即使出现纠正性观测，基线轨迹中错误仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 任务结果不足以反映记忆可靠性，应同时评估错误持续性与有效信息保留，作为递归记忆智能体的必要评价维度。

Abstract: Recursive textual memory can carry earlier errors into later updates, yet task performance alone does not establish memory reliability. We define self-consistency bias as the persistence of incorrect claims already present in the incoming memory. We present KEEPER, a comparative analysis of four auxiliary objectives along information-content and update-sensitivity axes within a shared recursive interface, examining how supervision targets relate to agent performance. Among evaluated conditions, reward-related prediction achieve the highest task performance before and after offline reinforcement learning, outperforming joint supervision. A claim-level analysis of one baseline trajectory documents error persistence despite corrective observations. Together, these analyses motivate evaluating error persistence and valid-information retention alongside task outcomes.

</details>


### [83] [Comparative Evaluation of LLM-Based Review Paradigms for Academic Paper Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41391)
*Fan Huang,Hongyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在232篇ICLR 2025分层抽样投稿上系统比较5种LLM评审范式、3种输入表示与3个LLM；发现绝对评分经基率匹配重校准后与人类评分一致性高于Elo且仅需24%的API调用；模型选择与范式选择同等重要；仅Group Rating在控制人类评分后仍保留引用相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投稿量激增推动使用LLM辅助同行评审，但评审应如何组织结构、模型选择影响多大、LLM评分能否预测研究影响力尚不清楚。

Method: 在232篇分层抽样的ICLR 2025投稿上评估5种评审范式×3种输入表示×3个LLM；引入基率匹配的重校准控制以分离排序与校准效应；在全部11,513篇有评分投稿上复现，并以引用数作为影响力指标、以"始终拒稿"为基线。

Result: Direct绝对评分与比较式排序在秩相关上相当，但预测压缩在接收阈值以上，几乎漏检人类评为低于阈值的论文；重校准后Direct Rating一致性超过Elo且API调用仅为其24%；仅摘要输入优于全文摘要；绝对一致性与评分分布跨度相关；所有范式准确率均未超过始终拒稿基线；人类评分对引用预测最好，唯Group Rating在控制人类评分后仍具引用相关性并能在接收池内排序。

Conclusion: 比较式排序提供的是位置信息而非更优排序；仅凭排序质量不足以评估评审质量，校准、阈值、模型与目标结果均需纳入考量。

Abstract: Growing submission volumes have renewed interest in using large language models (LLMs) to support peer review. It remains unclear how such evaluation should be structured, how much the model matters, and whether LLM scores predict research impact. We evaluate five paradigms across three input representations and three LLMs on 232 stratified ICLR 2025 submissions. Direct absolute rating matches comparative ranking on rank correlation with human scores, yet compresses most predictions above the accept threshold, flagging almost none of the papers reviewers scored below it. A base-rate-matched recalibration control locates the cause: comparative ranking supplies placement, not better ordering, and after recalibration Direct Rating reaches higher agreement than Elo while making 24% as many API calls. Abstract-only input beats full-paper summarization, and model choice matters as much as paradigm choice. Replicating on all 11,513 scored submissions reproduces these patterns, shows absolute agreement depends on the evaluated set's score spread, and finds none beating an always-reject baseline in accuracy. Human scores track citations better than any paradigm, with one exception: Group Rating retains its citation correlation after controlling for human scores, and alone ranks within the accepted pool. Ranking quality alone cannot assess review quality; calibration, thresholds, model, and target outcome all matter.

</details>


### [84] [Iterated Integrals as Lexical Fingerprints: Path Signature Embeddings for Lexical Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41855)
*Raj Shekhar Singh,Pratham Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用Word2Vec轨迹的路径签名（path signature）构造句向量，通过粗路径理论的迭代积分显式编码词序与词法结构；在五类词法字符串度量上的消融实验表明，深度2的签名在字符级与词级敏感性之间取得最佳平衡，而平均池化几乎无法捕捉字符级编辑距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句向量本用于语义泛化，但其隐含几何却编码了表层词法属性，这一关系尚缺乏理解；浅层池化丢弃词序，深层Transformer又因计算复杂性与不可解释空间而遮蔽词法对齐。因此需要一个几何上可处理、可解释的表示来显式刻画序列与词法结构。

Method: 以Word2Vec词向量序列构成轨迹并计算其路径签名（基于粗路径理论中的迭代积分）作为句向量；围绕Word2Vec维度、签名深度与PCA压缩做广泛消融，并将嵌入距离与五种词法字符串度量（Levenshtein、Jaro-Winkler、词Jaccard、音素Levenshtein、字符TF-IDF）进行相关性分析。

Result: 平均池化几乎完全无法捕捉字符级编辑距离，而路径签名与其高度对齐；深度2签名在字符级与词级敏感性之间达到最优平衡；关键词语方差集中于极紧凑的PCA子空间；与池化不同，签名在结构上保留并量化了词序。

Conclusion: 路径签名并非语义泛化器，而是显式编码离散词法结构与词序的严格数学透镜，为词法追踪提供了可解释的、替代不透明深度学习的方案。

Abstract: While sentence embeddings are primarily designed for semantic generalization, their latent geometry implicitly encodes surface-level lexical properties, a relationship that remains poorly understood. Shallow pooling methods discard word order, while deep Transformer models obscure these lexical alignments behind computational complexity and uninterpretable spaces. We propose constructing sentence embeddings via the path signature of Word2Vec trajectories, leveraging the iterated integrals of rough path theory to create representations that explicitly capture sequential and lexical structure in a geometrically tractable manner. Through extensive ablations spanning Word2Vec dimensions, signature depths, and PCA compression, we correlate embedding distances against five lexical string metrics: Levenshtein, Jaro-Winkler, word Jaccard, phoneme Levenshtein, and character TF-IDF. We reveal that while average pooling almost entirely fails to capture character-level edit distances, path signatures strongly align with them, with depth 2 signatures optimally balancing character and word-level sensitivities. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the critical lexical variance is concentrated in a highly compact PCA subspace, and unlike pooling, signatures structurally preserve and quantify word order. These findings establish path signatures not as semantic generalizers, but as rigorous mathematical lenses that explicitly encode discrete lexical structures and word order, offering an interpretable alternative to opaque deep learning for lexical tracking.

</details>


### [85] [BYOD: Build Your Own DLM - Efficient Autoregressive-to-Diffusion Language Model Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41574)
*Ruurd J. A. Kuiper,Robert A. Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BYOD提出一种仅用LoRA、可泛化的自回归到扩散语言模型转换方法，在Gemma 2 9B、Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen2.5 7B和Ministral 8B上成功诱导扩散生成，转换成本低（单卡3-4小时、≤102.4M tokens），但在低NFE预算下的开放生成质量仍是主要短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型具备并行token更新、灵活生成和双向注意力等优势，但现有系统依赖昂贵预训练或全模型微调，转换成本高、难以扩展到其他模型家族，缺少一种通用、参数高效的AR-to-DLM转换方案。

Method: 提出BYOD配方：仅训练LoRA适配器（占最终参数4.1-5.8%），冻结全部基座参数，无需架构特定调参；对Gemma 2 9B、Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen2.5 7B、Ministral 8B各进行25,000次迭代转换，单块Google Colab GPU上3.1-4.0小时、最多102.4M tokens；并通过受控注意力测试验证双向上下文使用，提供独立训练评估实现与交互demo。

Result: 四个模型均能从全掩码序列生成、使用双向上下文并保留大量父模型知识；BYOD-Gemma在九任务评测中平均准确率50.6%，接近同环境LLaDA 8B Instruct的51.9%。主要局限为低NFE预算下的开放生成质量：生成128 token/64步时BYOD困惑度8.3-11.1，而LLaDA为4.1、AR父模型为2.2-3.0。首次报告Mistral系列模型（Ministral 8B）的AR-to-DLM转换。

Conclusion: BYOD为将扩散生成扩展到其他现有模型家族提供了实用的低成本路径，同时明确了高效转换下一步需弥合的差距——低NFE预算下的生成质量。

Abstract: Unlike autoregressive (AR) models, diffusion language models (DLMs) permit parallel token updates, flexible generation, and bidirectional attention, but leading systems rely on costly pretraining or full-model fine-tuning. We present Build Your Own DLM (BYOD), a generalizable LoRA-only recipe for parameter-efficient AR-to-DLM conversion. Without architecture-specific tuning, BYOD induces diffusion generation in Gemma 2 9B, Llama 3.1 8B, Qwen2.5 7B, and Ministral 8B; to our knowledge, this is the first reported AR-to-DLM conversion of a Mistral model. The adapters comprise 4.1-5.8% of final parameters while every base-model parameter remains frozen; each 25,000-iteration conversion needs at most 102.4M tokens, processed in 3.1-4.0 hours on one Google Colab GPU. All four models generate from fully masked sequences, use bidirectional context in a controlled attention test, and retain substantial parent-model knowledge. BYOD-Gemma is the strongest BYOD conversion on our nine-task evaluation, reaching 50.6% average accuracy compared with 51.9% for LLaDA 8B Instruct in the same harness. Open-generation quality at low network function evaluation (NFE) budgets remains the principal limitation: BYOD perplexity is 8.3-11.1 versus 4.1 for LLaDA and 2.2-3.0 for the AR parents when generating 128 tokens in 64 steps. To enable further experimentation and inspection, we provide a standalone training and evaluation implementation and interactive demos for all four conversions. BYOD thus provides a practical route for extending diffusion generation to other existing model families while identifying the gap that efficient conversion must next close.

</details>


### [86] [From Transcription to Reformulation: Defining and Benchmarking Grounded Generative Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41292)
*Qing Wang,Qihan Hu,Hang Lv,Yan Yang,Boqing Liu,Xuechao Zhu,Zehan Li,Hongjie Chen,Jian Kang,Jie Li,Yongxiang Li,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Grounded Generative Speech Recognition (GSR)任务，主张语音识别应从"单条真值转写"转向"生成语义对齐的文本"；定义 grounded reformulation 空间与四级能力层级，并构建GSR-Bench基准及结合语音锚定评估与LLM语义判定的多维评价框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音识别基准仍假设每段语音只有唯一的真值转写，无法评价Speech-LLM在格式化、结构化、跨方言等场景下产出的可读性文本；词级指标（WER等）无法衡量语义正确性与语音支撑性。

Method: 1) 形式化GSR任务，定义"grounded reformulation"空间（输入语音所支持的全部有效文本实现）及四级能力层级：字面转写、规范化重述、语义重述、语音接地结构化（如会议纪要）。2) 构建GSR-Bench，覆盖精炼会议转写、跨方言翻译、上下文感知语音理解、静音/噪声鲁棒性等场景。3) 设计融合speech grounding（文本是否被音频证据支撑）与LLM语义判定的多维评测框架，并用人偏好实验校验其可靠性。4) 在从传统ASR到端到端Speech-LLM的多种架构上开展广泛实验与失效模式分析。

Result: 实验揭示转写保真度与生成式重述能力之间存在本质权衡；并识别三类关键失效模式：声学幻觉（acoustic hallucination）、无支撑生成（unsupported generation）与指令漂移（instruction drift）。

Conclusion: 语音识别评价需从"精确匹配"转向"语义接地"的多维框架；GSR-Bench与四级能力层级为未来接地式生成语音系统的设计与评测提供了范式与洞察。

Abstract: Speech language models have expanded automatic speech recognition (ASR) from transcription to general speech-to-text generation. Yet existing benchmarks still assume a single ground-truth transcript per utterance, an assumption that struggles when models produce readable, formatted, or structured outputs. In this work, we introduce Grounded Generative Speech Recognition (GSR), reformulating speech recognition as generating semantically grounded text instead of a single transcript. We define a grounded reformulation space that characterizes all valid textual realizations supported by the input speech, and establish a four-level hierarchy of reformulation capabilities, including literal transcription, canonical reformulation, semantic reformulation and speech-grounded structuring. Based on this formulation, we present GSR-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate generative speech recognition systems beyond exact transcript matching. GSR-Bench covers diverse scenarios including refined meeting transcription, cross-dialect translation, context-aware speech understanding, and robustness under silent or noisy acoustic conditions. To address the limitations of word-level evaluation metrics, we develop a multi-dimensional evaluation framework combining speech grounding assessment and LLM-based semantic judgment, whose reliability is validated through human preference studies. Extensive experiments across representative architectures, ranging from conventional ASR systems to end-to-end Speech-LLMs, reveal fundamental trade-offs between transcription fidelity and generative reformulation ability. Our analysis further identifies critical failure modes, including acoustic hallucination, unsupported generation, and instruction drift, providing insights into the design and evaluation of future grounded generative speech systems.

</details>


### [87] [Subquadratic Attention with a Hierarchical Tree-Structured Indexer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42112)
*Leon Lufkin,Cutter Dawes,Alexander Speicher,Kamesh Krishnamurthy,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出树状分层索引的稀疏注意力方法 ZHA，使注意力总体达到亚二次（超线性）复杂度，并可通过短程 KL 蒸馏低成本迁移到预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力二次复杂度是限制 LLM 长上下文能力的根本瓶颈；现有方案要么表达能力不足（滑窗、固定状态线性注意力），要么只有常数级加速（稀疏注意力的索引器本身仍是二次）。

Method: 在 KV cache 上构造递归压缩的 key 摘要树，查询自上而下遍历树以检索稀疏 key 子集；通过迭代加深树深度，使路由与最终注意力的总开销随序列长度次线性增长，从而实现整体亚二次但超线性的注意力复杂度。

Result: 仅需数十亿 token 的短程 KL 蒸馏即可迁移至已有预训练模型；在长上下文检索及推理、数学、Agent 编码等评测上保持性能；对树几何结构（最深 6 层）高度鲁棒；性能模型显示超过 100 万上下文后显著优于竞争方法，并有潜力将有效上下文扩展至数亿甚至数十亿 token。

Conclusion: ZHA 以分层树索引实现渐近亚二次稀疏注意力，兼顾表达力与效率、迁移成本低、结构鲁棒，为超长上下文提供可行路径。

Abstract: The quadratic scaling of attention is a fundamental bottleneck limiting the long-context capabilities of large language models (LLMs). A variety of approaches have attempted to circumvent this, but they either fail to match attention's expressiveness (e.g., sliding-window or linear attention variants with fixed-size state) or only achieve constant-factor speedups (e.g., sparse attention methods whose indexers themselves scale quadratically). In this paper, we derive an asymptotically subquadratic sparse attention method based on a hierarchical, tree-structured indexer, which we call Zyphra Hierarchical Attention (ZHA). ZHA constructs a tree of recursively compressed key summaries over the KV cache, which future queries traverse from the top down to retrieve a sparse subset of keys. Crucially, by iteratively growing the tree's depth, the total cost including routing and final attention grows sublinearly in sequence length, resulting in overall subquadratic (but superlinear) scaling for attention. We demonstrate that ZHA can be cheaply applied to existing pretrained models with only a short KL distillation training stage over a few billion tokens, and maintains performance on long-context retrieval and challenging reasoning, mathematical, and agentic coding evaluation tasks. Moreover, we show that our method is highly robust to tree geometry including very deep trees of up to 6 levels. Finally, we develop a detailed performance model and show that our approach scales subquadratically, dramatically outperforms competing methods beyond 1 million context, and ultimately has the potential to extend effective context windows to the hundreds of millions or billions of tokens.

</details>


### [88] [PersonaChatBench: Evaluating Chat Assistants with Diverse Simulated Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42060)
*Zheyuan Deng,Taoran Li,Jingwen Zhang,Yuante Li,Dachuan Zhao,Fulei Zhang,Yunkai Zhang,Zhikun Xu,Yajie Liao,Haichao Zhang,Haodi Hu,Cong Wang,Chen Yang,Siyang Wu,Defu Cao,Xu Hu,Haoming Li,Haixin Wang,Robert George,Weizhi Zhang,Yuchu Wang,Zhejian Peng,Yueying Li,Wanting Jiang,Shushu Liang,Zhiyuan Yu,Yifan Wang,Jintao Huang,Yijun Wang,Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PersonaChatBench，通过在固定任务下搭配多个带私有人设卡（需求、偏好、交互行为）的模拟用户，评估助手任务能力与对多样用户需求的响应性；对20个语言模型、6个领域评测表明任务表现与需求满足度随人设和领域变化，二者不可互相替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话助手评测多基于单一"合作型用户"，难以反映助手对背景、偏好与沟通风格各异的用户的可靠服务水平，聚合分数掩盖了不同用户间的表现差异。

Method: 构建PersonaChatBench：同一底层任务搭配多个模拟用户，每个用户由私有人设卡指定其需求、偏好与交互行为；保持任务不变、仅变换用户，以考察助手表现随用户条件的变化。实验覆盖20个语言模型、6个领域，分别评估原生任务结果、人设需求的满足程度以及模拟器保真度。

Result: 任务表现与需求满足度均随人设和领域波动；强任务表现不必然转化为对个体需求的满足；模型在满足需求方面的相对优势在不同领域间并不一致。

Conclusion: PersonaChatBench使上述差异可测量，揭示被聚合分数掩盖的差距，可识别助手擅长服务哪些用户、何处需要适配，为评估跨用户一致性与开发更好适应多样需求的助手提供基础。

Abstract: Conversational assistants are expected to help people with diverse backgrounds, preferences, and communication styles. Yet evaluations centered on a single cooperative user provide limited insight into how reliably an assistant serves users with different personas. We introduce PersonaChatBench, a framework for evaluating task competence and responsiveness to diverse user needs. PersonaChatBench pairs the same underlying task with multiple simulated users, each guided by a private persona card that specifies distinct needs, preferences, and interaction behaviors. By holding the task fixed while varying the user, the framework examines how assistant performance changes across user conditions. We evaluate 20 language models across six domains, separately assessing native task outcomes, fulfillment of expressed persona needs, and simulator fidelity. Our results show that task performance and need fulfillment vary across personas and domains. Strong task performance does not necessarily translate into satisfying individual needs, and models' relative strengths in meeting those needs are not consistent across domains. PersonaChatBench makes these differences measurable, revealing gaps obscured by aggregate scores and helping identify which users an assistant serves well and where adaptation is needed. It provides a basis for evaluating consistency across users and guiding the development of assistants that better accommodate diverse needs in everyday applications.

</details>


### [89] [CRoS: Causality Reward on Scrambled CoTs Improves Accuracy and Reasoning Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42054)
*Xiao Yu,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Zaid Khan,Hyunji Lee,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CRoS——基于"打乱推理步骤顺序而不改变内容"的CoT因果性奖励，避免扰动引入的信息变化混杂，在推理任务上提升域外准确率、CoT质量与忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR仅以结果正确性为奖励，无法保证CoT与最终答案存在因果联系，可能强化低质量推理。现有因果性度量通过删除步骤或注入错误来扰动CoT，但这类扰动不可避免改变各步信息，导致高分可能源于对信息变化的敏感性而非真实因果性，并作为训练奖励时诱发CoT信息量下降、探索与自我纠错能力减弱。

Method: CRoS（Causality Reward on Scrambled CoTs）：仅打乱推理步骤的先后顺序、保持每步内容不变，从而在保留步内信息的前提下度量CoT对最终答案的因果贡献，并将其作为训练奖励。

Result: 在Reasoning Gym上用Gemma-3-4B：五个未见逻辑与博弈数据集上准确率46.8%，优于最强基线43.8%；CoT-Pass@K（K=1~128）平均43.4% vs 41.4%；忠实度上hint acknowledgment得分为34.0% vs 20.2%。

Conclusion: 通过消除扰动带来的信息混杂，仅重排步骤顺序即可更纯粹地刻画CoT因果性；CRoS作为奖励在域外泛化、推理质量与忠实度上均优于现有因果性与结果奖励方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become the standard method for training language models to reason. However, RLVR typically relies solely on outcome correctness, which does not guarantee that the CoT causally contributes to the final answer. Without this connection, outcome rewards may fail to reinforce high-quality reasoning. Existing causality metrics assess CoT causality by perturbing the CoT, such as deleting reasoning steps or injecting errors, and measuring changes in the final answer. However, these perturbations inevitably alter information in each step. Consequently, high causality scores may reflect sensitivity to information changes introduced by perturbation rather than true causality. Such confounding effects further induce undesirable reasoning behaviors when these metrics are used as training rewards, including generating less informative CoTs and reducing exploration and self-correction. To address this problem, we propose CRoS (Causality Reward on Scrambled CoTs), a novel CoT causality metric that perturbs the order of reasoning steps without altering content, thereby preserving information within each step. On Reasoning Gym with Gemma-3-4B, CRoS outperforms existing causality and outcome rewards in out-of-domain accuracy, CoT quality, and CoT faithfulness. Specifically, CRoS achieves 46.8% accuracy on five unseen logic and games datasets, outperforming the strongest baseline (43.8%). It also achieves better CoT quality, consistently outperforming baselines in CoT-Pass@K from K=1 to 128, averaging 43.4% vs. 41.4%. For CoT faithfulness, CRoS achieves a hint acknowledgment score of 34.0% vs. 20.2%.

</details>


### [90] [BlindToChange: AI Agents Can Find Changes When Asked But Do They Look When Needed?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41435)
*Anirudh Phukan,Tanya Goyal,Danish Pruthi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出BlindToChange基准，检验AI智能体能否主动发现并考虑用户请求中未明示的近期现实事件变化；结果显示即便高推理强度下，系统在事件未被明示时性能可低至下降27.5分，过半失败源于未直接检索核实相关事实是否变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体可实时联网检索，易被误认为其输出已反映最新世界事件；而BrowseComp等主流基准会明示需查找的对象，无法暴露"智能体未意识到自身假设需被验证"的失败。真实用户请求通常只陈述最终信息需求，需智能体自行发现相关变化及其后果，此能力缺乏针对性评测。

Method: 构建BlindToChange基准：请求形式为推荐类任务，其中原本显而易见的候选对象因某近期真实事件而不再适用，事件自动从Wikidata抽取以支持周期性更新。对比两种设定——近期事件未被明示 vs. 直接询问该事件——测量性能差距，并分析检索轨迹以归因失败原因。

Result: 在事件未明示条件下性能显著下降，差距高达27.5分，即使在高推理强度下亦然；轨迹分析表明超过一半的失败源于智能体未直接检索以验证相关事实是否已发生变化。

Conclusion: 基准揭示并追踪一条新的能力进步轴线：系统需考虑用户请求中未明示的近期信息；仅具备检索能力不足以保证时效正确性，关键在于主动验证假设。

Abstract: Given that AI agents can search the web broadly and deeply in real time, it is tempting to conclude that their outputs account for recent world events. We show that this conclusion does not hold in practice. While agents can search and easily find recent changes, the correctness of their responses depends on whether they search and what they search for. Popular agentic search benchmarks like BrowseComp cannot expose failures wherein agents do not recognize their own assumptions that should be verified, since these benchmarks explicitly state what needs to be found. In contrast, many user requests state only the final information need (e.g., an itinerary for a trip), leaving the agent to discover relevant changes (e.g., the temporary closure of a museum) and account for their consequences. To highlight such gaps, we introduce BlindToChange, a benchmark that measures whether agents proactively discover recent events that affect their responses. Our benchmark comprises requests for recommendations where an obvious candidate is no longer applicable due to a recent real-world event. These events are drawn automatically from Wikidata, allowing BlindToChange to be periodically refreshed. Strikingly, we find a big gap -- as high as 27.5 points, even at high reasoning effort -- in systems' performance, when the recent event is not explicitly mentioned compared to when we ask about it directly. Analyzing search trajectories, we learn that more than half of the failures can be attributed to the fact that agents do not directly search to verify whether a relevant fact has changed. Our benchmark highlights, and tracks, a new axis of progress, wherein systems account for recent information that is not explicitly mentioned in the user request.

</details>


### [91] [Saidzoda-Tajik-LFM-2.6B: Adapting a 2.6B Hybrid Model to Tajik Tool Calling, and What Continued Pretraining Takes Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41576)
*Tohir Saidzoda,Nekruz Maxudov,Bedil Karimov,Abubakr Abdullozoda,Amin Jalilov,Jonibek Kishvarov,Rahmonali Sharipov,Mariyamkhon Shodyeva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 发布 Saidzoda-Tajik-LFM-2.6B：在 LFM2.5-2.6B-Base 上经 LoRA 继续预训练（148 万塔吉克语网页文档，3 epoch）加一 epoch SFT，使塔吉克语提示下的严格完整调用准确率从 20.69 升至 64.14，代价是英文 MMLU 从 60.8 降至 52.5，且拒答与多轮能力薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在普通硬件上支持塔吉克语工具调用，填补低资源语言在函数调用（function calling）能力上的空白。

Method: 两阶段配方：先用 LoRA 对 1,485,319 篇清洗后的塔吉克语网络文档做 3 epoch 继续预训练（CPT），再用 1 epoch SFT，数据混合为机器翻译的塔吉克语工具调用、作为 replay 保留的英文工具调用和塔吉克语指令。评测集为 BFCL v3 经机器翻译得到的 174 条塔吉克语工具调用测试集，并配合宽松解析器（额外接受基座模型自带调用格式）进行复核。

Result: 塔吉克语提示的严格完整调用准确率由 20.69 提升至 64.14（配对差 +43.45 点，95% 区间 +35.2 至 +51.7）；宽松解析下基座为 31.72。英文无显著变化（76.55→77.24）。所有 CPT checkpoint 得分为零：CPT 破坏了原生工具调用格式，且宽松重评分显示也损害了部分技能本身。工具调用之外：塔吉克语知识基准由 28.0 升至 35.0，Belebele 塔吉克语无显著变化，英文 MMLU 由 60.8 降至 52.5。

Conclusion: 该模型是专用塔吉克语工具调用模型而非通用塔吉克语助手：无合适工具时几乎不拒答（塔吉克语仅 6.9% 正确，基座宽松解析为 31.0%），多轮调用能力弱，调用结果执行前需校验；论文完整披露数据、训练配置与全部评测以便复现。

Abstract: We release Saidzoda-Tajik-LFM-2.6B, a Tajik adaptation of the 2.6B-parameter hybrid model LFM2.5-2.6B-Base, built for tool calling in Tajik on modest hardware. The recipe has two stages: LoRA continued pretraining (CPT) on 1,485,319 cleaned Tajik web documents for 3 epochs, then one epoch of supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a mix of machine-translated Tajik tool calls, English tool calls kept as replay, and Tajik instructions. On a 174-item tool-calling test set built from BFCL v3 and machine-translated to Tajik, strict full-call accuracy on Tajik prompts rises from 20.69 for the base model to 64.14 (paired difference +43.45 points, 95% interval +35.2 to +51.7); a lenient parser that also accepts the base model's own call formats puts the base at 31.72. English shows no detectable change (76.55 and 77.24). Every CPT checkpoint scores zero: CPT removes the native tool-call format and, as the lenient re-scoring shows, part of the skill itself. Outside tool calling, accuracy on a Tajik knowledge benchmark rises from 28.0 to 35.0, Belebele Tajik shows no significant change, and English MMLU falls from 60.8 to 52.5. The model is not a general Tajik assistant. It almost never declines when no tool fits (6.9% correct on Tajik, against 31.0% for the base under the lenient parser), it is weak at multi-turn calls, and its calls should be checked before they are executed. We describe the data, the training settings and every evaluation in enough detail to repeat them.

</details>


### [92] [Do Open Language Models Invent Scripture? BILAL-BENCH: Benchmarking Islamic-Source Fabrication in Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40996)
*Sabah Nushra,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BILAL-BENCH，一个240题、专家核验的孟加拉语开放式伊斯兰来源虚构基准，用双轴评分区分来源错误与断言强度，评测7个开源模型（1B–120B）发现6个模型在79.3–95.3%的溯源生成类问题上自信虚构，且规模扩大并不带来单调改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型虚构古兰经经文或圣训会给文本赋予虚假权威，而读者可能将其视为神圣内容；现有伊斯兰幻觉基准多为阿拉伯语且以封闭式为主，孟加拉语资源则主要研究通用领域幻觉或事实知识，缺乏针对孟加拉语开放式伊斯兰来源虚构的系统评测。

Method: 构建BILAL-BENCH：240道专家核验的孟加拉语开放式问答，覆盖古兰经归属、圣训、教法（fiqh）、当代议题及对抗性虚假前提提示；采用双轴评分框架，将来源错误与断言强度（自信程度）分离；对1B至120B的7个开源模型生成的1,680条双标注回答进行评测。

Result: 7个模型中有6个在79.3–95.3%的来源生成类问题上自信虚构，性能未随参数量单调提升；圣训溯源为最弱类别，315条回答中仅2条既正确又标注了正确来源；两个8B BanglaLLaMA变体自信虚构率相近但错误画像不同；模型间在"对无法支撑的主张是否弃权"上的差异远大于来源准确率差异——恰当弃权或纠正虚假前提的回答在基准中从6条到46条不等，且与参数量无关。

Conclusion: 在伊斯兰来源类开放问答中，模型的核心失效并非单纯知识缺失，而是缺乏校准的不确定性表达与拒答能力；规模扩展无法解决该问题，需针对弃权与虚假前提识别进行专门对齐，并强调低资源语言宗教领域的幻觉评测必要性。

Abstract: When a language model invents a Qur'anic verse or hadith, it gives false authority to text that readers may treat as sacred. Existing Islamic hallucination benchmarks are largely Arabic and often closed-ended, while Bengali resources mainly study general-domain hallucination or factual knowledge. We introduce BILAL-BENCH, a 240-question expert-verified benchmark for open-ended Islamic-source fabrication in Bengali. It covers Qur'anic attribution, hadith, fiqh, contemporary issues, and adversarial false-premise prompts, and uses a two-axis rubric that separates source errors from assertiveness. We evaluate seven open-weight models from 1B to 120B on 1,680 double-annotated responses. Six of the seven models confidently fabricate on 79.3-95.3% of source-production questions, with no monotonic improvement from scale. Hadith provenance is the weakest category across models: only two of 315 responses are correct and correctly sourced. The two 8B BanglaLLaMA variants have similar confident-fabrication rates but different error profiles. Models vary far more in whether they withhold a claim they cannot support than in source accuracy: appropriate abstention or false-premise correction ranges from 6 to 46 responses across the benchmark, and does not track parameter count.

</details>


### [93] [The Generative Gap Between Speculative Drafter and Continuous Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40847)
*Yujian Chen,Hao Jiang,Guang Cheng,Weijian Luo,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在无验证、统一预算的设定下，比较连续扩散语言模型LangFlow与投机草稿器架构DSpark（Markov头/循环头）的无条件生成能力，发现LangFlow在长序列上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDLM与投机草稿器通常在不同评测范式下被衡量（前者独立迭代精炼生成，后者仅向目标模型提草案以供验证修正），缺乏可比性。作者希望在剔除验证/目标特征干扰、统一预处理与训练预算的条件下，公平比较两类架构的独立生成能力。

Method: 从零训练无条件单块OpenWebText生成模型，长度16/128/1024，统一预处理、骨干维度与训练更新预算；LangFlow作为CDLM基线，DSpark的Markov头与循环头（RNN）架构被改造为独立生成。用外部语言模型计算生成困惑度(GenPPL)，用MAUVE度量与参考文本的分布相似度；并做条件KL分析考察Markov头仅依赖前一token所排除的依赖关系；另测试教师概率匹配与增加去噪步数的影响。

Result: 默认去噪步数等于序列长度时，LangFlow在长度128和1024上GenPPL与MAUVE均优于两种DSpark变体，而DSpark-RNN在长度16上领先。循环记忆消除了Markov头对前文依赖的限制并改善两项指标，但长序列差距依旧存在。教师概率匹配降低了Markov的GenPPL但MAUVE无可见提升。固定权重下增加LangFlow去噪步数可改善16和128长度的两项指标，并改变长度128处相对DSpark-RNN的排序。

Conclusion: 在所评估的预算下，LangFlow具备长序列优势；迭代式全序列精炼是超越循环头记忆增益、提升独立生成质量的有效路径。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) and speculative drafters offer efficient language generation but are typically evaluated differently: CDLMs generate independently through iterative refinement, whereas drafters propose text for target-model verification and correction. We compare their architectures without verification, using LangFlow for CDLMs and adapting DSpark's Markov and recurrent-head architectures for standalone generation. All models are trained from scratch for unconditional, single-block OpenWebText generation at lengths 16, 128, and 1024, without target features or verification, using common preprocessing, backbone dimensions, and training-update budgets. We evaluate generation perplexity (GenPPL) with an external language model and distributional similarity to reference text with MAUVE. With default denoising steps equal to sequence length, LangFlow outperforms both DSpark variants on both metrics at lengths 128 and 1024, while DSpark-RNN leads at 16. A conditional KL analysis characterizes dependencies excluded by the Markov head's previous-token conditioning. Recurrent memory removes this restriction and improves both metrics, but the longer-sequence gap persists. Teacher probability matching further lowers Markov GenPPL without observed MAUVE gains. Increasing LangFlow's denoising steps at fixed weights improves both metrics at lengths 16 and 128, changing the length-128 ordering relative to DSpark-RNN. These results establish LangFlow's longer-sequence advantage under the evaluated budgets and highlight iterative full-sequence refinement as a route to improving standalone generation beyond gains from recurrent head memory.

</details>


### [94] [Text as an Index for Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41883)
*Yin Yi,Haiping Ma,Siyu Song,Ziwen Wang,Shangshang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TIZSKT框架，将行为序列建模与领域本地的历史证据构建解耦，文本仅作"索引"而非直接迁移的预测表示，在无目标域训练数据的零样本跨域知识追踪中取得最高平均AUC与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新平台常仅有题目文本而无作答日志，难以训练KT模型，因而需要零样本跨域知识追踪。现有迁移与冷启动方法依赖题目语义，但跨平台语义差异会限制可迁移性，故需避免将文本嵌入直接当作预测表示。

Method: TIZSKT包含四个组件：(1) Mastery-Dynamics Encoder，不使用题目ID或文本嵌入，仅学习正确性动态；(2) Domain-Local Question Addressing，用冻结文本编码器加各域无监督投影构建固定题目地址；(3) Text-Addressing Kernel，以地址为索引，将当前学生既往作答结果聚合为题目特定的历史正确性摘要；(4) Cross-Segment Continuation，在不延长行为编码器输入序列的前提下保留对更早作答的访问；(5) Evidence-Volume Read-Out，保留归一化时丢弃的核质量，其对数压缩值与行为状态、历史正确性摘要共同预测下一作答。全部可训练参数仅在源域学习，目标域推理仅用公开题目文本与当前学生观测历史。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的留一域实验表明，TIZSKT在所有目标域上于所评估基线中取得最高的平均AUC与准确率；消融实验验证了文本寻址与跨段延续两个组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 将文本作为寻址索引而非迁移性语义表示，可有效缓解跨平台语义差异带来的迁移损失，实现无需目标域训练的零样本知识追踪。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) predicts a student's next response from their history. New platforms often have question texts but no response logs for training, motivating zero-shot cross-domain KT. Existing transfer and cold-start methods use question semantics, but cross-platform semantic discrepancies limit transferability. We propose TIZSKT, which separates behavioral sequence modeling from domain-local historical evidence construction and uses text as an index rather than a directly transferable predictive representation. The Mastery-Dynamics Encoder learns correctness dynamics without question identifiers or text embeddings. Domain-Local Question Addressing builds fixed question addresses using a frozen text encoder and an unsupervised per-domain projection. The Text-Addressing Kernel uses these addresses to aggregate the current student's previous outcomes into a question-specific historical correctness summary. Cross-Segment Continuation retains access to earlier responses without extending the behavioral encoder's input sequence. Evidence-Volume Read-Out preserves the total kernel mass discarded by normalization and combines its log-compressed value with the behavioral state and historical correctness summary to predict the next response. All trainable parameters are learned from source domains; target inference uses public question texts and the current student's observed history without target-domain training. Leave-one-domain-out experiments on three public datasets show that TIZSKT achieves the highest mean AUC and accuracy among evaluated baselines on all target domains. Ablations confirm the contributions of text addressing and cross-segment continuation.

</details>


### [95] [SimXAI: A Simple Drop-in Layer Increases Interaction Quality and Understanding in Conversational XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41856)
*Van Bach Nguyen,Qianli Wang,Nils Feldhus,Jörg Schlötterer,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SimXAI：以单一工具调用式LLM取代GUS式多模块流水线，作为模块化会话接口桥接现有XAI资源，并配套单/多轮解析、忠实性与上下文化评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUS式ConvXAI架构将意图识别、对话管理、模板化回复生成分离，虽忠实于底层XAI方法，但难以适配、对依赖上下文的追问处理不佳。

Method: 提出轻量级LLM中心会话层SimXAI，用单个tool-calling LLM统一实现上述模块功能，调用外部XAI资源；定位为可插拔接口而非独立智能体；构建涵盖单轮与多轮解析、忠实性、上下文化的评估套件；以替换TalkToModel会话层的用户研究验证。

Result: 在现有与新建数据集上取得高函数选择准确率与高忠实性，更好处理上下文相关请求；用户研究中任务完成更准确、所需轮次更少。

Conclusion: SimXAI在保留忠实性的前提下改进GUS式设计，提升会话质量与效率，可作为模块化会话层替代传统多模块流水线。

Abstract: Conversational explainable AI (ConvXAI) agents often use GUS-style architectures with separate modules for intent recognition, dialogue management, and template-based response generation. While faithful to the underlying XAI methods, these pipelines are hard to adapt and often struggle with context-dependent follow-up questions. We present SimXAI, a lightweight LLM-centric conversational layer that replaces these modules with a single tool-calling LLM that bridges access to existing XAI resources. Rather than a standalone agent, SimXAI serves as a modular interface that enables conversational access to external XAI resources. We further introduce an evaluation suite for single- and multi-turn parsing, faithfulness, and contextualization. Across existing and new datasets, SimXAI achieves strong function-selection accuracy and high faithfulness while better handling context-dependent requests. In a user study replacing only the conversational layer of TalkToModel, a representative GUS-based agent, users completed tasks more accurately and in fewer turn. Overall, SimXAI improves GUS-based design while preserving faithfulness and improving conversational quality.

</details>


### [96] [GraphNIAH: A Benchmark for Long-Context Reasoning over Real-World Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41704)
*Minh-Nam Tran,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出动态基准 GraphNIAH，利用知识截止日期之后发表的论文间真实引用关系构建长上下文多跳推理问答，避免合成数据与基准污染，并发现八个大模型在深度16/32时接近随机猜测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NIAH 基准存在四类局限：依赖合成数据导致外部效度不足；复用已发布数据集带来污染风险；依赖人工标注、成本高且易再污染；以及对长上下文多跳推理的简化不真实——每一步在前一步给定后都变得平凡，而现实推理常需在多个可能证明路径上做规划。

Method: 构建完全自动化的流程：取知识截止日期之后发表的科学论文，抽取其引用图，采样具有代表性的子图，并将每个子图转化为带可验证答案的长上下文问答样例，从而在利用真实世界信息的同时保证无污染。随后用该基准评测 GPT-5.6、Gemini-3 与 Qwen3.5 三个系列共八个模型。

Result: 准确率随所需推理路径变长而下降：在问题深度为16和32时，GPT-5.6 与 Qwen3.5 模型回答 YES/NO 问题的表现不优于随机猜测，且大多回答 NO；八个模型中有四个未能正确回答任何计数类样例，其余模型则随子图规模增大而性能下降。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当前 LLM 往往在遍历到答案之前就停止检索上下文中的引用关系，尽管所需全部事实均已存在于上下文中，说明其在长上下文多跳证据链上的搜索与规划能力仍显著不足。

Abstract: The needle-in-a-haystack (NIAH) benchmark was introduced as a test for measuring a model's ability to find small relevant facts within a very large body of input text. However, existing NIAH benchmarks each suffer from one or more of the following limitations: (1) reliance on synthetic data, so their evaluations may not generalize to real-world settings; (2) reuse of previously published datasets, leading to the risk of benchmark contamination; (3) dependence on manual annotation, which requires human effort to create and update data and is prone to future benchmark contamination; and (4) unrealistic simplification of long-context multi-hop reasoning where each next step is trivial given previous steps, while real-world reasoning often requires planning over multiple possible proof paths. To address these limitations, we introduce \Benchmark, a dynamic benchmark built from real citation relationships among scientific papers published after the evaluated models' knowledge cutoff date, which utilizes real-world information while ensuring no benchmark contamination. The fully automated benchmark construction pipeline samples representative subgraphs of the citation graph formed by these papers and converts each subgraph into a long-context QA example with a verifiable answer. We use GraphNIAH to evaluate eight LLMs from the GPT-5.6, Gemini-3, and Qwen3.5 families. We find that accuracy falls as questions require longer reasoning paths: at question depths of 16 and 32, the GPT-5.6 and Qwen3.5 models answer YES/NO questions no better than random guessing, mostly by answering NO; four of the eight models answer no counting example correctly, and the others model get worse as the subgraph size increases. These findings suggest that current LLMs often stop searching the citation relationships in their context before they reach the answer, even though every fact they need is in the context.

</details>


### [97] [Enhancing Knowledge Entity Relation Extraction in Abstracts and Full-text Content of Academic Articles Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41552)
*Yingyi Zhang,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了多种基于大语言模型（LLM）的学术论文知识实体关系抽取（KERE）策略，并在自建NLP领域标注数据集上比较其效果、纠错能力与优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅考察单一类型的LLM策略，且多以precision、recall等指标评价，缺乏对多种策略的横向、多维度评估，也缺少对SLM错误缓解与优化方向的深入分析。

Method: 调研并对比四类既有策略（闭源LLM的CoT提示、LoRA微调的开源LLM、LLM增强的小型预训练语言模型SLM、SLM增强的闭源LLM），并新增两类策略（SLM增强的开源LLM、闭源LLM增强的开源LLM）；从指标表现、对SLM错误的缓解能力、优化潜力等多视角评估；人工构建Abstract与Full-Text两个NLP领域标注数据集。

Result: LoRA微调的T5在Abstract与Full-Text数据集上分别取得最高macro F1（0.7408与0.7013）；在LLM增强SLM策略下，SLM可从LLM生成的实体描述中获益；误差分析表明不同策略对不同错误类型的缓解效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 基于策略局限与错误模式提出若干优化方向，并发现组合具有不同错误模式的策略可进一步提升KERE性能。

Abstract: This study investigates the effectiveness of large pre-trained language models (LLMs) for knowledge entity relation extraction (KERE) from academic papers. Existing studies have examined individual types of LLM-based strategies and evaluated model performance using metrics such as precision and recall. To provide a broader evaluation, this study investigates multiple strategies, including closed-source LLM-based CoT prompting, LoRA-adapted open-source LLMs, LLM-enhanced small pre-trained language models (SLMs), and SLM-enhanced closed-source LLMs. In addition, this study introduces two strategies, namely SLM-enhanced open-source LLMs and closed-source LLM-enhanced open-source LLMs. These strategies are evaluated from multiple perspectives, including metrics, their ability to mitigate errors observed in SLMs, and potential directions for model optimization. This study manually constructs two annotated datasets in the NLP domain, covering Abstract and Full-Text datasets. Experimental results show that the LoRA-adapted T5 model achieves the highest macro F1 scores of 0.7408 and 0.7013 on the Abstract and Full-Text datasets, respectively. SLMs also benefit from LLM-generated entity descriptions under the LLM-enhanced SLM strategy. Error analysis further reveals that the effectiveness of LLM-based strategies in mitigating SLM errors varies across error types. Based on the identified limitations and error patterns of these strategies, several optimization directions are proposed. In addition, combining strategies with different error patterns further improves KERE performance.

</details>


### [98] [TraceSpec: Understanding and Improving Speculative Decoding for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41177)
*Jihao Zhao,Shuaishuai Zu,Pengfei Li,Biao Qin,Guanghuan Fang,Ben Feng,Baochuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TraceSpec框架，将LLM agent轨迹评估与自适应草稿模型（推测解码）训练相连接，并发布TraceSpec-Traj数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码的聚合指标把agent执行中的多种不同决策混在一起，无法指导何处需要草稿监督。

Method: 结合动态执行与固定前缀重放，构建含1万训练状态、2500测试状态的TraceSpec-Traj语料（覆盖浏览与软件工程）；分析五个推测解码基线；在中间assistant轮次上训练，基于状态条件化的前缀感知接受差距估计分配固定预测预算。

Result: 在三种目标模型规模、两个温度下，重放吞吐较DSpark基线提升12.5%–22.6%；Qwen3-4B消融中，自适应分配相比固定全轮监督使接受长度提升3.3%–10.0%。

Conclusion: 轨迹条件化评估与自适应草稿训练可有效改进agent场景下的推测解码，并将执行分析与实用草稿优化连接起来。

Abstract: LLM agents execute tasks through repeated model calls over evolving histories of reasoning, actions, and tool feedback. Aggregate speculative-decoding metrics merge these distinct decisions, providing limited guidance on where draft supervision is needed. We present TraceSpec, a framework connecting trajectory evaluation with adaptive draft training. It combines dynamic execution with fixed-prefix replay and introduces TraceSpec-Traj, a corpus of 10,000 training states and 2,500 test states spanning browsing and software engineering. Analysis of five speculative baselines reveals that aggregate acceptance can rise with context length while declining within matched late browsing actions. Workload and phase further distinguish these trends, motivating supervision guided by trajectory conditions. TraceSpec trains on intermediate assistant turns and allocates a fixed prediction budget using state-conditioned estimates of prefix-aware acceptance gaps. Across three target sizes and two temperatures, it improves replay throughput by 12.5% to 22.6% over its DSpark base. Qwen3-4B ablations show that adaptive allocation increases accepted length by 3.3% to 10.0% over fixed full-turn supervision, with additional comparisons substantiating the roles of state conditioning and consecutive-token agreement. The resulting evaluation resources, trajectory findings, and training strategy connect the analysis of agent execution to practical draft improvement.

</details>


### [99] [S2TDR: Structured Multimodal Reconstruction for Sticker-Aware Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41176)
*Yuanchen Shi,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Sticker-to-Text Dialogue Reconstruction (S2TDR)框架，将对话中的表情包轮次结构化重建为符合上下文的文本话语，从而让现成文本对话模型可直接消费表情包语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情包在社交媒体对话中承载情感、立场与意图，含义多为非字面且依赖语境，通用的视觉融合或单独图像描述（captioning）难以准确处理其语用含义。

Method: S2TDR将表情包解读分解为三步：视觉线索抽取、情感—意图推断、上下文感知的对话重建；OCR文本仅在可用时作为辅助线索。重建后的对话交由现有文本对话模型处理。

Result: 在情感分析、意图识别和对话摘要三项任务上，S2TDR相较直接将表情包视觉特征融合的方法，整体上提升了多个下游模型的表现；组件消融、无OCR表情包以及重建质量分析进一步验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化重建是一种可解释且有效的途径，可将表情包语义整合进对话理解流程。

Abstract: Stickers are widely used in social media dialogues to express affect, stance, and intent beyond literal text. Their meanings are often non-literal and context-dependent, making them difficult to handle with generic visual fusion or standalone captioning. We propose Sticker-to-Text Dialogue Reconstruction (S2TDR), a structured multimodal reconstruction framework that converts sticker turns into contextually appropriate textual utterances. S2TDR decomposes sticker interpretation into visual cue extraction, affective-intent inference, and context-aware dialogue reconstruction, using OCR text only as an auxiliary cue when available. The reconstructed dialogue can then be consumed by existing text-based dialogue models. Experiments on sentiment analysis, intent recognition, and dialogue summarization show that S2TDR generally improves multiple downstream models over direct sticker integration methods. Further analyses on component ablations, OCR-empty stickers, and reconstruction quality indicate that structured reconstruction provides an interpretable and effective way to integrate sticker semantics into dialogue understanding.

</details>


### [100] [Adaptive Personalization Under Preference Epistemic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40930)
*Priyanka Dey,Rajarshi Das,Vikas Bahirwani,Qi Zeng,Julia Kruk,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"认知不确定性下的自适应个性化"任务，构建多语言基准（250角色、5语言、25K轮对话），揭示模型的"识别—生成鸿沟"：选项选择时能识别合适策略，开放式生成时策略校准下降35–46%，且系统性偏向"先利用"；针对性训练可缩小泛化差距约三分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好随时间演变（保持、变得不确定或被改写），对话式AI应随偏好不确定性动态调整回应策略，而非依赖被动的记忆检索；现有系统缺乏对这种自适应能力的评估与训练手段。

Method: 形式化"认知不确定性下的自适应个性化"；开展人类偏好研究验证对状态依赖策略的偏好；构建多语言基准（250 persona、5语言、25K轮对话），将策略选择与回答执行解耦；评测8个闭源/开源模型，比较提示干预与监督模型内部策略表示向状态条件人类偏好对齐的训练方法。

Result: 发现"识别—生成鸿沟"：候选选项下策略识别可靠，开放式生成中策略校准骤降35–46%；失败具系统性，模型默认"先利用"，忽视偏好认知不确定性；提示干预无效，而针对性训练在分布外persona与对话风格上使用户可接受度泛化差距缩小约三分之一。

Conclusion: 策略选择与策略执行是可分离的能力维度，现有LM在开放生成中的自适应个性化系统性不足；该缺陷可通过监督内部策略表示加以缓解，为不确定性感知的个性化提供了可行训练路径。

Abstract: Human preferences change over time: an established preference may remain valid over time, become uncertain as context shifts, or be explicitly revised. Effective personalization requires conversational AI to adapt its response strategy to evolving preferences rather than relying on passive memory retrieval. We formalize this capability as adaptive personalization under epistemic uncertainty. A human preference study further confirms that people favor state-contingent response strategies as preference uncertainty changes. To evaluate whether language models mirror this adaptation, we create a multilingual benchmark spanning 250 personas, 5 languages, and 25K conversational turns that decouples strategy selection from response execution. Across eight closed- and open-weight models, we uncover a recognition-generation gap: models reliably identify appropriate strategies when selecting among candidate options, but collapse during open-ended generation, suffering a 35-46% drop in strategy calibration. Crucially, this failure is systematic: models default to exploit-first behavior, over-relying on existing preferences regardless of preference epistemic uncertainty. We show this failure is addressable: while prompting interventions fail, targeted training that supervises models' internal strategy representations toward state-conditional human preferences narrows the user acceptability generalization gap by a third on out-of-distribution personas and conversational styles.

</details>


### [101] [PsyInterviewee: Informational Invariance and Relational Adaptivity in Mental Health Interview Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41289)
*Jiawei Zhang,Huachuan Qiu,Ruocheng Guo,Zijun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 PsyInterviewee，通过分离“受保护真相通道”与“动态交互通道”，在保持画像临床事实不变的同时实现披露与行为的自适应，从而提升 LLM 模拟受访者的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 模拟受访者研究主要靠更丰富的患者画像与行为建模提升“拟人度”，但拟人度并不等于可靠；可靠模拟需同时满足信息不变性（保持画像所依据的临床事实）与关系自适应（根据访谈调整披露与交互行为）。

Method: 将受保护真相通道与动态交互通道分离，并对生成的临床陈述与画像进行核验；采用非对称写权限，使对话上下文只能更新披露状态与行为状态，而不能授权生成新的临床事实，再由这些状态决定披露哪些画像支持的信息及其表达方式。

Result: 在 LLM 访谈者与受访者的自动化交互评估中，两位 LLM 评审在两条轴（信息不变性、关系自适应）的大多数指标上均给 PsyInterviewee 高于四个代表性基线的评分；消融表明两个通道分别贡献临床事实锚定与响应调制。

Conclusion: 可靠性不应仅由拟人度衡量；通过真相通道与交互通道的解耦及非对称写权限约束，可在不牺牲临床事实一致性的前提下实现行为自适应，为心理健康访谈训练与系统评测提供更可信的模拟受访者。

Abstract: LLM-based simulated interviewees support interviewer training and the evaluation of mental health interviewing systems. Existing studies have primarily focused on improving human-likeness through richer patient profiles and behavioral modeling. However, human-likeness alone does not establish their reliability. Reliable simulation requires preserving profile-grounded clinical facts (informational invariance) while adapting disclosure and interaction behavior to the interview (relational adaptivity). To this end, we introduce PsyInterviewee, which separates a protected truth channel from a dynamic interaction channel and verifies generated clinical claims against the profile. Specifically, asymmetric write permissions allow dialogue context to update disclosure and behavioral states without authorizing new clinical facts; these states guide what profile-supported information is revealed and how it is expressed. In an automated evaluation of interactions between LLM-based interviewers and interviewees, PsyInterviewee achieves higher scores than four representative baselines on most metrics across both axes, as assessed by two LLM judges. Ablations suggest that the two channels contribute to clinical grounding and response modulation, respectively.

</details>


### [102] [Mage-VL-Cowork: A Full Training Recipe to Learn Hybrid Computer Use From Evolved Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40747)
*Xiaoyi Zhang,Haoqing Wang,Xinjie Zhang,Xiang Ming,Xing Wang,Senqiao Yang,Kaichen Zhang,Zhaoyang Jia,Andong Tan,Xinjiang Wang,Jinghao Guo,Yifei Shen,Xiao Li,Peng Zhang,Xun Guo,Jiahao Li,Shicheng Zheng,Jinglu Wang,Zongyu Guo,Wenxuan Xie,Bin Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出4B混合式计算机使用智能体Mage-VL-Cowork，通过五阶段渐进课程（midtraining/SFT/RL）训练，并配套可复用技能条件化数据生成系统Mage-CUA-Forge，在OSWorld-Verified上达到同参数量最优的66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA需在同一策略中融合视觉GUI交互与代码/终端执行能力；同时按任务逐个构建智能体与轨迹的数据生产方式成本高、难以规模化，且直接蒸馏默认教师难以获得高质量、分阶段的监督信号。

Method: 1）以通用多模态对齐检查点为起点，构建端到端渐进五阶段课程，覆盖midtraining、SFT与RL；2）Mage-CUA-Forge用可复用的技能条件化生成工作流与应用专属preparer替代逐任务智能体构建，合成293,806个桌面与Web任务、覆盖6,915项技能；3）不直接蒸馏默认教师，而是迭代演进教师harness并实例化专用CUA harness，产出高质量轨迹与阶段特定监督。

Result: 合成293,806个任务、覆盖6,915项技能，逐任务重复开销降低82.6%；Mage-VL-Cowork-4B经各训练阶段逐步提升，最终在OSWorld-Verified取得66.0%，且推理成本更高效，在其参数规模上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 可复用技能条件化数据供给与渐进式多阶段课程相结合，能有效将通用多模态模型塑造为高效混合CUA，并以4B规模逼近/推进端侧智能体能力前沿。

Abstract: Effective computer-use agents (CUAs) should seamlessly combine visual GUI interaction with code and terminal execution within a single policy. We present Mage-VL-Cowork, a 4B hybrid CUA trained from a general multimodally aligned checkpoint through an end-to-end, progressive five-stage curriculum across midtraining, SFT, and RL. To power this curriculum at scale, Mage-CUA-Forge replaces per-task agent construction with reusable, skill-conditioned generation workflows and application-specific preparers, synthesizing 293,806 desktop and web tasks across 6,915 skills while reducing recurring per-task expenditure by 82.6%. Rather than directly distilling a default teacher, we evolve the teacher harness and instantiate specialized CUA harnesses to yield high-quality trajectories alongside stage-specific supervision. Mage-VL-Cowork-4B finally climbs to 66.0% on OSWorld-Verified through different training stages with more efficient reasoning cost, achieving state-of-the-art performance at its parameter size and pushing forward the frontier of on-device intelligence.

</details>


### [103] [RangeSWA: Training-Free Length Extrapolation for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42084)
*Guangfu Guo,Xiaoqian Lu,Linsey Pang,Weiran Yao,Haolin Chen,Long Cheng,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RangeSWA：面向连续扩散语言模型的免训练长度外推方法，通过局部滑窗注意力、远程块摘要与动态内容检索保留局部细节并提供长程访问，结合位置重映射、条件注意力校正与去噪阶段自适应窗口调度，降低长文本生成的困惑度与重复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型在文本生成上具潜力，但当生成长度超过训练长度时，易出现文本重复、语义漂移与生成质量下降等外推退化问题，需要一种不额外训练即可缓解该问题的方法。

Method: 提出免训练的 RangeSWA，融合三类机制：局部滑动窗口注意力、远程块摘要、动态内容检索，以兼顾局部细节与长程上下文访问；并通过位置重映射、条件注意力校正、去噪阶段自适应窗口调度抑制外推导致的退化。

Result: 在多个连续扩散语言模型上实验，长文本生成中的生成困惑度与文本重复率均得到有效降低。

Conclusion: RangeSWA 作为一种免训练长度外推方案，可有效缓解连续扩散语言模型超训练长度生成时的质量退化，提升长文本生成表现。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models show promising potential in text generation tasks, but are prone to problems such as text repetition, semantic drift, and generation quality degradation when the generation length exceeds the training length. To address these challenges, we propose RangeSWA, a training-free length extrapolation method. The method combines local sliding-window attention, remote block summaries, and dynamic content retrieval to preserve local details and support long-range context access. In addition, generation degradation caused by length extrapolation is mitigated through positional remapping, conditioning attention correction, and denoising-stage-adaptive window scheduling. We conducted experiments on multiple continuous diffusion language models, and the results show that RangeSWA can effectively reduce generative perplexity and text repetition rates in long-text generation.

</details>


### [104] [Aligning MLLMs with the Latent Structure of Human Cognition via Behavior-Derived Semantic Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41951)
*Ning E,Changde Du,Yizhuo Lu,Huiguang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将MLLM与人类相似性判断背后的多维心理表征对齐的框架，通过行为衍生的66维语义空间生成维度依据的语言理由进行训练，提升人机相似性判断一致性并增强与fMRI脑表征的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在多模态推理任务上表现优异，但其内部表征与决策准则是否与人类认知的潜在结构一致尚不明确；作者希望以数据驱动的方式将人类认知结构注入MLLM对齐过程。

Method: 基于由470万次人类Odd-One-Out（O1O）判断构建的行为衍生语义嵌入空间（66个可解释维度）；对每个三元组推断最显著的决策维度，并转化为"维度依据的语言理由（dimension-grounded rationale）"，使MLLM同时学习最终选择与符合推断行为准则的解释。

Result: 模型与人类相似性判断的一致性提升，同时在标准多模态基准上保持有竞争力的性能；在独立fMRI自然图像数据集（NSD）上采用searchlight RSA，观察到模型与人类表征对齐程度提高。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一条数据驱动路径，将人类认知结构纳入MLLM对齐；代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance across a wide range of reasoning tasks, yet it remains unclear whether their internal representations and decision criteria align with the latent structure of human cognition. In this work, we propose a novel framework to align MLLMs with the multidimensional mental representations underlying human similarity judgements.
