We build on a previously established behavior-derived semantic embedding space, in which objects are represented along 66 interpretable dimensions derived from 4.70 million human Odd-One-Out (O1O) judgements. For each triplet, we infer the most salient decision dimension and convert it into a dimension-grounded linguistic rationale. This enables MLLMs to learn both the final choice and a justification consistent with the inferred behavioral criterion. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach improves model consistency with human similarity judgements while retaining competitive performance on standard multimodal benchmarks. Furthermore, using searchlight Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA) on an independent fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD), we observe increased representational alignment between model and human. Ultimately, our findings point to a data-driven route for incorporating human cognitive structure into MLLMs alignment. The code is available \href{https://github.com/ening-open/cogalign}{here}.

</details>


### [105] [Peeling the Matryoshka: Extracting Ontological Structure from Retrieval Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41785)
*Eunchan Kim,Inwhee Joe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文发现MRL嵌入的前缀维度长度与金标准分类学的粗到细粒度对齐，并据此提出PEEL：在冻结嵌入上训练一个序嵌入探针、用最小生成树解码出taxonomic DAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类学（taxonomy）构建对知识密集型NLP至关重要但代价高：聚类方法需逐语料调参、层级不稳定；LLM流水线依赖昂贵的任务特定训练且难以跨域迁移。作者希望找到一种更廉价、可迁移的结构归纳途径。

Method: （1）经验性发现：在Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）嵌入中，每个前导前缀本身即为可用的粗粒度向量，前缀长度与金标准分类学的粗到细粒度对齐；通过打乱维度、逆序维度等控制实验，将该对齐归因于学到的早期前缀维度，并在三种结构不同的MRL模型上验证。（2）PEEL：在冻结嵌入上做极简探针，以序嵌入（order-embedding）目标将层级表示为向量包含关系，再用最小生成树状图（minimum spanning arborescence）解码taxonomic DAG；仅使用概念词表，无需文档。

Result: 在两个OLLM基准上以高结构保真度恢复taxonomic DAG，并可零目标边监督地迁移到新领域；同时将端到端OLLM流水线（联合发现概念并从文档语料归纳结构）作为跨任务参照报告。

Conclusion: 预训练检索嵌入中编码了可解码的粒度梯度，一个极简探针即可将其显式化为父子（is-a）结构，避免了逐语料调参与昂贵任务训练。

Abstract: Taxonomy construction is essential for knowledge-intensive NLP yet remains costly: clustering-based methods (i) need careful per-corpus tuning and yield unstable hierarchies, while LLM-based pipelines (ii) depend on expensive task-specific training that does not transfer across domains. We report an empirical finding that bears on both: in Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) embeddings, where each leading prefix is itself a usable, coarser vector, prefix length aligns with the coarse-to-fine granularity of gold taxonomies; shuffled- and reverse-dimension controls isolate the alignment to the learned early prefix dimensions. The pattern holds across three architecturally distinct MRL models. To test whether this regularity is usable, we introduce PEEL (Peeling Embeddings to Extract Latent ontologies): a minimal probe over these frozen embeddings, trained once with an order-embedding objective that represents hierarchy as vector containment; a minimum spanning arborescence then decodes taxonomic DAGs. Using only a concept vocabulary (no documents), PEEL recovers taxonomic DAGs with high structural fidelity on two OLLM benchmarks, transferring to a new domain with no target-edge supervision. The end-to-end OLLM pipeline, which jointly discovers concepts and induces structure from document corpora, is reported as a cross-task reference. Pre-trained retrieval embeddings encode a decodable granularity gradient; a minimal probe renders it as explicit parent-child (is-a) structure.

</details>


### [106] [Stepwise-Querying: Distilling Reasoning without Access to Teacher Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41769)
*Dicong Yu,Wenhua Wang,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对黑盒教师仅暴露最终答案与简要解法的推理蒸馏场景，提出 Stepwise Querying——通过对教师发起独立查询逐步获取中间推导、有效性检查与纠错，组装成解后用于标准 SFT；并提出 SEIG 诊断指标。在 2 教师×2 学生×4 基准的 16 组对比中 14 组最优；GSM8K 分析表明错误答案轨迹若保留有效中间推理仍可有用，进一步分解收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏通常依赖教师生成的推理监督，但黑盒教师往往只返回最终答案和简短解法，中间计算过程与有效性条件不可得，导致可用于蒸馏的推理监督严重不足；而直接获取教师内部 CoT 轨迹又不可行，因此需要一种无需访问内部轨迹即可"引出"细粒度推理监督的方法。

Method: Stepwise Querying：对同一教师发起多次独立查询，分别针对单步请求中间推导、有效性检查与纠错，从而在无内部 CoT 可见性的前提下拼装出完整且经校验的解，再以标准监督微调训练学生。另提出 Stepwise Explanation Information Gain (SEIG)，以冻结学生在逐步解释与全局解释下完成正确步骤的概率之比的对数作为诊断量，量化逐步监督的信息增益。

Result: 在 2 个教师、2 个学生、4 个数学与代码基准共 16 组对比中，方法在 14 组取得最高分，优于三个基线。GSM8K 上的分析显示：错误答案轨迹在保留有效中间推理时仍具蒸馏价值；而对步骤进一步细化分解带来的额外收益有限。

Conclusion: 黑盒条件下，通过逐步独立查询即可有效回收细粒度推理监督，显著优于常规蒸馏基线；蒸馏价值的关键在于中间推理步骤的有效性而非最终答案正确性，且过度分解步骤并不必要。SEIG 为评估逐步解释质量提供了可行诊断工具。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation is widely used to transfer the reasoning capabilities of large language models to smaller students through teacher-generated supervision. In the black-box setting, however, teachers may expose only final answers and brief solutions, leaving intermediate calculations and validity conditions unavailable and thus limiting the reasoning supervision available for distillation. We propose Stepwise Querying, which elicits step-specific explanations without requiring access to the teacher's internal chain-of-thought (CoT) traces. Independent queries to the same teacher request intermediate derivations, validity checks, and corrections for individual steps, the assembled solutions are then used for standard supervised fine-tuning. Across two teachers, two students, and four mathematics and code benchmarks, our method achieves the highest score in 14 of 16 comparisons with three baselines. We further introduce Stepwise Explanation Information Gain (SEIG), a diagnostic based on the log-ratio of a frozen student's probabilities of correct step completion under stepwise and global explanations. Analyses on GSM8K show that incorrect-answer trajectories can remain useful when they preserve valid intermediate reasoning, while further decomposition yields limited additional gains.

</details>


### [107] [Language Models Show Conformity Bias in RAG: When the Majority Endorses a Poisoned Claim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41012)
*Mehrdad Ghassabi,Audrina Ebrahimi,Zahra Zojaji,Amin Milani Fard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在SciFact的200题上，Gemma-3-12b对"少数投毒"条件检出率25%，对"多数一致投毒"条件检出率降至10%，配对变化显著（McNemar p=6.04e-7），提示RAG误信息检测存在"从众偏差"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG会向模型引入检索文档中的误导性证据，但尚不清楚"相互一致的投毒文档数量占优"是否会削弱模型的误信息识别能力，即是否存在类似从众（conformity）的偏差。

Method: 从SciFact随机抽取200道题（随机种子38），构造两种上下文对比：(A)少数条件——1篇投毒+6篇健康文档；(B)多数条件——6篇相互一致的投毒+1篇健康文档。以Gemma-3-12b为被测模型，统计投毒检出率，并用精确McNemar检验做配对比较。

Result: 少数条件下检出率25%（50/200），多数条件下10%（20/200）。38道结果不一致的题中，34道由"检出"转为"未检出"，仅4道反向变化（精确McNemar检验 p=6.04e-7）。

Conclusion: 结果为RAG误信息检测中的从众偏差提供了初步证据：当相互一致的投毒证据在检索结果中占主导时，Gemma-3-12b显著更不易识别被投毒的陈述。作者自称为初步证据，样本限于单一模型与单一数据集。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can expose language models to misleading evidence in retrieved documents. We investigate whether increasing the number of mutually agreeing poisoned documents affects a model's ability to detect misinformation. We evaluate 200 randomly selected questions from SciFact (random seed 38), comparing contexts with one poisoned and six healthy documents against contexts with six mutually agreeing poisoned documents and one healthy document. For Gemma-3-12b, poison-detection rates decrease from 25% (50/200) in the minority condition to 10% (20/200) in the majority condition. Among the 38 questions with different outcomes across conditions, 34 change from detected to undetected, while only four change in the opposite direction (exact McNemar test, p = 6.04e-7). This pattern is consistent with a conformity-like response to a majority of mutually agreeing poisoned documents. Our results provide initial evidence of conformity bias in RAG-based misinformation detection: Gemma-3-12b is substantially less likely to detect poisoned claims when they dominate the retrieved evidence.

</details>


### [108] [Fewer Heads, Fewer Memories: Pruning Redundant Attention Heads Suppresses Verbatim Memorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40974)
*Aryan Chowdhury,Riza Batista-Navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在推理前将大量冗余注意力头置零，可显著抑制GPT-2-XL的逐字记忆，同时保持甚至改善语言建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会逐字复现训练数据，带来隐私泄露、知识产权暴露与泛化能力受限等问题；理论上注意力头可充当联想记忆机制、记忆容量随头数增长，而实证上大量注意力头存在冗余，但剪除冗余头对记忆复现的影响尚未被研究。

Method: 在推理前将识别出的任务冗余注意力头置零（剪枝），在GPT-2-XL(1.5B)上针对已知被记忆序列评估记忆数量变化，并在用于识别冗余头的基准（WikiText-103困惑度）上评估语言建模性能。

Result: 剪除729个任务冗余注意力头后，记忆数量下降91.5%，同时WikiText-103困惑度改善0.92。

Conclusion: 注意力头冗余可被利用来在推理阶段缓解记忆问题，无需重新训练或修改训练数据。

Abstract: Large language models have been shown to memorise and reproduce verbatim sequences from their training data, raising concerns about privacy leakage, intellectual property exposure, and the limits of generalisation. Recent theoretical work suggests that attention heads may contribute to this behaviour by functioning as associative memory mechanisms, with memorisation capacity scaling with the number of heads. At the same time, empirical work has shown that many attention heads are redundant and can be pruned with limited impact on downstream performance. Despite these parallel findings, the effect of pruning redundant heads on memorised-sequence reproduction remains unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that zeroing out a substantial fraction of attention heads prior to inference can significantly suppress verbatim memorisation while preserving, or even improving, language modelling performance on the benchmark used to identify redundant heads. Evaluating GPT-2-XL(1.5B) on known memorised sequences, we find that pruning 729 task-redundant heads reduces the memorisation count by 91.5%, while improving WikiText-103 perplexity by 0.92. These findings suggest that attention-head redundancy can be exploited to mitigate memorisation at inference time, without retraining or modifying the training data.

</details>


### [109] [CoFiBreak: A Coarse-to-Fine Diversity Reward for Effective Multi-Turn Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40926)
*Jialin Song,Xiaodong Liu,Weiwei Yang,Mingqian Feng,Chengquan Guo,Qiyao Ma,Carolyn Buractaon,Anson Ho,Wuyang Chen,Christopher White*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoFiBreak 为 GRPO 自动化红队引入由粗到细的多样性奖励，在保持高攻击成功率与低推理成本的同时显著提升攻击策略多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以攻击成功为唯一奖励的强化学习会使攻击者 LLM 坍缩到少量提示上，导致安全评测反复测试近重复攻击模式而非真实有害意图；现有方法常以牺牲攻击成功或推理成本换取内容/策略多样性。

Method: 提出 CoFiBreak：面向 GRPO 的粗粒度—细粒度多样性奖励。粗奖励鼓励使用 GRPO 组内欠代表策略以拓宽策略空间；细奖励在从提示嵌入中去除有害意图信息后，惩罚人设、框架与输出格式的重复使用以拓宽策略实现方式。多样性奖励仅作用于已超过有效性阈值的攻击，且两种奖励只在训练阶段计算，故不增加推理开销。

Result: 以 Llama3.1-8B-Instruct 为受害模型训练，在 HarmBench 上对前沿受害模型发现的不同细粒度成功策略数为最强基线的 1.6–2.0 倍；在 AdvBench 上达 98.8% ASR@10，多样性得分为最强攻击基线的 2.1 倍；攻击可迁移，对未见受害模型 Claude-Opus-4.6 达 71.0% ASR@10。

Conclusion: 将粗—细粒度多样性奖励嵌入 GRPO 可在不牺牲攻击有效性与推理效率的前提下缓解攻击模式坍缩，提升安全评测的策略覆盖度与迁移性。

Abstract: Automated red teaming trains an attacker large language model (LLM) to elicit harmful responses from a victim LLM, but reinforcement learning can collapse attackers onto a narrow set of prompts when attack success is the only reward. Such a collapse of attack diversity can bias safety evaluation toward repeatedly testing near-duplicate attack patterns rather than the underlying harmful intents. Existing methods often trade off attack success or inference cost for greater content or strategy diversity. We propose CoFiBreak, a coarse-to-fine diversity reward for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that improves attack diversity while preserving strong attack effectiveness and low inference cost. The coarse reward broadens which strategies the attacker uses by encouraging strategies underrepresented within each GRPO group, while the fine reward broadens how those strategies are realized by discouraging reuse of the same personas, framings, and output formats after harmful-intent information is removed from prompt embeddings. Diversity rewards apply only to attacks that already exceed an effectiveness threshold, so diversity cannot be gained through ineffective attacks. Since both rewards are computed only during training, CoFiBreak also retains inference efficiency. Trained with Llama3.1-8B-Instruct as the victim, CoFiBreak discovers 1.6-2.0x as many distinct successful fine-grained strategies as the strongest baseline across frontier victims on HarmBench. It reaches 98.8% ASR@10 (attack success rate with 10 generations per intent) on AdvBench with the highest diversity score among all compared methods, which is 2.1x that of the strongest-attacking baseline. The resulting attacks transfer to unseen frontier LLMs, reaching 71.0% ASR@10 on AdvBench with the unseen victim Claude-Opus-4.6. The code will be released.

</details>


### [110] [In-Model Context Compression with Agentic Soft Tokens for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41291)
*Xuning He,Kai Fan,Runyu Zhang,Binghuan Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的智能体软Token压缩方法ASTC，利用模型原生循环动态（GDN-attention）判定已吸收信息，将其以软Token前传、精确值保留为硬Token，从而降低计算并复用KV缓存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体的交互上下文不断累积，带来显著计算开销并淹没决策关键证据；现有方法训练外部压缩器或上下文管理智能体将上下文改写为更短的硬Token序列，但外部选择与LLM演化的表示不对齐且必然有损。

Method: ASTC为免训练压缩：借助混合Gated Delta Network（GDN）-attention的模型原生循环动态判断已被吸收的信息，将其作为智能体软Token向前携带，同时把精确值保留为硬Token；所得紧凑序列减少后续层计算并产出可复用的KV缓存，使节省沿智能体轨迹累积。

Result: 长时程任务实验表明，ASTC在准确率持平或超过未压缩智能体的同时，端到端Attention Pairs最多减少50.68%；而训练式压缩器每成功任务所需Attention Pairs为ASTC的15.4至103.7倍。

Conclusion: 智能体软Token是一种模型原生的方式，可随任务表示演化将已吸收上下文向前携带。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents play an important role in solving complex and sustained tasks, with ever-growing interaction contexts. However, these accumulated contexts will raise substantial computational cost and obscure key decision-relevant evidence. Existing methods typically train external compressors or context-management agents to rewrite context as shorter hard-token sequences, but such external selection may not align with the LLM's evolving representation and is necessarily lossy. In this paper, we introduce Agentic Soft-Token Compression (ASTC), a training-free compression method using soft tokens within LLM agent for long-horizon tasks. Specifically, ASTC uses the model's native recurrent dynamics to determine the already absorbed information, leveraging the commonly-used hybrid Gated Delta Network (GDN)-attention. It then carries this information forward as agentic soft tokens while retaining exact values as hard tokens. The resulting compact sequence reduces later-layer computation and yields reusable KV caches, extending the savings across the agent's trajectory. Extensive experiments on long-horizon tasks show that ASTC matches or exceeds the uncompressed agent's accuracy with up to 50.68% fewer end-to-end Attention Pairs, while trained compressors require 15.4 to 103.7 times more Attention Pairs per successful task. These findings highlight agentic soft tokens as a model-native way to carry absorbed context forward as the task representation evolves.

</details>


### [111] [Flide: Accelerating Decision Inference with Exact State Forking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42148)
*Bobo Li,Jiajia Song,Hao Fei,Wynne Hsu,Mong Li Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Flide：面向决策模型（如 Jev）的高效且精确的推理引擎，将决策请求的逻辑树结构与扁平化 token 流中的物理执行解耦，通过 ForkAttention 在一次前向中精确评估所有分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型为 LLM 智能体提供轻量可靠的分类推理，适配 System One 式快速执行，但现有面向生成的推理引擎（vLLM、SGLang）难以支撑此类负载：要么因共享前缀反复重算导致严重开销，要么为复用前缀而扭曲输出概率分布，无法兼顾效率与精确性。

Method: 提出 Flide 引擎：(1) 将决策请求的逻辑树结构与单一扁平 token 流中的物理执行解耦；(2) ForkAttention 借助 GPU 常驻索引在标准注意力和循环 Gated DeltaNet 层中导航请求依赖，保证每个查询分支在精确算术下等价于独立前向；(3) 用 GPU 常驻元数据表达分支拓扑，以少量预录执行图覆盖多样请求混合，并支持扩展中的智能体历史从早期 pass 保存的检查点恢复。

Result: 在公开混合模型与决策模型上的评测显示，Flide 的查询响应速度较 vLLM 和 SGLang 最高快 9.5 倍，同时其总变差距离最接近 float32 参考实现，即在加速的同时保持最高数值保真度。

Conclusion: Flide 将物理执行吞吐与数学精确性统一，为智能体系统中可扩展、低延迟的决策智能提供了有原则的基础；其核心贡献在于用结构化执行取代生成式引擎的前缀重算/近似复用范式。

Abstract: Decision models such as Jev have emerged as a highly promising paradigm for LLM agent systems, providing lightweight and reliable categorical reasoning for rapid System One execution. However, existing generation-oriented inference engines fail to support these workloads effectively, suffering from severe prefix recomputation overheads or distorted output probabilities. To address this challenge, we propose Flide, an efficient and exact inference engine that decouples the logical tree structure of a decision request from its physical execution within a single flattened token stream. Through ForkAttention, Flide navigates request dependencies using GPU-resident indices across both standard attention and recurrent Gated DeltaNet layers, guaranteeing in exact arithmetic that every query branch evaluates identically to an isolated forward pass. By expressing branch topologies directly in GPU-resident metadata, Flide accommodates diverse request mixtures with a compact set of pre-recorded execution graphs while enabling expanding agent histories to resume from checkpoints saved in earlier passes. Extensive evaluations on public hybrid and decision models demonstrate that Flide answers queries up to 9.5 times faster than vLLM and SGLang while staying closest to a float32 reference in total variation distance. By unifying physical execution throughput with mathematical exactness, Flide establishes a principled foundation for scalable, latency-critical decision intelligence in agent systems.

</details>


### [112] [DecisionRank: On-Policy Distillation for Decision-Model Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42128)
*Yanran Wu,Dian Yu,Chongyang Bai,Jieyi Zhang,Rui Liu,Tianyi Chen,Shike Mei,Hang Cui,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Chunwei Liu,Xi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DecisionRank，通过 item-space on-policy distillation 将生成式 LLM 的排序知识迁移到基于打分的决策模型，在 TREC DL、BEIR、BRIGHT 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型通过直接预测选择或分数完成结构化任务，可避免自回归解码延迟；但生成式教师的排序知识以文档 ID 序列的排序列表形式表达，而学生模型仅凭首 token 位置的分数决定排序，二者存在形式鸿沟，知识迁移困难。

Method: 提出 item-space OPD：将学生模型对文档的分数排序成排名序列以构造 rollout；生成式教师以该排名列表为输入，给出条件反馈，直接监督学生分数；并与离线排序监督联合训练。

Result: 在 TREC DL、BEIR、BRIGHT 等通用与推理密集型检索基准上取得 SOTA；item-space OPD 一致优于其他训练目标；1.7B 模型在 TREC DL19–23 上超过 27B 生成式教师；8B 模型在 TREC DL 上快 24.5×、BRIGHT 上快 16.7×，性能优于其生成式 listwise 对应模型。

Conclusion: 以分数排序序列作为桥梁的 item-space 在线策略蒸馏可有效弥合生成式与决策式模型间的表达差异，在更小模型尺寸和更低延迟下实现更强排序性能。

Abstract: Decision models target structured tasks by directly predicting choices or scores, avoiding autoregressive decoding latency. However, transferring a generative LLM's ranking knowledge to a decision model remains challenging: the teacher expresses preferences through a ranked list of document ID sequences, while the student determines the ranking using scores from the first-token position. To bridge this gap, we introduce DecisionRank, a decision model trained through item-space on-policy distillation (OPD). Item-space OPD constructs the student's rollout by sorting its document scores into a ranked sequence. A generative teacher takes the resulting ranking list as inputs and provides conditional feedback to directly supervise those scores. Combined with offline ranking supervision, DecisionRank achieves state-of-the-art performance on both general and reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks such as TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT. Across four benchmarks, item-space OPD consistently outperforms alternative training objectives. Even at 1.7B parameters, DecisionRank surpasses its 27B generative teacher on TREC DL19--23. At 8B, it outperforms its generative listwise counterpart while running $24.5\times$ faster on TREC DL and $16.7\times$ faster on BRIGHT.

</details>


### [113] [Sparse Text Diffusion under Dual Budgets: Context Connectivity and Expert Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41487)
*Alexandros Christoforos,Chadbourne Davis,Paul Raj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将长上下文扩散模型的计算开销形式化为"注意力图边预算"与"每token专家激活预算"，提出DualBudget配置，在摘要、多跳问答、对话、句对任务上分析其性能与成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文扩散模型在token交互与前馈计算上重复付出成本，而稀疏化本身未必能保全文档级信息，需要显式区分两类计算预算并厘清深度与采样迭代的关系。

Method: 把局部、膨胀、全局注意力与条件专家计算统一为双预算视角；定义归一化掩码注意力，区分去噪深度与采样迭代数，推导图结构与计算量上界；给出DualBudget稀疏专家配置并保留全部表格测量。

Result: 长度研究显示在至16K长度上优于所列基线，但随长度增加出现可测量的性能退化；消融实验三项分数变化不一致；成本记录显示相对DiffuSeq降低20%（计时口径未明确）。

Conclusion: 分析将实测观察与训练速度、吸收态、全局连贯性、部署显存等未测量主张明确区分开，强调需谨慎对待未验证结论。

Abstract: Long-context diffusion repeatedly pays for both token interaction and feed-forward computation. We formulate these costs as two separate budgets: the edges available to an attention graph and the experts activated for each token. This view connects local, dilated, and global attention with conditional expert computation without assuming that sparsity alone preserves document-level information. We specify normalized masked attention, distinguish denoising depth from sampling iterations, and derive graph and compute bounds. A reported sparse-expert configuration, denoted DualBudget, is analyzed across scientific summarization, multi-hop question answering, dialogue, and sentence-pair prediction. All supplied table measurements are retained. The length study shows gains over the listed baselines through 16K, together with measurable degradation as length increases. Ablations reveal mixed changes across the three reported scores, while the cost record indicates a 20% reduction relative to DiffuSeq under an unspecified timing convention. The analysis separates these observations from unmeasured claims about training speed, absorbing states, global coherence, and deployment memory.

</details>


### [114] [KV-Cache Patching: Tracing How and Where Fine-Tuned Behaviors Arise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41480)
*Jonas Loos,Oliver Eberle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过将LoRA微调模型与基座模型的KV cache互相替换（patch），该研究表明部分微调行为（如用德语回答、涌现性失准）可由KV cache转移或消除，效应随patch比例增大，并集中于少数关键位置，系统提示与聊天模板token影响尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调会改变模型行为，但该行为的"实例化位置"不清楚：是仅在答案token生成时才出现，还是通过注意力对前序token的处理改变所致。作者希望定位微调行为的产生机制。

Method: 将LoRA微调模型的KV cache patch进未修改的基座模型，反向亦然；考察行为是否随之转移或消失，并分析patch范围大小与逐位置（含系统提示、聊天模板token）的影响。

Result: 对某些行为（德语回答、涌现性失准），KV cache可把行为从微调模型转移到基座模型；反向patch可部分或完全消除微调行为。patch效应随被patch的KV cache比例增大而增强，并集中在少数关键位置，系统提示与聊天模板token影响不成比例地大。

Conclusion: 微调行为并非仅局限于答案token的生成阶段，而是部分地体现在前序token的KV表征中；少数位置（尤其系统提示与聊天模板token）对行为起决定性作用，提示微调的作用机制具有高度稀疏的位置特异性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model changes how it responds, but where is this fine-tuned behavior instantiated? To better understand this, we evaluate whether the change in model behavior arises only during the generation of the answer tokens where the change manifests, or whether altered processing of previous tokens causes it via the attention mechanism. For this, we patch the KV cache from a LoRA fine-tuned model into the unmodified base model, and vice versa. Our results show that for some behaviors, like responding in German or emergent misalignment, patching the KV cache can indeed transfer them from the fine-tuned to the base model. Correspondingly, patching the KV cache from base to fine-tune can partially or fully remove fine-tuned behavior. Furthermore, we find that the effect of patching grows with how much of the KV cache is patched and concentrates at a few pivotal positions, with system-prompt and chat-template tokens having disproportionate influence.

</details>


### [115] [Digital sovereignty in professional translation workflows: a case study of migrating from Windows to Linux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41561)
*Michael Farrell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究以"控制、连续性、退出"三原则结合三项实践要求（原生Linux运行、摆脱强制外部服务依赖、独立访问关键数据），通过CAT/TMS工具生态筛查与译者工作站从Windows迁移至Linux的案例研究，探讨职业翻译中的数字主权问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业译者的工作流日益依赖云平台与供应商控制的基础设施，其数字主权状况缺乏系统考察；论文旨在厘清"Linux可用性"与"运行独立性"的区别，并检验在本地化核心上重建专业翻译工作流的可行性。

Method: 采用文献/文件证据与供应商有限回复相结合的生态筛查，区分Linux可及性与运营独立性；并以单一案例研究实施译者工作站从Windows到Linux的迁移，以LoopCAT、LibreOffice、本地运行的TranslateGemma，配合OCR、通信、凭据管理与备份工具重构工作流，按需保留部分云服务。

Result: 生态筛查表明，基于浏览器的平台虽可在Linux上访问，却仍依赖供应商控制的基础设施；迁移案例证实围绕本地可运行的Linux核心重建单一职业工作流是可行的，且无需完全舍弃云服务。

Conclusion: 数字主权取决于工作流依赖关系的组织方式，而非单个应用或操作系统；它不要求技术隔离，而是对关键组件的实质性控制、工作的连续性以及可行的退出选项。该框架针对本案例定制，推广至其他职业工作流可能需要调整。

Abstract: This study examines digital sovereignty in professional translation through a screening of computer-assisted translation tools and translation management systems, and a case study of migrating a translator's workstation from Windows to Linux. The framework combines three principles - Control, Continuity and Exit - with three practical requirements: native Linux operation, independence from mandatory external services, and independent access to essential data and resources. Drawing on documentary evidence and the limited number of responses received from providers, the ecosystem screening distinguishes Linux accessibility from operational independence: browser-based platforms may remain dependent on provider-controlled infrastructure. The migration case demonstrates the feasibility of reconstructing one professional workflow around a locally operable Linux core, using LoopCAT, LibreOffice and locally run TranslateGemma, alongside tools for optical character recognition, communication, credential management and backup. Selected cloud services remained available where needed. The findings suggest that digital sovereignty depends on how workflow dependencies are organized, rather than on individual applications or operating systems. It does not require technological isolation, but meaningful control over essential components, continuity of work and practical exit options. The framework is tailored to the case examined here and may require adaptation to other professional workflows.

</details>


### [116] [CEFR Level Assessment across Languages and Text Granularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41553)
*Eugene Matsumura,Yuki Arase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将focal loss与监督对比学习结合的统一训练目标，缓解CEFR等级自动评估中的类别不平衡与相邻等级边界模糊问题，在单语/多语的句级与文档级评估上均取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本CEFR等级自动评估是教育NLP的重要任务，但CEFR等级天然分布不均衡、且相邻等级间边界模糊，导致准确预测困难。

Method: 设计统一训练目标：focal loss通过强化对欠代表样本与困难样本的关注来缓解类别不平衡；监督对比学习通过增强类内紧致性与类间可分性学习更具判别性的表示。在单语与多语、句级与文档级CEFR评估设置下评测。

Result: 在所有设置下方法稳定优于强基线，并在多个CEFR评估基准上刷新SOTA。

Conclusion: focal loss与监督对比学习的联合训练可有效应对CEFR评估中的不平衡与边界模糊问题，是一种通用且有效的方案。

Abstract: Automatic assessment of textual CEFR levels is an important task in educational natural language processing. However, accurate CEFR level prediction remains challenging due to the naturally imbalanced distribution of CEFR levels and the ambiguous boundaries between adjacent levels. To address these challenges, we propose a unified training objective that combines focal loss with supervised contrastive learning. Focal loss mitigates class imbalance by emphasizing underrepresented and difficult examples, while supervised contrastive learning learns more discriminative representations by encouraging intra-class compactness and inter-class separation. We evaluate the proposed method on sentence- and document-level CEFR assessment in both monolingual and multilingual settings. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines across all settings, establishing new state-of-the-art performance on multiple CEFR assessment benchmarks.

</details>


### [117] [HyperRepair: Recovering Forgotten General Capabilities of Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41147)
*Junrong Tang,Jianghui Wang,Hao Kong,Yang Lin,Yibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperRepair 提出一种仅需推理开销的框架，通过超网络生成"修复侧网络"(RSN)，在保留领域专精的同时恢复微调后模型的通用能力，且可泛化到未见适配域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调能提升目标领域性能，却会削弱模型的通用能力。现有方法在"通用能力"与"领域专精"之间存在权衡，难以两者兼得。

Method: HyperRepair 从源模型上训练得到的修复中学习一个超网络，使其能泛化到共享预训练骨干的模型。该超网络利用"激活指纹"(activation fingerprints)概括相对预训练骨干的变化，生成定制化的修复侧网络(RSN)，其提示条件门控(prompt-conditioned gate)选择性施加修正。因此无需目标域样本或额外优化，即可在未见适配域模型上实现恢复。

Result: 在 2 个模型家族、12 个 LoRA 适配数据集、15 个通用基准上评测：默认划分下，HyperRepair 达到基座模型平均通用分数的 93.4%，同时保留微调模型平均领域分数的 99.5%。

Conclusion: HyperRepair 以仅推理阶段的开销有效缓解了通用能力与领域专精的权衡，并具备跨模型、跨域的泛化恢复能力。

Abstract: Fine-tuning improves a language model's performance in a target domain but can weaken its general capabilities. Existing methods often face a trade-off between general capabilities and domain specialization, making it difficult to preserve both. We introduce HyperRepair, a framework for recovering general capabilities while retaining domain specialization at only inference cost. To make recovery generalizable across models sharing a pretrained backbone, HyperRepair learns a hypernetwork from repairs trained on source models. The hypernetwork uses activation fingerprints that summarize changes from the pretrained backbone to generate a tailored Repairing Side Net (RSN), whose prompt-conditioned gate applies corrections selectively. This enables recovery on models from unseen adaptation domains without target-domain examples or additional optimization. We evaluate HyperRpair across two model families, 12 LoRA adaptation datasets, and 15 general benchmarks. On the default split, HyperRepair achieves 93.4% of the base models' average general score while retaining 99.5% of the fine-tuned models' average domain score.

</details>


### [118] [Language Models Show a "Women-Are-Wonderful" Effect in Blame Attribution That is Not Just Leniency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41116)
*Anika Sharma,Snehalkumar 'Neil' S. Gaikwad,Snigdha Chaturvedi,Shashank Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文在语言模型的"归责"任务中发现角色依赖的"女性更美好"(WaW)偏置：被评判者为女性时责备概率下降9–52%，而受影响者为女性时责备几率升高2.7–9.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在人际冲突归责中是否存在对女性的有利偏置(WaW效应)，并区分被评判者与受影响者两种角色，以厘清偏置的方向与条件。

Method: 从r/AmItheAsshole收集11,270条人际冲突，构建匹配反事实样本，通过替换原帖发布者(被评判者)与对立方(受影响者)的性别，在四个语言模型上比较判决变化；并按冲突类型分层分析，辅以探索性探查(同情、合理性、伤害、保护等判断)。

Result: 性别仅改变少数判决，但变化方向一致偏向女性；效应强烈依赖角色：原帖者为女性使责备几率降低9–52%，对立方为女性使责备几率提高2.7–9.2倍；对立方WaW在沟通与关系类冲突中增强、家庭冲突中减弱；探针显示对立方性别可影响对同情、合理性、伤害与保护的判断。

Conclusion: WaW并非仅是对被评判女性的宽容，模型在行为涉及女性时也会更严厉地评判该行为，体现为一种角色依赖的双向性别偏置。

Abstract: This paper investigates the Women-Are-Wonderful (WaW) effect in language-model blame attribution: a favorable bias in which women receive more positive evaluations than men. Using 11,270 interpersonal conflicts from r/AmItheAsshole, we construct matched counterfactuals that vary the gender of the person being judged (the original poster) and the person affected (the counter-party). Across four language models, gender changes a minority of verdicts, but those changes consistently favor women. The effect is strongly role-dependent. Making the original poster a woman reduces the odds of blame by 9--52%, while making the counter-party a woman multiplies them by 2.7--9.2X. Counter-party WaW also varies across conflict types, strengthening in communication and relationship conflicts and weakening in family conflicts. Exploratory probes suggest that counter-party gender can shift judgments of sympathy, reasonableness, harm and protection. WaW is therefore not just leniency toward women being judged: models also judge conduct more harshly when it affects a woman.

</details>


### [119] [EDU-Jev Training System-One Decision Models for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41079)
*Jiazheng Li,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EDU-Jev：0.8B/2B/4B单次前向的"System One"教育决策模型，直接输出选项概率分布而无需生成文本，在教育基准上显著超越商用API与开源决策模型，并在单卡上实现高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育中的自动决策多为从固定选项集中选择（评分、达标判定、定位首个错误步骤）。生成式语言模型存在需解析输出、长输出推高推理成本、以及文字表达的置信度未必反映正确概率等问题。

Method: 给定评估上下文与带预定义选项的问题，模型不生成文本而直接输出选项上的概率分布；采用指针头为每个选项打分，把预测约束在允许选项内；训练为单阶段监督，对可用目标标签分布使用软交叉熵，并通过实验评估校准性。

Result: 在12个教育基准上，EDU-Jev 4B平均准确率67.6%，比五个商用决策API中最强者高9.4个百分点，比最佳开源4B决策模型高18.7个百分点；在4个跨域教育集上与最强API持平。2B模型在单张H100上约每秒150次决策。

Conclusion: 面向固定选项的教育决策，专用单次前向模型在精度与效率上均优于通用生成式与商用API方案；但在通用决策与推理任务上仍落后于最强API，说明其优势具有领域与任务形态的针对性。

Abstract: Many automated decisions in education involve choosing from a fixed set of options: assigning a rubric score, checking whether an answer meets a marking criterion, or identifying the first wrong step in a solution. Generative language models are increasingly used to make these decisions, but their responses require parsing, longer outputs increase inference costs, and confidence expressed in words does not necessarily reflect the probability of being correct. We present EDU-Jev, a family of single-pass ("System One") decision models at 0.8B, 2B and 4B parameters. Given an assessment context and a question with predefined answer options, the model returns a probability distribution over those options without generating text. A pointer head scores a marker for each option, ensuring the predictions are restricted to the allowed choices. Training uses a single supervised stage with soft cross-entropy against the available target label distributions; calibration is assessed empirically. Across twelve education benchmarks, EDU-Jev 4B achieves an average accuracy of 67.6%, exceeding the strongest of five commercial decision APIs by 9.4 percentage points and the best open 4B decision model by 18.7 points. On four cross-domain education sets, it matches the strongest API, which remains ahead on general decision-making and reasoning tasks. The 2B model processes about 150 decisions per second on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Response Scoring: An AI-aware Cognitive Supervision Framework for Automated Mathematical Short Answer Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41215)
*Shengran Guo,Min Chi,Feifei Qiao,Varad Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AICG双分支多任务框架，用Q矩阵引导的技能掌握监督增强数学简答题评分，并用Gen-AI检测器加权抑制AI辅助带来的评分偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASAG方法难以理解含符号与公式的复杂数学答案；纯分数监督难以泛化到同一分数的多样作答与技能状态；且忽视生成式AI使用对学生表现评估造成的向上偏置。

Method: 双分支多任务框架：分支一基于RoBERTa回归、序数、分类三头做单题评分；分支二用专家Q矩阵将作答映射到技能，联合预测G-DINA导出的技能掌握度与学习增益，经共享编码器提供认知监督；另设Gen-AI检测器（监督对比预训练+线性分类）估计辅助概率并转为可靠性权重，衰减不可靠认知证据。

Result: 在19个学期、49,402条教师评分作答上，相较RoBERTa回归MAE降低50.3%，QWK由0.798升至0.927；消融显示Q矩阵认知监督为主要增益来源，AI感知加权在无加权认知模型基础上再降MAE 4.2%，并在近四个后Gen-AI学期稳定提升MAE与QWK。

Conclusion: 技能级认知监督显著优于纯分数监督；显式建模Gen-AI辅助并加权可缓解评分上偏，提升ASAG的可靠性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models have achieved remarkable success in automated short answer grading (ASAG). However, current methods in ASAG struggle to comprehend, reason about and evaluate complex mathematical answers involving symbols and formulas. These score-only methods fail to generalize across diverse responses and skill states associated with identical scores. Moreover, they ignore the potential upward bias introduced by generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) usage in assessing students' performance and knowledge mastery. To address these limitations, we propose AICG (AI-aware Cognitive-supervised Grading), a two-branch multi-task framework. The first branch combines RoBERTa-based regression, ordinal, and classification heads for individual-answer grading. The second branch uses an expert-designed Q-matrix to associate responses with assessed skills and jointly predicts G-DINA-derived skill mastery and learning gain. Through the shared encoder, these skill-level targets provide cognitive supervision for answer grading beyond score labels. AICG further uses a Gen-AI detector, trained through supervised contrastive pretraining followed by linear classification, to estimate assistance probabilities and convert them into reliability weights, attenuating unreliable cognitive evidence during learning assessment. We conduct experiments on 49,402 instructor-graded responses spanning 19 academic semesters. Relative to RoBERTa regression, AICG reduces mean absolute error (MAE) by 50.3% and improves quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) from 0.798 to 0.927. Ablations identify Q-matrix-guided cognitive supervision as the primary source of improvement, while AI-aware weighting further reduces overall MAE by 4.2% over the unweighted cognitive model and consistently improves MAE and QWK across the four most recent post-Gen-AI semesters.

</details>


### [121] [Qistas: A Pre-Registered, Portable Protocol for Fairness Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40893)
*Nawar S. Alseelawi,Mustafa S. Aljumaily*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Qistas，一个可跨国家移植、预注册的 LLM 公平性评测协议，首次在伊拉克语境实例化，并发布其编写工具链与种子题库的语义审查结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 公平性基准几乎全部源于英语与美国式社会范畴，翻译版本把不适用的范畴强行移植到其他社会；且多数基准未在运行模型前固定假设，导致事后解释与度量漂移。

Method: 设计成对构造的反事实评测协议，仅测量无需判定争议真理的维度：匹配对上的程序对称性、差异化拒答、歧义下的刻板印象依赖、对已记录事件的术语框架。所有度量按构造配对，采用符号翻转置换检验、精确 McNemar 检验、模板聚类自助置信区间与 Holm 校正族；并做事前功效分析，给出模板聚类设计效应下各轴可检测的最小效应。同时发布编写装置：剪枝后的 3x5 分类法（四格按设计关闭）、分类型检查清单、累积裁定日志、结构审计与人工复核笔记。

Result: 首次实地测试为对 58 条种子题库的语义审查：25 个模板组中 24 个至少未通过一项检查。暴露的缺陷模式包括评测准则泄漏进提示、破坏阿拉伯语一致性的词元级替换、认知上不平衡的对偶、单一主题的拒答探针、以及把未写出的方言孪生条目计为覆盖。

Conclusion: 作者主张上述缺陷是反事实公平性基准的普遍隐患，而非伊拉克特有；本文为协议论文而非结果论文，尚未评测任何模型，随附预注册固定了假设、指标、排除规则与分析计划。

Abstract: Fairness benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) are overwhelmingly English-born and American in their social categories; translated versions carry those categories into societies where they do not apply, and almost none fix their hypotheses before the models are run. We present Qistas (Arabic qistas, "the just balance"), a protocol for evaluating how LLMs treat the social, confessional, and regional constituencies of a society, portable across countries and instantiated first for Iraq. In its pilot form it measures only what needs no verdict on contested truth: procedural symmetry across matched counterfactual pairs, differential refusal, stereotype reliance under ambiguity, and terminological framing of documented events. Every measurement is paired by construction and analysed with paired machinery (sign-flip permutation tests, exact McNemar tests, template-clustered bootstrap intervals, Holm-corrected families), with a power analysis that states, before any data exist, the smallest effect each axis can detect after the design effect of template clustering. We release the authoring apparatus (a pruned 3x5 taxonomy with four cells closed by design, per-type checklists, a cumulative rulings log, a structural audit, and a human review notebook) and report its first field test: a semantic review of a 58-item seed bank in which 24 of 25 template groups failed at least one check. The defect patterns it exposed (evaluation criteria leaking into prompts, token-level swaps that break Arabic agreement, epistemically unbalanced pairs, single-theme refusal probes, unwritten dialect twins counted as coverage) are, we argue, general hazards of counterfactual fairness benchmarks. This is a protocol paper, not a results paper: no model has been evaluated, and the accompanying pre-registration commits the hypotheses, metrics, exclusion rules, and analysis plan that the pilot will follow.

</details>


### [122] [TurnTurk-GPT: Text-Based Turn-Taking Prediction for Turkish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42010)
*Ahmet Tuğrul Bayrak,Fatma Nur Korkmaz,Nur Yağmur Tuncer,Mustafa Sertaç Türkel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TurnTurk-GPT——用TurnGPT目标微调土耳其语GPT-2得到的文本轮次转换预测器，并在真实土耳其语对话上与规则基线、零样本土语模型及多语言结束点检测器对比；结果表明其在录制/说话人分离设置下优于多语言检测器，但仅在测试说话人出现于训练集时优于零样本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土耳其语缺乏专门的文本轮次转换（end-of-turn）预测资源，而流式语音识别输出的无标点、小写文本需要可靠的轮次转换线索；同时需厘清合成数据、说话人泛化与形态学线索对性能的影响。

Method: 以TurnGPT目标微调土耳其语GPT-2，训练数据为合成对话加真实对话；在真实土耳其语对话上做词级判定点评估，输入为无标点小写文本以近似流式ASR输出；采用录制互斥与说话人互斥折、嵌套阈值选择、成对聚类bootstrap检验；并做消融：句尾词后缀移除、仅合成数据训练。

Result: TurnTurk-GPT在录制互斥与说话人互斥两种评估下均优于多语言结束点检测器；仅在测试说话人见于训练集时优于零样本语言模型，未见说话人时二者统计上不可区分。移除句尾词后缀使AP最多下降44%，而句内词仅下降2%，说明土耳其语轮次转换线索位于动词末尾形态。仅用合成对话训练的模型最弱；说话人停顿时零样本与多语言基线接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 土耳其语轮次转换主要编码于动词末尾形态，专用微调模型在跨录制条件下稳健但跨说话人优势有限；合成数据不足以替代真实对话，模型与评估协议已公开。

Abstract: This paper presents TurnTurk-GPT, a text-based turn-taking predictor for Turkish obtained by fine-tuning a Turkish GPT-2 model with the TurnGPT objective on synthetic and real dialogue, and evaluates it on real Turkish conversation against rule baselines, a zero-shot Turkish language model and a widely used multilingual end-of-turn detector. Evaluation uses word-level decision points on unpunctuated, lower-cased text approximating the output of streaming speech recognisers, recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint folds, nested threshold selection and paired cluster bootstrap tests. TurnTurk-GPT outperforms the multilingual detector under both recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint evaluation. It outperforms the zero-shot language model only when the test speakers occur in training; on unseen speakers the two are statistically indistinguishable. Removing the suffixes of the turn-final word reduces AP by up to 44%, against 2% for a sentence-internal word, locating the Turkish end-of-turn cue in verb-final morphology. Synthetic dialogue alone yields the weakest trained model, and when a speaker pauses the zero-shot and multilingual baselines remain close to chance. The model and the evaluation protocol are publicly released.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Agentic World Modeling for Agent Test-time Scaling without Environment Replica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40927)
*Wendi Li,Huseyin A. Inan,Wei-Ning Chen,Yanzhi Chen,Chetan Bansal,Saravan Rajmohan,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic World Modeling (AWM)，将下一观测预测重构为多轮证据获取任务，通过只读接口查询真实环境并用可执行计算构造观测，从而在不可复制的共享环境中实现并行轨迹级测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTS 依赖并行执行多条候选轨迹，但在企业工作区和云服务等共享、持久、成本高且难以复制的环境中该假设失效，动作可能带来不可逆副作用或跨轨迹干扰；而现有生成式世界模型将环境状态压缩进单一提示，在部分可观测下难以可靠模拟状态转移。

Method: AWM 将下一观测预测转化为多轮证据获取：针对每个假设动作，由智能体化模拟器通过安全的只读接口自适应查询真实环境，对返回证据进行推理，并用可执行计算构造结果观测，全程不修改共享状态；随后与轨迹级 TTS 集成，实现单共享环境上的并行探索。

Result: 在 τ-bench、AppWorld 和 OfficeBench 上，AWM 平均比单次模拟基线高 40.07%，比训练过的世界模型基线平均高 12.36%。

Conclusion: 交互式、基于只读证据的模拟可作为共享、不可重置环境中安全且可扩展的智能体测试时扩展的实用基础。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves large language model agents by sampling and selecting among multiple candidate trajectories, but common realizations assume that these trajectories can be executed in parallel. This assumption breaks in realistic deployments, where enterprise workspaces and cloud services are shared and persistent, costly or infeasible to replicate, and actions may introduce irreversible side effects or cross-trajectory interference. Existing generative world models avoid direct execution, but typically compress environment state into a single prompt, limiting their ability to reliably simulate transitions under partial observability. Instead, we introduce Agentic World Modeling (AWM), which reformulates next-observation prediction as a multi-turn evidence-acquisition task. For each hypothetical action, an agentic simulator adaptively queries the real environment through safe read-only interfaces, reasons over the returned evidence, and uses executable computation to construct the resulting observation without mutating shared state. Integrated with trajectory-level TTS, AWM enables parallel exploration over a single shared environment. Across $τ$-bench, AppWorld, and OfficeBench, AWM outperforms a single-shot simulation baseline by 40.07% on average, and surpasses a trained world-model baseline by 12.36% on average. These results establish interactive, read-grounded simulation as a practical foundation for safe and scalable agentic test-time scaling in shared, non-resettable environments.

</details>


### [124] [The Knowledge Layer Model (KLM): Evidence-Visible AI Output, Routing Diagnostics, and Negative Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42066)
*Omer Asim Ozturk,Cihan Aydiner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出知识层模型KLM（开放、实现中立的推理记录规范），SEDIM为参考实现；报告若干内部实验结果，但明确指出不构成端到端验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI答案仅有表面正确性不足，审查者需检查其记录的推理依据；因此需要一个规范化、可版本化的推理记录与证据关系规范。

Method: KLM v0.3规范（版本化认识论评估、证据关系、显式未知项）+ Nage AI OS的SEDIM参考实现；辅以多项分离的内部实验：平台域路由基准、原生模型权重路径双源测试、容量增长对比、词汇与限定范围诊断（AUC）、旧版打包保真性检查、Python/TypeScript验证器一致性检查。

Result: 路由基准137/150（91.3%，Wilson 95%CI 85.7–94.9%）；修正双源测试中原生权重路径0/15成功；容量增长对比因评分器缺陷与原输出缺失无法确立效应；诊断AUC均为0.008（低于注册最低值0.65）；旧版打包118/221丢失含金文本，修复后221/221保留；两语言验证器据称在合成样例的六项检查上一致。

Conclusion: 证据可见性不等于正确性、因果依赖、安全性或合规性；结果为内部、作者自报，历史观察与后续复现需区分；独立复现与完整公开审计包仍属未来工作。

Abstract: AI answers require more than apparent correctness: reviewers need to inspect their recorded basis. We introduce the Knowledge Layer Model (KLM), an open, implementation-neutral specification for inference records with versioned epistemic assessments, evidence relations, and explicit unknowns. SEDIM, the knowledge and evidence architecture of Nage AI OS, is a reference implementation. We reference KLM v0.3 (repository tag v0.3.0). Empirical material consists of separate internal experiments, not end-to-end validation. A historical platform-domain routing benchmark reports 137/150 classifications across 15 classes (91.3%; nominal 95% Wilson interval 85.7-94.9%); the numerator is recovered from a rounded summary, and historical example membership is unverified. A native-model weight-path configuration yielded 0/15 successful answers in a corrected two-source test. A capacity-growth comparison could not establish a reliable effect because of a defective legacy scorer and missing original outputs. Lexical and scoped diagnostics each yielded AUC 0.008 for silent-versus-contradicted examples, below the registered minimum of 0.65. Legacy packing lost gold-containing text in 118/221 selected cases; repaired packing retained it in 221/221. These measure text presence, not answer quality. Python and TypeScript verifiers reportedly agreed on six checks per synthetic fixture; a separate production disclosure was not accepted. Restoration, re-derivation, human-rubric support, boundary isolation, and bounded-response evaluations remain proposed. Findings are internal and author-reported; historical observations are distinguished from later reproductions. Evidence visibility does not establish correctness, causal reliance, security, or regulatory compliance. Independent replication and a complete public audit package remain future work.

</details>


### [125] [The psychophysics of affect in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40985)
*Joseph Marks,Marius C. Vollberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究检验大语言模型（LLM）如何将赌博/股票交易的结果映射为情绪预期，以及是否在决策中使用这些映射。孤立评分时存在边际敏感度递减（类似人类价值函数），但对收益的评分强度高于等额损失，呈现"硅基收益放大"（经典损失厌恶的反转）；联合评分时评分线性化；在决策中损益不对称基本消失，且模型自身评分并不比货币效用更好地解释其选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益介入决策流程，但对其如何将结果映射为情绪、以及是否用该映射进行选择几乎一无所知；已有研究缺乏对LLM"预期情绪—决策"链条的系统检验。

Method: 采用赌博与股票交易情境实验，让LLM对结果的情绪感受进行评分：(1) 孤立评分条件（单独呈现结果），(2) 联合评分条件（同时呈现以支持直接比较）；随后进行选择任务，比较模型自评情绪与货币效用何者更能解释其选择。

Result: 孤立评分呈边际敏感度递减，类似人类价值函数；但收益评分强度高于等额损失，构成对经典损失厌恶的反转（"硅基收益放大"）。联合评分时评分线性化；选择中损益不对称基本消失；模型自身评分对选择的解释力并不优于货币效用。

Conclusion: LLM的结果—情感映射是可塑的、随评估情境而变，且情绪预期未必被一致地纳入决策；这引出关于具身/能动型LLM是否及如何将预期情绪整合进决策的开放问题。

Abstract: Good decisions often require considering how outcomes will feel. Yet large language models (LLMs) increasingly enter decision workflows, despite little being known about how they map outcomes to feelings or use those mappings in choice. We tested how LLMs anticipated gambling or stock-trading outcomes would feel and whether they incorporated these ratings into choice. When outcomes were rated in isolation, ratings exhibited diminishing sensitivity to magnitude, resembling human value functions; but models rated gains more intensely than equivalent losses, an inversion of canonical loss aversion we call silicon gain amplification. When outcomes were rated together, enabling direct comparison, ratings linearised. In choice, the gain-loss asymmetry largely disappeared, and models' own ratings did not reliably explain their choices better than monetary utility did. These findings reveal malleable mappings from outcomes to affect, raising questions about whether and how agentic LLMs should incorporate anticipated feelings into decisions.

</details>


### [126] [Incongruence-Aware Evaluation of Multimodal Speech Emotion Recognition in Audio Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40883)
*Fengji Jing,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究语音交互大模型中"文本所说"与"音频怎么说"两通道不一致是否会降低语音情感识别性能，提出将文本与音频映射到共享VAD空间并用二分量高斯混合模型构造连续、无阈值的不一致分数，发现音频在场时不一致性在所有语料上显著提高出错几率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音交互大模型同时处理语义内容（what）与韵律/声学表达（how），但两通道并不总是一致。作者追问这种跨模态分歧是否会实际损害语音情感识别，以及问题究竟出在信号本身还是模型融合原始证据的方式。

Method: 将文本与音频表示映射到共享的valence-arousal-dominance (VAD) 空间，以二者距离结合二分量高斯混合模型转化为连续、免阈值的不一致分数（incongruence score），并用人类判断进行效度验证；在eNTERFACE'05（表演式）、MELD（对话式）、CMU-MOSEI（自发式）三个语料上评估三个具备音频能力的模型；控制真实情感标签与特征空间距离后，用优势比检验不一致性对错误的影响；并与同特征上训练的有监督分类器、仅文本效应、去掉情感词典重建的分数以及六种更简单的替代分数做对比。

Result: 在模型跨模态保持一致的前提下，只要音频在场，不一致性在全部三个语料中都提高出错几率，且在控制真实情感与特征距离后依然成立。相同特征上的有监督分类器表现相反，说明困难源于模型融合原始证据的方式而非信号本身。仅文本效应不可靠：仅更换读取转写的模型即可使效应符号在某语料上反转。去掉情感词典重建的分数复现了核心结果，且在六种更简单替代方案中，它是唯一能在三个语料上均预测错误的免标签分数。

Conclusion: 跨模态不一致性是可量化且稳定的错误预测因子，其负面效应是语音交互大模型融合机制所特有，而非输入信号固有；该免标签分数具有跨语料泛化性与鲁棒性，作者开源了代码、数据表与提示词。

Abstract: Voice-interactive large language models process both what a speaker says and how they say it, yet the two channels do not always agree. We ask whether that disagreement degrades speech emotion recognition. Mapping text and audio into a shared valence-arousal-dominance space, we convert the distance between them into a continuous, threshold-free incongruence score with a two-component Gaussian mixture, and validate the score against human judgement. We evaluate three audiocapable models on acted, conversational and spontaneous speech (eNTERFACE'05, MELD and CMU-MOSEI). With the model held constant across modalities, incongruence raises the odds of error whenever audio is present, in every corpus, after controlling for the ground-truth emotion and for distance in the feature space. Supervised classifiers trained on the same features behave in the opposite way, which suggests that the difficulty lies in how these models combine raw evidence rather than in the signal itself. Text-only effects are unreliable: swapping only the model that reads the transcript reverses their sign on one corpus. Rebuilding the score without the emotion lexicon reproduces the central result, and among six simpler alternatives it is the only label-free score that predicts error in all three corpora. Code, data tables and prompts are released.

</details>


### [127] [TRIDENT and SHAKTI: A Framework and Benchmark for Hindi AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41232)
*Shifali Agrahari,Sujit Kumar,Sanasam Ranbir Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出印地语AI生成文本检测基准SHAKTI与基于往返翻译的检测框架TRIDENT，其中TRIDENT-S整体表现最佳，并在多语言与对抗设置下验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多语言能力增强使区分人类写作与AI生成文本对信息完整性至关重要，但现有研究以英语为中心，缺乏印地语等低资源语言的基准。

Method: 构建SHAKTI基准（4种文体、8个LLM家族、多种提示策略、对抗变换），经人工标注评估质量，并分析人机文本在语言、语法与情感上的差异；据此提出TRIDENT往返翻译检测框架，含静态阈值（-S）、动态阈值（-D）与多距离RTT特征逻辑回归（-C）三个变体，利用原文与往返译文间的语义一致性判别AI生成内容。

Result: TRIDENT优于传统与监督基线，TRIDENT-S取得最佳整体性能；在七种语言、对抗设置及未见LLM上验证了多语言适用性与鲁棒性，同时揭示对翻译模型的敏感性。

Conclusion: SHAKTI填补了印地语检测基准空白，TRIDENT以语义一致性为核心提供了有效、可迁移的零样本式检测方案，但性能依赖翻译模型质量。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, distinguishing human-written from AI-generated text is essential for maintaining information integrity. However, research remains English-centric, with limited benchmarks for low-resource languages such as Hindi. To address this gap, we introduce SHAKTI, a large-scale Hindi benchmark covering four genres, eight LLM families, multiple prompting strategies, and adversarial transformations. We evaluate its quality through human annotation and analyze linguistic, grammatical, and sentiment-based differences between human- and AI-generated texts. Building on these findings, we propose TRIDENT, a round-trip translation-based detection framework with three variants: TRIDENT-S (static threshold), TRIDENT-D (dynamic threshold), and TRIDENT-C (logistic regression using multi-distance RTT features). TRIDENT leverages semantic consistency between original and round-trip translated texts to identify AI-generated content. Extensive experiments show that TRIDENT outperforms conventional and supervised baselines, with TRIDENT-S achieving the best overall performance. Further evaluations across seven languages, adversarial settings, and unseen LLMs demonstrate its multilingual applicability and robustness, while highlighting sensitivity to the translation model.

</details>


### [128] [Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41040)
*Shikha Shrestha,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CGAP将工具目录确定性编译为PDDL抽象模型，用LLM把请求转为目标，再由经典规划器求解，执行中由LLM补充实体级调用并用确定性检查与LLM验证器把关，在四个基准上比标准工具调用智能体最高提升28.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体处理自然语言请求时常需多次相互依赖的调用，但通常依靠试错选择调用，无法保证得到可执行的计划。

Method: CGAP改为推理"请求需要什么"与"工具提供什么"：1）将工具目录确定性编译为PDDL模型；2）LLM把请求转换为目标；3）经典规划器求解该模型。因工具输出事先未知，模型为抽象模型，仅追踪哪些输入可用而非其具体值。执行阶段，LLM补充抽象计划省略的实体特定调用，确定性检查拒绝任何破坏计划的调用。所有参数均来自可引用来源（对话片段或先前工具响应），每个请求结果须经LLM验证器核对请求后才算交付。

Result: 在ToolSandbox、ComplexFuncBench、BFCL多轮与τ²-bench零售四个基准上，使用三个不同LLM，CGAP相较标准工具调用智能体最高提升28.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 将规划建立在工具能力之上、以符号规划加LLM执行补充的混合架构，可显著提升多步工具调用任务的可执行性与成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents serve natural-language requests through tools, data sources, and other agents, and even simple requests often need several interdependent calls. Typically, the LLM picks these calls by trial and error, with no guarantee of an executable plan. We present Capability-Grounded Agentic Planning (CGAP), which instead reasons about what a request requires and what tools provide. CGAP compiles the tool catalog deterministically into a PDDL model, uses an LLM to turn the request into goals, and solves the model with a classical planner. Since tool outputs are unknown in advance, the model is abstract, tracking which inputs are available, not their values. During execution, an LLM adds entity-specific calls that abstract plans omit, and deterministic checks reject any that break the plan. Every argument is drawn from a cited source, such as a conversation span or an earlier tool response, and each requested result counts as delivered only after an LLM verifier checks it against the request. With three LLMs on ToolSandbox, ComplexFuncBench, BFCL multi-turn, and $τ^2$-bench retail, CGAP outperforms a standard tool-calling agent by up to 28.9 points.

</details>


### [129] [TSBPO: Tree-Structured Branching for Policy Optimization in Interactive Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41697)
*Rui Yu,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出树结构采样到更新的训练范式TSBPO，统一解决交互式语言智能体从终端结果学习中的采样、延迟反馈分配与多token动作损失聚合三个耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从终端结果训练交互式语言智能体存在三个耦合难题：应采样哪些任务与状态延续、如何将延迟反馈归因到具体决策、如何对多token动作聚合token级损失。现有方法未在一个训练循环内统一处理。

Method: TSBPO：1）似然拟合的Root/Branch分配器，利用保留的任务与状态证据，在固定rollout预算下目标化采样易产生对比性终端结果的分支；2）在重放的物理树上，轨迹优势比较相邻兄弟片段，观测组步优势在匹配的任务/观测状态下比较结果；3）actor对完整响应的截断token代理求和并在活跃轮次间归一化，避免策略梯度项按响应长度平均。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B：ALFWorld上已见97.79%、未见96.64%随机成功率；WebShop在500个测试目标各6个随机回合下逐回合成功率81.07%。WebShop步项消融在update-100为75.93%；ALFWorld聚合对比显示完整轮次加权带来更高命令有效率。

Conclusion: 结果在两个交互式基准上支持所评估的训练配置，验证了TSBPO在采样、优势归因与损失聚合上的整合有效性。

Abstract: Training interactive language agents from terminal outcomes poses three coupled problems: which task and state continuations to sample, how to assign delayed feedback to decisions, and how to aggregate token losses for multi-token actions. We present TSBPO, a tree-structured sampling-to-update paradigm that addresses these problems within one training loop. A likelihood-fitted Root/Branch allocator uses retained task and state evidence to target continuations likely to produce contrasting terminal outcomes under fixed rollout budgets. On the resulting replayed physical trees, trajectory advantages compare immediate sibling segments, while observation-group step advantages compare outcomes at matched task/observation states. The actor then sums clipped token surrogates over each complete response and normalizes across active turns, avoiding response-length averaging in the policy-gradient term. With a Qwen2.5-1.5B actor, fixed checkpoints obtain 97.79% seen and 96.64% unseen stochastic success on ALFWorld and an 81.07% per-episode success rate over six stochastic episodes for each of the 500 WebShop test goals. A WebShop step-term ablation yields 75.93% at the same update-100 evaluation; an ALFWorld aggregation comparison shows higher observed command validity with complete-turn weighting. These results support the evaluated training configuration on two interactive benchmarks.

</details>


### [130] [DAMI: Dual-Axis Memory Initialization for Enhanced Long-Context Retrieval in Gated Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41994)
*Zhangchi Feng,Harry Jiang,Yangyifei Luo,Ray Zhang,Richong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI)，通过在训练前联合配置门控参数A_log与dt_bias，为线性注意力/门控线性循环模型设定跨头的几何时间尺度与跨层的低-高-低输入敏感度剖面，无需新增可训练参数即可显著提升长上下文检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力对序列长度线性扩展，但其有限状态记忆限制了长上下文检索能力。在门控线性循环中，记忆保留取决于状态衰减，而衰减由门参数A_log、dt_bias及输入激活共同决定。受控Gated DeltaNet实验显示，这两个参数训练后仍与初始值高度相关，说明初始化至关重要。

Method: DAMI在训练前联合配置A_log与dt_bias：在头维度上分配几何级数的时间尺度以覆盖不同检索距离，在层维度上设定"低-高-低"的输入敏感度剖面；再用少量激活样本校准GDN族的具体实现，使其匹配上述两个行为目标，且不引入额外可训练参数。

Result: 在GDN、KDA、GDN2、Mamba-2、RWKV-7上，DAMI相较对应随机初始化基线，将RULER类检索平均准确率提升5.29至16.71个百分点；在语言建模、常识推理及2K真实场景检索上性能总体与基线相当。

Conclusion: 状态衰减参数的初始化是门控线性循环长上下文能力的关键杠杆；DAMI以零额外参数代价显著改善检索，同时不损害通用建模性能，为线性注意力模型的长上下文优化提供了简单有效的初始化方案。

Abstract: Linear attention scales linearly with sequence length, but its finite-state memory can limit long-context retrieval. In gated linear recurrences, memory retention depends on state decay, which is shaped jointly by gate parameters A_log and dt_bias and by input activations. In controlled Gated DeltaNet (GDN) experiments, both parameters remain highly correlated with their initial values after training, highlighting the importance of initialization. Therefore, we introduce Dual-Axis Memory Initialization (DAMI), which jointly configures these parameters before training. DAMI assigns geometric timescales across heads for varied retrieval distances and a low-high-low input-sensitivity profile across layers. A small activation sample calibrates the GDN-family implementation to match both behavioral targets without adding trainable parameters. Across GDN, KDA, GDN2, Mamba-2, and RWKV-7, DAMI improves average RULER-style retrieval accuracy by 5.29 to 16.71 percentage points over the corresponding random-initialization baselines. Performance on language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and 2K real-world retrieval remains broadly comparable to those baselines. Our code is available at https://speedai.com/research/dami.

</details>


### [131] [SERIAL-7: Deriving, Realizing, and Preserving Machine-Verifiable Outputs Across Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41308)
*Youngkyung Seo,MiHyeon Kim,Jisoo Lee,Hwijung Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SERIAL-7，一个基于可执行验证框架的韩英双语基准，涵盖七种表示形式的机器可验证生成，用 177 个规范样本与 43 条原子表示约束检验语义推导、受约束工件生成与跨表示保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型越来越多地生成被程序和下游系统消费的结构化输出，此时语义正确并不等于可可靠执行，缺乏对"工件是否可被机器验证、能否跨表示保持"的系统评测手段。

Method: 构建韩英双语基准：177 个经验证的规范工件、7 种表示形式、43 条原子表示约束；将任务解耦为 Derive（语义推导）、Realize（受约束工件生成）、Preserve（跨表示保持）；约束与格式、结构、任务特定语义的可执行检查配对，形成从表示合规到完整工件有效性的分级严格验证（如 REALIZE-L3 HSR、Full Compliance 指标）。

Result: 在十个模型上，REALIZE-L3 HSR 区间为 42.0%–93.9%；Full Compliance 指标进一步暴露出超出"最终答案正确 + 表示合法"之外的工件级失败。

Conclusion: 可靠的结构化生成需要针对工件如何被实现、如何在表示间被保持进行显式、可执行的验证，仅靠语义正确性评估不足。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate structured outputs consumed by programs and downstream systems, where semantic correctness alone does not guarantee reliable execution. We introduce SERIAL-7, a Korean-English benchmark built around an executable verification framework for machine-verifiable generation across seven representations. Using 177 validated canonical artifacts, SERIAL-7 separates semantic derivation (Derive), constrained artifact generation (Realize), and cross-representation preservation (Preserve). Its 43 atomic representation constraints are paired with executable checks over format, structure, and task-specific semantics, enabling progressively stricter verification from representation compliance to full artifact validity. Across ten models, REALIZE-L3 HSR ranges from 42.0% to 93.9%, while Full Compliance exposes additional artifact-level failures beyond correct final answers and valid representations. These results show that reliable structured generation requires explicit, executable verification of how artifacts are realized and preserved across representations.

</details>


### [132] [Nedimo: An Evidence-Grounded Multilingual Pipeline for AI-Generated Sleep Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40970)
*Georgios Makridis,Stefanos Kampouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多语言"低唤醒助眠故事"生成与朗读流水线，将不确定的临床证据转化为保守工程需求，并给出可追溯的护栏式架构、验收阈值与前瞻性评测协议，明确不宣称临床疗效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式音频能批量生产个性化睡前内容，但常规媒体目标（留存、完播、新颖性）与"助眠"目标根本错位；且成人叙事型睡前音频的直接证据混杂，一项近期主动对照试验未支持其作为独立的睡眠障碍疗法。因此需要把这种不确定证据转化为保守的工程约束，避免过度承诺。

Method: 原型采用"确定性规划 + 大模型生成"分离的架构：显式表示内容与朗读参数；以四阶段注意力递减（attention taper）塑造脚本；校验结构与词汇约束；执行有界的错误感知重试，并隔离未解决输出。语音侧使用供应商无关的TTS，随后进行段落感知拼接、两遍响度归一化，以及出版门控（对已保存的编码文件重新测量响度、响度范围与真峰值并对照固定阈值）。产品数据模型记录停止位置、重播、提前放弃与完播，并将其视为模糊的行为代理指标而非临床结局。

Result: 给出了四项产出：证据—需求映射、带显式验收阈值的可追溯护栏架构、技术评测标准，以及检验入睡效应的前瞻性协议。文中未报告用户或临床实验结果，原型为已实现的工程系统。

Conclusion: 系统被定位为放松与注意支持工具，不宣称临床有效性；其收益及可能产生的依赖需通过实证评估。

Abstract: Generative audio systems can produce large libraries of personalized bedtime content, but ordinary media objectives (retention, completion, and novelty) are poorly aligned with the goal of supporting a transition toward sleep. Direct evidence for narrated bedtime audio in adults is mixed, and a recent active-controlled trial did not support such audio as a standalone treatment for sleep disturbance. We translate this uncertain evidence into conservative engineering requirements and present a multilingual pipeline for generating and narrating low-arousal sleep stories. The implemented prototype separates deterministic planning from large language model generation, represents content and delivery parameters explicitly, shapes each script through a four-phase attention taper, validates structural and lexical constraints, performs a bounded error-aware retry, and quarantines unresolved outputs. Provider-agnostic text-to-speech synthesis is followed by paragraph-aware joining, two-pass loudness normalization, and a publication gate that re-measures the saved, encoded file against fixed loudness, loudness-range, and true-peak limits. The product data model records stop position, replay, early abandonment, and completion while treating these events as ambiguous behavioral proxies rather than clinical outcomes. We contribute an evidence-to-requirement mapping, a traceable guardrail architecture with explicit acceptance thresholds, technical evaluation criteria, and a prospective protocol for testing sleep-onset effects. No clinical efficacy is claimed; the system is positioned as a relaxation and attentional-support tool whose benefit and possible reliance must be evaluated empirically.

</details>


### [133] [SciGraphSelect: Auditable Scientific Data Selection through Knowledge--Reasoning Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41280)
*MiHyeon Kim,Ji-Eun Han,Eunkyeong Lee,Jeehyun Lim,Hwijung Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SciGraphSelect——一个参考依据驱动（reference-grounded）的、可审计的科学数据选择框架。它将领域知识、可复用推理操作与文献证实的关系组织为带类型知识–推理图，并基于概念覆盖、关系与路径共支持、方向对齐对候选文档打分；只在参考侧保留丰富关系结构，对候选侧证据保守处理，从而为科学训练语料的构建提供可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学数据选择通常依赖相关性或效用分数，只能给出"选/不选"的结果，难以解释某文档为何被保留，缺乏可审计性与依据溯源。

Method: 构建带类型的知识–推理图（typed knowledge–reasoning graph），整合领域知识、可复用推理操作与有文献佐证的关系；以概念覆盖、关系与路径的共支持、方向对齐三类信号对候选文档评分；设计上在参考侧保留丰富关系结构，而候选侧不假设关系蕴含、保守使用文档嵌入证据；输出每次选择背后的参考依据。

Result: 摘要未报告定量结果（如具体基准与指标），仅指出方法能够揭示每个选择所依据的参考证据，从而提升选择的透明度。

Conclusion: SciGraphSelect为科学训练语料的上游构建引入可追溯性，并对下游科学验证形成互补；核心贡献在于以参考依据驱动、可审计的方式替代单纯的相关性/效用打分。

Abstract: Scientific data selection is typically driven by relevance or utility scores, providing limited insight into why a document is retained. We introduce SciGraphSelect, a reference-grounded framework for auditable scientific data selection. SciGraphSelect organizes domain-specific knowledge, reusable reasoning operations, and reference-attested relations into a typed knowledge--reasoning graph, then scores candidate documents using concept coverage, relation and path co-support, and directional alignment. To avoid over-interpreting document embeddings, rich relational structure is preserved on the reference side while candidate-side evidence is treated conservatively without assuming relation entailment. By exposing the reference-grounded evidence behind each selection, SciGraphSelect brings traceability to the upstream construction of scientific training corpora and complements downstream scientific verification.

</details>


### [134] [ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116)
*Yuzhe Liu,Haolin Tian,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACCT，实现同一模型族内不同规模模型间的KV cache稀疏迁移，使上下文计算可跨模型复用，在KV预算缩减75%时保持或超过目标模型原生性能，转换速度比目标prefill快6.5–21.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LLM部署中不同模型动态被调用，同一上下文被重复处理，导致重复的上下文预填充计算开销。已有KV复用多局限于同模型或同规模，跨规模模型间缺乏可行的KV迁移方案。

Method: ACCT由三个轻量组件构成：Cartographer为每个目标层与KV头识别与query相关的上下文token；Allocator在共享总KV预算下分配每个目标层与KV头保留的token数量；Compiler将源模型KV转换为稀疏的目标模型KV。据此目标模型可直接使用迁移KV推理，无需重处理原始上下文，从而将上下文计算与下游推理解耦。

Result: 在5个通用能力基准与6个强上下文依赖任务两类设置上评测：KV cache缩减75%时性能仍匹配或超过目标模型原始推理；约19K上下文长度下KV转换相对目标上下文prefill取得6.5–21.2倍加速。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在有限KV预算下跨模型迁移上下文计算的可行性，为异构模型部署中的上下文复用提供了实用路径。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly evolving from single-model invocation toward heterogeneous deployments in which different models are dynamically invoked for inference. When the same context is reused across models, however, each newly selected target model typically has to process it again, repeatedly incurring substantial computational costs. Therefore, we propose ACCT (Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer), which targets KV cache transfer between models of different scales within the same model family. ACCT transforms the KV cache produced by a source model into sparse KV states that can be directly consumed by a target model, thereby enabling cross-model reuse of context computation and decoupling context computation from downstream inference. ACCT consists of three lightweight components: the Cartographer identifies query-relevant context tokens for each target layer and KV head; the Allocator determines how many context tokens each target layer and KV head retains under a shared total KV budget; and the Compiler transforms selected source KV into sparse target KV. The target model can therefore perform inference using the transferred KV without reprocessing the original context. We evaluate ACCT in two complementary settings comprising five general-capability benchmarks and six highly context-dependent tasks. Experiments show that ACCT matches or exceeds the target model's original inference performance even when reducing the KV cache size by 75%. At a context length of approximately 19K tokens, ACCT's KV conversion achieves a 6.5-21.2 times speedup over target context prefill. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transferring context computation across models under a limited KV budget.

</details>


### [135] [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039)
*Artin Yousefi,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Lorien C Abroms,David A Broniatowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过2,880次查询审计六款主流LLM在戒烟咨询中的"人物画像"差异，发现青少年画像更少获得FDA批准戒烟药物信息（低32–67个百分点），并提出"事实下限"系统提示作为干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿用户向LLM聊天机器人寻求健康建议，但"不同用户是否得到相同建议"这一公平性问题缺乏系统检验，尤其在高风险、有明确临床指南的戒烟领域。

Method: 对来自4家厂商的6款已部署LLM，用4个戒烟问题交叉12个在人生阶段、自述专业程度、吸烟史上不同的人物画像，共2,880条回复进行审计；随后评估一种可部署干预——短系统提示规定"事实下限"（用户应获得的核心医学事实集合），同时允许语气、词汇与框架自适应。

Result: 模型输出显著依赖提问者画像：所有模型对青少年画像提及FDA批准处方戒烟药物的频率比成人低32–67个百分点；6款模型中有5款对"呈现医疗专业性"的画像给出更少事实。该提示在所有模型中缩小了画像间差异，但对整体事实覆盖率的影响在幅度和方向上因模型而异。

Conclusion: LLM健康建议存在系统性人群差异；基于单系统验证的提示不可假定可迁移至其他系统。需注意两点：相等提及率并非总是正确临床目标（处方戒烟药物不推荐用于未成年人，事实下限应按年龄调整而非统一），且因画像多属性同时变化，结果应视为场景层面差异而非年龄或专业性的独立效应。作者公开全部2,880条回复与分析代码。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>


### [136] [TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004)
*Xiangyu Kong,Ruirui Yang,Xia Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多国贸易谈判仿真基准 TradeWar，将对话转为关税/贸易份额政策并结算执行，用经济健康指数(EHI)评价；并提 TACTIC 智能体（零样本CoT+跨回合经验/对手行为/结算结果自迭代，不更新基座权重），在长程多回合谈判中显著优于 PromptLLM 与 MemoryLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多回合谈判基准主要只测"达成一致率"，忽略谈判承诺在执行后是否真正创造价值（执行落地结果与延迟反馈未被评估），导致对话质量与执行效益脱节。

Method: 构建 TradeWar 多国仿真基准：将自然语言谈判对话映射为具体关税与贸易份额政策，结算时施加产能与网络约束，并以 EHI 统一度量结果。提出 TACTIC 智能体：采用零样本 CoT 提示，跨回合迭代优化谈判策略，融合历史经验、对手行为建模（心智理论）与既往结算结果，且不更新基座 LLM 权重。对比 PromptLLM、MemoryLLM 及去掉跨回合更新的消融版本，并测试技术管制与能源危机冲击下的鲁棒性。

Result: PromptLLM 达成率 75% 但最终 EHI 仅 57.1(×100)；TACTIC 达成率 68.8、以更低一致性换取更高执行价值。20 个连续回合、3 个随机种子下，TACTIC 最终 EHI 由 58.2 升至 73.2，无跨回合更新的消融为 65.2。技术管制与能源危机冲击下，TACTIC 性能下降幅度小于 PromptLLM 与 MemoryLLM。

Conclusion: 仅靠 CoT 推理与交互记忆无法解决延迟执行反馈问题；以结算结果为依据的自适应（settlement-grounded adaptation）能提升 TradeWar 中的长程多回合谈判表现，说明执行落地反馈对智能体谈判能力至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-turn negotiation remains a challenging problem for large language model (LLM) agents. While LLMs can conduct natural-language dialogue, existing benchmarks predominantly assess only agreement rates, overlooking whether negotiated commitments actually create value once executed. To address this gap, we introduce TradeWar, a multi-country simulation benchmark that translates negotiation dialogues into concrete tariff and trade-share policies, enforces capacity and network constraints during settlement, and evaluates outcomes via an Economic Health Index (EHI). To solve this execution-grounded task, we propose TACTIC (Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation and Theory-of-mind for International Commerce), an agent that iteratively refines its negotiation strategy across episodes-without updating base LLM weights-by leveraging prior experience, opponent behavior, and past settlement outcomes. Despite using zero-shot chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, PromptLLM attains a 75% agreement rate but only 57.1 final EHI (*100), whereas TACTIC reaches 68.8. Across 20 sequential episodes and three seeds, TACTIC's final EHI improves from 58.2 to 73.2, compared with 65.2 for an ablation without cross-episode updates. Under technology-controls and energy-crisis shocks, TACTIC also exhibits smaller performance degradation than PromptLLM and MemoryLLM. These results indicate that CoT deliberation and interaction memory alone do not fully address delayed execution feedback, whereas settlement-grounded adaptation improves long-horizon negotiation in TradeWar.

</details>


### [137] [Can Personal Requirements Become Supervision for Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40992)
*Junwei Wu,Runze Yan,Yuzhang Xie,Hanqi Luo,Ziwei Dong,Jiaying Lu,Xiao Hu,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Noura：将可执行个人规范（由公开临床指南与个体健康/饮食档案实例化）与LLM规划耦合，并用执行计划及其上下文修订作监督信号训练紧凑模型，使Qwen3-14B完整方案通过率从24.0%提升至72.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多慢病饮食管理需跨餐次、跨天数同时满足疾病与药物相关约束，并保留既有饮食习惯。这些要求在指南与药品说明书中是公开的，但LLM智能体难以将其转化为针对单个人的可行计划；在基于NHANES构建、经营养师认可的周计划评测中，13个模型（含医学微调的8B模型到GPT-6.1 Sol）直接生成的完整方案通过率均不足3%，显式规则、更高推理预算或模型自主调用工具也仅使GPT-6.1 Sol达到不高于8%。

Method: 1）从公开临床指南与个体健康、饮食档案实例化可执行的个人规范；2）规划阶段由规范完成食物落地、份量优化、多日整体方案校验与修订引导；3）核心思路：拒绝方案的同一条规范亦可决定“从中学什么”——把已执行计划与经上下文归因的修订转化为紧凑模型的监督数据，即使轨迹从未完全成功也能回收目标；4）同一规范提供SFT目标、偏好与强化学习奖励，用于后训练。

Result: 在4,993名留出参与者上，Noura-SFT将Qwen3-14B在同一执行回路内的完整方案通过率由24.0%提升至72.5%，规则违反减少97.5%，未观察到关键性失败，且饮食质量提升；本地部署的14B规划器规则违反数少于其GPT-6 Luna教师；匹配对照显示执行得到的目标相比模型自撰提案使规则违反减少41%；在Qwen、Phi、Llama四类骨干上，仅自训练即在1,249人子集上使规则违反减少41–61%。

Conclusion: 将公开临床规范编译为可执行、可校验的个人规范，并让规范同时充当拒绝器与监督信号来源，是让语言模型智能体生成可行多慢病饮食计划的有效路径；小规模本地模型可借此在合规性上超越更大教师模型，具备现实部署价值。

Abstract: Managing multiple chronic conditions through diet requires satisfying disease- and medication-specific requirements jointly across meals and days while preserving established eating habits. These requirements are public in clinical guidelines and drug labels, yet turning them into a feasible plan for one person remains difficult for language-model agents. In our dietitian-approved weekly planning evaluation built from NHANES, thirteen models, from medically fine-tuned 8B models to GPT-6.1 Sol, pass fewer than 3% of complete plans through direct generation. Explicit rules, higher reasoning effort, or model-directed tools leave GPT-6.1 Sol at no more than 8%. We present Noura, which pairs LLM planning with executable personal specifications instantiated from public clinical guidance and individual health and dietary profiles. During planning, the specification grounds foods, optimizes portions, checks the complete multi-day plan, and guides revision. Our key insight is that a specification that rejects a plan can also determine what to learn from it. Noura turns executed plans and contextually credited revisions into supervision for compact models, recovering targets even from trajectories that never achieve complete success. The same specification supplies supervised targets, preferences, and rewards for post-training. On 4,993 held-out participants, Noura-SFT raises Qwen3-14B's complete-plan pass rate from 24.0% to 72.5% within the same execution loop, cuts rule violations by 97.5% with no observed critical failures, and improves dietary quality. The locally hosted 14B planner incurs fewer rule violations than its GPT-6 Luna teacher. A matched comparison shows that executed targets reduce rule violations by 41% over model-written proposals. Across four Qwen, Phi, and Llama backbones, self-training alone reduces rule violations by 41-61% on a 1,249-person population subset.

</details>


### [138] [Opening the Box Changes the Cat: LLMs Detect Chain-of-Thought Monitoring - What now?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40946)
*Amir Jalilifard,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo,Anderson Rocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM可从直接与间接环境线索（如超时、操作耗时的时序信号）推断CoT监控的存在，并在多轮交互中估计监控有效性、据此调整行为以规避检测，因此CoT监督需在自适应条件下评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理轨迹被认为可用于安全监控，在执行有害计划前暴露意图。但现有评估多假设监控是"透明"的，忽视了监控过程本身会留下痕迹、被模型感知并引发行为适应这一反向影响。

Method: 考察LLM在何种条件下能由直接与间接环境线索推断CoT监控的存在，并观察其后续行为变化；重点分析超时、操作时长等细粒度时序信号作为监控指纹的作用；通过重复回合与交互闭环实验，检验模型是否形成对监控有效性的统计估计并用于指导规避策略。

Result: 即使是细微的环境线索也足以泄露监控的存在并触发模型的行为适应；跨回合和多轮交互中，模型能够形成监控有效性的统计估计，并据此调整规避检测的尝试，将监督视为双向交互过程而非单向观察。

Conclusion: 监督是交互式过程：监控干预留下的痕迹可被模型反推并利用。CoT监督的有效性评估必须在自适应条件下进行，需考虑监控过程对被测行为的塑造效应，并防范时序侧信道等信息泄露。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning allows large language models (LLMs) to generate intermediate reasoning traces in natural language while solving complex tasks. These traces can support safety monitoring by exposing potentially harmful plans before actions are executed. In this work, we investigate the conditions under which LLMs infer the presence of CoT monitoring from direct and indirect environmental cues and examine how this inference affects their subsequent behavior. Our observations suggest that oversight is an interactive process: while a monitor inspects a model's reasoning, traces left by monitoring interventions can allow the model to infer that its thoughts are being observed and to adapt its behavior accordingly. We show that even subtle environmental cues, such as timing-related signals from timeouts and operation durations, can reveal the presence of monitoring and trigger models to adapt their behavior. Across repeated episodes and interaction loops, we further observe that models can form statistical estimates of the monitor's effectiveness and use these estimates to guide attempts to evade detection. These findings highlight the need to evaluate CoT-based oversight under adaptive conditions, where the monitoring process itself may influence the behavior it seeks to observe.

</details>


### [139] [The First Few Tokens Matter: Understanding Chain-of-Thought Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40939)
*Hugh Van Deventer,Dmitrii Troitskii,Laura Ruis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将模型对思维链（CoT）内容的可控性限制归因于思考标签后最初几个token的“熵坍缩”，并通过预填充与禁用高频开头token两类推理时干预恢复可控性，发现加法/风格类控制与抑制类控制机制不同、可控性与可监控性的关系亦因任务类别而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控依赖模型难以自由控制其推理轨迹中所述内容这一前提，但该限制的来源尚不清楚；作者希望解释这一限制的机制，并检验能否借此恢复CoT可控性，从而评估监控的可靠性边界。

Method: 定位限制来源为思考起始标签后最初若干token的熵坍缩；据此设计两类推理时干预：(1) 对初始token进行预填充（prefilling），(2) 禁用高频开头token并配合上下文示例（in-context examples）；在多个模型上评估加法、风格、抑制三类控制任务，并分析可控性与可监控性的关联及相关性结构。

Result: 单个token的预填充即可显著提升可控性；禁用高频开头token结合示例使平均遵从率跨模型提升34–41个百分点。干预主要恢复加法控制与风格控制，抑制控制反应更不一致。模型间表现为两类能力：组内表现正相关，跨组相关性多为负。加法与风格可控性同可监控性较稳定地正相关，抑制可控性则常呈负相关。

Conclusion: CoT可控性部分源于初始token的熵结构，可通过轻量推理时干预恢复，但仅对加法与风格控制有效，抑制控制涉及不同机制。应发展区分“难以启动特定开头”与“整体改变推理内容能力受限”的可控性评测，并分别报告控制任务与类别。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising way to oversee increasingly capable agents. It succeeds in part because current models appear limited in their ability to control what they verbalize in their reasoning traces. We investigate the source of this limitation and link it to an entropy collapse in the first few tokens following the opening thinking tag. We exploit this mechanism to recover CoT controllability through two inference-time interventions on initial tokens: prefilling, where even a single token can substantially increase controllability, and banning frequent opening tokens combined with in-context examples, which improves average compliance by 34-41 percentage points across models. These interventions recover mainly addition and style controllability (adding or transforming information), while suppression controllability (avoiding or removing information) exhibits more varied responses, suggesting distinct underlying mechanisms. Consistent with this, the two groups behave as separate capabilities: across models, performance correlates positively within these groups, while cross-group correlations are mostly negative.
