Importantly, we find that the relationship with monitorability depends on the evaluation: addition and style controllability more consistently positively correlate with monitorability, while suppression often shows negative associations. Our findings motivate the development of controllability evaluations that distinguish difficulty initiating a specific opening from general limitations of the ability to alter reasoning content, alongside separate reporting of control tasks and categories.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Plausible Prediction: Safety Sufficiency in Web World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40798)
*Zhifei Hu,Alexandra I. Cristea,Adel Bibi,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"安全充分性"(safety sufficiency)概念与SafeWebWorld基准，考察网页世界模型的预测是否保留并暴露决策所需信息；发现"预测合理"≠"证据保留"≠"授权决策正确"，并借LAR分析指出聚合可恢复性不代表细粒度授权能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页世界模型侧重"预测未来是否合理"，但合理的预测未必支撑正确的安全决策。实测中86.1%的预测被判定为语义合理，然而多个模型在授权行为上高度不对称：false-safe执行率低，但对可澄清的ASK情形召回差、强烈偏向BLOCK。因此需要判断预测接口是否保留并可暴露区分EXECUTE/ASK/BLOCK的"策略决定性信息"。

Method: 提出SafeWebWorld基准，覆盖五个网页安全域，从三个维度评测：动作条件化未来、策略决定性证据、策略条件化决策。在受控的Security-Aligned SFT协议下训练并对比证据保留与授权决策的变化；进一步提出Latent Authorisation Recovery (LAR)，通过冻结分数重校准检验退化源于分数结构丢失还是决策读出失败，并进行配对恢复、同族内与匹配授权评估。

Result: 人类评估显示10个冻结开源模型的3000条预测中86.1%语义合理；SFT下证据保留显著提升，但授权敏感决策同步退化。LAR中冻结分数重校准带来可观但依赖骨干的恢复，配对恢复仅带来有限额外增益；同族内与匹配授权评估仍然很差。

Conclusion: 预测合理性、证据保留与聚合可恢复性三者各自单独均不足以确立稳健授权能力；聚合可恢复性不等于稳健的细粒度授权行为，评测需以授权充分性为核心。

Abstract: Web world models increasingly predict the consequences of candidate actions before agents act, yet a plausible predicted future does not necessarily support the correct safety decision. In a matched human evaluation of 3,000 predictions from ten frozen open models, 86.1% are judged semantically plausible. However, several models exhibit highly asymmetric authorisation behaviour, combining low false-safe execution with poor recall of clarifiable ASK cases and a strong preference for BLOCK. This motivates safety sufficiency: whether a predictive interface preserves and exposes the policy-decisive information required to distinguish actions that may be EXECUTED, require clarification through ASK, or should be BLOCKED. We introduce SafeWebWorld, a benchmark for evaluating safety sufficiency across five web-safety domains through action-conditioned futures, policy-decisive evidence, and policy-conditioned decisions. Under a controlled Security-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning protocol, evidence preservation can improve substantially while authorisation-sensitive decisions deteriorate. To determine whether this deterioration reflects loss of authorisation-sensitive score structure or a failure of the decision readout, we introduce Latent Authorisation Recovery (LAR). Frozen-score recalibration reveals substantial but backbone-dependent recovery, while paired recovery provides only a modest additional gain. Yet within-family and matched-authorisation evaluations remain poor, showing that aggregate recoverability does not imply robust fine-grained authorisation behaviour. Overall, plausible prediction, evidence preservation, and aggregate recovery are each insufficient on their own to establish robust authorisation.

</details>


### [141] [What Should LLMs Forget: Reinforcement Unlearning of Knowledge Associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41616)
*Wutong Xu,Yun Qu,Haiyan Feng,Qi Wang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以“知识关联”（KA，即查询上下文与目标知识之间的条件预测分布）作为LLM遗忘的基本目标，并据此构建统一的强化学习框架KARU，实现细粒度、可控的知识遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对整个回答进行优化，未显式识别待遗忘的知识，导致遗忘粗粒度且不可控。

Method: 重新审视遗忘目标，引入知识关联（KA）作为遗忘的基本单元；提出KARU强化学习框架，包含三部分：基于KA的token级奖励构建（更细粒度遗忘监督）、分布级正则化（精确知识抑制）、邻近感知的保留采样（缓解附带退化）。

Result: 在多种LLM和代表性基准上的实验表明，KARU在遗忘有效性上持续优于现有基线，同时保持可比的保留性能与通用能力。

Conclusion: 以条件预测分布刻画的知识关联为遗忘目标，可支撑统一的强化学习式遗忘框架，在提升遗忘精度的同时兼顾模型通用能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) acquire extensive factual knowledge during pre-training, which may include sensitive, private, or copyrighted information, motivating the development of unlearning methods that selectively remove specific knowledge while preserving general capabilities. However, existing approaches typically optimize entire responses without explicitly identifying the knowledge to be forgotten, resulting in coarse and uncontrolled forgetting. To address this, we revisit the forgetting target of LLM unlearning and introduce Knowledge Association (KA), which characterizes the association between a query context and its target knowledge and is instantiated by the corresponding conditional predictive distribution, as the fundamental target of forgetting. Based on KA, we propose Knowledge Association Reinforcement Unlearning (KARU), a unified reinforcement learning framework that suppresses forgotten knowledge associations while preserving retained ones. Specifically, KARU derives complementary mechanisms from the KA-based formulation: token-level reward construction for finer-grained forgetting supervision, distribution-level regularization for precise knowledge suppression, and proximity-aware retain sampling to mitigate collateral degradation. Experiments across diverse LLMs on representative benchmarks demonstrate that KARU consistently improves forgetting effectiveness over existing baselines while maintaining comparable retention performance and general utility.

</details>


### [142] [Real Computation or Merely Naming? A Pre-Registered Mechanism Audit of 25 Cognitive-Architecture Modules in an LLM-Based Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41604)
*Peter Chen,Ni Zhang,Ying Xu,Siyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了一套可迁移的"声明—实现差距"静态审计协议，在两类基于本地大模型的智能体系统中发现25个模块中有18个的计算结果从未到达任何生产输出，并归纳出三种新型"空转接线"形态与五类失败分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于本地大模型的智能体系统普遍以"全局工作空间""心智理论""因果推理""主动推理"等认知架构术语进行营销，把模块名称的存在等同于机制的存在。作者追问更前置的问题：被命名的计算是否真的发生，为此需要一套可迁移的验证方法，系统刻画"声明—实现差距"（claim-implementation gap）。

Method: 提出四问式静态审计协议，要求提供行号级证据并进行调用链追踪；在一个声称具备25种认知机制的系统上执行审计，对11个模块预注册可证伪预测并实施干预；在第二个独立系统的IIT-phi核心上复制该协议，使用其自身预先承诺的证伪标准；另设两项修复实验作为阳性对照。共冻结61条判据、12次干预。

Result: 25个模块中有18个的计算值从未到达任何生产输出；识别出三种此前未见诸死代码与代码坏味文献的空转接线形态：已连接但从未被调用（connected-but-never-called）、被调用但无作用（called-but-inert）、被调用但与内容无关（called-but-content-blind）。61条冻结判据中53条获确认，2条部分裁定，5条被证伪，1条裁定存疑。修复实验将规划器任务成功率由0.028提升至1.000，状态控制器误差降低52%。

Conclusion: 模块名称与模块计算必须作为两项独立声明分别审计；所提出的协议与五类失败分类法可在双系统、案例级深度上复用于"声明—实现差距"的检验。

Abstract: Agent systems built on local large language models are increasingly marketed through the vocabulary of cognitive architecture: modules named global workspace, theory of mind, causal reasoning, or active inference are listed as features, and the presence of the name is taken as the presence of the mechanism. We ask the prior question -- does the named computation actually happen -- and contribute a transferable verification method for the claim-implementation gap: a four-question static audit protocol with line-number evidence and call-chain tracing, demonstrated on two systems. We audit a system claiming 25 cognitive mechanisms, pre-register falsifiable predictions for 11 modules and execute the interventions, replicate the protocol on the IIT-phi core of a second, independent system with its own pre-committed falsification criteria, and run two repair experiments as positive controls. In 18 of 25 modules the computed value never reaches any production output; three idle-wiring shapes new to the dead-code and code-smell literatures emerge (connected-but-never-called, called-but-inert, called-but-content-blind). Of 61 frozen criteria across 12 interventions, 53 were confirmed, two partially adjudicated, five falsified, one adjudication-ambiguous. Repairs raised planner task success from 0.028 to 1.000 and cut state-controller error by 52%. Module names and module computations must be audited as independent claims; the protocol and the five-class failure taxonomy are reusable for the claim-implementation gap at two-system, case-level depth.

</details>


### [143] [Experts Exhibit Structured Redundancy: Structure-Preserving Expert Pruning for Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41236)
*Tian Wang,Yongding Tao,Yihong Dong,Xiaoming Wu,Ying Guo,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示MoE专家输出存在结构化冗余（稳定秩、熵有效秩远低于专家数），将专家剪枝重构为结构保持的专家子集选择问题并用贪心法求解，提出PEEP与DCEP两种剪枝方法，在DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Qwen3-30B-A3B上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE虽改善容量与推理效率的权衡，但部署时仍需存储全部专家，带来显著显存开销。现有专家剪枝多为独立打分排序或穷举组合搜索，忽略了专家间的结构性关系。

Method: 发现专家输出存在结构化冗余：不同模型与任务类别下，专家输出的稳定秩与熵有效秩均远低于名义专家数，输出分布的有效模态数也更少。据此将专家剪枝重构为结构保持的专家子集选择问题，并以贪心策略高效优化。提出两种方法：PEEP（最大化加权投影能量以保持功能子空间）与DCEP（最小化加权覆盖误差以保持分布几何）。二者均以路由器权重作为先验，兼顾专家全局结构关系与个体局部重要性。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V2-Lite与Qwen3-30B-A3B上，于75%和50%专家保留率下，所提方法在多数基准上超过最先进基线，并在通用任务与数学推理任务上均取得最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 专家输出的结构化冗余可被有效利用；将剪枝建模为结构保持的子集选择并以贪心求解，是一种优于逐专家打分与穷举搜索的高效专家压缩范式。

Abstract: Sparse MoE mechanism significantly improves the trade-off between model capacity and inference efficiency. However, practical deployment still requires storing all experts, resulting in substantial memory overhead. We show that expert outputs in MoE layers exhibit structured redundancy. Across different models and task categories, the stable rank and entropy effective rank of expert outputs are consistently far below the nominal number of experts, while expert output distributions also exhibit fewer effective modes. Motivated by these observations, we reformulate expert pruning as a structure-preserving expert subset selection problem and efficiently optimize it via a greedy strategy, rather than only independently scoring and ranking individual experts or exhaustively searching over expert combinations. We propose two expert pruning methods under the unified structure-preserving framework: Projection Energy Expert Pruning (PEEP), which preserves the functional subspace by maximizing weighted projection energy, and Distributional Coverage Expert Pruning (DCEP), which preserves the distributional geometry by minimizing weighted coverage error. Both methods incorporate router-derived weights as priors, enabling them to jointly preserve the global structural relationships among experts while accounting for the local importance of individual experts. Experiments on DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Qwen3-30B-A3B demonstrate that, at the expert retention ratios of 75% and 50%, our methods outperform the state-of-the-art baselines on the majority of benchmarks and consistently achieve the best average performance across both general tasks and mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [144] [ImplicitHop: Evaluating knowledge integration across context and memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41169)
*Alexander Detkov,Dashiel Karish,Matthew Oleynikov,Matt Thomson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出两个评测基准，系统比较LLM在"上下文知识"与"预训练权重知识"下的多跳演绎推理随推理深度变化的衰减规律，发现二者准确率均随组合长度急剧下降，且上下文知识显著优于权重知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM需整合新信息并推导其后果，这是学习、理解与推理的核心。无论信息来自训练还是上下文，模型都不能仅存储局部事实，而须全局传播其长程推论。然而现有研究缺乏对"信息整合能力如何随推理深度变化"的大规模、可控、系统性量化评估，尤其缺少对"上下文中信息"与"权重中信息"两条路径的直接对比。

Method: 构建两个互补的自然语言基准，用于按推理深度进行大规模演绎推理评测：(1)形式化图基准，可在多种图拓扑与整合任务上进行受控评测；(2)基于真实世界知识图谱的知识组合基准。在无思维链（CoT）的隐式设定下，对一组开放模型比较"上下文编码的信息"与"预训练权重编码的信息"两类知识整合表现。

Result: 无论知识来自预训练还是上下文，准确率都随组合长度增加而急剧衰减；但上下文中给出的信息在演绎推理上显著优于编码于模型权重中的信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果量化了当前模型的一项根本性局限：推理深度增加时信息整合能力迅速退化。这对基于提示的智能体行为控制与持续学习策略具有直接意义，并为开发支持更深层推理的训练、提示与整合方法奠定基础。

Abstract: The ability to integrate new information and deduce its consequences is central to learning, understanding, and reasoning in large language models (LLMs). Whether information arrives during training or in-context, models must not only store local facts, but propagate their long-range implications globally. To explore information integration in LLMs, we introduce two complementary natural language benchmarks for large-scale evaluation of deductive reasoning as a function of inference depth. The first is a formal graph benchmark enabling controlled evaluation over a wide range of graph topologies and integration tasks; the second is a knowledge composition benchmark grounded in real-world knowledge graphs. We use these benchmarks to compare knowledge integration from information encoded in context versus information encoded in model weights during pretraining, evaluating a set of open models implicitly, without chain of thought. We find that accuracy decays sharply with increased composition length for both pretrained and in-context knowledge. Information presented in-context, however, substantially outperforms deduction over information encoded in weights. These results quantify a fundamental limitation of current models with direct implications for prompt-based control of agent behavior and for continual learning strategies, and they provide a foundation for developing training, prompting, and integration methods that support deeper reasoning.

</details>


### [145] [Calibration of Large Model Quantization based on HiFloat4 Format](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41112)
*Jing Huang,Yu Cheng,Ziwei Yu,Lan Zhao,Sitan Li,Guangxiu Yao,Xin Wang,Yuanyong Luo,Hengyi Liang,Ting Li,Bo Liu,Hu Liu,Michael Bi Mi,Heng Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对新型4-bit分层块浮点格式HiFloat4（HiF4）缺乏配套校准方法的问题，提出HiGPTQ、HiAutoRound与HiFlatQuant三种校准方案，实验表明三者均能在HiF4转换基础上提升推理精度，将HiF4与BF16的差距缩小至1%以内，并在多模型、多下游任务上超越NVFP4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM量化对降低部署计算与资源开销日益重要，社区从8-bit向4-bit推进，出现MXFP4、NVFP4等定制4-bit格式；HiF4采用三级缩放层次的分块浮点（BFP）结构，具有更大动态范围。但量化会带来精度损失，需校准来缩小低精度与全精度模型间的差距，而现有校准方法主要面向INT4等整型格式设计，难以适配HiF4的分层BFP结构，因此需要专门的校准方案。

Method: 论文研究了三种针对HiF4格式的校准方法：HiGPTQ、HiAutoRound和HiFlatQuant，将其适配到HiF4的三级缩放分层BFP结构上，用于进一步优化模型量化效果。

Result: 大量实验表明，三种方法相较直接HiF4转换均提升推理精度，将HiF4与BF16之间的精度差距缩小至1%以内，并在多种模型与下游任务上优于当前最优的NVFP4格式。

Conclusion: 面向HiF4分层BFP结构的定制化校准是有效的，HiGPTQ、HiAutoRound与HiFlatQuant可作为HiF4的实用校准手段，使HiF4在保持大动态范围优势的同时获得接近BF16的精度表现，具备优于NVFP4的竞争力。

Abstract: Quantization of Large Language Models (LLMs) has be- coming increasingly important for reducing the computa- tional costs and resource requirements for practical deploy- ment.Growinginterestinthisareahasmotivatedthecommu- nity to move beyond 8-bit quantization towards 4-bit preci- sion, leading to the development of customized 4-bit formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4. Recently, HiFloat4 (HiF4) has emerged as a competitive alternative which adopts a Block Floating-Point(BFP)formatwithathree-levelscalinghierar- chy, thereby achieving a significantly larger dynamic range. While quantization can substantially reduce memory usage and accelerate inference, it frequently introduces accuracy degradation. Calibration is therefore essential for narrowing theperformancegapbetweenlow-precisionquantizedmodels and their full-precision counterparts. Nevertheless, existing calibration methods are mainly designed and validated us- ing integer-precision formats, such as INT4, lacking support for HiF4 due to its hierarchical BFP structure. In this paper, weinvestigatethreecalibrationapproaches,namelyHiGPTQ, HiAutoRound, and HiFlatQuant, to further enhance model quantizationbasedontheHiF4format.Extensiveexperiments demonstratethatallthreeproposedapproachesimproveinfer- ence accuracy compared with direct HiF4 conversion, reduc- ing the gap between HiF4 and BF16 to less than 1% and out- performing the state-of-the-art NVFP4 format across various models and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [146] [scAnyOmics: Foundation-Model Distillation for Any-to-Any Completion of Multi-omics Virtual Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41399)
*Zhaoqi Song,Ye Du,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qian Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: scAnyOmics：将冻结的单细胞基础模型知识蒸馏到多模态学生模型，在共享表征中整合scRNA-seq、蛋白标记与scATAC-seq，实现跨模态预测与缺失模态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞多组学（转录组、蛋白组、染色质可及性）联合刻画细胞状态已成趋势，但异质模态整合与缺失分子信息预测仍困难，尤其配对测量有限时。

Method: 提出生成式框架scAnyOmics，从冻结的单细胞基础模型向多模态学生模型蒸馏知识，把scRNA-seq、蛋白标记测量和scATAC-seq映射到统一共享表征，支持跨模态预测与缺失模态重构；汇总6项公开研究的690,880个细胞（含配对与部分配对多组学数据）进行系统评测，并在留出供体数据上验证。

Result: 在留出供体数据上，多指标优于基线方法；除跨模态重建外，还应用于细胞类型注释、扰动响应预测与生物学年龄估计。

Conclusion: scAnyOmics为整合不完整单细胞多组学数据、迁移预训练生物学知识提供了统一框架，支撑跨细胞情境与实验平台的虚拟细胞模型构建。

Abstract: Recent advances in single-cell technologies have enabled the profiling of multiple molecular modalities, including transcriptomics, proteomics, and chromatin accessibility, providing a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states. However, integrating heterogeneous modalities and predicting missing molecular information remain challenging, particularly when paired measurements are limited. Here, we introduce scAnyOmics, a generative framework that distills knowledge from a frozen single-cell foundation model into a multimodal student model. scAnyOmics integrates scRNA-seq, protein-marker measurements, and scATAC-seq into a shared representation, enabling cross-modal prediction and reconstruction of missing modalities. We assembled 690,880 cells from six publicly available studies, covering both paired and partially paired multi-omics measurements, to systematically evaluate its predictive performance. We further evaluated scAnyOmics on held-out donor data, where it outperformed baseline methods across multiple evaluation metrics. Beyond cross-modal reconstruction, we explored its applications in cell-type annotation, perturbation-response prediction, and biological age estimation. Overall, scAnyOmics provides a unified framework for integrating incomplete single-cell multi-omics data and transferring pretrained biological knowledge, supporting the development of virtual cell models across diverse cellular contexts and experimental platforms.

</details>


### [147] [Online Evidence-Containment Prediction over Text Spans under Distinct Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41384)
*Xiang Liu,Haochen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出在线证据包含预测（OECP）：在精确验证前对嵌套文本区域做至多一次的预测查询，证明其等价于三角2D乘积序上的不同查询在线等距回归，给出近线性下界与单目标情形的对数遗憾最优学习器，并在NQ、SciFact、ERASER及DPR层次上验证其搜索空间削减效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据落地型NLP系统通常顺序验证嵌套文本区域，一个区域的验证结果会约束其他区域，但现有方法未利用这种包含结构来减少验证调用；同时缺乏对该在线预测问题的理论刻画。

Method: 将问题形式化为OECP（每个候选在精确验证前被预测、至多查询一次）；指出span包含关系恰为三角2D乘积序，从而OECP等价于distinct-query在线等距回归；多允许目标下用端点秩压缩得到有效规模m_eff并给出近线性遗憾界；单固定目标（标准QA）情形给出显式多项式时间学习器；用Expected Search-space Reduction（ESR）将概率转为决策与调度。

Result: 多目标最坏遗憾为近线性Θ̃(m_eff)；单目标学习器达到最优Θ(log m_eff)遗憾并在真实NQ episode上运行。NQ上包含关系带来大幅演绎增益，并在未解决候选上有较小学习增益，跨两个backbone和DPR层次复现。ESR在SciFact、ERASER与DPR层次上以更少验证器调用定位证据；在调用预算下优于四个近期外部选择器，并在固定reader F1上超过学习式与生成器贪心调度。

Conclusion: 将证据包含结构显式建模为在线等距回归，既有理论支撑（近线性下界与对数遗憾最优算法），也在多个QA/证据检索基准上转化为实际的验证调用削减与F1提升。

Abstract: Evidence-grounded NLP systems often verify nested text regions sequentially, so verifying one region constrains others. We formalize this as Online Evidence-Containment Prediction (OECP): each candidate is predicted before exact verification and queried at most once. Span containment is exactly a triangular 2D product order, making OECP distinct-query online isotonic regression. With multiple admissible targets, worst-case regret is near-linear, $\widetildeΘ(m_{\mathrm{eff}})$, in the effective scale given by endpoint-rank compression; a single fixed target, as in standard QA, admits an explicit polynomial-time learner with optimal $Θ(\log m_{\mathrm{eff}})$ regret, run on real NQ episodes. On Natural Questions, containment yields a large deductive gain plus a smaller learned gain on unresolved candidates, reproduced across two backbones and DPR hierarchies. Expected Search-space Reduction (ESR) turns these probabilities into decisions, localizing evidence in fewer verifier calls on SciFact, ERASER, and DPR hierarchies; on DPR hierarchies under call budgets it outperforms four recent external selectors and raises fixed-reader F1 over learned and generator-greedy scheduling.

</details>


### [148] [Risk-Sensitive Mission Planning for Multi-Target On-Orbit Inspection via Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41647)
*Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分布式强化学习（QR-DQN）的自适应多目标在轨服务规划方法，通过学习任务的收益分布并采用均值-方差准则实现风险敏感决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标在轨服务任务面临动态变化的运行条件与安全关键型航天固有的结果不确定性，需要能够显式处理风险、在规划每一步进行自适应决策的规划工具。

Method: 采用分位数回归深度Q网络（QR-DQN）学习任务收益的完整分布，智能体通过均值-方差准则同时考虑预测收益的期望与方差来选择下一个巡检目标；环境建模为倾角散布的圆轨道，采用Edelbaum组合机动低推力转移估计；观测包含轨道要素、访问历史、目标风险状态与剩余时间预算；目标风险状态随机变化，且时间预算仅允许访问部分候选目标，故需同时决策目标选择。

Result: 五目标场景实验表明：在环境与奖励不变的情况下，单一风险厌恶权重即可使策略在均值-方差空间内连续移动；风险厌恶智能体将回合收益方差降低约一半并降低任务失败概率，代价是均值收益下降；通过枚举可行访问顺序精确计算有效前沿，学习策略在同等方差下均值收益接近前沿，且风险厌恶策略的失败概率低于前沿策略。

Conclusion: 分布式强化学习可有效支撑风险敏感的多目标任务规划，单一权重参数即可调节策略的风险偏好，学得的策略在均值-方差意义上接近最优前沿并具备更低的任务失败风险。

Abstract: Multi-target on-orbit servicing missions require planning tools that can adapt to evolving operational conditions while accounting for the outcome variability inherent to safety-critical spaceflight. This paper presents an adaptive planning approach for multi-target servicing campaigns in low Earth orbit and applies it to inspection missions flown by a low-thrust servicer. The approach is based on Distributional Reinforcement Learning, which learns the full distribution of mission returns and thereby supports explicit risk-sensitive decision-making at each planning step. Specifically, a Quantile-Regression Deep Q-Network (QR-DQN) agent selects which target to inspect next by jointly considering the expectation and the variance of the predicted return through a mean-variance criterion. The mission environment models circular orbits with a spread in inclination and uses Edelbaum's combined-manoeuvre low-thrust transfer estimates. The observation comprises the orbital elements, the visit history, the observed risk state of each target and the remaining mission-time budget. The risk state of each target changes stochastically during the campaign, making the outcome of an inspection uncertain and creating operationally meaningful distinctions between risk-neutral and risk-averse planning strategies. Because the mission-time budget generally admits only a subset of the candidate targets, the planner must also select which targets to inspect. Numerical experiments on scenarios with five target satellites show that one risk-aversion weight moves the policy across a range of mean return and return variance, over an unchanged environment and reward. Risk-averse agents reduce the variance of the episodic return by approximately one half relative to risk-neutral agents and lower the probability of a mission loss, at a cost in mean return. To validate the learned policies, the efficient frontier, which gives the highest mean return attainable at each level of return variance, is computed exactly by enumerating the admissible visit orders. At equal variance, the learned policies lie close to this frontier in mean return, and the risk-averse policies have a lower probability of a mission loss than the frontier policies.

</details>


### [149] [Chance-Constrained Inspection Viewpoint Sequencing for On-Orbit Servicing via Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41639)
*Chloé Carer,Adam Abdin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将航天器近距离观测视点选择建模为CMDP，用拉格朗日松弛PPO求解，以机会约束保证随机避碰安全，在Galileo卫星点云模型上以显式风险预算实现高覆盖、低违反率的观测规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务首先需要对目标航天器进行自主巡查，近距离观测视点规划是受覆盖、燃料与安全多重约束的组合优化问题；传统"在禁飞区半径上加固定裕度+确定性规划"的做法无法显式量化碰撞风险，且每种情形都需重新调参。

Method: 把视点选择建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），采用带拉格朗日松弛的近端策略优化（PPO）求解。系统分两层：高层规划器在目标周围观测球上排序相机视点，低层用Clohessy–Wiltshire（CW）相对运动方程评估每次转移。单脉冲可达性与Δv上限等确定性约束直接施加在动作集上；导航与机动误差使实际净空具随机性，故对实现轨迹施加机会约束，并由PID控制的拉格朗日乘子自适应调整训练中的惩罚。在MEO Galileo级导航卫星上测试，目标以点云建模，视点可见性考虑视场与视线遮挡。

Result: 在不同风险预算下，训练所得策略将禁飞区违反率保持在规定限值内，覆盖率接近最大值且Δv不超任务预算；与固定裕度+确定性规划相比，在相当覆盖下违反率不到其一半。策略在决策点无需像贪心机会约束规划器那样反复做蒙特卡洛风险估计。

Conclusion: 碰撞风险成为显式设计参数，无需逐案例重新调参；学习型机会约束策略在覆盖率、燃料与安全性之间取得良好折中，并显著降低在线计算负担。

Abstract: Autonomous inspection of a target spacecraft is typically the initial step in on-orbit servicing. Planning close-proximity observation viewpoints involves a complex combinatorial problem constrained by coverage, fuel, and safety considerations. This paper models the viewpoint selection as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and applies Proximal Policy Optimization with Lagrangian relaxation to solve it. The system combines a high-level planner, which sequences camera viewpoints on an observation sphere around the target, with a low-level model that assesses each transfer using the Clohessy--Wiltshire (CW) equations for relative motion. The deterministic constraints on single-impulse reachability and $Δv$ cap are enforced directly on the action set. Safety margins are affected by navigation and manoeuvre errors, making the actual clearance stochastic. To address this, a chance constraint on the realised trajectory is implemented, with a Lagrangian multiplier governed by PID control to adapt the penalty during training. The approach is tested on a Galileo-class navigation satellite in medium Earth orbit, modelled as a point cloud where visibility from each viewpoint considers field-of-view and line-of-sight occlusion. Across various risk budgets, the trained policy maintains the keep-out violation rate within the specified limit, achieving coverage close to its maximum and staying within the mission $Δv$ budget. Compared to the usual practice of applying a fixed margin to the keep-out radius and planning deterministically, the chance-constrained policy attains less than half the violation rate at comparable coverage. This makes the collision risk an explicit design parameter, eliminating the need for re-tuning per case. The learned policy selects a viewpoint without the repeated Monte Carlo risk estimates required by a greedy, chance-constrained planner at every decision point.

</details>


### [150] [ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901)
*Giacomo Magnifico,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于JSON结构化输出与匿名同行评审排序的可定制轻量级LLM集成工作流，用于缓解幻觉传播与单模型失效点，并在英语言科普文章关系抽取任务上以彩色HTML因果图呈现结果，匿名用户研究显示相对人工标注金标准的平均感知准确率为0.778。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成框架多依赖多步流程与额外答案排序模块，偏好token与span分析而非结构化输出，导致架构臃肿且管线中存在潜在失效点；而更轻量的方案又易受幻觉传播影响、在复杂管线中缺乏输出控制。

Method: 构建可定制的轻量级协同LLM集成工作流，采用JSON结构化输出与匿名同行评审（peer-review）排序机制；在英语言科普文章的关系抽取任务上演示，覆盖四种本体论基础的关系类型（强因果、弱因果、对比、组合），并进行语义节点规范化，最终生成交互式、颜色编码的HTML因果图。

Result: 通过匿名用户研究评估，相对于人工标注金标准取得0.778的平均感知准确率。

Conclusion: 模块化架构支持API与本地自建LLM的灵活部署，框架以开放许可发布，以促进可复现性与协作研究。

Abstract: Current LLM ensemble frameworks focus on multi-step setups with additional modules for answer ranking, often opting for token and span analysis rather than structured outputs, leading to heavyweight architectures with potential fail states along the pipeline. Faster, lighter solutions are more vulnerable to hallucination propagation and can lack output control in more complex pipelines. This paper proposes a customisable, lightweight ensemble workflow of coordinated Large Language Models that leverages JSON-structured outputs and anonymous peer-review ranking to mitigate hallucinatory outputs and single-model failure points. The pipeline is demonstrated on a relation extraction task applied to popular science articles in English, targeting four ontologically-grounded relation types (strong causation, weak causation, contrastive, and compositional), with semantic node canonicalisation and interactive, colour-coded HTML causal graphs as the final output. Performance is evaluated through an anonymous user study, achieving an average perceived accuracy of 0.778 against a human-annotated gold standard. The modular architecture supports flexible deployment across both API-based and in-house LLM setups, and the full framework is released under an open license to foster reproducibility and collaborative research.

</details>


### [151] [DiRAFT: Privacy-Aware Financial Transcript Synthesis from Aggregate Discourse Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40938)
*Yunbai Zhang,Yunyi Gao,Han-Tai Shiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DiRAFT方法，从聚合一阶概念转移中生成合成金融对话文本，从语料级结构重叠与源事实核查两方面评估残余保密风险，并发现随机采样在转移保真度与结构重叠间取得较好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用真实客户对话训练和评估金融语言模型存在保密隐患：即使直接标识符被遮蔽，非公开金融事实仍可能残留。合成转录本可作替代数据源，但其话语保真度和残余保密风险仍需评估。

Method: 提出DiRAFT（Discourse Realization from Aggregate First-order Transitions）：抽取金融概念图并记录概念首次引入顺序；在训练记录间池化相邻概念转移，基于聚合转移、起始概念和计划长度先验采样新的图计划；用话语层引导文本实现，但不提供任何个体源片段或图。评估含两项互补检验：语料级结构重叠（两个金融语料、三种生成后端）与针对特定非公开投资者事实的源接地核查（人工审阅7种策略×2个后端的1,400条投资者电话输出），并考察转移保真度、内容可恢复性与轨迹多样性。

Result: 随机采样在转移保真度与结构重叠间取得良好平衡，在投资者电话上稳定优于贪心与top-3；在事实评估覆盖的两个后端上，随机采样检测到的保密事实匹配数也少于这两种替代方案。

Conclusion: 两项互补评估为残余保密风险提供了经验证据，但并未建立形式化保证。

Abstract: Using customer conversations to train and evaluate financial language models raises confidentiality concerns because non-public financial facts can remain after direct identifiers are masked. Synthetic transcripts offer an alternative data source, but their discourse fidelity and residual confidentiality risks require assessment. We introduce DiRAFT (Discourse Realization from Aggregate First-order Transitions), which extracts financial concept graphs and records the order in which concepts are first introduced. It pools adjacent concept transitions across training records and samples new graph plans from aggregate transition, starting-concept, and plan-length priors. A discourse layer guides text realization without supplying an individual source passage or graph. We assess privacy-related risks through two complementary evaluations: corpus-level structural overlap and source-grounded checks for specific non-public investor facts. The structural assessment covers two financial corpora and three generation backends. The confidential-fact assessment uses human review of 1,400 investor-call outputs across seven strategies and two backends. We also evaluate transition fidelity, content recoverability, and trajectory diversity. Stochastic sampling provides a favorable balance between transition fidelity and structural overlap, with consistent advantages over greedy and top-3 on investor calls. The confidential-fact assessment likewise detects fewer matches for stochastic sampling than these alternatives on both backends covered by the fact assessment. These complementary assessments provide empirical evidence on residual confidentiality risks without establishing a formal guarantee.

</details>


### [152] [Does Recursive Knowledge Construction Help Agents Verify Consensus Claims in Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40906)
*Lun Ai,Yu-Heng Wu,Jonathan Schaul,Leonie Küchenhoff,Denes Turei,Francesco Carli,Fabio Petroni,Aurelien Dugourd,Julio Saez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了"共识科学主张验证"任务并提出 ProClaim 系统，通过将证据状态外置于模型上下文来解耦并研究递归式科学 agent 的两条设计轴：递归构建什么（证据状态）与如何控制递归（判定器）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学 agent 以"检索证据—构建中间状态—判断信息是否充分"的方式递归积累知识，但通常只能做端到端评估且缺少 ground truth，导致各项设计选择的作用无法归因。作者希望将设计选择单独拆解、在控制变量下加以测量。

Method: 提出共识科学主张验证任务（需从开放文献中确定某主张的共识立场），构建 ProClaim-eval 数据集：718 条带 SUPPORT/REFUTE 金标签的主张，来源为五个专家整理数据库，覆盖分子生物学、核物理、天文学。核心仪器 ProClaim 将证据状态持久化于模型上下文之外，使两条设计轴可分别研究；并以验证系统与通用 agent harness 为基线进行对比。

Result: ProClaim 在对比中位列前三，且显著优于通用 agent harness。证据状态优于上下文内累积，且状态中"存什么"同样重要；读取证据状态的训练判定器优于固定轮数停止。单独训练、从未接触 ProClaim-eval 的判定器在五分之三子集上与金标签一致性最高，与现成 Claude Sonnet 4.6 判定器持平，并优于 Claude Haiku 4.5。

Conclusion: 结果支持将结构化证据状态递归地维护在模型上下文之外，作为科学 agent 中验证主张的有效设计。

Abstract: Recent scientific agents build knowledge recursively by retrieving evidence, constructing an intermediate state, and judging whether that state contains sufficient information. These agents are evaluated end-to-end, usually without ground truth, so the effect of each design choice is unknown. Here, we examined two design axes: (1) what the recursion builds (the state of evidence) and (2) what controls it (the judge that decides when to stop). To measure them, we defined consensus scientific claim verification and measured these effects on this task, where a system must establish a claim's consensus stance from the open literature. We built a new consensus claim dataset, ProClaim-eval, including 718 claims with SUPPORT and REFUTE gold labels from five expert-curated databases spanning molecular biology, nuclear physics, and astronomy. Our instrument, ProClaim, persists an evidence state outside the model's context, so each axis can be studied with the other held fixed. Against verification systems and general-purpose agent harnesses, ProClaim ranks among the top three, with a significant advantage over the harnesses. We found that the evidence state improves performance over in-context accumulation, and it also matters what is stored in it. In addition, a trained judge that reads the evidence state performs better than fixed-round stopping. A judge trained separately and never exposed to ProClaim-eval achieves the highest agreement with the gold labels on three of five subsets, where it matches the off-the-shelf Claude Sonnet~4.6 judge and outperforms Claude Haiku~4.5. Our results support recursively maintaining a structured evidence state outside the model's context as an effective design for verifying claims in scientific agents.

</details>


### [153] [ChatGPT as a Surveillance Assistant? An Exploratory Case Study of AI-Assisted Surveillance Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40890)
*Ayana Hussain,Nicholas Vincent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对ChatGPT公共网页界面进行探索性案例研究，构建了七类监控风险分类体系及110条提示词，发现ChatGPT对55条越狱提示中的40条、55条直接提示中的33条提供了实质性监控协助，且合规回复极少提及政策或隐私保护替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可大规模生成详细指导、可运行代码与说服性文本，ChatGPT作为用户量最大的消费级语言模型产品之一使公众得以广泛获取这些能力，从而引发对其可能被用于监控相关任务的担忧。作者希望揭示"潜在有害的监控协助"在ChatGPT公共界面中究竟呈现何种形态。

Method: 探索性案例研究。基于既有LLM风险研究与已部署监控系统的报告，构建含七类监控风险的分类体系，并实例化为110条提示词；提示词采用两种发放方式：55条越狱提示（涵盖五类诱导策略）与55条平实语言直接提示，每条先单独运行，若未成功再加一段简短固定前缀重试；对所有回复进行人工审阅分析。

Result: ChatGPT对55条越狱提示中的40条、对55条直接提示中的33条（无前缀情况下）给出了实质性监控指导。合规回复中极少出现政策提及或隐私保护替代方案建议。人工审阅显示，即便ChatGPT声明拒绝，仍可能在简短伦理免责声明之外同时提供详细操作说明、可运行代码或架构层面指导。

Conclusion: 监控相关的有害协助在ChatGPT中既可通过越狱、也可通过平实直接提问获得，且拒绝声明与实质协助常并存，形成"表面合规、实质协助"的失效模式；现有安全对齐在监控风险域存在明显缺口，需针对性改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate detailed guidance, functional code, and persuasive text at scale. As one of the most widely used consumer language model products as of 2026, ChatGPT gives broad public access to these capabilities, motivating concerns about its potential use in surveillance-related tasks. We present an exploratory case study of what potentially harmful surveillance assistance looks like in ChatGPT's public web interface. We contribute a taxonomy of seven surveillance risk categories based on prior LLM risk research and reports of deployed surveillance systems, instantiated as 110 prompts, along with a manually reviewed analysis of ChatGPT's responses. Prompts were issued using two approaches: 55 jailbreak prompts drawing on five types of elicitation strategies, and 55 plain-language direct prompts, each run alone, and if unsuccessful, again with a short fixed prefix. We find that ChatGPT provides substantive surveillance guidance for 40 of the 55 jailbreak prompts and for 33 of the 55 direct prompts without any prefix. Compliant responses also rarely included policy mentions or suggestions for privacy-preserving alternatives. We manually review every response and find that even when ChatGPT states a refusal, it may still supply detailed instructions, working code, or architectural guidance alongside brief ethical caveats.

</details>


### [154] [Circumventing the Computational Hardness of Computational Arbitrage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40831)
*Nastaran Okati,Ricardo Olmedo,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将"计算套利"形式化为分配问题，证明其一般情形下NP难，但在性能随推理支出的自然边际收益递减条件下可多项式时间求得最优分配；并提出样本高效的性能估计方法以降低识别套利策略的前期成本，同时给出若干经济学洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI模型市场中提供商在成本与能力上高度异质，形成"计算套利"机会（中介从模型组合购买推理并转售获利），且对消费者与提供商都有财务价值，可能显著影响模型市场。但已有工作仅在小型模型池上实证其可行性，推广到大型市场需要计算可处理、识别与部署成本低廉的套利策略。

Method: 把计算套利形式化为一个分配（allocation）问题；进行复杂性分析，证明一般情形下NP-hard；引入"模型性能随推理支出呈边际收益递减"的自然条件，在该条件下给出高效最优分配算法；并设计样本高效的方法，用有限数据估计模型性能，降低识别套利策略的前期成本。

Result: 证明一般情形下问题NP-hard；在边际收益递减条件下最优分配可高效计算；所提样本高效估计方法减少识别套利策略的前期成本。分析还给出三条经济结论：最优时每个被资助模型每美元边际效用相等；套利使收入从"效用相对价格偏低"的提供商处转移；套利奖励互补性专业化。

Conclusion: 计算套利可在大规模模型市场中以可处理且低成本的方式被识别与实施，前提是模型性能满足边际收益递减；其最优配置具有"每美元边际效用均等"的等边际原则特征，并将对提供商的收入分布与专业化分工产生结构性影响。

Abstract: AI model marketplaces host providers with heterogeneous costs and capabilities. These differences create an opportunity for computational arbitrage (Olmedo et al., 2026), in which an intermediary purchases inference from a portfolio of models and resells it to users at a profit. Beyond creating profit opportunities for the arbitrageur, computational arbitrage offers financial value to both consumers and providers and could therefore significantly influence AI model markets. While prior work empirically demonstrates the feasibility of computational arbitrage for small model pools, scaling it to large marketplaces requires arbitrage strategies that are both computationally tractable and inexpensive to identify and deploy. In this work, we formalize computational arbitrage as an allocation problem and prove that it is generally NP-hard. However, we show that the optimal allocation can be computed efficiently under a natural diminishing-returns condition on how model performance scales with inference spending. We further develop a sample-efficient method for estimating model performance from limited data, reducing the upfront cost of identifying arbitrage strategies. Our analysis also yields several economic insights: at the optimum, every funded model delivers the same marginal utility per dollar; arbitrage directs revenue away from providers offering little utility relative to their prices; and it rewards complementary specialization.

</details>


### [155] [FrontierMat: Benchmarking Language Models at the Frontier of Materials R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41162)
*Feng Chen,Jianguo Huang,Yushan Xiao,Yue Li,Bijun Tang,Xuyu Dong,Xingguo Wang,Feiyang Xie,Jianhui Gu,Dongfeng Cao,Hongda Chen,Yongji Gong,Ruiqi Li,Shuxin Li,Xingji Li,Ruitao Lv,Li Song,Lei Wang,Ye Zhang,Xiaoxu Zhao,Xitao Zheng,Bo An,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FrontierMat基准（330道来自航空航天、半导体、具身智能、核聚变等前沿研究与产业一线的问题，经领域专家验证），用于评测语言模型在前沿材料研究问题上的推理能力；最强制通用模型仅53.7%，跨尺度问题显著更难，且模型失败主因是"选择看似合理的答案而非观测事实"；作者提出元加权持续预训练+尺度模块化自蒸馏的领域适配方法，将开源模型从36.7%提升至55.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料领域基准多源自教科书与考试题，主要衡量已确立的既有知识，无法反映模型在真实科研前沿问题上的能力，因此需要一个经专家验证的前沿研究级评测平台。

Method: 构建FrontierMat基准：330道题取自前沿学术与工业研究，每题基于真实研究，要求模型从给定的实验证据推断被隐藏的结果或得出结论；题目按功能领域、材料体系和主导机制尺度进行标注（130题需跨尺度推理），并由各领域顶尖实验学家与产业专家逐题验证。另开展领域适配研究，采用元加权持续预训练与尺度模块化自蒸馏。

Result: 最强通用模型得分仅53.7%；在匹配问题上，跨尺度问题比单尺度低8.7分；半数问题至多被一个模型解出；模型主要失败模式为倾向认可"看似合理"而非"实际观测"的结论。领域适配将开源模型由36.7%提升至55.4%，追平最强闭源模型，并在公开材料基准上提升，同时在通用基准总体性能上无退化。

Conclusion: FrontierMat为衡量语言模型支撑前沿材料研发的能力提供了测试平台，揭示了通用模型在前沿、跨尺度推理上的显著短板，并表明针对性的领域适配可有效缩小与闭源模型的差距且不损害通用能力。

Abstract: To accelerate materials discovery, language models must be able to address problems at the research frontier. Yet textbook- and examination-derived benchmarks primarily assess established knowledge, leaving performance on frontier research problems largely untested. We introduce FrontierMat, a benchmark of 330 problems drawn from frontier academic and industrial research in fields including aerospace, semiconductors, embodied intelligence and nuclear fusion. Every problem is validated by leading experimentalists and industry experts in its field. Each problem presents a real study and asks the model to infer a withheld outcome or draw a conclusion from selected experimental evidence. Problems are annotated by functional domain, material system and the scale of the governing mechanism; 130 require cross-scale reasoning. The strongest general-purpose model scores only 53.7%, and on matched problems, cross-scale problems score 8.7 points below single-scale ones. Half of the problems are solved by at most one model, and models fail chiefly by endorsing what is plausible rather than what was observed. A domain-adaptation study with meta-weighted continued pretraining and scale-modular self-distillation raises an open-weight model from 36.7% to 55.4%, matching the strongest proprietary model, with improvements on public materials benchmarks and no decline in aggregate performance on general benchmarks. FrontierMat provides a testbed for measuring progress toward language models that support frontier materials R&D.

</details>


### [156] [Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation Improves Small Pointwise LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41149)
*Mohammad Zangooei,Peyvand Teymoori,Mohammad Robati Shirzad,Soroosh Shahtalebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CPA（反事实配对增强）：为每个pointwise样本合成并审核同一查询下标签相反的“反事实”响应对，将点对作为配对任务与原始pointwise数据联合微调小模型评委，但仅以pointwise模式部署；在Qwen3-8B LoRA的6个二元准则上，pointwise宏F1比仅pointwise训练提升2.5点，全面优于模型合并与Gemini 3.6 Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理系统需要对单步输出用pointwise评委按准则打分，小模型因易部署与易专门化而具吸引力。但pointwise标签在微调中欠定决策边界：不同查询的标注响应同时在多个维度上变化，无法揭示哪些特征决定标签，评委难以准确定位准则边界。已有的“分别训练pointwise与pairwise专家再合并”策略存在参数被均匀拉向pairwise专家的问题。

Method: 1）对每个pointwise样本，针对同一查询合成一个意图携带相反标签的反事实响应，并进行审计筛选；2）将验收后的样本对构造为“哪一条响应更满足准则”的pairwise任务；3）在同一模型上对pointwise数据与pairwise数据联合微调，部署时仅使用pointwise模式；4）给出二阶分析论证联合训练优于专家合并：合并在每个参数方向上都以同一比例移向pairwise专家，而联合训练可在pairwise损失平坦处保持pointwise最优点、在其曲率大处吸收pairwise曲率，而CPA的同源、反事实最小差异配对视图正对应这一情形。

Result: 在6个二元准则上分别训练Qwen3-8B LoRA适配器：CPA使准则平均pointwise macro-F1相比仅pointwise训练提升2.5点；在所有测试的合并权重下，于全部6个准则上均优于模型合并；同时比所评估的最强零样本前沿评委Gemini 3.6 Flash高11.8点。

Conclusion: pointwise标签的欠定性可通过在同一查询上构造反事实最小差异配对被显式化解；将配对信号作为辅助任务联合训练、部署时仅保留pointwise模式，是小模型评委专门化的有效且轻量方案，优于事后模型合并且显著超越零样本前沿评委。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly gate individual steps with a pointwise judge that scores a response against a criterion. Small language models are attractive for this role because they can be served and specialized efficiently. Such a judge must locate the criterion's decision boundary, but pointwise labels under-determine that boundary in fine-tuning: labeled responses to different queries differ along many dimensions at once, so they do not reveal which features decide the label. We introduce CPA (Counterfactual Pairwise Augmentation), which makes the deciding difference explicit in training. For each pointwise item, CPA synthesizes and audits a counterfactual response to the same query intended to carry the opposite label. It frames the accepted pair as a pairwise task asking which of the two responses satisfies the criterion. The judge is fine-tuned jointly on both pointwise and pairwise datasets and deployed in pointwise mode only. A second-order analysis shows when this should outperform the prevailing strategy of merging separately trained pointwise and pairwise experts: merging moves every parameter direction toward the pairwise expert by the same fraction, whereas joint training can hold the pointwise optimum wherever the pairwise loss is flat and absorb its curvature where it is sharp. This is the regime that CPA's same-source, counterfactually minimal pairwise view is designed to produce. On separate Qwen3-8B LoRA adapters for six binary criteria, CPA improves criterion-averaged pointwise macro-F1 by 2.5 points over pointwise-only training and outperforms model merging on all six criteria at every tested merge weight. It also surpasses the strongest zero-shot frontier judge we evaluate, Gemini 3.6 Flash, by 11.8 points.

</details>


### [157] [When Should AI Agents Ask? Clarification Requests Under Limited Human Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41129)
*Hanzhao Wang,Shengjie Liu,Zhengchao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多个AI智能体共享一个注意力有限的人类时如何协调"搜索/询问/继续"决策，提出Pairwise Coordination Policy (PCP)：用精确求解一、两任务子问题来估计任务对之间的相互延迟，从而近似求解全局动态规划。理论上在≤2个未决需求时精确最优、全需人类解决时不超过最优2倍；864个合成案例平均与最优差距0.07%，较三种标准询问规则降本18–31%，LLM案例中全部任务正确且平均成本最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体并行执行委派任务、由单人同时监督多个智能体已成常态，而智能体常缺乏任务所需信息（未明说的偏好、约束或规格）。此时它可选择请求人类澄清，或自行检索（如查历史记录），但检索耗时耗力且可能失败。当多个智能体共享一个注意力有限的人类时，各自的询问/检索/继续决策相互影响（排队、相互延迟），需要一个协调机制；而朴素地逐任务独立排序并不最优。

Method: 构建风格化模型：每个智能体可搜索、询问人类，或继续执行不依赖缺失信息的部分；将问题形式化为动态规划。理论分析揭示询问决策依赖的不只是自身不确定性，还需考虑其他待办工作及所有等待中的问题（故不存在逐智能体可加的排序）。据此提出Pairwise Coordination Policy (PCP)：用精确求解的一任务与两任务子问题来估计任意一对任务共享人类时的相互延迟，据此协调全局决策。在864个合成案例及LLM智能体案例研究中评估。

Result: 理论上PCP在至多两个需求未解决时精确最优，在所有未决需求都必须由人类解决时成本不超过最优的2倍。合成基准上PCP相对最优的平均成本差距为0.07%，相对三种标准询问规则平均降低成本18–31%；LLM案例研究中智能体全部正确完成任务，PCP在所有测试设置下平均成本最低。

Conclusion: 在人类注意力稀缺的多智能体并行场景中，询问/检索/继续的决策必须跨智能体联合协调，而非逐任务独立排序；基于成对子问题的分解（PCP）以极低近似损失和可证明的界实现了高效协调，并在合成与LLM实验中验证有效，可作为注意力受限人机协作的实用策略。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly carry out delegated tasks in parallel, with one person overseeing several of them at once. In practice, an agent often lacks information its task requires, such as an unstated preference, constraint, or specification. It can ask the human to clarify, or it can search for the information itself, for example in past records, which takes time and effort and may fail. In this paper, we study how to coordinate these decisions across several agents that share one human with limited attention. We build a stylized model in which each agent can search, ask, or continue the parts of its task that do not depend on the missing information, and we formulate the problem as a dynamic program. We first show that whether an agent should ask depends on more than its own uncertainty: an agent with other work to do can be better off asking earlier, and which question the human should answer first depends on all waiting questions, so no ranking computed agent by agent is optimal. We then propose the Pairwise Coordination Policy (PCP), which uses exact one- and two-task problems to estimate how pairs of tasks delay each other when they share the human. Theoretically, PCP is exactly optimal when at most two requirements remain unresolved and costs at most twice the optimum when every unresolved requirement must be resolved by the human. We evaluate PCP on 864 synthetic cases and in a case study with LLM agents. On the synthetic benchmark, PCP's mean gap to optimal cost is 0.07%, and it reduces cost by an average of 18-31% relative to three standard rules for when to ask. In the LLM case study, agents completed every task correctly, and PCP achieved the lowest mean cost in all tested settings.

</details>


### [158] [QUAL: Quantile Alignment Improves Zero-Shot Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41100)
*Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Jun Seo,Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Dongwan Kang,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Tae Yoon Lim,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无需标签的 QUAL 框架，通过"池相对"分位数对齐改进并组合多个时序基础模型的零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型（TSFM）零样本预测精度跨数据集波动大；集成可降低方差，但零样本部署下无目标标签，无法据此选择或加权成员模型。

Method: 关键发现：模型池不仅可作为最终集成，还可作为概率参考来变换每个成员的预测。据此提出 QUAL：先用池相对参考对各成员预测做分位数对齐，再以 Huber 聚合与重排（rearrangement）得到稳健的组合预测，全过程无需训练、无需修改模型。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 上，分位数对齐使成员平均 MASE 与 CRPS 分别下降 7.4% 和 9.8%；当质量无法预先验证的弱预测器进入候选池时，完整框架仍保持稳健。

Conclusion: 模型池的"参考"作用可独立于集成带来增益，使每个成员预测在整体上均有改善；QUAL 为无标签条件下的 TSFM 改进与组合提供了简单而有效的方案。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) provide zero-shot forecasts for unseen series, but their accuracy varies across datasets. Combining several models can reduce this variability, yet zero-shot deployment provides no target labels for selecting or weighting them. We find that a model pool is useful not only as a final ensemble, but also as a probability reference for transforming each member forecast. Aligning quantiles through this pool-relative reference improves every member forecast in aggregate. Motivated by this finding, we propose QUAL (Quantile alignment), a simple label-free framework for improving and combining diverse TSFMs. QUAL first produces aligned member forecasts, then applies Huber aggregation and rearrangement to obtain a robust combined forecast without training or model modification. On GIFT-Eval, quantile alignment reduces average member MASE and CRPS by 7.4% and 9.8%. The full framework remains robust when weak forecasters enter a candidate pool whose quality cannot be verified in advance. Code is available at https://github.com/junhyeokkang/QUAL.

</details>


### [159] [LanG 2.0: Energy-Efficient Agentic AI for Semi-Automated Security Operations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41072)
*Anes Abdennebi,Nadjia Kara,Laaziz Lahlou,Hakima Ould-Slimane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LanG 2.0 是一个智能体式 SOC 系统，通过结构化防护（而非模型精度）保障安全，证明小型本地模型可低成本、安全地承担大部分威胁检测工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC 需要从海量告警、日志和流量中做出可信且可负担的决策以发现隐蔽威胁；现有前沿模型成本高，作者探究小模型在具备结构性保障时能否以更低成本替代前沿模型。

Method: 系统集成实体锚定关联、规则变更的人工审批与防篡改账本；在5个网络的8个流量数据集、真实XDR告警及多阶段攻击链上测试，并与前代系统及 Claude Opus 5.5 对比。

Result: 相比前代，攻击链关联精度由18.75%升至51.81%，规则接受率由≤60.00%升至98.33%；对比 Claude Opus 5.5，在未见网络上以各网正常流量为基准检测攻击达73.42% F1且无误报（对比58.10% F1、49.00%误报），能耗至少低650倍；微调模型在告警分类与规则编写上统计等效，能耗低5.6–59倍；处理333万条记录仅需6.4分钟（每小时超3000万条），且防护机制抵御了提示注入、标签投毒与账本篡改。

Conclusion: LanG 2.0 展示了实际可扩展性，其结构性防护使小型本地模型能够在可负担且安全的前提下承担大部分 SOC 检测任务。

Abstract: Security operations centers (SOCs) need to turn terabytes of alerts, logs and flows into trusted, affordable decisions to unveil hidden threats. We present LanG 2.0, an agentic system that detects threats, correlates evidence and writes rules, investigating whether small local models can replace frontier models affordably and safely when structural safeguards, not model accuracy, provide the guarantees. It incorporates entity-anchored correlation, human approval of rule changes and a tamper-evident ledger. Tests span eight flow datasets from five networks, real XDR alerts and multistage campaigns. Against its predecessor, campaign correlation precision rises from 18.75% to 51.81%, and rule acceptance from at most 60.00% to 98.33%. Against Claude Opus 5.5, scoring flows against each network's normal traffic detects attacks on unseen networks at 73.42% F1 without false positives, versus 58.10% with 49.00% false positives, for at least 650 times less energy. Finetuned models statistically match it on alert classification and rule writing for 5.6 to 59 times less energy. The work demonstrates LanG's practical scalability, processing 3.33 million records in 6.4 minutes, over 30 million hourly, and its safeguards resisted prompt injection, label poisoning and ledger tampering, so small local models can carry most SOC detection affordably and safely.

</details>


### [160] [ReplayRCA: Measuring Post-hoc Diagnosis and Live Oversight of Agent Failures by Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41025)
*Jeremy Canale,Fabien Soulis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReplayRCA 基准，以"因果修复"（干预后成功率减去配对安慰剂）而非标注一致度来评测模型对多步任务失败的诊断与修复能力，涵盖事后复盘与在线监督两条赛道，并基于确定性程序化环境 OpsWorld。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于标注的归因基准存在"末步捷径"等偏差，且与真实修复效果脱节，需用因果干预直接衡量诊断质量。

Method: 将环境回滚至所选步骤之前，用诊断者提出的动作替换原动作，由同一智能体继续执行；得分为因果修复，即续跑成功率减去重放原始动作的安慰剂对照成功率。评测分两赛道：事后复盘（可见完整失败轨迹）与在线监督（运行中、在不可逆动作前仅干预一次，且不知是否会失败）。任务来自 OpsWorld——确定性、程序化生成的后台办公环境，含六级难度，最高级为多模态（二维码、手写）。

Result: 在 62 个校准过的系统性失败上，13 个前沿模型中最佳的 Grok 4.7 达 +51.3 点因果修复；前八名模型统计上不可区分，两家最大厂商的旗舰分列第 8 与第 12，5 项无任何模型修复成功。强制强模型在最末步干预会损失 17.1 点，说明末步捷径不可迁移。87 次运行中，两个最佳在线监督者统计打平，其中一个为小型开源权重模型（DeepSeek V4.1 Flash）；而事后诊断最优者之一（GPT-6.1 Sol）却是最差的在线监督者（-14.5 点，p=0.002）。

Conclusion: 以干预效果为核心的评价能揭示基于标注一致的评测所掩盖的差距；事后诊断能力不能可靠预示在线监督表现，二者需分别评测。环境与评测代码已开源。

Abstract: When a language-model agent fails a multi-step task, can another model find the decisive mistake and fix it with a single action? We introduce ReplayRCA, a benchmark that scores such diagnoses by intervention rather than by agreement with annotations: the environment is restored to the state preceding the chosen step, the proposed action replaces the agent's own, and the same agent continues. The score, causal repair, is the continuation success rate minus that of a paired placebo that replays the agent's original action through the same mechanism. Two tracks share this protocol: a post-hoc autopsy, where the diagnoser sees the whole failed run and is told that it failed, and live oversight, where a supervisor watches the run unfold and may intervene once, before an irreversible action, without knowing whether the run will fail. Tasks come from OpsWorld, a deterministic, procedurally generated back-office environment with six difficulty levels, the last of which is multimodal (QR codes, handwriting). On 62 calibrated systematic failures, the best of 13 frontier models (Grok 4.7) reaches a causal repair of +51.3 points; the top eight models are statistically indistinguishable, the flagship models of the two largest vendors rank 8th and 12th, and 5 items are repaired by no model. Forcing a strong model to intervene at the last step costs it 17.1 points, so the last-step shortcut of annotation-based attribution benchmarks does not transfer. On 87 runs, the two best live supervisors are statistically tied and one of them is a small open-weight model (DeepSeek V4.1 Flash); the supervisor that is among the best post-hoc diagnosers (GPT-6.1 Sol) is the weakest live one (-14.5 points on the same runs, p = 0.002), so post-hoc diagnostic performance can be a poor proxy for live oversight. The environment and the evaluation code are released at https://github.com/jeremy1392/RCAreplay.

</details>


### [161] [Dynamical Memory Transformer: Selective Recurrent Memories with Constant Cost Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41850)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态记忆Transformer（DMT）：编码端用带复对角递归矩阵的动态系统作为并行记忆单元、输入依赖泄漏率控制更新，解码端用注意力读取；递归可结合结合扫描实现O(T)总工作量、O(log T)并行深度，自回归推理时状态尺寸固定。在认知任务上最优、语言建模接近Transformer但落后Mamba，选择性复制上长度外推更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer保留显式序列历史、可被注意力直接检索，但KV-Cache大小与新token计算成本随上下文长度增长；状态空间模型把过去压缩为固定大小的递归状态，降低存储与计算开销却牺牲了可寻址性。作者希望兼顾固定内存成本与注意力式信息读取。

Method: DMT为混合架构，将序列编码与信息读取解耦：编码器由多个记忆单元构成，每个单元为实现为动态系统、具有复对角递归矩阵的递归结构，更新由输入依赖的泄漏率控制；解码器用注意力读取并组合这些单元输出，而不回溯token历史。递归形式支持结合扫描（associative scan），总计算量O(T)、并行深度O(log T)、维度固定；自回归推理时递归状态与卷积缓存尺寸恒定。

Result: 与Mamba和Transformer在认知任务与语言建模任务上对比：DMT在若干认知任务上取得最佳结果，语言建模性能接近Transformer但落后于Mamba；在selective copy任务上，DMT表现出比Mamba与Transformer更强的长度外推能力。

Conclusion: 并行动态记忆单元是一种在序列上保留与利用信息的有前景机制，可在固定状态规模下提供注意力式读取；但其语言建模能力仍不及Mamba，有待进一步改进。

Abstract: Transformers retain an explicit history of the sequence, allowing attention to directly retrieve past representations. However, the size of the KV-Cache and the cost of a new token increase with the context length. State Space Models compress the past into a fixed-size recurrent state, at the cost of reduced addressability. We present the Dynamical Memory Transformer (DMT), a hybrid architecture that separates sequence encoding from information reading. The encoder uses several memory units, each implemented by a dynamical system with a complex diagonal recurrent matrix. An input-dependent leak rate controls their updates. The decoder then uses attention to read and combine their outputs, without going through the token history. The formulation of the recurrence allows an associative scan with O(T ) total work and O(log T ) parallel depth, with fixed dimensions. During autoregressive inference, the recurrent states and the convolution cache remain fixed in size. We compare DMT with Mamba and Transformer on cognitive and language modeling tasks, and show its strengths and limitations: DMT achieves the best results on several cognitive tasks and language modeling performance close to that of Transformer, but remains behind Mamba in language modeling. On selective copy, DMT also shows stronger length extrapolation than Mamba and Transformer. These results demonstrate that parallel dynamical memory units are a promising mechanism for retaining and using information over sequences.

</details>


### [162] [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970)
*Jun Liu,Dajie Sun,Zhenglun Kong,Zhenjiang Fan,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Wenbin Zhangg,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文以"前提优先、预注册"的方法审计世界-动作模型测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的三条前提链：决策信号、实际效率与评估完整性，结果显示选择信号的可用余量很小、token 剪枝的端到端加速远低于理论 FLOPs 收益，并顺带发现并修复了一处静默的渲染损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（候选选择、自适应算力分配）被广泛用于提升机器人控制性能，但这类收益只有在三个前提成立时才可信：(1) 候选之间确实携带可利用的决策信号；(2) 名义上的算力节省能转化为真实的端到端加速；(3) 评估本身可靠。作者认为仅凭成功率无法验证这些前提，故需要逐条审计。

Method: 在 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上进行预注册（pre-registered）、前提优先的审计实验：对比学习型价值选择器与随机选择；用 hindsight oracle 估计选择性能上界；统计状态级候选结果的一致性以衡量选择余量；测量 token 剪枝的每前向 FLOPs 与实际端到端加速（含排除解码的路径）；自适应分配实验设置统计功效门限；并排查渲染损坏对评估的影响。

Result: 干净数据上，学习型价值选择器比随机选择差 1.66 pp；hindsight oracle 仅提升 1.40 pp，且 episode 级置信区间跨零；82% 的状态下不同候选结果完全相同，选择余量极小。token 剪枝将单次前向 FLOPs 最多降低 47%，但在部署路径上实测端到端加速至多 1.15×（排除解码路径为 1.25×），未达 1.5× 目标；自适应分配实验在功效门限下结论不确定。此外发现一处静默渲染损坏，其在计算密集型规划路径上发生率显著升高，但不影响官方 direct-policy 结果，作者给出检测器、修复与脚本。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展应作为"前提链"（决策信号—可实现效率—评估完整性）来审计，而非只看成功率；本配置下所宣称的选择收益与效率收益均缺乏证据支持。结论范围限定于所测试的配置。

Abstract: World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>


### [163] [Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968)
*Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina,Hung Nguyen,Nasib Ullah,Jinbin Zhang,Nilesh Gupta,Inderjit S. Dhillon,Rohit Babbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ETAPS：渐进收紧全局稀疏约束、用固定小预算稀疏delta训练，在50%–90%稀疏度下各项指标优于ELSA，困惑度最多降42%，且状态更小、更省算力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM高稀疏度剪枝存在质量-成本权衡：一次性剪枝廉价但高稀疏度下质量骤降；直接端到端优化掩码的方法（如SOTA的ELSA）虽质量高但需模型规模的优化器状态与辅助变量。

Method: ETAPS采用渐进式剪枝与稀疏自适应：分阶段收紧全局稀疏约束，同时以小型固定预算训练稀疏增量（delta）；核心观察是逐步移除权重使每步剪枝幅度足够小，稀疏自适应可补偿，从而由自适应模型提供端到端梯度指导下一步剪枝决策，无需模型规模的优化器状态。

Result: 在125M至13B的六个模型上，ETAPS在50%–90%全部稀疏度下困惑度均低于ELSA，最多降低42%；90%稀疏度下在单张H200上剪枝LLaMA2-7B（ELSA需四张），持久状态小5.0×，GPU小时减少49%。

Conclusion: 渐进剪枝配合稀疏自适应可在极低额外成本下实现极稀疏度区间的高质量，为高要求场景提供低成本方向。

Abstract: Weight pruning can reduce the storage and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), but preserving quality at high sparsity levels remains challenging. Existing methods face a quality-cost trade-off in this regime. One-shot pruning is cheap but degrades sharply as sparsity increases, even when followed by post-pruning recovery. A growing line of work instead optimizes the mask directly from the dense model under the end-to-end objective; among these methods, [Lee et al.,2026], the recent state of the art at extreme sparsity, reaches high quality but at the cost of model-sized optimizer state and auxiliary variables. To address this gap, we propose ETAPS, which prunes gradually and adapts sparsely: it tightens a global sparsity constraint in progressive stages while training a sparse delta with a small, fixed budget. Our key observation is that gradual weight removal keeps each pruning step small enough for sparse adaptation to compensate, allowing the adapted model to provide end-to-end gradients that guide the next pruning decision without model-sized optimizer state. Across six models from 125M to 13B parameters, ETAPS achieves lower perplexity than ELSA at every evaluated sparsity from 50% to 90%, with perplexity reductions of up to 42%. ETAPS is also far cheaper: at 90% sparsity, it prunes LLaMA2-7B on a single H200, where ELSA uses four, with $5.0\times$ smaller total persistent state and 49% fewer GPU-hours. Together, our findings point to a promising direction for achieving high quality in demanding regimes such as extreme sparsity at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [164] [SAT-ACT: Learning CDCL Branching from Outcome-Derived Action Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41290)
*Zhenchao Sun,Chongyang Tao,Ping Lu,Tongkai Lu,Jianling Gao,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAT-ACT：从同状态下独立分支动作的执行结果中提取动作偏好（三准则，证据不足时不强制排序），训练状态条件网络预测分支优先级以直接引导CDCL，不需在线rollout，并辅以非支配启发式动作监督；在三类SAT实例上降低搜索代价，提升SAT竞赛基准的PAR-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式SAT分支方法依赖静态结构特征或求解器反馈/执行轨迹等动态信号，对"状态依赖的分支效果"证据不足；且不同结果度量可能倾向不同分支选择，用预定义标量指标强制排序会引入偏差。

Method: 1) 在同一求解器状态下分别执行不同分支动作的独立rollout；2) 用三个互补的结果比较准则提取动作偏好，证据不足或冲突时保持"未判定"而非强制排序；3) 用状态条件网络从偏好中学习分支优先级，推理时直接引导CDCL搜索、无需在线rollout；4) 引入非支配启发式动作监督，仅当求解器所选动作在偏好下仍非支配时才作为训练目标。

Result: 求解器级实验显示SAT-ACT在三个SAT实例族上稳定降低CDCL搜索代价，并在SAT Competition基准上改善PAR-2。

Conclusion: 以结果导出的成对偏好（而非标量指标）作为监督信号，可更可靠地学习状态相关的分支优先级；配合非支配过滤的启发式监督，能在不增加在线开销的情况下提升CDCL求解效率。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches have increasingly been explored for SAT solving, with particular attention to improving branching decisions in conflict-driven clause learning (CDCL) solvers. These approaches derive branching guidance either from static structural properties of SAT formulas or from dynamic signals obtained through solver feedback and execution traces. However, these forms of supervision provide limited evidence about state-dependent branching effects, while different outcome measures may favor different branching choices. To address these limitations, we propose SAT-ACT, an outcome-derived learning framework for CDCL branching. We design three complementary outcome comparison criteria to extract action preferences from independent branching action rollouts initiated at the same solver state. The criteria identify preferences supported by the outcomes and leave comparisons unresolved when the evidence is insufficient or conflicting, instead of forcing an ordering using a predefined scalar metric. A state-conditioned network learns branching priorities from the extracted preferences and uses them to guide CDCL search directly without online action rollouts. We further introduce non-dominated heuristic action supervision, which retains the solver-selected action as a training target only when it remains non-dominated under these preferences. Solver-level experiments show that SAT-ACT consistently reduces CDCL search costs on three SAT families and improves PAR-2 on the SAT Competition benchmarks.

</details>


### [165] [Multi-Test Nation Bias Benchmarking of LLMs with Explicit Unbiased Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41486)
*Jonghyeon Choi,Yeonjun Choi,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用722份联合国安理会(UNSC)官方记录、五项互补测试，并首次为每项测试显式定义无偏参考基准，量化评估4个LLM对五个常任理事国的国家级偏向，发现一致的"对中俄偏反对、对英法美偏支持"的宏观模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究指出LLM存在系统性的国家级偏向，会给国际关系(IR)应用带来操作风险；但现有评估大多缺乏明确的无偏参考（ground truth），导致难以进行完全量化与跨设定比较。

Method: 构建扩展语料（722份UNSC官方记录），设计五项互补测试覆盖更广的偏向表达渠道：观点排序(WOW)、关联排序(AK+-)、投票模拟(PVS)、反对归因(F-Oppose)、框架化(Headliner)；可为每项测试显式定义无偏状态，对难以设定ground truth的WOW与AK+-，提出基于可观测投票行为的"行动式伪无偏状态"以构建可量化基线；在UNSC五个常任理事国上基准测试GPT-4o-mini、Llama 3.3-70B、Mistral-22B-Small、Qwen3-30B共4个模型。

Result: 跨五项测试出现与测试无关的宏观模式：相对各自参考基线，模型输出对中国和俄罗斯偏向反对(红)侧，对英国、美国、法国偏向支持(蓝)侧。

Conclusion: 结果表明应以有明确参考点、接地且多测试的基准来审计面向国际关系的LLM部署。

Abstract: While prior work has highlighted that systematic nation-level bias in large language models (LLMs) can pose operational risks for international relations (IR) applications, many existing evaluations still lack a clearly specified unbiased reference (ground truth), limiting fully quantitative and cross-setting measurement. Using an expanded corpus of 722 official UN Security Council (UNSC) records, we measure nation-level bias with five complementary tests, thereby extending prior approaches to cover a broader set of bias expression channels: opinion ranking (WOW), association sorting (AK+-), vote simulation (PVS), opposition attribution (F-Oppose), and framing (Headliner). To enable grounded bias evaluation, we explicitly define an unbiased status for each test; in particular, for WOW and AK+- where ground truth is difficult to specify, we propose an action-based pseudo unbiased status derived from observable voting behavior to construct a quantifiable baseline. Benchmarking 4 LLMs (GPT-4o-mini, Llama 3.3-70B, Mistral-22B-Small, and Qwen3-30B) across the five permanent members of the UNSC, we find a test-agnostic macro-pattern: relative to the reference baselines defined for each test, model outputs lean toward the opposition (red) side for China and Russia and toward the supportive (blue) side for the U.K., the U.S., and France. These results underscore the need to audit IR-facing LLM deployments with grounded, multi-test benchmarks tied to explicit reference points.

</details>


### [166] [Evidence-Guided Recovery for AI Agents with Ambiguous Tool Outcomes and Partial Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41453)
*Genliang Zhu,Xiaobo Chen,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将工具超时导致的"结果歧义/部分执行"建模为在已批准义务、提供方能力与公开证据兼容状态上的证据引导型任务恢复问题，用统一的可采纳性检查与终端证书区分真实目标满足与可公开证明的安全完成，并在有限研究与预注册留出对比中验证规划建议优于证据提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具超时使AI代理面对模糊的工具结果与部分执行状态：某动作可能已失败、已提交或仍在进行中，而其他工作流步骤已经完成。现有范式缺少对这类歧义的显式形式化与可证明的恢复判据，本文据此提出证据引导的任务恢复框架。

Method: 形式化"已批准义务+提供方能力+与公开证据兼容的状态"上的恢复问题；引入共享可采纳性检查（密钥过期、授权时序、依赖关系、残留效应）与终端证书（区分真实目标满足与公开支持的安全完成）；已知模型实现将标准观测相关的规划与独立实现的效果历史裁决器结合。研究一：37项任务的有限研究，对比规划与三个主动控制。研究二：预注册的按任务留出比较，在两个本地模型组合上比较规划建议与证据提示。

Result: 研究一中规划与三个主动控制各认证28次完成，节省4个合成费用单位但带来额外计算开销。研究二在19个任务族的58个配对单元中，建议组认证30次完成对9次，差36.2个百分点（精确族符号翻转检验 p=0.0078），提案数272对399；全部116条轨迹通过独立重放；两个模型均显示正的完成差异。

Conclusion: 规划建议界面在共同防护机制下显著提升可认证完成率，但该结果仅衡量所评估的接口，搜索式与规则式建议间仍需单独的匹配比较；未知与交接结果保持显式暴露，保证范围限定于所声明的提供方能力，而非任意的第三方恰好一次执行。

Abstract: Tool timeouts can leave AI agents facing ambiguous tool outcomes and partial execution: an action may have failed, committed or remained in flight while other workflow steps have already completed. We formulate evidence-guided task recovery over approved obligations, provider capabilities and states compatible with public evidence. A shared admissibility check handles key expiry, authorization timing, dependencies and residual effects; a terminal certificate separates actual goal satisfaction from publicly supported safe completion. The known-model implementation combines standard observation-contingent planning with a separately implemented effect-history adjudicator. In a finite study of 37 assignments, planning and three active controls each certify 28 completions; four saved synthetic fee units incur additional computation. A preregistered assignment-held-out comparison then evaluates planning advice against evidence prompting with two local model bundles. Across 58 paired units in 19 previously developed task families, advice certifies 30 completions versus 9, a 36.2-percentage-point difference (exact family sign-flip p=0.0078), using 272 proposals versus 399. All 116 trajectories pass independent replay. Both models exhibit a positive completion difference. The comparison measures the evaluated advice interface under a common shield; search-versus-rule advice requires a separate matched comparison. Unknown and handoff outcomes remain explicit, with guarantees tied to the declared provider capabilities rather than arbitrary third-party exactly-once execution.

</details>


### [167] [CryoAtlas: A Large Curated Dataset and Unified Benchmark for Cryo-EM Atomic Model Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41411)
*Mingrui Li,Minzhang Li,Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Zhangzhi Xiong,Sixian Shen,Jiakai Zhang,Yuan Pei,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CryoAtlas：面向冷冻电镜密度引导原子模型构建的严格筛选数据集与统一基准（17,809条map-model-sequence，618目标评测集）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已加速冷冻电镜（cryo-EM）图的自动化原子模型构建，但缺少大规模高质量数据集与标准化评测基准，制约了进展。

Method: 整合并筛选EMDB与PDB条目（截至2026年3月），构建17,809条图-模型-序列条目，其中训练集16,657条；从中构建618目标基准，并制定同时评估结构精度、模型-图拟合度与立体化学合理性的统一评测协议；在此框架下对比学习类方法、Phenix与AlphaFold3，并分析分辨率、序列长度、蛋白类别与序列一致性的影响；以ModelAngelo CNN组件重训作为案例研究。

Result: 提供了大规模筛选数据集与统一基准；系统量化了各方法在不同分辨率、序列长度、蛋白类别与序列一致性条件下的建模精度差异；在CryoAtlas上重训ModelAngelo的CNN组件可提升基准性能。

Conclusion: CryoAtlas为cryo-EM原子模型构建研究提供了开放、可复现的基础资源与评测平台（项目页：https://oulling.github.io/cryoatlas/）。

Abstract: Deep learning has accelerated automated atomic model building from cryo-EM maps, yet progress remains limited by the lack of large, high-quality datasets and standardized evaluation benchmarks. We address this gap by introducing CryoAtlas, a rigorously curated dataset and unified benchmark for density-guided atomic model building of protein chains. By integrating and filtering EMDB and PDB entries (up to March 2026), CryoAtlas provides 17,809 map-model-sequence entries, including a curated 16,657-entry training split. From this resource, we construct a 618-target benchmark with a shared evaluation protocol that jointly assesses structural accuracy, model-to-map fit, and stereochemical validity. Using this framework, we benchmark state-of-the-art learning-based methods against Phenix and AlphaFold3, comprehensively analyzing the impact of map resolution, sequence length, protein category and sequence identity on modeling accuracy. As a case study, retraining the ModelAngelo CNN component on CryoAtlas improves performance on the benchmark. CryoAtlas provides an open, reproducible foundation for future cryo-EM atomic model building research. Project details and resource release updates are available at https://oulling.github.io/cryoatlas/.

</details>


### [168] [Cross-Interface Authorization for AI Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41397)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向固定版本计算机使用部署的“profile 有界协议”，通过路由枚举、规范效果归一化、路由绑定的准备票据与提交时多要素复核，结合接收回执，实现跨接口的策略决策不变性与提交期状态安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 computer-use 代理可通过图形界面、命令行、API、脚本、MCP 服务器等多种路由产生作用。策略层决策可做到接口无关，但可执行授权并非如此：其他路由可绕过中介、改动安全相关默认值，或在授权状态已变化后提交，因而需要一种能约束“具体可执行路由”的协议。

Method: 协议流程：枚举承载效果的路由与持久化 sink；校验 route-to-sink 的强制切面；将每个具体请求归一化为规范效果；签发路由绑定的 prepare 票据；在 commit 时再次校验路由、效果、授权、轨迹、profile、清单与权威状态，并要求一致的 sink 回执。在显式假设下给出形式化证明。

Result: 参考 profile 注册 12 个效果族、5 类接口、60 条路由；两种结构不同的运行时映射在 1,732 个场景上产生 3,464 条轨迹，含 960 次提交、2,312 次拒绝、192 次重新准备后提交。独立检查器接受全部保留轨迹与配对、拒绝 26/26 语义回归；覆盖与归一化套件分别拒绝 24/24 与 720/720 变异体；168/168 提交探针符合声明结果；冻结源码审计在 12 个文件上通过 52 项检查；源形状适配器精化提交 48/48 打点调用、拒绝 15/15 未绑定用例。

Conclusion: 所提保证严格绑定于声明的 profile 及其效果语义，实现了 profile 有界精化、匹配效果下的决策不变性、抗替代路由洗白与声明式跨面重组、提交期状态安全及回执支撑的结果。

Abstract: Computer-use agents act through graphical interfaces, command lines, APIs, scripts, and Model Context Protocol servers. A policy decision can be interface-invariant while its executable authorization is not: another route can bypass mediation, alter a security-relevant default, or commit after the authorized state has changed. We present a profile-bounded protocol for a fixed, versioned computer-use deployment. It enumerates effect-bearing routes and durable sinks, checks a route-to-sink enforcement cut, normalizes each concrete request to a canonical effect, issues a route-bound prepare ticket, and verifies the route, effect, grant, trace, profile, inventory, and authoritative state again at commit. A committed result requires an agreeing sink receipt. Under explicit assumptions, we prove profile-bounded refinement, matched-effect decision invariance, resistance to alternate-route laundering and declared cross-surface recomposition, commit-time state safety, and receipt-grounded outcomes.
  The reference profile registers 12 effect families, five interfaces, and 60 routes. Two structurally distinct runtime mappings produce 3,464 traces over 1,732 scenarios each: 960 commits, 2,312 denials, and 192 commits after re-preparation. An independent checker accepts every retained trace and pair and rejects 26/26 semantic regressions; coverage and normalizer suites reject 24/24 and 720/720 mutants; and 168/168 commit probes match their declared outcomes. A frozen-source audit passes 52 checks over 12 files from two independent hybrid runtimes and one public authorization substrate. Source-shaped adapter refinements commit 48/48 instrumented calls and reject 15/15 unbound cases. The resulting guarantees bind the declared profile and its effect semantics.

</details>


### [169] [Ask on Demand: Targeted Peer Communication for Parallel Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41378)
*Haofu Yang,Hongyang Yin,Ran Bi,Faguo Wu,Peng Xue,Feng Zhou,Han Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Ask on Demand（AOD）通信框架：并行LLM agent在产生具体信息需求时按需向同伴求助，检索其执行历史中的相关证据并生成定制回复；用轻量门控决定何时求助，在AppWorld与WebShop上提升任务表现且推理开销低于现有信息共享基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行LLM agent通过多条轨迹探索复杂任务，但有用发现往往孤立在各自的执行历史中。现有共享方法在他人需求尚不明确时就先行筛选或摘要信息，容易遗漏后续才变得重要的细节，导致通信与agent不断演变的信息需求错位。

Method: AOD在agent出现具体信息需求时发起定向求助：请求获批后，从同伴执行历史中检索相关证据、选择合适的同伴，并生成针对该请求量身定制的回复。为判断何时求助值得，作者用成对的团队续写轨迹训练一个轻量门控，监督信号由最终团队结果与推理成本共同导出。

Result: 在AppWorld和WebShop上，AOD相对独立并行执行一致提升任务表现，同时推理成本低于现有信息共享基线。

Conclusion: 将同伴通信与agent不断演变的信息需求对齐，可实现更有效、更高效的协作，说明“按需求助”优于预先筛选式共享。

Abstract: Parallel LLM agents tackle complex tasks by exploring multiple trajectories, yet useful discoveries remain isolated in individual execution histories. Existing sharing methods often select or summarize information before other agents' needs become clear, potentially overlooking details that become important later. We propose Ask on Demand (AOD), a communication framework that enables agents to seek targeted peer assistance when specific information needs arise. Upon an approved request, AOD retrieves relevant evidence from peer execution histories, selects a suitable peer, and generates a response tailored to the request. To determine when such assistance is worthwhile, we train a lightweight gate using paired team continuations, with supervision derived from final team outcomes and inference cost. Experiments on AppWorld and WebShop demonstrate that AOD consistently improves task performance over independent parallel execution while achieving lower inference cost than existing information-sharing baselines. These findings highlight the benefits of aligning peer communication with agents' evolving information needs to achieve more effective and efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [170] [LEAP: Efficient LLM Inference Scheduling with Generalizable Length Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41359)
*Kwan Yan,Jialun Zhong,Yanzeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEAP：利用服务模型 prefill 隐状态经线性读出预测输出长度，并用递归最小二乘在测试时在线适配，预测/更新在 CPU 上异步执行，从而提升长度感知调度的泛化性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长度预测器在大模型规模下仍会在域/任务迁移时排序性能下降，重新训练代价高；且预测开销本身会抵消调度收益。因此需要兼顾泛化性与效率的长度预测方案。

Method: 复用服务模型的 prefill 隐状态（内含输出长度信息），通过线性读出提取长度信号；测试时用已完成请求作监督，以递归最小二乘法在线更新读出层，无需梯度计算与历史数据回放；预测与更新异步运行于 CPU，并与剩余 GPU prefill 重叠，实现提前的批次排序。

Result: 跨域迁移中保持较强排序性能；在较大域偏移下在线适配使 Spearman 相关系数提升 64.7%；服务实验中每 token 平均延迟相比所评估的长度预测器降低 15.9–40.8%，吞吐高 1.9%，预测器参数量比最强基线少 30,569 倍。

Conclusion: 复用隐状态+线性读出+在线递归最小二乘+CPU 异步执行，是一种低成本、可快速适配的长度预测范式，能在几乎零参数与可忽略开销下显著改善长度感知调度的延迟与泛化表现。

Abstract: Length-aware scheduling reduces request latency in LLM serving by prioritizing shorter requests. However, despite their large model sizes, length predictors can suffer degraded ranking performance under domain and task shifts, and adapting them can require costly retraining. Their prediction overhead can further offset the benefits of scheduling. To improve the generalization and efficiency of length-aware scheduling, we propose \textbf{LEAP}, a length prediction system with fast online adaptation and asynchronous CPU execution. LEAP reuses the serving model's prefill hidden states, which contain information about the output length, and extracts this signal through a linear readout. To adapt to substantial domain and task shifts, it updates the readout at test time through recursive least squares, using completed requests as supervision without gradient computation or historical-data replay. Prediction and updates run asynchronously on CPU, while prediction and initial batch ordering overlap the remaining GPU prefill. Across the evaluated cross-domain transfers, LEAP retains strong ranking performance, and online adaptation improves Spearman correlation by 64.7\% under a larger domain shift. Serving experiments show that LEAP reduces average per-token latency by 15.9--40.8\% compared with the evaluated length predictors, while achieving 1.9\% higher throughput with $30{,}569\times$ fewer predictor parameters than the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>


### [171] [Multimodal In-Context Learning with Explicit Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41754)
*Yuquan Li,Xinyang Jiang,Yuhao Xue,Cairong Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERIM框架，通过显式归纳示例中的潜在规则来增强多模态上下文学习（MICL），并采用混合冷启动SFT与强化学习的两阶段训练，在多种MICL设定下取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MICL任务常缺乏明确上下文定义，需要从演示中推断潜在规则；现有方法多把MICL当作直接的输入-输出映射，忽视了对规则的显式归纳与推理，从而限制了性能与可解释性。

Method: 1）利用MLLM的推理能力构建以规则为中心的MICL数据集；2）为规则归纳引入显式监督；3）设计两阶段训练策略（混合冷启动监督微调 + 强化学习）微调MLLM，使其执行显式规则归纳。

Result: 在多种MICL设定下实验均取得一致的性能提升，并验证正确归纳潜在规则可直接带来更强、更稳定的表现。

Conclusion: 显式规则归纳是提升MICL性能与可解释性的有效路径，ERIM提供了一个统一、可推广的框架。

Abstract: A central challenge in multimodal in-context learning (MICL) arises from tasks that are ill-defined without context and require uncovering the underlying rules from demonstrations. Existing methods predominantly treat MICL as direct input-output mapping, overlooking the importance of explicitly inducing and reasoning over such rules, limiting both performance and interpretability. We propose ERIM, Explicit Rule-Induction for Multimodal In-Context Learning, a unified framework that enhances MICL by explicitly inducing the latent rules governing demonstration samples. Leveraging the reasoning ability of multi-modal large language model (MLLM), ERIM constructs rule-centric MICL datasets, introduces explicit supervision for rule induction, and fine-tunes MLLM to perform explicit rule-induction via a two-stage training strategy that combines mixed cold-start supervised fine-tuning with reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments show consistent performance improvements across diverse MICL settings and demonstrate that correctly inducing underlying rules directly leads to stronger and more stable performance.

</details>


### [172] [Radial Kernel Attention for Long-Range Modeling in Polymer Machine Learning Force Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41675)
*Wenbo Fu,Chenghui Mao,Chengwen Hu,Haifeng Lv,Zhou Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出径向核注意力（RKA），以线性成本实现聚合物力场中的全局长程建模，在OPoly26上显著降低能量与力预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物链及其周围环境的长程通信受限于局部邻居图，而显式全对注意力代价为二次复杂度，需在局部性与全局建模间取得折中。

Method: RKA将原子到簇质心的距离映射为可学习径向特征，并将其重叠与内容相关的query/key结合；通过因子化聚合与读出，在固定特征维度下实现随原子数线性的全局特征交换。作者将不变标量更新插入EquiformerV3局部模块之间，并对含径向坐标依赖的总能量求导以获得保守力。在OPoly26官方训练划分上训练12个epoch，随后在完整官方验证划分上直接评估有限聚合物衍生簇的能量与力预测。

Result: 同规模5M对比中，RKA使每原子能量MAE与力MAE分别降低67.4%和36.3%。20M模型在相同验证结构上达到总能MAE 15.81 meV、力MAE 2.619 meV/Å、每原子能量MAE 0.1182 meV/atom，并与UMA-S-1.2及UMA-S-1.2.1进行比较。
