Conclusion: RKA提供了一条紧凑的全局通信通路，可在评估的聚合物衍生簇上改善能量与力预测，为长程建模提供了线性复杂度的可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce Radial Kernel Attention (RKA) for long-range modeling in polymer machine learning force fields. Local neighbor graphs constrain communication across polymer chains and their surrounding environments, while explicit all-pair attention incurs quadratic cost. RKA maps atomic distances from the cluster centroid to learned radial features and combines their overlap with content-dependent queries and keys. Factorized aggregation and readout enable global feature exchange at linear cost in atom count for fixed feature dimensions. We insert the invariant scalar update between EquiformerV3 local blocks and obtain conservative forces by differentiating the total energy, including its radial coordinate dependence. We evaluate energy and force prediction for finite polymer-derived clusters in OPoly26. Our models are trained on the official training split for 12 epochs and evaluated directly after training on the complete official validation split. A matched 5M comparison shows that RKA reduces per-atom energy and force MAEs by 67.4% and 36.3%, respectively. The 20M RKA model is compared with UMA-S-1.2 and UMA-S-1.2.1 on the same validation structures. The 20M model attains a total-energy MAE of 15.81 meV and a force MAE of 2.619 meV/angstrom; its per-atom energy MAE is 0.1182 meV/atom. RKA provides a compact global communication pathway that improves energy and force prediction on the evaluated polymer-derived clusters.

</details>


### [173] [Verify--Backtrack Loop: Structured Precondition Recovery for Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41564)
*Mingyang Liu,Qingcan Kang,Shixiong Kai,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Xin Li,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VBL（Verify–Backtrack Loop）是一种免训练的ReAct扩展，将前置条件发现与失败诊断转化为结构化的规划状态更新：以有界目标栈维护任务分解，通过“双视野验证＋类型化失败归因”触发Push/Pop/retry/BacktrackTo等显式操作，在AppWorld、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld上一致优于ReAct与反思类控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务的核心难点不在轨迹长度，而在“恢复”能力：当前置条件缺失或早期子任务分解失败时，换一个看似可行的动作往往无济于事。ReAct及后续反思/自适应规划方法虽支持局部纠错，但把恢复过程表达为自由文本，缺乏显式控制状态来关联终端目标、当前活动目标与失败来源，导致恢复缺乏结构性依据。

Method: 提出免训练框架VBL，作为ReAct的结构化扩展。其维护一个“有界目标栈”表示任务分解；在目标激活、执行失败或环境实质状态变化时，联合执行双视野验证：(1) 当前活动目标是否对终端目标有贡献（目标一致性/相关性），(2) 在当前状态下是否可行（可行性）。结合类型化失败归因（typed failure attribution），触发四类显式操作：Push（压入子目标）、Pop（弹出已完成/无效目标）、retry（重试）、BacktrackTo parent（回溯至父目标）。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-9B、GPT-4o、DeepSeek-V3.2三个骨干模型与AppWorld、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld三个基准的所有“骨干×基准”组合上，VBL均优于ReAct与基于反思的控制方法；在较难的AppWorld设置上，任务成功率较ReAct最高提升22.07个百分点。消融实验显示，移除验证或移除失败诊断任一机制都会导致性能下降，部分设置下甚至低于ReAct，表明二者互补。

Conclusion: 可靠的长时程交互依赖于对“规划状态”的修正，而不仅仅是对下一步动作的修正；将验证与失败诊断显式化、结构化为规划状态更新，是提升语言智能体恢复能力的有效且无需训练的途径。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, where actions often depend on latent preconditions. The core difficulty is not trajectory length but recovery: when a prerequisite is missing or an earlier decomposition fails, trying another plausible action rarely helps. ReAct and later reflection or adaptive-planning methods support local correction, but express recovery as free-form text, with no explicit control state linking the terminal goal, the active goal, and the failure source. We introduce the Verify--Backtrack Loop (VBL), a training-free extension of ReAct that turns precondition discovery and failure diagnosis into structured planning-state updates. VBL maintains the task decomposition as a bounded goal stack and, on goal activation, execution failure, or material state change, jointly verifies two horizons---whether the active goal contributes to the terminal goal and whether it is feasible in the current state. These dual-horizon judgments, combined with typed failure attribution, trigger explicit operations---Push, Pop, retry, or BacktrackTo the parent. We evaluate VBL with Qwen3.5-9B, GPT-4o, and DeepSeek-V3.2 on AppWorld, ALFWorld, and ScienceWorld. VBL improves over ReAct and reflection-based control on every backbone--benchmark pair. On the challenging AppWorld setting, it improves task success by up to 22.07 percentage points over ReAct. Ablations show that removing either verification or diagnosis degrades performance, in several settings below ReAct, confirming that the two mechanisms are complementary. These results show that reliable long-horizon interaction benefits from revising the planning state, not merely the next action.

</details>


### [174] [Automated Keyword Generation Using LLMs in a High-Volume Data Virtualization Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41893)
*Saiful Khan,Yiwen Xing,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Philip Beaucamp,Samuel Jackson,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用大语言模型为数据虚拟化服务中的虚拟数据集自动生成关键词，比较了五种文本选取策略，并在五个案例、三种LLM生成器和两种嵌入技术下评估，结果表明策略表现依赖文本描述质量，据此采用多策略实现关键词生成服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据虚拟化服务（DVS）中用户会基于源数据集创建大量虚拟数据集，用户需频繁发现已有虚拟数据集以避免重复劳动，因此需要有效的搜索引擎，而为每个虚拟数据集自动生成关键词是提升可检索性的关键。

Method: 基于大语言模型（LLM）生成关键词，设计五种不同的文本数据收集策略供LLM处理；在五个不同源数据集的应用案例所构建的虚拟数据集上，使用三种LLM生成器和两种嵌入技术进行实验。

Result: 五种策略的性能取决于文本描述的质量；实验中三种LLM生成器与两种嵌入技术的对比为其策略选择提供了依据。

Conclusion: 据此在扩展的DVS中采用多策略实现关键词生成服务（KGS）；自动化关键词生成能力对于数据集丰富、高频访问的大规模DVS不可或缺。

Abstract: In modern data infrastructure, a data virtualization service (DVS) allows users to create many versions of source datasets for different processing tasks. For example, a multivariate or multi-modality dataset can be used for different machine learning tasks (e.g., classification, detection, segmentation, forecasting, and so on). Data selection, participation, integration, and preprocessing in different tasks result in many virtual datasets [Khan et al., SSC 2026]. Meanwhile, different users need to discover existing virtual datasets frequently and easily to avoid duplicate efforts. To enable an effective search engine in a DVS, it is desirable to automatically generate keywords for each virtual dataset. In this work, we present a solution for generating keywords for virtual datasets using large language models (LLMs). We propose five strategies for selecting different collections of text data for processing by the LLMs. We applied the five strategies to virtual datasets created in five application case studies with different source datasets. We conducted experiments with three LLM generators and two embedding techniques. The experiments show that the performances of the five strategies depend on the quality of the text descriptions. The results help us adopt a multi-strategy implementation of a new keyword generation service (KGS) as part of an extended DVS. The work demonstrates that the capability of automated keyword generation is indispensable for a high-volume DVS that is dataset-rich and frequently accessed.

</details>


### [175] [Patch-Based Deep Kernel Learning for Data Assimilation and Bias Correction with Extremely Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41596)
*Emanuele Mele,Angelo Coluccia,Massimo Cafaro,Italo Epicoco,Paola Nassisi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CNN与可学习核高斯过程耦合的混合框架，从极稀疏观测中联合完成数据同化(DA)与偏差校正(BC)，训练成本由O(N^3)降至O(N)，推理仅需解m维线性系统，精度超越3D-Var等方法且推理时间降低98%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典变分方法（如3D-Var）依赖手工构造、假设误差无偏的背景误差协方差，无法刻画状态相关相关性与系统性模型偏差，且代价随域规模增长；深度学习DA方法虽可非线性同化并校正偏差，但可解释性有限、缺乏校准的不确定性估计。

Method: 将卷积神经网络(CNN)与可学习核高斯过程(GP)耦合，端到端以最大化边际对数似然训练；GP在整个网格上学习数据驱动协方差；采用分块(patch)策略将精确GP的O(N^3)训练代价降至固定块大小下的O(N)；推理保持精确，仅需解规模为观测数m≪N的线性系统(O(m^3))；学到的协方差可直接替换3D-Var中的手工协方差而不改变变分形式。

Result: 在海表温度与盐度数据上，使用合成及真实极稀疏现场观测，框架在精度上优于3D-Var、经典GP及竞争性深度学习模型，同时相对3D-Var推理时间减少98%以上。

Conclusion: 该混合框架以可学习协方差为核心，兼得非线性建模能力、可校准不确定性（由设计给出MAP分析及其不确定性）与计算可扩展性，并可无缝增强传统变分同化。

Abstract: Data Assimilation (DA) reconstructs the state of a spatio-temporal system by combining sensor observations with the output of a numerical model. Since model states are high-dimensional while observations cover only a small fraction of the domain, DA is a challenging large-scale estimation problem, central to weather forecasting and oceanography. Classical variational methods such as 3D-Var rely on a hand-crafted background error covariance that assumes unbiased errors, and therefore cannot capture state-dependent correlations or systematic model biases. Moreover, their cost grows with the size of the domain. Deep learning (DL) methods can perform DA and bias correction (BC) non-linearly, but typically offer limited interpretability and no calibrated uncertainty estimates. We propose a hybrid framework that couples a convolutional neural network (CNN) with a learnable-kernel Gaussian Process (GP) to jointly perform DA and BC from extremely sparse observations. The model is trained end-to-end by maximizing the marginal log-likelihood, learns a data-driven covariance over the whole grid, and provides both the Maximum A Posteriori analysis and its uncertainty by design. A patch-based strategy reduces the $O(N^3)$ training cost of exact GPs over $N$ grid points to $O(N)$ for a fixed patch size. Inference remains exact and only requires solving a linear system whose $O(m^3)$ cost depends on the number of observations $m \ll N$, not on the domain. The learned covariance can also replace the hand-crafted one in 3D-Var, improving its accuracy without changing the variational formulation. Experiments on sea surface temperature and salinity, with both synthetic and real, extremely sparse in-situ observations, show that the framework outperforms 3D-Var, a classical GP and a competing DL model, while reducing inference time by more than 98% with respect to 3D-Var.

</details>


### [176] [More Rules, Longer Lists? Benchmarking Self-Evolving Agents on a Hidden-Rule Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40758)
*Chenjie Qiu,Boyan Xu,Shaobin Shi,Bingfeng Chen,Ruichu Cai,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLR-Bench 用"课程式潜在规则"链测试自演化智能体是否真能学到未被告知的规则，而非逐条记忆例外；六种方法在认证模型上几乎学不会，给出"命名分组"约定后提升 14.5–16.5 分，强模型可直接从反馈中学到规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体通过反馈重写持久状态，但"是否能学到没人告诉它的规则"基本未被检验；智能体可能靠把每次纠正写成例外条目而"过关"，导致状态随规则增多而线性膨胀。现有基准无法区分"真学规则"与"记例外"。

Method: 构建 CLR-Bench（Curriculum of Latent Rules）：每条链每层在上层基础上叠加一条隐藏规则；规则作用于不透明标签，该标签对应 Web 上公布的具名分组（如某航空联盟创始成员）。任务给一个示例值，练习反馈再揭示一个成员与一个非成员，测试考察未见成员与相似非成员。用探针认证：基座模型已知各分组成员且在无反馈时不猜测；证据在把标签解读为具名分组后可唯一识别该分组。自动化流水线从新数据域构建，发布 10 域 20 条链，评测六种自演化方法。

Result: 认证模型下各方法几乎不学：相对冻结智能体最高提升仅 ≤5 分，对未见成员的排除率仅 6–12%，低于直接保留原始练习历史的 20%。加入固定"命名分组约定"课程后，同一批方法提升 14.5–16.5 分。状态中保存已揭示成员的比例达 75–98%，却几乎不含类名；练习中出现成员未在列表时即追加该成员——即典型"记例外"行为。换用更强的 GPT-6-Sol 后，ACE 与 Dynamic Cheatsheet 仅凭反馈即可学习，达 78% 准确率、解开 59–60% 的单格，而认证模型下为 53% 与 8%，且超过原始历史基线。

Conclusion: CLR-Bench 能度量智能体的"学习能力"而非记忆能力，且该能力随模型强度显著分化；结果提示自演化方法当前的瓶颈在于缺乏高层归纳/约定先验，而非反馈通道本身。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents rewrite a persistent state from feedback, but it is largely untested whether this lets them learn a rule nobody told them. An agent can also pass by writing down each correction as an exception, and its list then grows with every new rule. We introduce CLR-Bench, a Curriculum of Latent Rules benchmark. Each chain adds one hidden rule per level on top of the earlier ones. A rule acts on an opaque label that stands for a named grouping published on the Web, such as the founding members of an airline alliance. The task gives one example value, practice feedback reveals one more member and one non-member, and the test checks unseen members and similar non-members. Probes certify that the base model knows the members of each grouping and does not guess it without feedback, and that the evidence identifies the grouping once labels are read as named groupings. An automated pipeline builds chains from a new data domain, and we release 20 chains over 10 domains. We evaluate six recent self-evolution methods. With the certifying model they barely learn. The best gain at most 5 points over a frozen agent and exclude only 6-12% of the unseen members from the count, fewer than the 20% reached by simply keeping the raw practice history. A fixed lesson that states the named-grouping convention lifts the same methods by 14.5 to 16.5 points. Their states hold the revealed member in 75-98% of cases and almost never the class name, and when practice exposes a member the list misses, they add that member to the list. With the stronger GPT-6-Sol, ACE and Dynamic Cheatsheet learn from feedback alone. They reach 78% accuracy and solve 59-60% of the single cells, against 53% and 8% with the certifying model, and they surpass the raw history. CLR-Bench therefore measures how well an agent learns, and this ability differs across models.

</details>


### [177] [Advancing Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction with Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41710)
*Burak Bakkar,Hana Moalla,Mohamed Salem Jedidi,Haifa Ben Messaoud,Nicolas Lopez Carranza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在TOP基准上用BioBERT与BioMistral比较微调与探测（probing）两种方案进行临床试验分期结局预测，其ROC-AUC与PR-AUC均超过HINT，且探测与微调性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验失败推高研发成本并延迟患者获得有效疗法。HINT等结构化方法虽显式建模药物、疾病与方案间交互，但对注册信息中的自由文本利用不足，因此需评估领域特定语言模型在该任务上的潜力与成本效益。

Method: 在TOP基准（12,477项试验）上，将每项试验表示为取自ClinicalTrials.gov十个字段拼接的文本；采用BioBERT（108M）全量微调与BioMistral（7B）低秩适配，并对比冻结预训练模型、仅训练轻量分类器的探测方式，按各分期留出测试集评估。

Result: 四种语言模型变体在各期测试集上ROC-AUC为0.613–0.772、PR-AUC为0.649–0.901，均优于已发表的HINT结果，PR-AUC相对提升10–20%；F1差异较小且方向不一，任何模型（含HINT）的F1均未明显超过"全部预测成功"的平凡分类器。探测与微调表现相当：微调在I、II期ROC-AUC与PR-AUC最高，冻结BioMistral嵌入在III期最佳（ROC-AUC 0.772，PR-AUC 0.901），但多数变体间差异小于0.03且未做显著性检验。

Conclusion: 预训练生物医学表示已编码与试验结局相关的信号，探测是相较微调更省算力的可行替代方案；但F1指标的局限性提示需关注类别不平衡下的评价方式。

Abstract: Clinical trial failures are a major barrier to efficient drug development, incurring substantial costs and delaying patient access to effective therapies. Structured approaches such as the Hierarchical Interaction Network (HINT) predict trial outcomes by explicitly modelling interactions between drugs, diseases and protocols, but make limited use of the free text in trial registrations. We evaluate two domain-specific language models, BioBERT (108M parameters) and BioMistral (7B parameters), for phase-level outcome prediction on the TOP benchmark (12,477 trials), representing each trial as text built from ten ClinicalTrials.gov fields. We compare fine-tuning (full fine-tuning for BioBERT, low-rank adaptation for BioMistral) with probing, in which the pretrained model is frozen and only a lightweight classifier is trained on its embeddings. On the held-out test set of each phase, all four language-model variants achieved higher ROC-AUC (0.613--0.772) and PR-AUC (0.649--0.901) than the published HINT results, corresponding to relative PR-AUC gains of 10--20\%. F1 differences were small and mixed, and no model, including HINT, clearly exceeded the F1 of a trivial classifier that predicts success for every trial. Under the configurations tested, probing performed comparably to fine-tuning: fine-tuned models scored highest on ROC-AUC and PR-AUC in Phases I and II, and frozen BioMistral embeddings in Phase III (ROC-AUC 0.772; PR-AUC 0.901), although most differences between variants were below 0.03 and were not tested for significance. These results suggest that pretrained biomedical representations already encode signal relevant to trial outcome, and that probing is a computationally cheaper alternative to fine-tuning for this task.

</details>


### [178] [Confident Fools or Careful Searchers? A Behavioural Study of When LLMs Search Beyond Their Static Internal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41124)
*Nipun D. Pathirage,Kwan Srijomkwan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出400题多选题基准，同时含"应直接回答"的常识题与"应触发检索"的训练截止后新闻题，配以NAS/KIS两个动作级分数，审计5个商用LLM产品的搜索触发行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式LLM助手需反复判断"凭记忆回答"还是"调用工具检索"：多余检索浪费时延与成本，漏检索则产生自信却无依据的答案。既有工作多测量口头化置信度或训练新的自适应检索方法，鲜有对已部署产品实际搜索决策进行审计。

Method: 构建400道多选题基准，混合广为人知的内部知识（IK）题与模型训练截止后新闻事实的外部知识（OK）题；每题含弃答选项；定义两个动作级指标NAS与KIS，分别奖励在OK题上检索、在IK题上克制；在5个商用LLM（开启/关闭厂商网页搜索）上评测，并分析显式时间线索的影响。

Result: GPT-5 mini与Grok 4 Fast在超90%的OK题上检索、仅约1%的IK题上检索；Mistral Small仅在20%的OK题上检索，常以弃答代替调用工具；某产品的检索指示器不可用。在无显式时间线索的OK题上，两个高选择性模型的NAS从约0.72降至0.57–0.58。

Conclusion: 该基准与分数衡量的是特定产品的搜索触发行为，而非严格意义上的元认知；产品间检索策略差异显著，且部分依赖题面时间线索。

Abstract: Agentic LLM assistants must repeatedly decide whether to answer from memory or to call a tool such as web search. Unnecessary searches cost latency and money; missing ones yield confident but unsupported answers. Prior work mostly measures verbalised confidence or trains new adaptive-retrieval methods, and rarely audits the search decisions that deployed products actually make. We introduce a benchmark of 400 multiple-choice questions that combines widely known inside-knowledge (IK) questions, which should be answered directly, with outside-knowledge (OK) questions about facts from news published after the models' training cut-offs (to our knowledge), which should trigger search or, without tools, abstention. Each item offers an abstain option, and two action-level scores, NAS and KIS, reward searching on OK questions and restraint on IK questions. Across five commercial LLMs with and without their vendors' web search, GPT-5 mini and Grok 4 Fast search on over 90% of OK but only 1% of IK questions, whereas Mistral Small searches on just 20% of OK questions, often abstaining instead of using its tool; one product's search indicator proved unusable. Searching is partly triggered by explicit temporal cues: on uncued OK questions, the NAS of the two selective models falls from about 0.72 to 0.57--0.58. The scores thus measure the search triggering of specific products, not metacognition in a strict sense.

</details>


### [179] [Predicting Human-Gain Curves under Local Proxy Optimization from Repeated Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41114)
*Moonwon Choi,Kisung Nam,Seunggeun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PACE，一种优化前的诊断方法，用重复人工评分估计代理目标-人类感知的增益曲线参数 A（一阶对齐）与 B（二阶修正），在多个数据集上降低留出增益预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理指标与人类感知的平均相关性无法揭示"优化强度增加时人类感知质量如何变化"，而实际增益随优化强度变化，单靠平均相关性不足以指导优化前决策。

Method: 将代理优化建模为 KL 正则化的指数倾斜：在 KL 半径 d 处局部增益近似为 A√d + Bd。A 度量一阶代理-人类对齐，B 捕获沿代理优化路径的首个二阶修正项。用校准样本上的重复人工评分估计 A、B，并在留出评估样本上检验增益曲线预测。

Result: 在 SummEval、分析合规的 WMT MQM 簇、OpenMEVA-MAGS（ROC/WP）重复评分切片及 GPT-4o 评判代理实验中，PACE 相较仅用 A、零增益和仅可靠性基线持续降低留出增益预测误差；在同等池化前信息下与灵活的代理条件校准相当；LoRA-DPO 实验提供通向真实优化的适度桥接。跨数据集与评估切片的迁移实验显示源系数可作有用先验，目标域校准可进一步提升预测。

Conclusion: 该诊断是局部且单向的：仅刻画固定题目池上沿正向倾斜分支的温和优化；源域系数可迁移为先验但目标校准仍必要。

Abstract: Optimizing a proxy objective can improve human-perceived quality, but the resulting gain can vary with optimization strength. Average proxy-human correlation alone does not reveal how human-perceived quality changes as optimization strength increases. We propose PACE (Proxy Alignment and Curvature Expansion), a pre-optimization diagnostic that uses repeated human ratings to predict the human-gain curve induced by a fixed proxy. The method models proxy optimization as a KL-regularized exponential tilt. At KL radius $d$, the local gain is approximated by $A\sqrt{d}+Bd$, where $A$ measures first-order proxy-human alignment and $B$ captures the leading second-order correction along the proxy-optimization path. We estimate $A$ and $B$ from repeated human ratings on calibration items and test the resulting gain-curve prediction on held-out evaluation items. Across SummEval, analysis-eligible WMT MQM clusters, an OpenMEVA-MAGS ROC/WP repeated-rating slice, and a GPT-4o judge proxy experiment, PACE consistently reduces held-out gain-prediction error relative to $A$-only, zero-gain, and reliability-only baselines. With the same pre-pool information, it is competitive with flexible proxy-conditional calibration, and a LoRA-DPO experiment provides a modest bridge to actual optimization. Transfer experiments across datasets and evaluation slices show that source coefficients are useful priors, but target calibration further improves prediction. The resulting diagnostic is local and one-sided: it characterizes gentle optimization along the positive tilt branch of a fixed item pool.

</details>


### [180] [Differential rank preservation manifold learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41107)
*Henry Han,Luyu Niu,Diane Li,Jiacun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SR-tSNE 在标准 t-SNE 损失中加入可微的 Sigmoid 秩近似项，显式保持高维邻居的顺序，并提出 O-DLP 指标；在八类基准及标签迁移、细胞类型发现、日内交易等下游任务中优于 12 个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE、UMAP 等流形学习方法仅保持局部邻域集合，却不保持邻域内部的排序，两个嵌入可拥有相同邻居集合但最近点不同，这种顺序丢失会损害重视排序的下游应用。

Method: 在标准 t-SNE 损失上增加一个可微的秩项，采用 sigmoid 秩近似以支持高效训练，显式迫使真实高维邻居排在无关"闯入者"之前并保持其原有次序；同时提出 Ordinal DLP（O-DLP），仅在邻居以原始秩返回时给予信用。

Result: 在覆盖图像、文本、语音、遥感与单细胞数据的八个基准上，SR-tSNE 在秩保持与邻域重叠上优于 12 个基线；消融显示仅秩项即可将 O-DLP 提升 5%–28%，且该增益无法用标准成对损失获得。下游任务中：七个视觉数据集标签迁移最佳；六个单细胞数据集 18 个数据集-指标组合中 17 项第一（无论聚类分辨率是否使用标签选择）；日内交易中收益更高、风险更低、换手率更低。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，SR-tSNE 是首个显式保持顺序的 t-SNE 家族方法，其秩保持优势可迁移至多种下游应用。

Abstract: Manifold learning methods like t-SNE and UMAP preserve local neighborhoods, but they do not preserve the ranking of the neighbors inside them. Two embeddings can return the same neighbor set while disagreeing on which point is closest. This loss of order degrades downstream applications that value the order in the embedding. We propose Smooth Rank t-SNE (SR-tSNE), which adds a differentiable rank term to the standard t-SNE loss. To enable efficient training, this term uses a sigmoid rank approximation to explicitly force a point's true high-dimensional neighbors to outrank unrelated intruders and retain their original order. We also introduce Ordinal DLP (O-DLP), an extension of the Degree of Locality Preservation (DLP) metric that credits a neighbor only when it returns at its original rank. Across eight benchmarks spanning image, text, speech, remote-sensing and single-cell data, SR-tSNE outperforms 12 baselines in rank preservation and neighborhood overlap. Ablation studies demonstrate our rank term alone boosts O-DLP by 5% to 28%, a gain not achievable using standard pairwise loss. This advantage transfers to downstream applications. SR-tSNE achieves the best label-transfer performance across seven vision datasets. For cell-type discovery across six single-cell datasets, it ranks first in 17 of 18 dataset-metric combinations, whether clustering resolution is selected with or without cell-type labels. In intraday trading, SR-tSNE achieves higher returns, lower risk, and lower turnover than the baselines. To our knowledge, SR-tSNE is the first t-SNE-family method with explicit order preservation.

</details>


### [181] [Verifying Text-to-SQL Queries under Changing Business Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41090)
*Genliang Zhu,Xiaobo Chen,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出可执行的"验证档案"（verification profile），用于在业务定义（退款归属、税务处理、合格人群、汇率日期）变更后重新校验固定SQL查询的语义正确性；在32个新撰写的修订族上确认：非合规单元中63/144在当前快照上仍"看起来正确"，而基于义务引导的检查在8账本预算下比通用排序多检出10个差异（+6.9个百分点，p=0.0156），且所有方法对36个合规单元零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在text-to-SQL与AI agent分析场景中，SQL能成功执行并不等于语义正确；当业务定义随时间修订（指标口径、税务处理、人群范围、汇率日期）时，既有固定查询可能悄悄失准。当前缺乏可审计、可复现的"重校验"机制，也缺乏对"当前快照通过≠历史/多版本口径正确"这一风险的量化证据。

Method: 构建可执行验证档案：将请求的修订版本与知识截止时间绑定到合法的反例数据与精确的货币比较上。预注册确认实验覆盖32个新撰写的修订族与2个本地生产者数据包，共192个"查询-定义"单元（144非合规、36有限测试集一致、12未决）。采用义务引导（obligation-guided）检查与通用排序对照，在8账本预算下比较检出率，使用族级bootstrap与精确族符号翻转检验；并设置当前义务打分、等权打分消融，以及受控实验、自然语言查询试点与重复采样作为机制与稳健性证据。

Result: 非合规单元中63个在当前快照上表现一致（即"假正确"）；8账本预算下义务引导检出137个差异，通用排序检出127个，配对差6.9个百分点（族bootstrap 95%区间[2.1,13.6]，精确符号翻转p=0.0156）。所有账本方案对36个一致单元的拒绝数为0（无假阳性）。当前义务打分与等权打分均达到引导方案的效果，表明收益来源于覆盖度而非"修订特异性优先级"。

Conclusion: 法律/定义层面的差异可证伪一致性，而"一致"仅限所记录测试集内成立，因此通过当前快照不能证明语义合规。研究在自撰模式与本地模型范围内建立了可审计的业务语义重校验档案，其收益主要来自检查覆盖度；结论受作者自建schema与本地模型范围限制，外推需谨慎。

Abstract: In text-to-SQL and AI-agent analytics, successful SQL execution does not establish semantic correctness under changing business definitions. We study revalidation of fixed queries after changes to refund attribution, tax treatment, eligible populations and exchange-rate dates. An executable verification profile binds the requested revision and knowledge cutoff to legal counterexample data and exact monetary comparison. A preregistered confirmation on 32 newly authored revision families and two local producer bundles retains 192 query-definition cells: 144 nonconforming, 36 finite-suite agreeing and 12 unresolved. Of the nonconforming cells, 63 agree on the current snapshot. At an eight-ledger budget, obligation-guided checking detects 137 discrepancies versus 127 for generic ordering, a paired difference of 6.9 percentage points (family bootstrap 95% interval [2.1, 13.6]; exact family sign-flip p=0.0156). Every ledger arm rejects zero of the 36 agreeing cells. Current-obligation and equal-weight scoring match the guided result, identifying coverage rather than revision-specific priority as the observed source of benefit. Controlled assays, a natural-query pilot and repeated samples provide separate mechanism and robustness evidence. A legal discrepancy refutes conformance; agreement remains restricted to the recorded suite. The study establishes an auditable business-semantic revalidation profile within its authored schema and local-model scope.

</details>


### [182] [CIRCE Divines LLM Task Complexity from Constraint Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41089)
*Chenhao Hong,Jiakai Xu,Kaushiki Singh,Yusen Zhang,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CIRCE 用廉价 LLM 将问题转写为约束中间表示并编译成约束图，用轻量 GNN 编码该结构以预测 LLM 能否正确作答，在七个推理基准和四个 LLM 上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型作答前估计问题对 LLM 是否过于复杂，可用于模型路由、测试时计算分配以及提示需要引入新工具。现有估计器依赖问题文本、LLM 标注的难度标签或模型作答后的信号（如隐状态），均未显式利用问题内在的约束结构——即待赋值的变量及其约束如何交互。

Method: 提出 CIRCE：用廉价 LLM 抽取问题结构，构建"约束中间表示"（Constraint Intermediate Representation），并将其确定性地编译为约束图；再用 GNN 编码器对该图编码以预测 LLM 回答正确性。实验中即便使用随机初始化的 GNN 编码器。

Result: 在七个推理基准（含 SAT、逻辑、数学）和四个 LLM 上，相较各指标最强基线，负对数似然由 0.465 降至 0.423，AUROC 由 0.691 提升至 0.727。

Conclusion: 显式建模问题的约束结构可有效预判 LLM 作答正确性，且结构编码本身即携带强预测信号（随机初始化 GNN 亦可奏效），为作答前的难度估计与计算分配提供新途径。

Abstract: Estimating whether a problem is too complex for a large language model (LLM) before the model attempts it can guide model routing, how to allocate test-time compute, and suggest the need for new tools to aid the agent. While existing estimators rely on the problem text, LLM-annotated task difficulty labels, or signals obtained after a model attempts to solve the problem, such as its hidden states, none explicitly leverage the constraint structure inherent in the problem: the variables to assign and how the constraints over them interact.
  We introduce CIRCE, which extracts a problem's structure by using a cheap LLM to construct a Constraint Intermediate Representation that deterministically compiles into the constraint graph. Across seven reasoning benchmarks (including SAT, logic, and math) and four LLMs, even a randomly initialized GNN encoder improves predictions of LLM response correctness, reducing negative log-likelihood from 0.465 to 0.423 and increasing AUROC from 0.691 to 0.727 relative to the strongest baseline for each metric.

</details>


### [183] [Violet Teaming in Practice: Lessons from a Pilot Expert Workshop on Governing Complex AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41018)
*Steph Batalis,Kait Connors,Ben C. Snyder,Vikram Venkatram,Josh Wentzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文记录了一次"violet teaming"（紫队协作）专家工作坊试点，用于评估生物防御相关AI能力的治理方法，并总结设计与经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估往往将技术、政策与社会因素割裂考量；作者希望探索一种能把多方视角整合进新兴技术评估的治理方法，并以生物防御相关AI能力为试点对象。

Method: 采用假设性技术情景的专家工作坊，按"能力开发—滥用潜力—落地实施"三阶段顺序推进，并纳入技术、安全、运营与政策四类视角。

Result: 该顺序化形式揭示了非显而易见的滥用路径；将潜在干预从纯技术防护扩展到更广范围；并识别出影响实施的操作与政策约束。

Conclusion: 顺序整合式的工作坊设计可作为评估复杂两用技术的有效治理方法；报告总结了工作坊设计、产出与经验，供后续violet teaming应用参考。

Abstract: Violet teaming has been proposed as a method to integrate technical, policy, and societal considerations into the evaluation of emerging technologies. Here, we document a pilot expert workshop used to explore violet teaming as a governance methodology for biodefense-relevant AI capabilities and describe observations from the exercise. The workshop used a hypothetical technology scenario across three sequential stages - capability development, misuse potential, and implementation - while incorporating technical, security, operational, and policy perspectives. The sequential format provided an integrated way to examine potential benefits, misuse risks, and real-world implementation challenges, rather than considering these issues in isolation. The exercise surfaced non-obvious misuse pathways, expanded potential interventions beyond purely technical safeguards, and highlighted operational and policy constraints that could affect implementation. This report summarizes the workshop design, outputs, and lessons learned for applying violet teaming to the evaluation of complex, dual-use technologies.

</details>


### [184] [Benchmarking Forecasting Agents on Yesterday's Tomorrows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40940)
*Yuxuan Wang,Yishu Wang,Jiaqi Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FABench：面向预测智能体的可扩展回测环境，通过自动流水线为任意历史日期生成事件/问题/结果，结合"离线档案+防时间泄漏的实时检索"环境评估智能体预测能力，首版含9领域287题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测智能体基准不适于度量与改进该能力：实时（live）基准需等待事件揭晓，评估周期受日历约束；固定语料基准虽即时返回结果，却把研究限制在预先收集的文档内，易受训练截止污染。

Method: 构建自动化流水线以发现事件、生成问题并为任意历史预测日期解析结果，使基准可滚动更新以领先模型训练截止；评估环境融合离线档案与经时间泄漏筛查的实时网页检索；首版发布9个领域287道问题；对基于8个近期模型的智能体进行评估，并与Polymarket市场共识（54题）对比，辅以研究轨迹分析。

Result: 在所有检索设置下，检索均改善全部8个模型的Brier分数；领先智能体在54道Polymarket关联题上达到市场共识水平，最佳三者略高于共识；研究轨迹揭示智能体在证据加权与应用上的反复性失效模式，仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: FABench为预测智能体提供了可扩展、可续期、抗泄漏的度量与改进基础，公开代码、数据与档案环境以支撑社区研究。

Abstract: Digital agents can now gather and organize information, reason over it, and use tools to complete complex tasks. Forecasting puts all of these abilities to the test: it requires integrating incomplete evidence, inferring causal drivers, and committing to calibrated probabilistic judgments. Existing benchmarks are poorly suited to measuring and improving this ability. Live benchmarks provide feedback only after events resolve, so every evaluation cycle waits on the calendar; fixed-corpus evaluations return results immediately but confine research to pre-collected documents. We introduce FABench, a scalable backtesting environment for Forecasting Agents. An automated pipeline discovers events, generates questions, and resolves outcomes for any past forecast date, so the benchmark can be renewed to stay ahead of model training cutoffs; its first release poses 287 questions across nine domains. Agents research each question freely in an archive environment that combines an offline archive with live web search screened to prevent temporal leakage. Evaluating agents built on eight recent models, we find that retrieval improves the Brier score of all eight in every retrieval setting and lifts the leading agents to the market consensus on the 54 questions linked to Polymarket, with the best three scoring slightly above it. Research traces reveal recurring failures in how agents weigh and apply evidence, leaving ample room for improvement. We release our code, data, and archive environment as a foundation for community research on forecasting agents.

</details>


### [185] [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReuScope 审计协议，将数据关系度量与两种"固定一侧、改变另一侧"的比较结合，以 Capsule Vision 2024 为案例，区分观察到的数据复用与条件下的性能敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据泄漏/数据复用审计常把"性能下降"直接等同于"泄漏导致的虚高"，缺乏对评估总体、类别支撑与不确定性单元的明确界定，也混淆了数据复用事实与性能敏感性两类结论。

Method: ReuScope 协议：①度量数据关系（字节同一性、跨划分来源记录重叠、患者标识可用性、近匹配检测）；②改变评估总体而固定预测（同一拟合模型换验证子集）；③改变训练数据而固定评估（剔除 Kvasir-Capsule 相关训练行及与其近匹配的 5 行）；并设置匹配对照（样本量、类别计数）与探索性实验（剔除验证帧的近邻训练行 vs. 等量远距离行，以匹配未标记帧为参照做双重差分）。

Result: 字节完全相同文件跨越训练-验证公共边界，且全部 41 个贡献帧的 Kvasir-Capsule 记录同时出现在两个划分中；共享源记录确证跨划分重叠，但缺少跨记录患者标识，无法统计唯一共享患者。十个配对种子上，剔除相关训练行使公共验证平衡准确率由 82.5% 降至 61.2%；而在其他来源验证行上评估同一模型时，剔除反而提升 1.38 个百分点。匹配对照表明样本量与类别计数不能解释差异。探索性双重差分估计为真类概率 −0.355 个百分点，准确率的配对 t 区间跨零。

Conclusion: ReuScope 将"观察到的数据复用"与"条件性性能敏感性"区分为两类独立结论，并为每个结论明确指定评估总体、类别支撑与不确定性单元；公共验证集的下降应解读为对来源剔除的敏感性，而非泄漏导致的虚高幅度。

Abstract: We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero. The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

</details>


### [186] [RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813)
*Qi Wang,Kang Xu,Chengcheng Wan,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RECAST：在闭源前沿推理模型仅暴露最终答案、且不存在显式思维链（CoT）侧信道的情况下，以「问题+最终答案」为条件重建第一人称结构化推理轨迹，并清洗为可直接用于 SFT 的 CoT 数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 抽取方法（可见 reasoning 字段、工具调用 scratchpad、签名/加密推理块）均依赖侧信道；在标准 chat-completions 接口访问 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 时三类 SOTA 方法均返回 0 个 CoT token，属结构性失效而非实现缺陷。

Method: 复现三种代表性抽取方法以验证侧信道缺失；随后构建 RECAST 框架：解码器以问题与目标模型最终答案为条件，重建第一人称结构化 rationale，经清洗后构建 SFT-ready 数据；以 agent skill 形式开源，单一 prompt 即可部署并跑通完整流水线。

Result: 三种 SOTA 抽取方法在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上抽取 token 数为零；RECAST 在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上完成实例化，并以同一「仅答案」协议在 GPT-6.1 与 Gemini 3 上取得成功的定性重建；附带受控的产物验证。

Conclusion: RECAST 为首个不依赖隐藏推理通道、从闭源前沿模型重建 CoT 的系统；本文为预印本，仅描述系统、威胁模型与受控产物验证，不宣称大规模基准性能提升。

Abstract: Large closed-source reasoning models expose only their final answers. The intermediate reasoning that produces those answers is hidden behind API surfaces, tool-call policies, and safety filters. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) extraction methods assume a side channel: a visible reasoning field, a scratchpad carried through tool calls, or a signed/encrypted reasoning block. We reproduce three representative state-of-the-art extraction methods and find that, on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 accessed through the standard chat-completions interface, all three return zero CoT tokens. The failure is structural rather than a bug: the reasoning field is absent, named tool selection is rejected, free-text scratchpad arguments are filtered, and no replayable reasoning block is exposed. We therefore take a different route. We present RECAST, a reconstruction-based framework that conditions a decoder on the question and the target model's final answer, reconstructs a first-person structured rationale, cleans it, and builds SFT-ready data. To the best of our knowledge, RECAST is the first system to reconstruct CoT from frontier closed models such as Claude-Sonnet-5.5, GPT-6.1, and Gemini 3 without using hidden reasoning channels. We instantiate RECAST on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 and additionally obtain successful qualitative reconstructions on GPT-6.1 and Gemini 3 with the same answer-only protocol. The system is open-source and packaged as an agent skill: one prompt deploys it and runs the complete pipeline to produce SFT-ready CoT data. This preprint describes the system, its threat model, and a controlled artifact validation; it does not claim large-scale benchmark improvements.

</details>


### [187] [Whose Values, Which Contexts, and What Signals? Grounding Cultural Value Alignment with Re3Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41793)
*Jing Yao,Hyunjin Hwang,Weihua Zheng,Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan,Jia Wang Peh,Dylan Raharja,JinYeong Bak,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Rui Cao,Yuki Arase,Jacki O'Neill,Zhicheng Dou,Xing Xie,Xiaoyuan Yi,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Re3Culture 数据集，从"代表性社群、真实情境、显性价值信号"三方面探讨文化价值对齐中"目标应如何以人类数据为据"的问题，并给出目标定义的三大启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化价值对齐不仅需要模型优化，更需要一个基于真实人类证据、明确定义的目标；但如何将此类目标扎根于人类数据仍缺乏探索，由此产生三个基本问题：（1）应代表谁的价值？（2）应在何处观察文化价值？（3）何种信号能揭示潜在价值？

Method: 构建 Re3Culture 大规模数据集，包含三部分：（i）代表性社群——通过文化归属筛查与人口特征感知采样，招募覆盖四种文化、700+ 名参与者；（ii）真实情境——由社群成员选定的文化敏感场景（如家庭与亲属关系）；（iii）显性价值信号——1,400+ 份价值问卷、20,000+ 条主题锚定的行为原则与显性价值优先级，以及 6,000+ 个情境化问题的回答。并据此分析目标定义选择的影响。

Result: 三点分析结论：（1）估计文化价值既需社群归属识别，也需广泛的内部覆盖；替换其他文化或限制子群体覆盖会改变价值估计，LLM 模拟也过度反映特定子群体；（2）价值须在具体、社群引导的情境中观察；抽象问卷画像难以复原情境化优先级，且不同社群判断哪些场景更能揭示文化价值存在差异；（3）价值优先级应被显性引出，而非仅从回答偏好或底线式答案中学习；偏好会把价值一致性与其它回答层面属性混淆，相似答案可能源自不同价值优先级。

Conclusion: 通过满足上述要求，Re3Culture 提供了首个以人类为基础、可为文化价值对齐的目标定义提供显式依据的资源。

Abstract: Cultural value alignment requires not only model optimization but also a well-specified target grounded in real-human evidence. Yet how such targets should be grounded in human data remains largely unexplored, raising three fundamental questions: (1) whose values should be represented? (2) where should cultural values be observed? and (3) what signals reveal underlying values? We investigate these questions through Re3Culture, a large-scale dataset linking (i) Representative communities: 700+ human participants across four cultures recruited through cultural-affiliation screening and demographic-aware sampling; (ii) Realistic scenario: culturally sensitive scenarios selected by community members, e.g., family and kinship; and (iii) Revealed value signals: 1,400+ value surveys, 20,000+ topic-grounded behavioral principles and explicit value priorities with responses for 6,000+ situated questions. Our analysis shows why these choices matter for defining the target, with three implications. First, estimating cultural values requires both community affiliation and broad internal coverage. Substituting other cultures or restricting subgroup coverage shifts value estimates. LLM simulations also disproportionately reflect particular subgroups. Second, values must be observed in concrete, community-guided situations. Abstract questionnaire profiles weakly recover situated priorities, and communities differ in which scenarios they identify as culturally value-revealing. Third, value priorities should be elicited explicitly, not learned from response preferences or bottom-line answers alone. Preferences conflate value agreement with other response-level qualities, and similar answers can be derived from different value priorities. By addressing these requirements, Re3Culture provides the first human-grounded resource that explicitly informs target definition for cultural value alignment.

</details>


### [188] [Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949)
*Zhuguanyu Wu,Congjia Chen,Ruihao Gong,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Yi Xu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Geo-FSRL：在保系数各向同性高斯转移族中，以"任意非零速度更新诱导的条件KL最小"为准则，导出唯一的闭式最优策略几何因子，并配合梯度尺度匹配的闭式PG/KL权重，用4步少步采样训练且推理保持确定性求解器，奖励优于Flow-GRPO与Flow-CPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步流模型的单次去噪跨越较大噪声区间，将GRPO应用于此需在去噪转移中引入随机性；现有做法仅约束策略保持目标流系数，这一约束对应一族可行策略，却缺乏在族内择优的准则，导致随机策略选择成为优化中的主要不确定因素。

Method: 选择原则：在固定条件状态与时间步对下，选取使任意给定非零速度输出更新所诱导的条件KL散度最小的可行策略。在保系数的各向同性高斯转移族中，该准则退化为最小化单一标量几何因子（同时支配速度空间Fisher信息与条件转移对数似然比方差）；推导出该族内唯一的闭式最优解，且对所有非零更新幅度与方向共享，转移系数由源与目标噪声水平共同决定。进而针对最小化后几何因子仍随时间步变化的问题，采用梯度尺度匹配，导出归一化显式score尺度与参考KL曲率的闭式策略梯度权重与KL权重。训练沿用原始少步日程，推理保留确定性求解器。

Result: 在4步设置下，图像生成与视频生成模型上最终奖励分数均高于Flow-GRPO与Flow-CPS。

Conclusion: 条件KL最小化提供了少步流模型RL中随机策略选择的原理性准则，且闭式可解、与更新无关、可与梯度尺度匹配结合；在不改变少步日程与推理求解器的前提下提升奖励表现。

Abstract: Few-step flow models generate samples through a handful of large denoising transitions. Applying GRPO to such models requires introducing stochasticity into the denoising transitions, and when a single transition spans a wide range of noise levels, the choice of the resulting stochastic policy becomes a major factor in optimization. Recent constructions constrain the policy to preserve the target flow coefficients, but this constraint admits a family of policies and offers no criterion for choosing among them. We introduce Geo-FSRL (Geometry-Optimized Few-Step Reinforcement Learning), built on a selection principle: at a fixed conditioning state and timestep pair, choose the admissible policy that minimizes the conditional KL divergence induced by any given nonzero velocity-output update. Within a coefficient-preserving family of isotropic Gaussian transitions, this criterion reduces to minimizing a single scalar geometry factor that determines the conditional KL for every velocity-output update. The same factor governs velocity-space Fisher information and conditional transition log-likelihood-ratio variance. We derive the unique closed-form optimizer within this family, shared across all nonzero update magnitudes and directions, with transition coefficients determined by both the source and target noise levels. Because the minimized geometry factor still varies across timesteps, we further adopt gradient-scale matching from prior work and derive closed-form policy-gradient and KL weights that normalize explicit score scales and reference-KL curvature. Geo-FSRL trains on the original few-step schedule and retains the deterministic solver at inference. Our method achieves higher final reward scores than Flow-GRPO and Flow-CPS on both image generation and video generation models under the 4-step setting. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/lightx2v_rl

</details>


### [189] [Specification-Guided Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41511)
*Alessandro Previti,Konstantinos Vandikas,Mario Alviano,Mats Carlsson,Albin Larsson Forsberg,Alexandros Nikou,Athanasios Karapantelakis,Aneta Vulgarakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经-符号、规范引导的解码框架，将LLM生成与符号推理结合，通过逐步过滤无法扩展为满足形式规范输出的取值，实现结构化输出的规范满足；含前向保证变体与回溯变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束解码方法主要只强制语法合法性，无法直接强制输出满足形式规范（formal specification），导致生成的结构化输出虽语法正确却未必符合规范要求。

Method: 在每一步解码时用符号推理精化解码语法，过滤掉无法扩展为满足规范输出的值。前向变体（forward-only）按构造保证规范满足；回溯变体（backtracking）在下游后果仅剩LLM自身判定不合适的值时，回溯修正此前可行的选择。框架与模型无关、无需重训练，并基于现有语法约束解码机制构建。

Result: 在配置与约束求解基准上，前向变体可靠地产生符合规范的输出，相比拒绝采样减少了重复LLM推理，且仅带来适度的符号推理开销；回溯变体进一步减少了模型被迫选择其自身判定不合适取值的情形，显著提升了对LLM所述提示解释的遵从度。

Conclusion: 将符号规范推理嵌入解码过程可有效弥补语法约束解码的不足，在无需重训练、模型无关的前提下兼顾规范满足性与推理效率，回溯机制进一步改善模型语义意图的保持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate structured outputs, but existing constrained-decoding methods mainly enforce syntax and cannot directly enforce satisfaction of a formal specification. We introduce a neuro-symbolic, specification-guided decoding framework that combines LLM generation with symbolic reasoning. At each step, the framework refines the decoding grammar by filtering out values that cannot be extended to an output satisfying the specification. We present a forward-only variant that guarantees specification satisfaction by construction, and a backtracking variant that revises earlier feasible choices when their downstream consequences leave only values that the LLM itself judges unsuitable. The framework is model-agnostic, requires no retraining, and builds on existing grammar-constrained decoding mechanisms. Experiments on configuration and constraint-solving benchmarks show that the forward-only variant reliably produces specification-compliant outputs while reducing repeated LLM inference compared with rejection sampling and incurring modest symbolic-reasoning overhead. The backtracking variant further reduces cases in which the model is forced to select values that it has itself judged unsuitable, thereby substantially improving adherence to the LLM's stated interpretation of the prompt.

</details>


### [190] [EvoMCP: Co-evolving MCP agents and training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41479)
*Xuanang Gao,Kepeng Lin,Xinyan Shi,Yong Deng,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Xiaofeng Wu,Jie Yang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoMCP，一种agent–data互演进框架，通过任务演进、轨迹演进与监督微调的闭环，使MCP智能体持续适应自身演化能力并补齐新暴露的短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP智能体的监督微调基于静态数据分布，无法适应学生模型不断变化的能力，导致新暴露的弱点得不到及时处理。

Method: 基于rollout反馈评估学生已有能力与能力缺口以确定学习目标；任务演进通过改写已验证种子任务的指令与可观测环境状态将其转化为可执行任务并保持验证器对齐；轨迹演进利用可复用技能修复失败的教师rollout并精炼成功的rollout，生成任务–轨迹对用于微调学生；被接受的任务作为后续演进的种子；再对更新后的学生重新评估，用其反馈指导下一轮数据演进，形成闭环。

Result: 在Toolathlon、MCPMark和MCP-Atlas上分别取得16.5%、10.2%和12.6%的绝对性能提升，均优于初始学生模型。

Conclusion: agent–data协同演进可有效突破静态数据分布的局限，实现MCP智能体的持续自提升。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of Model Context Protocol (MCP) agents is typically conducted on a static data distribution, which fails to adapt to the student's evolving capabilities and leaves newly exposed weaknesses insufficiently addressed. To address these challenges, we propose \method, an agent--data mutual evolution framework that enables MCP agents to improve through a closed loop of task evolution, trajectory evolution, and supervised fine-tuning. Specifically, \method\ assesses the student's established capabilities and capability gaps from rollout feedback to identify learning targets. Task evolution then translates these targets into executable tasks by adapting the instructions and observable environment states of compatible verified seed tasks while keeping their verifiers aligned. To provide reliable and efficient demonstrations for these tasks, trajectory evolution uses reusable skills to recover failed teacher rollouts and refine successful ones. The resulting task--trajectory pairs are used to fine-tune the student, while accepted tasks seed subsequent evolution. To close the loop, we re-evaluate the updated student and use its feedback to guide the next round of data evolution. Experiments on Toolathlon, MCPMark, and MCP-Atlas demonstrate consistent improvements over the initial student, yielding absolute gains of 16.5\%, 10.2\%, and 12.6\%, respectively. Project page: https://github.com/xagao/EvoMCP.

</details>


### [191] [Researcher-in-the-Loop Evolutionary Discovery of Entanglement-Assisted Quantum Code Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41183)
*Jae-Hyun Baek,Byung-Sun Won,Jon-Lark Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出研究者参与（researcher-in-the-loop）的LLM引导程序演化发现流水线，结合程序演化、精确求值、数学推广、认证性不存在性检查与形式化证明验证，应用于纠缠辅助量子纠错码，将有限算例推广为无穷族并闭合归档表中54个空缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM引导的程序演化虽能产出数学构造，但有效性、一般性与可复现性需要彼此独立的证据支撑；单纯获得有限构造不足以形成可证明的数学结果。

Method: 构建研究者参与的发现流水线：程序演化生成构造→精确求值验证→研究者进行数学推广→带DRAT证明检查的认证性不存在性检验→Lean形式化验证代数证书；并对八组配对生成块开展控制实验，比较验证集筛选的迭代程序与独立生成程序在未见长度上的成功率。

Result: 演化程序产生有限比特构造，经推广得族 [[n,1,n-1;n-q-1]]_q（q≥3为素数幂、n≥2q+1；q=2时奇数n≥5），Lean检验其代数证书；结合纠缠提升闭合归档EAQECC表中54个空缺；发布115条已验证码记录、10项带DRAT证明的不存在性结果、族实现及可复现搜索程序；在n=13、15上，迭代程序成功率46.1%和31.6%，独立生成程序为19.5%和3.1%。

Conclusion: 在此设定下，程序演化与研究者分析相结合，可将有限计算发现转化为具有可独立检验证据的已证明族，说明人类分析与自动搜索在数学发现中的互补作用。

Abstract: LLM-guided program evolution can produce mathematical constructions, but validity, generality, and reproducibility require distinct evidence. We develop a researcher-in-the-loop discovery pipeline that combines program evolution, exact evaluation, mathematical generalization, certified nonexistence checks, and formal proof checking. We apply it to entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes (EAQECCs). Starting from tasks that did not specify a family formula, evolved programs produced finite qubit constructions whose structure researchers generalized to the family $[[n,1,n-1;n-q-1]]_q$ for prime powers $q\ge3$ at $n\ge2q+1$ and for $q=2$ at odd $n\ge5$. Lean checks the algebraic certificate underlying this family. Together with entanglement lifting, the family closes 54 gaps in archived EAQECC tables. The released evidence includes 115 verified code records, ten nonexistence results with checked DRAT proofs, family implementations, and preserved search programs for exact checks and seeded replay. In a controlled cohort of eight paired generation blocks, validation-selected iterative programs achieve success in 46.1% and 31.6% of held-out executions at unseen lengths $n=13,15$, compared with 19.5% and 3.1% for independently generated programs. In this setting, program evolution and researcher analysis turn finite computational discoveries into a proved family with independently checkable evidence.

</details>


### [192] [Sensorium: An Open-Source Multimodal Data Collection and Curation Library for Embodied Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41946)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Ariel Rodriguez,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sensorium 是一个开源 Python 库，基于 ROS/ROS2 中间件，为具身手术 AI 提供多模态手术数据的采集、处理、标注与数据集整理能力，并在 1516 次幻影、离体与在体猪实验中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身手术 AI 依赖可靠的多模态数据，但手术数据采集成本高、难以重复，且高度依赖特定硬件配置，缺乏统一、可配置的数据工具链。

Method: 提出 Sensorium 开源 Python 库，面向可配置的机器人与传感器数据流，集成四类功能：操作者导向的数据采集、可配置的处理流程、语义标注、以及情节式（episodic）数据集生成；底层依赖 ROS 与 ROS2 中间件。

Result: 在涵盖多类手术任务与多种传感配置的 1516 次记录上完成评估，数据来源包括幻影、离体与在体猪实验，并报告了初步的可用性反馈。

Conclusion: Sensorium 为多模态手术数据的规范化采集与整理提供了可复用、硬件可配置的开源方案，具备跨实验类型的实用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Embodied surgical AI requires reliable multimodal data, yet surgical data collection remains costly, difficult to repeat, and strongly dependent on the hardware setup. We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. We evaluate Sensorium across 1516 recordings of phantom, ex vivo, and in vivo porcine experiments involving multiple surgical tasks and sensing configurations, and report preliminary usability feedback.

</details>


### [193] [ID2Pattern: Pretraining Generalizable Identity Behavior Patterns for Transaction Risk Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41988)
*Ze Zhao,Haoyu Jiang,Weiyu Han,Ruobing Zuo,Xiao Tan,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ID2Pattern 预训练框架，通过 Local Anonymous ID (LAID) 分词器与多尺度模式码本，将高基数身份字段（卡、设备、账号 ID）的"身份行为模式"而非具体数值引入交易基础模型，在工业数据与 6 个公开数据集上分别平均提升 4.13% 与 8.83%，且在身份值完全未见时依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线支付系统中，卡/设备/账号等高基数身份字段蕴含丰富行为信号却未被充分利用。其词表持续增长且缺乏语义，使基于词表或语义的编码方式失效；真正可泛化的信息不在具体取值，而在身份在交易序列中的出现与复发模式。

Method: ID2Pattern 分两步：(1) 用 LAID 分词器编码身份字段，仅刻画每个身份在序列中的出现方式与复发规律，与具体取值无关；(2) 在无标注序列上预训练多尺度模式码本，将 LAID 序列抽象为离散行为模式，供下游预测任务使用。

Result: 在超 1 亿条序列的工业数据集与 6 个公开数据集上持续优于强基线：工业数据平均提升 4.13%，公开数据集平均提升 8.83%；当训练中所有身份值均为未见值时，效果仍保持同等水平。

Conclusion: 以"模式"替代"取值"来表征身份字段，可有效解决高基数、动态增长词表的编码难题，并为新用户与新市场的泛化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Large-scale behavioral pretraining offers a promising route to generalizable representations in online payment systems. However, high-cardinality identity fields such as card, device, and account IDs, though rich in behavioral signals, remain underexploited. Their continually growing vocabularies and limited semantic meaning make existing vocabulary-based and semantics-based encodings ill-suited. Their generalizable signal lies not in specific values, but in the identity behavior patterns they exhibit across a transaction sequence. Building on this insight, we propose ID2Pattern, a pretraining framework that brings these patterns into transaction foundation models. ID2Pattern first encodes identity fields with a Local Anonymous ID (LAID) tokenizer, which captures how each identity appears and recurs within the sequence, independent of its specific value. It then pretrains a multi-scale pattern codebook on unlabeled sequences, which abstracts LAID sequences into discrete behavior patterns for downstream prediction. Experiments on an industrial dataset with over 100M sequences and six public datasets show that ID2Pattern consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving industrial by 4.13% and public datasets by 8.83% on average. Notably, ID2Pattern remains equally effective even when all identity values are unseen during training, highlighting its potential to generalize to new users and markets.

</details>


### [194] [Consensus Within, Competition Between: Decentralized LLM Teams for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41044)
*Khawaja Abaid Ullah,Ananya Acharya,Mohammad Javad Khojasteh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在简化周界防御仿真中比较集中式指挥官、拍卖式共识团队与独立LLM智能体的任务分配架构，发现带专用外部冲突消解协议的共识团队入侵率最低（15%），且伪装类干扰物是LLM的主要失效点，可由一句提示预警显著缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM协调多智能体团队时存在根本性架构选择问题：任务分配应集中于单一LLM控制器，还是分散给自主LLM智能体，尤其在通信不可靠条件下缺乏实证依据。

Method: 构建刻意简化的周界防御仿真，对手为LLM控制的入侵者；对比集中式指挥官（central commander）、拍卖式共识团队（auction-based consensus）与独立智能体三种架构，覆盖五种网络条件；对共识团队还测试了将出价计算权限下放给低成本LLM的影响；在空域加入鸟类与民用无人机照片输入，并测试伪装成标本鸟无人机的入侵者及一句提示预警的干预效果。

Result: 入侵率：共识团队15%、指挥官32%、独立智能体29%；五种网络条件中仅有限无线电通信距离显著改变入侵率（提高指挥官组）。共识团队中让低成本LLM更多介入出价计算，入侵率上升38个百分点。含鸟类与民用无人机照片时，仅接近前沿（near-frontier）模型能将其与入侵者区分；伪装的标本鸟无人机被两类模型误判为鸟类，near-frontier模型未追击率达59–61%（此前1–16%），加入一句预警提示后降至0–25%。

Conclusion: 配备专用外部冲突消解协议的LLM团队入侵率最低；通信可靠性并非主要瓶颈，而模型层级与提示层面的干扰物辨别是决定防御效能的关键因素。

Abstract: The use of large language models (LLMs) to coordinate multi-agent teams raises a fundamental architectural question: whether task allocation should be centralized in a single LLM controller or distributed across autonomous LLM agents, particularly when communication is unreliable. In a deliberately simplified perimeter-defense simulation against LLM-controlled intruders, we compared a central commander, an auction-based consensus team and independent agents under five network conditions. Breach rates were 15% for the consensus team, 32% for the commander and 29% for independent agents, and of the different network conditions, only limited radio range changed a team's breach rate, raising the commander's. In consensus team, giving a low-cost LLM more authority over the bid computation raised breaches by 38 percentage points. With birds and civilian drones in airspace, we added a photograph for each to the input text state. Only the near-frontier model treated them apart from intruders. Intruders disguised as existing taxidermy bird drones were mistook as birds by both low-cost and near-frontier LLMs, going unpursued in 59-61% of near-frontier decisions (1-16% before). However, a one-sentence forewarning in the prompt lowered it to 0-25%. We find that LLM team with a dedicated external conflict resolution protocol results in fewest breaches. Code: https://github.com/the-kas-lab/consensus-within-competition-between.

</details>


### [195] [From 1D Benchmarks to Complex 3D Geometries: Can Agents Reliably Specify Problems for Automated PDE Simulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41267)
*Akshat Jani,Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究智能体能否可靠构建完整可执行的仿真JSON规范，并提出结合模型抽取、确定性科学/能力校验、显式澄清与局部修补的工作流；在120个请求上首轮规范正确率达98.3%，无错误"就绪"决策，下游30例成功执行、12例实测中9例端到端成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将工程需求转化为仿真需要一致的几何、物理、边界条件与数值设置。现有智能体辅助系统虽能自动搭建仿真，但"能跑通"并不等于"规范忠实保持了原问题"或"正确处理了缺失信息"，因此需要分别评估规范正确性、执行完成度与数值验证。

Method: 提出一个工作流：模型抽取 + 确定性的科学性与能力检查 + 显式澄清机制 + 经校验的局部补丁（保留已接受的无关字段）。分别评估规范正确性、执行完成与数值验证；进行组件消融（科学性检查、条件式修复的贡献），并开展1D/2D/3D稳态与非稳态、线性与非线性问题（含复杂三维机翼几何的流动与弹性）的下游研究。

Result: 120个模板类请求上，完整工作流首轮规范与决策正确率98.3%，对比schema辅助单次生成的92.5%与纯模型82.5%（提升5.8与15.8个百分点）；42个需澄清或拒绝的请求中零错误"就绪"判定。消融实验显示科学性检查与条件修复有贡献。30例画廊实现自动执行；12例真实用户端到端测试中9例同时满足规范、执行、导出与数值参考成功（75.0%）。

Conclusion: 在测试范围内支持可追溯、工程师复核的仿真辅助；但新响应回归问题表明一致性仍是后续改进的重点。

Abstract: Turning an engineering request into a simulation requires consistent geometry, physics, boundary conditions, and numerical settings. Recent agent-assisted systems automate simulation setup, but successful execution alone does not establish whether the specification preserves the intended problem or correctly handles missing information. We study whether agents can reliably construct complete executable JSON specifications for automated geometry creation, meshing, PDE solving, and result export. Our workflow combines model extraction with deterministic scientific and capability checks, explicit clarification, and validated local patches that preserve unrelated accepted fields. We evaluate specification correctness, execution completion, and numerical verification separately. On 120 template-related requests, the full workflow achieves 98.3% correct first-turn specification and decision outcomes, compared with 92.5% for schema-assisted single shot and 82.5% for model-only: gains of 5.8 and 15.8 percentage points, respectively. It makes no false-ready decisions among the 42 requests requiring clarification or rejection. Component ablations identify contributions from scientific checks and conditional repair. Separate downstream studies cover 1D, 2D, and 3D steady and unsteady, linear and nonlinear problems, including flow and elasticity with complex three-dimensional wing geometries. A 30-case gallery demonstrates automated execution from retained JSON specifications, while 9 of 12 selected live user-to-results tests achieve joint specification, execution, export, and numerical-reference success (75.0%). These results support traceable, engineer-reviewed simulation assistance within the tested scope, while fresh-response regressions identify consistency as a priority for further improvement.

</details>


### [196] [Rome Wasn't Built in One Turn: Replay-Grounded Reward Signals for Long-Horizon Freeciv Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41264)
*Yanchen Liu,Xinyu Che,Brihi Joshi,Renzo Fukuda,Junhua Liu,Ruifeng Li,Yunfei Ge,Hang Yan,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"记分板陷阱"现象：策略游戏中即时分数不能可靠预测最终胜负（8-68%的位置对中高分位置结果更差）。作者构建CivHarness（可保存状态、分支决策并前向回放）训练LLM裁判CivTelescope预测两个局面中哪个最终结果更优，在陷阱对上准确率是现成模型1.3-2.0倍；以其作为稠密奖励训练Freeciv智能体CivMarshmallow，中期训练阶段收益8.94分，比最优基线（终局奖励）高77%，全场混合奖励效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程策略游戏（如Freeciv）中确定性反馈稀疏且往往只在终局出现；游戏内分数虽稠密但不可靠地预测最终结果，形成"记分板陷阱"，直接使用会导致奖励错配。因此需要更可靠的、可度量的稠密奖励信号。

Method: 1) 构建CivHarness：开源系统，可保存当前游戏状态、对不同决策分支、回放到未来回合，从保存位置获取可度量的长时程反馈；2) 用其训练CivTelescope：一个LLM裁判，预测两局面中哪个会得到更好结果；3) 将CivTelescope作为稠密奖励训练Freeciv智能体，形成CivMarshmallow；4) 全场采用混合奖励：前期用记分板/终局奖励，随游戏推进逐渐切换到CivTelescope。

Result: 在八款策略游戏的局面对中，8-68%的高分局面最终结果更差；CivTelescope在记分板陷阱对上比现成语言模型准确率高1.3-2.0倍，并能泛化到新开局、更晚回合和未见规则；CivMarshmallow在陷阱频发的中期阶段平均训练增益8.94分，比最优基线高77%；120回合全程混合奖励在所有比较的奖励中得分最高。

Conclusion: 游戏内分数作为奖励存在系统性偏差（记分板陷阱），而基于前向回放监督训练的LLM裁判可提供可靠稠密奖励；结合随游戏进程动态切换的混合奖励策略，能显著提升长时程策略游戏智能体的训练效果。

Abstract: Training language-model agents to play long-horizon strategy games is hard because deterministic feedback is sparse and often arrives only at the end of a game. In-game scores can offer dense signals, but they do not reliably predict future outcomes: in 8-68% of position pairs across eight strategy games, the higher-scoring position leads to the worse outcome. We term this phenomenon scoreboard traps. In Freeciv, an open-source turn-based empire-building game where several players manage units, cities, and diplomacy over hundreds of turns, we look into better reward signals for gameplay agents. To facilitate this, we build CivHarness, an open system that saves current game states, branches on different decisions, and replays the game to future turns to provide measurable long-horizon feedback from saved positions. We use CivHarness to train CivTelescope, an LLM Judge that predicts which one of two game positions will yield the better outcome. On scoreboard-trap pairs, CivTelescope is 1.3-2.0 times more accurate than off-the-shelf language models, and it generalizes to new game starts, later turns, and unseen rules. We then train a Freeciv gameplay agent with CivTelescope as a dense reward, and call this recipe CivMarshmallow. When used in the middle stage of the game where scoreboard traps are common, CivMarshmallow reaches an average training phase gain of 8.94 points, 77% more than the best baseline, the end-of-game reward. Over the full 120-turn game, CivMarshmallow uses a hybrid reward that starts from the scoreboard or the end-of-game reward and shifts to CivTelescope as the game advances; this hybrid reward scores highest among the rewards we compare.

</details>


### [197] [Payroll Fraud Detection in Synthetic HR/ERP Graphs: An Inductive Comparison of Rules, Path Features, and Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41085)
*Xiaobo Chen,Genliang Zhu,Ziyuan Wang,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在合成HR/ERP图上的归纳式链接预测与实体排序任务中，一个基于链式连接特征的调优梯度提升模型在整体链接AP（0.823 vs 0.663）与4跳AP（0.646 vs 0.272）上均优于GraIL式GNN，说明多跳增益并非GNN专属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薪酬欺诈筛查需在隐藏的员工—收款人关系中区分可疑与合法关联，而现有方法（分析师规则、显式路径特征、GNN）缺乏在未见组织与配对良性孪生样本下的统一比较。

Method: 将其形式化为合成HR/ERP图上的归纳式链接预测与实体排序问题；每个植入案例配一个匹配的良性孪生；在24个留出组织上进行原始研究，并加入前瞻性冻结扩展；对比分析师规则、显式路径特征、GNN（GraIL式链接GNN与关系式实体GNN），并使用链式连接特征的调优梯度提升模型；采用仅结构审计检验生成器捷径。

Result: 链式连接提升模型链接AP最高（0.823，GNN 0.663），4跳AP 0.646 vs 0.272；新合成队列（2.0%正实体）中仍领先（AP 0.798），实体GNN优于记录感知规则（0.707 vs 0.504）；2%审查预算下精确率0.695 vs 0.602；40%标识符连接失败时实体GNN略优（AP 0.712 vs 0.683，仅描述性）；仅结构审计发现初始生成器存在捷径，匹配孪生使孪生池AUROC降至0.493–0.514。

Conclusion: 在显式合成评估设计下，可将多跳候选发现、基于记录的判别与审查优先级分开评估；多跳优势不依赖GNN，而鲁棒性场景下关系式实体GNN更有潜力；匹配孪生对消除生成器捷径至关重要。

Abstract: Payroll-fraud screening requires finding hidden employee--payee links while distinguishing suspicious relationships from legitimate ones. We formulate this problem as inductive link prediction and entity ranking on synthetic human-resources and enterprise resource planning (HR/ERP) graphs, with unseen organizations and a matched benign twin for every planted case. An original study on 24 held-out organizations and a prospectively frozen extension compare analyst rules, explicit path features and graph neural networks (GNNs). A tuned gradient-boosted model on chained-join features achieved the highest overall link average precision (AP; 0.823), above the GraIL-style GNN (0.663); its 4-hop AP was 0.646 versus 0.272. Thus, the observed multi-hop gain is not specific to GNNs. On a new synthetic cohort with 2.0% positive entities, chained-join boosting again led (AP 0.798); the relational entity GNN also exceeded record-aware rules (0.707 versus 0.504). At a 2% review budget, their precision was 0.695 versus 0.602. Under 40% failed identifier joins, the entity GNN ranked above chained-join boosting (AP 0.712 versus 0.683, descriptively). Structure-only auditing exposed a shortcut in the initial generator; matched twins reduced the audited twin-pool AUROC to 0.493--0.514. These results separate multi-hop candidate discovery, record-based discrimination and review prioritization within an explicit synthetic evaluation design. Code and data are available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [198] [Optimization Techniques for Solving the Minimum Red--Blue Separating Set Problem with an Application to Human--AI Text Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41081)
*Marko Djukanović,Staša Vujičić Stanković,Žiga Zebec,Christian Blum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究计算几何中的最小红蓝分离集问题(MRBSSP)，提出紧凑集合覆盖ILP模型、安全预处理规则、GRASP启发式，以及基于逻辑的Benders分解(LBBD)和新型分支-Benders割(BBC)两种精确算法，并在随机图与NLP应用上进行对比实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRBSSP源自计算几何，需在求解质量、下界强度与最优性证明能力之间权衡，现有方法缺乏系统比较，且缺少可解释的NLP应用场景。

Method: 提出紧凑的集合覆盖ILP建模、安全预处理规则、GRASP元启发式；两种精确算法：基于逻辑的Benders分解(LBBD)与新型Branch-and-Benders-Cut(BBC)；与ILP、文献中的VNS对比。实验用250个至多500顶点的随机图；并引入基于词汇图特征的NLP应用区分人类与AI生成文本(42个实例)。

Result: 随机图上各方法优势互补：ILP给出最佳可行解，LBBD给出最强下界和最小平均最优性间隙，BBC最频繁证明最优性。NLP实例中BBC平均最优性间隙最小并证明16个最优解，GRASP优于VNS；最优词汇分离子结构分析在272个测试中识别出113个必需特征与151个可替换特征。

Conclusion: 互补优化策略组合有效；最优词汇判别解具有可解释性且存在显著多样性，为MRBSSP及文本判别应用提供了兼顾求解效率与可解释性的框架。

Abstract: We study the so-called Minimum Red''Blue Separating Set Problem (MRBSSP) which arises from computational geometry. In particular, we propose a compact Set-Cover ILP formulation, safe preprocessing rules, a Greedy Randomized Adaptive Search Procedure (GRASP), and two exact approaches: one is based on Logic-Based Benders Decomposition (LBBD) approach, and the second represents a novel Branch-and-Benders-Cut (BBC) approach. These two exact approaches are compared to ILP--alongside GRASP and the Variable Neighborhood Search (VNS) from literature. Experiments on 250 random graphs with up to 500 vertices reveal complementary strengths: ILP yields the best feasible solutions, LBBD provides the strongest lower bounds and smallest mean optimality gap, and BBC certifies optimality most frequently. We further introduce an NLP application distinguishing human- and AI-generated texts through vocabulary-based graph features. On 42 instances, BBC achieves the smallest mean optimality gap, certifying 16 optima, while GRASP outperforms VNS. Structural analysis of optimal vocabulary separators identifies 113 mandatory and 151 replaceable features across 272 tests, highlighting substantial variability among optimal solutions. Overall, our results demonstrate the effectiveness of complementary optimization strategies and the interpretability of optimal vocabulary-based discrimination.

</details>


### [199] [Clinical-Protocol-Aware Cyber Resilience for Connected Clinical Technologies Under AI-Enabled Adversarial Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41784)
*Pranav Patel,Siva Kannathasan,Prerak Patel,Jigar Patel,Vansh Patel,Sheel Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有界临床微网络与受控互联的临床协议感知网络韧性框架，并用参数化蒙特卡洛仿真区分"技术暴露度"与"临床后果"，量化架构连通性、对抗能力、安全控制等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗日益依赖互联医疗设备、临床信息系统、云服务与分布式诊疗技术，形成贯穿临床工作流的网络安全风险；AI能力加速漏洞发现与攻击路径分析，使限制入侵横向扩散更为紧迫。现有安全设计缺乏将临床因素纳入防护、管理与评估的统一方法。

Method: 构建临床协议感知的网络韧性框架，以有界临床微网络与受控互联为核心，将临床因素融入网络安全的设计、管理与评估，覆盖预防、遏制与事件响应三个环节；采用参数化蒙特卡洛仿真，将技术性网络暴露度与建模的临床后果相区分，并针对架构连通性、对抗能力、安全控制、协议依赖度、风险对齐、通信方向性、生命周期集成与响应时序等八类因素开展实验。

Result: 在仿真假设下：互联架构相对分段架构使平均网络暴露度提高56.3%；预防与遏制联合使用相对无控制条件使暴露度降低60.1%；提高协议依赖度使建模临床影响指数由1.021升至7.567，而平均网络暴露度不变，表明技术暴露与临床后果可以相互背离；风险对齐实验显示，当更高技术风险对应更高临床重要性时，建模临床影响更高。

Conclusion: 该框架为互联医疗环境中的安全边界设计、控制优先级排序与协同遏制提供了临床情境感知的方法；研究结果支持比较性架构分析，而非对真实网络风险、控制有效性或患者伤害的校准估计。

Abstract: Healthcare increasingly relies on connected medical devices, clinical information systems, cloud services, and distributed-care technologies, creating cybersecurity risks across clinical workflows. AI-enabled capabilities can accelerate vulnerability discovery and attack-path analysis, increasing the need to limit compromise propagation. This paper proposes a clinical-protocol-aware cyber-resilience framework based on bounded clinical micro-networks and controlled interconnections. The framework integrates clinical factors into cybersecurity design, management, and evaluation, covering prevention, containment, and incident response. A parameterized Monte Carlo simulation distinguishes technical cyber exposure from modeled clinical consequence. Experiments investigate architectural connectivity, adversarial capability, security controls, protocol dependency, risk alignment, communication directionality, lifecycle integration, and response timing. Under the simulation assumptions, connected architecture increases mean cyber exposure by 56.3% relative to segmented architecture, while combined prevention and containment reduce exposure by 60.1% relative to no controls. Increasing protocol dependency raises the modeled clinical-impact index from 1.021 to 7.567 without changing mean cyber exposure, demonstrating that technical exposure and clinical consequence can diverge. Risk-alignment experiments further indicate higher modeled clinical impact when greater technical risk corresponds to greater clinical significance. The findings support comparative architectural analysis, not calibrated estimates of real-world cyber risk, control effectiveness, or patient harm. The framework provides a clinical-context-aware approach to security-boundary design, control prioritization, and coordinated containment in connected healthcare environments.

</details>


### [200] [Towards Portable Specification of the Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40917)
*Nicolas Gertler,Claudio Novelli,Luciano Floridi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出大语言模型只能通过其周围的"harness"（基础设施）发挥作用，但harness缺乏统一定义。作者将其分解为八个可跨系统规定与比较的功能组件，并提出可移植的规范框架，用于记录"哪个模型运行在哪种harness中"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: harness决定模型能完成什么、如何失败，却无公认定义；不同harness配置可使同一模型报告分数相差数倍，且agent的大部分攻击面位于harness中。现有model card/agent card只提供发布与运行信息，无法确立单次运行内有效配置的序列，而harness会在版本间、运行间，甚至运行中被自我修改工具与配置的agent改变。

Method: 将harness分解为八个可规定、可比较的功能组件；设计可移植的规范框架，记录模型与harness的配对关系；与现有model card、agent card及已生效AI法律中的文档义务进行对比分析。

Result: 提出八组件分解与harness记录框架；论证不同harness配置会导致同一模型评分出现数倍差异，且现有卡片与法律文档义务均未规定所需harness记录。

Conclusion: harness必须作为系统的版本化组成部分被显式描述与规定，并以统一记录形式跨模型、跨系统、跨运行保存。

Abstract: Large language models act only through the infrastructure that surrounds them. That infrastructure, the harness, determines what a model can accomplish and how it fails, yet it has no settled definition. To address this gap, we decompose the harness into eight functional components that can be specified and compared across systems, and we propose a portable specification framework that records which model ran in which harness. The record is needed because different harness configurations can yield several-fold differences in reported scores for the same model, and because much of an agent's attack surface lies in its harness. Existing model cards and agent cards provide release and operational information, but do not establish the sequence of effective configurations within a run. Harnesses can change between releases and between runs, and, where agents edit their own tools and configuration, within a run. Enacted AI laws contain documentation duties relevant to covered systems, subject to their respective scopes and application dates, but do not prescribe the common harness record proposed here. Therefore, the harness has to be explicitly described and specified as a versioned part of the system.

</details>


### [201] [From the Zoo to the BULLPEN: Sample-Efficient LLM Profiling via Multi-Channel Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41375)
*Russell Ro,Qiming Cui,Reza Abbasi-Asl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BULLPEN 通过联合读取模型答案文本、正确性与题目文本构建模型嵌入，并新增20个行为/风格/来源类基准，在260个开源模型上与EmbedLLM等基线相比提升行为任务预测精度，且仅用400题即可保留≥91%的全题池能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型选择需评估多维度属性，逐属性跑各自基准成本高；现有模型嵌入方法（如EmbedLLM）依赖正确性模式与题目特征，且需大量探针，难以刻画冗余度、安全性、风格等更多体现在"如何作答"而非"是否正确"的行为。

Method: 提出BULLPEN框架，对EmbedLLM做三点扩展：(1) 重整下游任务体系并新增20个基于行为、风格与来源（provenance）的基准；(2) 首次将模型自身答案文本与正确性、题目文本联合编码；(3) 用数百道题（400题，占全池1.3%推理算力）而非完整24,118题池来编码模型。

Result: 在260个开源权重模型上，答案文本编码器在新任务上得分0.48±0.10，优于最佳先前编码器的0.35±0.10；读答案文本的编码器仅用400题即保留≥91%全池性能，而仅用正确性的基线为83%。

Conclusion: 嵌入仅依赖模型输出，可支持行为审计与低成本画像，并有望推广到仅API可访问的模型。

Abstract: Choosing among the hundreds of thousands of available large language models (LLMs) requires a thorough understanding of each candidate model's properties. In practice, each property is measured by its own benchmark suite, so profiling a model on many properties means paying for many separate evaluations. To address this, model embeddings compress each model into a fixed-length vector from which many properties can be predicted. Existing methods, such as EmbedLLM, build these vectors from correctness patterns and question features, and often need large probe batteries to do so. However, many behaviors that are important when choosing a model, such as its verbosity, safety and style, show up more in how it answers than in whether it is correct. We introduce BULLPEN (Behavioral Understanding of Large Language models via Profile ENcodings), a framework that extends EmbedLLM in three ways. First, BULLPEN organizes the downstream tasks that prior work uses and adds 20 task benchmarks we built on behavior, style and provenance. Second, it is the first framework to read a model's own answer text jointly with its correctness and the question text. Third, it encodes models from a few hundred questions instead of the full 24,118-question pool. Across 260 open-weight models, our answer-text encoder scores 0.48 +/- 0.10 on the new tasks against 0.35 +/- 0.10 for the best prior encoder. Our encoders that read answer text keep at least 91% of their full-pool skill from only 400 questions (1.3% of the inference compute of the full question pool), while a correctness-only baseline keeps 83% with the same 400 questions. Because these embeddings need only model outputs, they support behavioral auditing and low-cost profiling, and could extend to API-only models. Code and data: https://github.com/wcquesera/bullpen

</details>


### [202] [Do Transaction Prices Reflect Visual Preference? A Diagnostic Study with Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41358)
*Fengxian Ji,Jingpu Yang,Fan Zhang,Junhong Liang,Qian Jiang,Yuke Li,Jinri Guo,Rongxin Zhu,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MarketImage 诊断框架，检验在线交易价格何时能作为视觉偏好的低成本监督信号，发现仅在有限条件下价格可编码可恢复的视觉偏好，应视为不确定代理而非真值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉偏好评估依赖昂贵人工标注或偏好奖励模型；电商交易价格易得且反映真实购买行为，但价格混杂来源、需求、竞争等非视觉因素，聚合准确率无法区分模型误差、价格信号不可靠与协议混淆，形成可识别性难题。

Method: MarketImage 含三部分：(i) MarketImageArt，266 个六图比较集，含交易价格与盲价格人工排序；(ii) MarketImageLens，保平局协议，将 3,990 个组内图像对划分为价格-人类一致、冲突、价格平局区域，并单独审计呈现顺序效应；(iii) MarketValuator，六种引导方法（直接比较、独立打分、预算约束、多智能体聚合），评估静态视觉评分器与三种多模态语言模型的信号恢复能力。

Result: 仅 24.2% 图像对具有非平局价格方向，其中 34.7% 与人类多数一致；该区域内 HPSv2 以 66.4% Recoverable Accuracy 领先；Qwen 的预算与聚合变体落后于基线；GPT 的界面增益在统计上未达显著。

Conclusion: 交易价格仅在有限条件下编码可恢复的视觉偏好，应将其视为不确定代理而非真值；该框架为价格监督的适用边界提供了可诊断的评估路径。

Abstract: Existing visual preference evaluation relies on costly human annotations or preference reward models, whereas online marketplace transaction prices are readily available and reflect real purchasing behavior, offering potential low-cost supervision. However, transaction prices conflate visual preferences with non-visual factors such as provenance, demand, and competition, making aggregate accuracy insufficient to distinguish model errors, unreliable price signals, and protocol confounds. To address this identifiability challenge, we introduce MarketImage, a diagnostic framework that identifies when transaction prices reflect visual preferences, where they fail, and whether multimodal language models can recover human-supported visual preference signals. MarketImage comprises three components: (i) MarketImageArt, 266 six-image comparison sets with transaction prices and price-blind human rankings; (ii) MarketImageLens, a tie-preserving protocol that partitions 3,990 within-set image pairs into price-human agreement, conflict, and price-tie regions while separately auditing presentation-order effects; and (iii) MarketValuator, six elicitation methods spanning direct comparison, independent scoring, budget constraints, and multi-agent aggregation to evaluate signal recovery by static visual scorers and three multimodal language models. Experiments show that only 24.2% of image pairs have a non-tied price direction, of which 34.7% agree with the human majority. Within this region, HPSv2 leads at 66.4% Recoverable Accuracy; Qwen's budgeted and aggregated variants trail baselines, while GPT interface gains remain statistically unresolved. Thus, transaction prices encode recoverable visual preferences only under limited conditions and should be treated as uncertain proxies rather than ground truth.

</details>


### [203] [Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879)
*P. A. Golovinski,A. A. Drobyshev,A. O. Shatalova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Mamba型选择性状态空间模型的客户偏好动态预测方法，隐状态为三角模糊数，在Matveev双分量代数下运算，保证模糊不随时间累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售连锁客户的偏好演化预测需要同时给出可解释的模糊性（不确定性）度量。传统模糊递推模型在长时间迭代中模糊度会不断累积扩张；而纯清晰（crisp）模型虽可用现代序列模型建模，却无法输出语言可解释的模糊估计。作者希望兼顾序列模型表达力、模糊语义可解释性与代数严谨性。

Method: 客户偏好用三角模糊数表示；其演化由Mamba型选择性状态空间模型驱动，隐状态为三角数，计算在Matveev双分量模糊数代数中进行。递推参数由清晰特征计算得到，模糊部分保持线性且系数非负。基于此证明状态始终为合法三角数，且是历史观测的凸组合，使代数运算与Zadeh扩展原理一致。

Result: 在含竞争对手促销与客户流失的合成算例上，107参数轻量模型对预测中心的MAE为0.154，优于常惯性演化算子的0.163与朴素预测的0.179；性能与同架构清晰模型持平，并额外给出语言可解释的预测模糊性估计。还可通过语言化读出检测客户向竞争对手的永久性转移。

Conclusion: 将Mamba型选择性状态空间模型与Matveev双分量模糊代数结合，可在保证模糊语义严格性的前提下实现偏好动态预测，并避免模糊度累积，为零售客户流失预警提供可解释工具。局限在于仅在合成数据上验证。

Abstract: We consider forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences of retail chain customers. A customer's preference is described by a triangular fuzzy number. The evolution of the preference over time is governed by a Mamba-type selective state space model whose hidden state is a triangular number, with computations carried out in M.G.~Matveev's two-component algebra of fuzzy numbers. The recurrence parameters are computed from crisp features, while the fuzzy part of the model remains linear with non-negative coefficients. We show that the state then always remains a proper triangular number and is a convex combination of past observations, so that the fuzziness of the forecast does not accumulate over time and the algebraic computations coincide with Zadeh's extension principle. On a synthetic example with competitor promotions and customer switching, a lightweight 107-parameter model reaches a mean absolute error of the forecast centre of 0.154 versus 0.163 for the constant-inertia evolution operator and 0.179 for the naive forecast, matches a crisp model of the same architecture and additionally provides a linguistically interpretable estimate of forecast vagueness. We also show how the linguistic readout of the forecast can be used to detect a permanent switch of a customer to a competitor.

</details>


### [204] [Structure-Aware Skill Retrieval and Arbitration for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41909)
*Anran Liu,Benzhao Hou,Zongsheng Cao,Daichao Wang,Lang Chen,Shitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ArchOR——面向"原型/范式"级别的技能智能体，在OptSkills基础上加入CARL与S³R两个检索模块，以更低成本实现更稳健的优化任务求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可将自然语言优化任务转为求解器可校验的答案，但现有智能体在叙述改写下表现脆弱，且经验多以实例级别存储、复用性差。

Method: 在OptSkills上增加两个检索导向模块：(1) CARL（Centroid-Aware Representation Learning）通过低秩原型残差精炼缓存技能表示的顺序；(2) S³R（Signature-Guided Retrieval）为技能附加开放维结构签名，以加权Jaccard重叠优先排序候选，并将语义重排限制在Top-K内；辅以保持基线的仲裁规则，决定结构检索路径何时取代原选择器。

Result: 在相同DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash骨干与已发布技能库下，五个基准上Micro-Avg. Pass@1达65.00%，较OptSkills提升1.64pp（Micro-Avg.）与2.68pp（Macro-Avg.）。

Conclusion: 以范式级技能组织配合结构签名检索，能在不更换骨干模型的前提下有效提升鲁棒性与跨任务泛化（Macro提升大于Micro，提示对长尾/难任务收益更明显）。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can turn natural-language optimization tasks into solver-checked answers, but current agents remain brittle under narrative rewrites and often store reusable experience at the instance level. We introduce \textsc{ArchOR}, an archetype-centric skill agent that builds on \textsc{OptSkills} by adding two retrieval-focused modules. \emph{Centroid-Aware Representation Learning} (CARL) refines the ordering of cached skill representations through a low-rank prototype residual. \emph{Signature-Guided Retrieval} (S\textsuperscript{3}R) attaches open-dimensional structural signatures to skills, prioritizes candidates by weighted Jaccard overlap, and bounds semantic re-ranking to a top-$K$ set.
