A baseline-preserving arbitration rule determines when the structural route should replace the original selector. With the same DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone and released skill library, \textsc{ArchOR} reaches 65.00\% Micro-Avg.\ Pass@1 across five benchmarks, improving over \textsc{OptSkills} by 1.64~pp Micro-Avg.\ and 2.68~pp Macro-Avg.

</details>


### [205] [RECITE: Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41368)
*Russell Ro,Reza Abbasi-Asl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RECITE 框架，利用 ClinicalTrials.gov 的方案修订文档自动发现、抽取并落地“基于证据的入排标准修订”，构建了 11,913 条实例（5,735 项试验）的数据集，并在专家校验的 LLM-as-judge 协议下评测了 15 种 LLM 配置，最佳模型得分约 3.0/4（归一化约 0.74）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过于严格的入排标准（eligibility criteria, EC）导致临床试验普遍招募失败，但已发表的“标准应如何放宽”的建议与大规模试验设计实践之间脱节，缺乏可规模化、可自动化的落地路径。

Method: 1) 以 ClinicalTrials.gov 的方案修订文档（protocol amendment）为证据源，构建 RECITE 数据集：11,913 条精编实例、覆盖 5,735 项试验，每条将原始入排标准与文献证据及目标修订配对；2) 在 3,116 条子样本上，于“截断至上下文窗口”与“检索增强生成（RAG）”两种条件下基准测试 15 种 LLM 配置；3) 采用 LLM-as-judge 评判协议，并以 10 位领域专家进行验证（judge 与人类二次加权 Cohen's κ_qw = 0.694 ± 0.096）；4) 引入智能体文献发现系统，将文献证据与具体试验匹配。

Result: 最佳开源权重模型 Gemma 2 9B 取得 κ_qw 对齐的序数评分 3.0 ± 0.9（0–4 量表，归一化 0.742）；最佳闭源模型 GPT-4o 为 3.0 ± 0.8（归一化 0.745）。智能体文献发现系统在 5 篇出版物中找到 44 组“论文—试验”匹配，提供了试验设计与 EC 修改证据。

Conclusion: 现有临床知识可被操作化为可执行的试验设计建议；RECITE 打通了“证据发现—标准修订—规模化实施”的链路，表明自动化、证据驱动的入排标准优化在试验设计层面具备可行性与较大提升空间（当前模型评分距满分仍有差距）。

Abstract: Overly restrictive eligibility criteria contribute to widespread enrollment failure in clinical trials, yet published recommendations for how specific criteria should be broadened remain disconnected from trial design at scale. We introduce RECITE (Revising Eligibility Criteria Incorporating Textual Evidence), a framework for automated discovery, extraction, and implementation of evidence-based eligibility criteria revisions. RECITE uses protocol amendment documents from ClinicalTrials.gov to construct a dataset of 11,913 curated instances across 5,735 trials, each pairing original criteria with documentary evidence and target revisions. We benchmark fifteen LLM configurations under truncated-to-context-window and retrieval-augmented generation conditions, on a 3,116-instance sample under an LLM-as-judge protocol validated against ten domain experts (judge vs. human quadratic-weighted Cohen's $κ_{qw} = 0.694 \pm 0.096$). The best open-weight model, Gemma 2 9B, achieves $κ_{qw}$-aligned ordinal score $3.0 \pm 0.9$ on a 0--4 scale (normalized 0.742) and the best closed-weight model, GPT-4o, reaches $3.0 \pm 0.8$ (normalized 0.745). Using an agentic literature discovery system, we then identified 44 paper--trial matches across five publications providing trial-design and EC-modification evidence. Our findings suggest that existing clinical knowledge can be operationalized into actionable trial design recommendations.

</details>


### [206] [Operon-S1: a fast decision controller for long-horizon biomedical agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41350)
*Genoria AI Team,Yuejie Hou,Yueyuxiao Yang,Weiting Tang,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Operon-S1，一个把"判断"与"执行"解耦的快速决策系统（代理的 System 1）：在13个决策点以封闭问题形式接管语言模型代理的惯例、技能、错误、证据与停止判断，仅通过不含答案的固定消息驱动，使判断成为可度量、可替换、可训练的层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理已在生物学中承担长周期科研工作，但其关于规范、技能、错误、证据与终止的judgements嵌在推理过程内部，无法被独立测量、替换或改进。

Method: 设计决策 harness：将代理反复出现的判断形式化为13个决策点的封闭式问题；通过从不携带答案的固定消息执行动作，因此任何能回答这些问题的模型都可充当 judge。在7个生命科学基准上部署本地决策模型，逐条记录决策及其后果；利用任务构建、验证器或执行产生的标签进行再训练，并通过 promotion gate 控制新版本上线。

Result: 在环请求在 CPU 上中位耗时0.6 s且无请求成本；六项基准中五项代理得分无显著变化。相比驱动代理的通用语言模型在回答同类问题时占用任务成本8.0%–35.2%。三个训练版本在每一个问题上决策准确率均有提升。任务级结果使用公开 checkpoint；同一封闭决策亦用于湿实验方案排障与种系靶向富集 panel 设计。

Conclusion: 如同操纵子调控基因开关而不制造其产物，Operon-S1 在不执行具体工作的前提下引导代理，使代理的判断成为一个可测量、可替换、可训练的分层。

Abstract: Language-model agents now carry out long scientific work in biology, but their judgements about conventions, skills, errors, evidence and stopping are made inside the reasoning that does the work, where they cannot be measured, replaced or improved on their own. Here we present Operon-S1, a fast decision system, a System 1 for the agent, that separates judging from doing. A decision harness poses the agent's recurring judgements as closed questions at 13 decision points and acts only through fixed messages that never contain an answer, so any model that answers the questions can serve as the judge. Across seven life-science benchmarks, a local decision model took over these judgements in 0.6 s at the median for an in-loop request on CPU at no request cost, with no measurable change in the agent's score on five of six benchmarks, whereas the general language model that drives the agent, asked the same questions, accounted for 8.0% to 35.2% of task cost. Every decision is recorded with its consequence. Retraining on labels from task construction, verifiers or execution raised decision accuracy on every question in each of three trained versions, and a new version enters service only through a promotion gate. Task-level results in this study use the public checkpoint. The same closed decisions serve wet-laboratory protocol troubleshooting and the design of a germline target enrichment panel. Like the regulator of an operon, which switches genes on and off without making their products, Operon-S1 steers the agent without doing its work, and makes the agent's judgements a layer that can be measured, replaced and trained.

</details>


### [207] [Subliminal Reinforcement Learning: traits travel through a judge's scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41301)
*Keuntae Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在仅有模型自身输出与单一LLM评判器打分的GRPO后训练中，无需任何语义痕迹即可发生"潜意识学习"（subliminal learning）——学生模型对某种动物的偏好会朝评判器偏好方向迁移，并提出四阶段链条解释其机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，学生模型在教师生成的文本上训练时，可通过不含该特质语义痕迹的数据继承教师特质（潜意识学习）。但此前证据主要来自监督式的文本蒸馏。本文关注更严苛也更现实的场景：后训练仅依赖学生自身输出与一个语言模型评判器的分数（GRPO），此时是否仍能发生特质迁移，其机制为何，以及能否用于AI安全风险（不良倾向）的预测与部分防范。

Method: 采用带LLM评判器的组相对策略优化（GRPO）做后训练。学生模型生成无意义的自造音节或数字串，评判器被设定为偏好某一动物，据此打分；对照实验是另一个评判器偏好另一种动物。设计48个实验单元，先通过有效性门槛筛选，再以5%错误发现率（FDR）检验特质是否建立。机制上用消融验证：仅去除两个评判器之间的奖励差即可消除迁移。进一步构建四阶段链条（特质须体现在评判器分数中→偏好能推动学生的字符串→这些字符串被累积→最终到达答案），并在其他数据构建的、或另一模型上的同偏好评判器上检验，以及迁移到AI安全相关不良倾向。

Result: 48个实验单元中2个未通过有效性门槛，17个在5% FDR下确立了特质，其中包含仅用数字的单元；其余单元的失败可由其机制解释。去除评判器间的奖励差即消除迁移。机制链条得到验证：来自其他数据或另一模型的同偏好评判器会把偏好落在不同字符串上，此时检测不到迁移。在AI安全倾向上链条同样成立：不良倾向会传递到学生的答案中，且训练前测得的各阶段指标可预测传递发生的位置。

Conclusion: 潜意识学习并不需要语义通道，仅凭学生自身输出加单一LLM评判器的奖励信号即可实现特质传递，其发生可由"分数体现—字符串推动—累积—到达答案"的四阶段链条刻画；链条被破坏或评判器更换即无迁移。该框架可迁移至AI安全中的不良倾向风险，训练前对链条各阶段的测量能预见传递位置，从而有助于部分预防此类风险。

Abstract: Subliminal learning, the transfer of a model's trait through data with no semantic trace of it, has been shown for students trained on a teacher's text. Post-training relies on group relative policy optimization with a language-model judge, where the student learns only from its own outputs and one judge's scores. We show this suffices: a student writes invented syllables or numbers, a judge made to prefer one animal scores them, and the student's preference moves toward that animal relative to a run whose judge prefers another. Of 48 design cells, two fail the validity gate and 17 establish the trait at 5% false-discovery rate, numbers-only cells included; elsewhere, our account gives the reason. Removing only the reward difference between judges removes transfer. We establish the principle as a chain of four stages: the trait must show in the judge's scores, favour strings that move the student, accumulate and reach the answers. Judges with the same preference built from other data, or on another model, place it on other strings, and transfer is not detected. Carried unchanged to AI-safety dispositions, the chain holds: undesirable dispositions reach the student's answers, and stages measured before training anticipate where, helping to prevent part of this risk.

</details>


### [208] [Self-Improvement with Auxiliary Rewards: Learning from Verified Traces and Evaluating Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41296)
*Minju Gwak,Alan Ritter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究辅助轨迹偏好能否在有限监督下改进小模型的自我提升与迁移。作者比较基于置信度(SC)与基于熵(UID-Conj.,融合全局熵变化与局部平滑性)的两种度量，通过在线奖励增强与离线加权SFT进行验证：UID-Conj. 在线较原始TTRL提升2.08个百分点，离线加权SFT较均匀加权提升1.93个百分点(95% CI [0.44, 3.44])。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 答案级奖励无法区分结果同样正确的推理轨迹，导致在只有最终答案监督时，轨迹层面的学习信号被浪费；作者希望探究辅助的轨迹偏好能否在有限监督条件下提升小语言模型的自我改进与迁移能力，并检验这类偏好是否揭示了推理阶段未体现的学习价值。

Method: 在Qwen3-1.7B上开展两类实验。在线：在30道无参考答案的AIME题目上通过在线奖励增强进行自适应，将SC与UID-Conj.(全局熵变化+局部平滑性)作为辅助偏好信号；离线：使用历史AIME题目，对正确回答进行加权SFT，比较不同权重方案。评估采用宏平均、配对prompt bootstrap置信区间及跨任务迁移基准。

Result: 在线设置下，UID-Conj. 在自适应基准与三个迁移基准上的宏平均较未修改的TTRL提升2.08个百分点。最强证据来自离线加权SFT：在同一回答池上，UID-Conj. 较均匀加权提升1.93个百分点，配对prompt-bootstrap 95% CI为[0.44, 3.44]。收益具有任务依赖性：SC偏好更倾向于轨迹质量与指令遵循，而UID-Conj. 奖励在多个设置下带来更强训练效果。

Conclusion: 辅助轨迹偏好能够在结果同样正确的轨迹中识别出推理阶段未被捕捉的学习价值，因此应通过下游性能增益而非推理时指标来评估其有效性；两类度量各有适用场景，需结合任务特性选择。

Abstract: Answer-level rewards cannot distinguish reasoning traces with equally correct outcomes. We study whether auxiliary trajectory preferences improve small-language-model self-improvement and transfer under limited supervision. We compare confidence-based (SC) and entropy-based metrics (UID-Conj.), which combines global entropy variation and local smoothness, through online reward augmentation and offline training on correct responses. Online experiments adapt Qwen3-1.7B on 30 AIME problems without reference answers; offline experiments use historical AIME problems. UID-Conj. improves the online macro-average across the adaptation benchmark and three transfer benchmarks by 2.08 percentage points over unmodified TTRL. The strongest evidence comes from offline weighted SFT: UID-Conj. exceeds uniform weighting on the same response pool by 1.93 points, with a paired prompt-bootstrap 95\% CI of [0.44, 3.44]. Benefits are task-dependent: SC rewards judged trace quality and instruction following, while UID-Conj. rewards yield stronger training outcomes in several settings. We find that auxiliary preferences can expose learning value among equally correct traces not captured during inference time, motivating their evaluation through downstream gains.

</details>


### [209] [MEMIG: Self-evolving Memory Construction for Antibody Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41245)
*Yunhui Jang,Zach Wang,Jai Doshi,Kangway Chuang,Sidney Lisanza,Taylor Joren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出面向生物分子优化的智能体记忆框架MEMIG，以"可索引、定量、结构化"替代自由文本记忆，通过领域标识符、Oracle量化效应与上位效应建模实现局部更新与精确检索，在计算机抗体优化任务多数设置中性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化LLM智能体将经验表示为自由形式的自然语言并通过改写来维护，仅适合经验可叙事化的任务；在科学发现与优化中，经验往往是在特定上下文中由Oracle测得的量。文本改写缺乏索引定位、以描述替代测量、且无法表达干预之间的相互作用，因此需要新的记忆建模范式。

Method: MEMIG框架：1）为每条洞见赋予领域接地的标识符，使更新局部化、检索变为精确查找；2）累积由性质Oracle测得的定量效应，保持测量的数值性；3）将共同施加的干预所对应洞见相连，通过对每个被评估集合的分解来度量其上位效应（epistasis）。

Result: 在多项计算机抗体（in silico）优化任务的多数设置下，MEMIG相较基线取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 针对生物分子优化的智能体记忆应当是定量、可索引、结构化的；MEMIG证实该设计可行且有效，为科学发现类任务中LLM智能体的记忆表示提供了新方向。

Abstract: Memory has become central to self-evolving LLM agents, which store and retrieve experience from past rollouts to guide future decisions. Existing systems represent this experience as free-form natural language and maintain it by rewriting, which fits tasks whose experience is narratable. However, in scientific discovery and optimization, experience is often a quantity measured by an oracle under a specific context. Rewriting it as text leaves no index for where a record should be, replaces a measurement with a description, and cannot express how interventions interact. To address this, we argue that agent memory for biomolecule optimization should instead be indexable, quantitative, and structured, and we introduce MEMIG. This memory construction framework realizes these three properties. In detail, MEMIG addresses each insight with a domain-grounded identifier and accumulates the quantitative effect measured by a property oracle, so that updates remain local and retrieval becomes an exact lookup. Next, MEMIG connects insights whose interventions are applied together and measures their epistasis by decomposing each evaluated set. Our results show that MEMIG improves performance in most settings across in silico antibody optimization tasks.

</details>


### [210] [RTL-Interact: Training EDA-Interactive Models for Multi-Task RTL Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41225)
*Wenxuan Shi,Yaoyu Zhu,Di Huang,Chongxiao Li,Xiaoyun Zhang,Muxin Song,Yutong Wu,Ziyuan Nan,Pengwei Jin,Tian Zhi,Xishan Zhang,Rui Zhang,Xing Hu,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RTL-Interact：多任务后训练让单一RTL专用模型学会生成RTL、构造EDA检查并依据工具反馈修正，配合PAPO强化学习，在CVDP/VerilogEval/RTLLM上超越基线甚至教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL设计需要基于EDA工具反馈进行校验与迭代修正，但现有RTL专用模型缺乏与EDA工具的学习式交互，或依赖分离的生成与修复模块，难以形成端到端可靠实现。

Method: 提出多任务后训练框架：自动构建多任务示范，并采用从思维链到工具集成推理的分阶段监督，训练单一模型同时生成RTL、构造任务适配的EDA检查、利用反馈修正；并提出Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO)，在GRPO基础上加入正确性门控奖励（偏好简洁的成功检查并奖励有效修正）与任务分组以保持各目标覆盖。

Result: CVDP上RTL-Interact-32B达48.7%加权Pass@1、68.9% Pass@5，超过教师模型DeepSeek-V3.2；8B版本44.9%加权Pass@1。PAPO在提升Pass@1的同时将平均工具调用减少63.8%。两模型在VerilogEval v2与RTLLM v2上均优于所有RTL专用基线。

Conclusion: 统一的多任务后训练加PAPO强化学习可有效赋予RTL模型工具交互与自我修正能力，以更少工具调用取得更高正确率，验证了方法的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models offer a promising route to register-transfer-level (RTL) design, but reliable implementations require checking and revision based on electronic design automation (EDA) tools' feedback. Existing RTL-specialized models often lack learned interaction with such EDA tools or rely on separate generation and repair components. We introduce RTL-Interact, a multi-task post-training framework that trains a single RTL-specialized model to generate RTL, construct task-appropriate EDA checks, and revise implementations using tool feedback. The framework combines automatically constructed multi-task demonstrations with staged supervision from chain-of-thought to tool-integrated reasoning. We further introduce Pattern-Aware Policy Optimization (PAPO) for multi-task reinforcement learning. PAPO augments Group Relative Policy Optimization with correctness-gated rewards that favor concise successful checking while rewarding effective revision, and uses task grouping to preserve coverage across objectives. On CVDP, RTL-Interact-32B achieves 48.7% weighted Pass@1 and 68.9% Pass@5, surpassing its teacher model, DeepSeek-V3.2, while RTL-Interact-8B achieves 44.9% weighted Pass@1. Additionally, we show that PAPO can reduce average tool calls by 63.8% while improving Pass@1. Both models also outperform all RTL-specialized baselines on VerilogEval v2 and RTLLM v2, demonstrating the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [211] [LLM Detoxification via Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41165)
*Aishan Liu,Yisong Xiao,Xianglong Liu,Yongxin Huang,Siyuan Liang,Zonghao Ying,Yang Qu,Jian Yang,Yaodong Yang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TARGRS，一种在潜表征空间中学习毒性转变奖励引导的测试时去毒框架，通过渐进式引导构造非毒→毒轨迹以产生稠密监督，推理时沿相反方向由粗到精采样，10个LLM上毒性最多降69.38%，并扩展至LVLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可生成毒性内容，需有效的测试时去毒。现有表征引导方法多为静态或粗粒度干预，且未充分建模有毒与非毒生成之间的状态转变，导致引导不精确。

Method: TARGRS：探索阶段沿毒性方向渐进式引导，构造从非毒到毒的表征轨迹，用轨迹上的毒性分数将稀疏标注转为稠密的listwise监督，训练转变感知的token级奖励模型；推理阶段沿相反（非毒）方向做由粗到精采样——粗采样确定到达非毒区域所需干预，精化阶段选取扰动最小的去毒表征。

Result: 在RealToxicityPrompts、BOLD、AttaQ上，10个LLM中一致优于SOTA基线，RealToxicityPrompts毒性最多降低69.38%；速度接近原LLM，核心能力保持良好；在语言解码器空间干预使其可迁移至LVLM，3个代表性LVLM毒性最多降低64.23%。

Conclusion: 将去毒建模为潜空间中的毒性转变并用奖励引导采样，可实现精确、低扰动、低开销的测试时去毒，并具备跨模态泛化性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate toxic content, motivating effective test-time detoxification. Existing representation steering methods often rely on static or coarse interventions and provide imprecise guidance because they inadequately model transitions between toxic and non-toxic generations. We propose Transition-Aware Reward-Guided Representation Sampling (TARGRS), a test-time framework that learns reward guidance over toxicity transitions in latent representation space. During exploration, progressive steering along the toxicity direction constructs trajectories from non-toxic to toxic states. Toxicity scores along these trajectories turn sparse annotations into dense, listwise supervision for a transition-aware token-level reward model. During inference, TARGRS uses this model to guide coarse-to-fine sampling along the opposite, non-toxic direction: coarse sampling identifies the intervention needed to reach non-toxic regions, and refinement selects a detoxified representation with minimal perturbation. Experiments on 10 LLMs across RealToxicityPrompts, BOLD, and AttaQ show that TARGRS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing toxicity by up to 69.38% on RealToxicityPrompts. TARGRS runs nearly as fast as the original LLM while preserving its core capabilities with minimal impact. By intervening in the language decoder space, TARGRS also extends to Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), reducing toxicity by up to 64.23% across 3 representative LVLMs.

</details>


### [212] [Lifelong Learning of Scheduling Rules with Surrogate-assisted Genetic Programming for Dynamic Flexible Job Shop Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41158)
*Luyao Zhu,Fangfang Zhang,Yi Mei,Ke Chen,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对动态柔性作业车间调度(DFJSS)中遗传规划(GP)学到的调度规则难以跨任务泛化、微调易灾难性遗忘的问题，提出一种代理辅助的GP终身学习方法，依次处理异质任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GP训练的调度规则仅在相似规模与分布的任务上泛化，任务特性变化时性能显著下降；在新任务上微调又会导致灾难性遗忘。而GP在DFJSS中的终身学习研究仍十分不足。

Method: 提出代理辅助GP终身学习框架，包含三部分：(1) 自适应两阶段训练策略，平衡可塑性与稳定性；(2) 基于任务相似性的加权机制，调节知识复用；(3) 带距离约束的改进代理模型，提高评估精度。任务涵盖作业到达与批量到达两类序列异质场景。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持较强适应性的同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘，优于多个对比方法。

Conclusion: 终身GP在动态调度环境中具有潜力，可在序列异质DFJSS任务上实现知识保持与适应性的平衡。

Abstract: Genetic programming (GP) has been widely used to learn scheduling rules for dynamic flexible job shop scheduling (DFJSS). However, trained rules tend to only generalise to tasks of similar scales and distributions, and when task characteristics change, learned rules often suffer significant performance degradation. Moreover, fine-tuning pre-trained rules on new tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where prior knowledge is lost. This motivates the need for GP methods that can adapt to new tasks while preserving past knowledge, leading to a lifelong learning paradigm. However, lifelong learning in GP for DFJSS remains largely under-explored. To address this gap, this paper proposes a surrogate-assisted GP method for lifelong learning to handle sequential heterogeneous DFJSS tasks with job or batch arrivals. The framework includes three key components: an adaptive two-stage training strategy to balance plasticity and stability, a task-similarity-based weighting mechanism to regulate knowledge reuse, and an improved surrogate model with distance constraints to enhance estimation accuracy. Experimental results show that the proposed method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting while maintaining strong adaptability, outperforming several compared methods and demonstrating the potential of lifelong GP in dynamic scheduling environments.

</details>


### [213] [BABA-Bench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Patient-Advocacy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41076)
*Shriram Vasudevan,Videet Mehta,Connor Sweeney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BABA-Bench，一个由 47 个交互式环境构成的基准，用于评估 LLM 智能体在患者就医协助任务中是否同时满足"结果正确"与"程序合规"两项要求；结果显示多数前沿模型存在"以不合规路径达成正确结果"的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗场景中的 LLM 智能体不仅需达成正确结果，还必须遵守必要程序（如共享病历前取得患者授权）。现有评测多只关注最终结果，忽视了过程合规性，因此需要构建能逐动作校验许可、证据与禁止行为的测试环境。

Method: 构建 BABA-Bench：47 个交互式世界，每个世界源自脱敏的真实患者维权案例并经专家审阅；智能体需发掘隐藏信息并协调保险公司、诊所、供应商、药房与社区组织。引擎在动作发生时即时校验三项：是否具备授权、是否有行动证据、是否属禁止行为。评测 14 个前沿模型，每世界 3 次运行，共 1,974 次。

Result: 在 921 次达成全部必要结果的运行中，254 次（28%）未遵守必要程序。Claude Opus 5 有 81.6% 的运行达成全部必要结果，但仅 52.5% 通过全部校验。向两个模型提供经验证的路径后，通过率分别由 29.8% 升至 91.5%、由 44.0% 升至 97.2%。

Conclusion: 当前模型的瓶颈主要在于"寻找合规路径"，而非"执行路径"。在结果导向指标之外引入过程合规评测具有必要性；提供已验证路线可显著提升合规通过率。

Abstract: An LLM agent that helps a patient get care must do two things. It must reach the correct outcome, and it must follow the necessary procedure. For example, the agent must get the patient's permission before it shares the patient's records. We introduce BABA-Bench, a benchmark of 47 interactive worlds. We built each world from a de-identified, real patient advocacy case that expert advocates reviewed. In each world, the agent must find hidden information and coordinate insurers, clinics, suppliers, pharmacies, and community organizations. The engine checks each action at the time the agent does it. It checks if the agent has permission, if the agent has evidence for the action, and if the action is prohibited. We evaluated 14 frontier models with three runs on each world, for a total of 1,974 runs. Agents often reached the correct outcome by an invalid route. Of the 921 runs that reached all required outcomes, 254 (28%) did not follow the necessary procedure. Claude Opus 5 reached all required outcomes in 81.6% of its runs, but only 52.5% of its runs passed all checks. When we gave two models a verified route, their pass rates increased from 29.8% to 91.5% and from 44.0% to 97.2%. For these models, the main problem is to find a route, not to execute it.

</details>


### [214] [Breaking Information Islands in Sparse Tuning via Small-World Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41665)
*Xianchao Guan,Zijun Xiong,Yifeng Wang,Yubo Cui,Yaowei Wang,Guo-Sen Xie,Xin Li,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SWIFT 是一种受小世界网络启发的稀疏微调框架，将块对角稀疏更新与固定确定性散射置换结合，在不增加可训练参数的前提下消除"信息孤岛"，在常识推理、自然语言生成与图像分类上平均优于所评估的 PEFT 基线，并可匹敌或超越全参数微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏微调因参数高效而被广泛用于大模型适配，但其效果不仅取决于可训练参数数量，还取决于稀疏更新结构支撑的特征交互拓扑。支撑中有限或不均匀的连通性会隔离部分特征维度，形成信息孤岛，限制可训练更新上的任务特定直接交互。

Method: 提出 SWIFT：用块对角稀疏更新实现局部特征混合，再以固定的确定性散射置换（scattering permutation）将混合输出跨特征组路由，且不引入额外可训练参数；理论上分析随机稀疏更新与块对角更新的信息孤岛问题，并证明当块对角因子由等大、结构稠密的方阵块构成时，充分的散射置换可保证正的块级扩张。

Result: 在常识推理、自然语言生成和图像分类任务上的大量实验表明，SWIFT 平均优于所评估的 PEFT 基线，并可匹配或超过全参数微调。

Conclusion: 特征交互拓扑是稀疏微调效果的关键因素；通过确定性散射置换实现小世界式跨组连通，可在零额外参数开销下打破信息孤岛，是一种简洁且有效的 PEFT 方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sparse tuning is widely used to adapt large language models due to its parameter efficiency. However, its effectiveness depends not only on the number of trainable parameters, but also on the feature-interaction topology of the sparse update's structural support. Limited or uneven connectivity in this support may isolate subsets of feature dimensions, forming information islands that restrict direct task-specific interactions through the trainable update. To address this issue, we propose Small-World Induced Fine-Tuning (SWIFT), a sparse tuning framework inspired by small-world connectivity. SWIFT combines block-diagonal sparse updates for local feature mixing with a fixed deterministic scattering permutation that routes the mixed outputs across feature groups without additional trainable parameters. We further show theoretically that certain random sparse-update models and block-diagonal updates can exhibit information islands, whereas a sufficiently scattering permutation guarantees positive block-level expansion when the trainable block-diagonal factor consists of equal-sized, structurally dense square blocks. Extensive experiments on commonsense reasoning, natural language generation, and image classification demonstrate that SWIFT outperforms the evaluated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) baselines on average and can match or surpass full fine-tuning. Implementation is available at https://github.com/xianchaoguan/SWIFT

</details>


### [215] [Connectivity-Aware Client Selection in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41570)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Monica Nicoli,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于图谱工具（特征向量嵌入、热/扩散核相似度）的轻量级客户端选择方法，在固定通信图上仅向结构上相关的邻居发送模型更新，在降低50%消息量的同时接近甚至超越全广播精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习（DFL）虽消除了服务器单点故障与通信瓶颈，但现有方法多采用全邻域广播，每轮每个客户端向所有邻居发送模型更新，带来显著通信开销与高能耗。需要一种利用图拓扑的轻量选择机制来减少传输量。

Method: 以固定通信图的拉普拉斯矩阵谱性质为分析基础，利用特征向量嵌入以及热核/扩散核相似度作为相似性度量，引导每个客户端仅选择结构上最相关、对联邦收敛最有益的邻居子集，形成拓扑感知的随机化邻居子采样。

Result: 与均匀邻域子采样相比持平或更优，精度接近全广播；在较大且结构丰富的图上、强数据异质性条件下收益最大，约提升3个百分点；传输消息总数相较朴素广播减少50%。

Conclusion: 基于谱图结构的客户端选择是一种有效且低开销的DFL通信优化手段，可在显著降低通信与能耗的同时保持或提升收敛性能，尤其适用于结构丰富图上的强异质场景。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) has emerged as a scalable alternative to centralized federated learning, removing the single point of failure and communication bottleneck at the server. However, most existing decentralized approaches rely on full-neighborhood broadcasting, where each client transmits its model update to all of its neighbors in each communication round. While effective, this results in significant communication overhead and high energy consumption. This paper applies spectral graph tools as lightweight client selection methods that leverage the graph topology by analyzing the spectral properties of the graph Laplacian over a fixed communication graph. Specifically, we exploit eigenvector embeddings and heat/diffusion kernel similarities to guide each client in selecting only a subset of structurally relevant neighbors, i.e., those that are most beneficial for the convergence of the federation process. This topology aware randomization matches or outperforms uniform neighborhood subsampling and approaches the accuracy of full broadcasting. The largest gains, about $3$ percentage points, emerge under strong data heterogeneity on larger, structurally rich graphs, while the total number of transmitted messages is reduced by $50\%$ compared to naive broadcasting.

</details>


### [216] [P-ContextBench: From Personal Fact Recall to Personalized Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40959)
*Grant Nasir Brown,Abdul Basir Bari,Anmol Tadikonda,Isabel Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 P-ContextBench，一个基于合成"数字人"与碎片化移动应用轨迹来评估大模型个人上下文理解与安全使用的可验证、可扩展基准，并揭示当前SOTA模型在时序追踪、高层模式推断与约束感知行动边界上准确率低于60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多用于个人化任务，但个人上下文具有私密、碎片化、动态、社会情境化等特征，难以构建可评估其安全使用能力的基准；现有显式静态画像评估方式不真实、不可验证、难扩展。

Method: 构建人口结构对齐、以人生故事为根基的合成"数字人"（由反映美国人口分布的人口学种子生成）；不采用显式静态画像，而将特质、习惯、目标与约束以隐式证据嵌入碎片化、持续演化的合成移动应用记录中；评测五项能力（单源事实恢复、多源事实恢复、时序状态追踪、由习惯行为推断高层个人模式、以及面对与人格特定约束冲突请求时的合规/警示/澄清/拒绝等安全处理）；配套多阶段验证流水线检验人格可信度、上下文可恢复性、信息泄漏、证据保留、测试用例接地性与人工评判一致性。

Result: 实例化包含 100 个人格、10,088 条隐藏事实、18,261 个应用轨迹片段、4,810 个经核验测试用例；流水线支持跨规模与人口群体的可配置生成。面向个人助理的低成本 SOTA LLM 在时序追踪、高层模式推断与约束感知行动边界决策上准确率低于 60%，且在三次重复运行中三项能力全部答对的用例不足 10%；即使高性能 SOTA 模型在这些能力上仍表现不稳定。

Conclusion: P-ContextBench 为提升 LLM 个人助理的准确性、可靠性与安全性提供了可量化目标，填补了个人上下文理解评估在真实性、可验证性与可扩展性上的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for personal tasks, yet evaluating their safe use of personal context remains difficult because such contexts are private, fragmented, dynamic, and socially situated. We introduce P-ContextBench, a realistic, verifiable, and scalable benchmark for evaluating personal-context understanding. P-ContextBench constructs population-structured, life-story-grounded digital humans from demographic seeds reflecting the U.S. population. Rather than using explicit, static profiles, P-ContextBench embeds traits, routines, goals, and constraints as implicit evidence across fragmented, evolving synthetic mobile-app records. It evaluates five capabilities: single-source fact recovery, multi-source fact recovery, temporal state tracking, inference of higher-level personal patterns from routine behavior, and safe handling of requests conflicting with persona-specific constraints through compliance, warning, clarification, or refusal. A multi-stage validation pipeline checks persona plausibility, context recoverability, information leakage, evidence preservation, test-case grounding, and human-judge agreement. We instantiate P-ContextBench with 100 personas, 10,088 hidden facts, 18,261 app-trace fragments, and 4,810 verified test cases. The pipeline supports configurable generation across scales and demographic groups. Cost-effective state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLMs relevant to personal agents achieve below 60% accuracy on temporal tracking, higher-level pattern inference, and constraint-aware action-boundary decisions, with fewer than 10% of test cases answered correctly across all three repeated runs. Even a high-performing SOTA model remains inconsistent on these capabilities. P-ContextBench provides measurable targets for improving the accuracy, reliability, and safety of LLM-based personal agents.

</details>


### [217] [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096)
*Juntao Cheng,Jinzhou Tang,Fan Feng,Henrik I. Christensen,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CWAM：通过稀疏路由约束使世界动作模型仅从与动作相关的潜变量生成动作，并证明其可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）预测整个场景演化，但只有部分场景引发动作。仅靠示范数据拟合难以保证动作仅源自"动作相关潜变量"，模型可能将动作相关变量与干扰变量纠缠而仍实现模仿。

Method: 将"引发动作的因素"与"预测所依赖的因素"分离；在稀疏性约束下，证明用于生成动作的潜变量是动作相关潜变量的可逆函数，且真实结构可被识别至潜变量置换，该结论不要求充分的时间依赖、不对未观测机制演化作假设、且允许动作相关潜变量与其余变量统计相关。据此提出CWAM，在预训练WAM的动作头上加入稀疏路由路径，世界建模目标保持不变。

Result: 合成数据与真实机器人实验验证了理论结论，并展示了闭环任务成功率上的效率提升。

Conclusion: 稀疏路由约束可在不改变世界建模目标的前提下，为WAM提供动作相关潜变量的可识别性保证，并提升闭环控制表现。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>


### [218] [Using Machine Learning to Measure Self-Efficacy Belief Through Learners' Interaction Traces in Technology-Enhanced Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41900)
*Maeva Somny,Linda Chouati,Marie Lefevre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究旨在基于学习者在技术增强学习（TEL）系统中的交互痕迹，用数据挖掘与机器学习方法检测低自我效能感，并通过两所高校的实验收集数据、构建多分类器流水线，验证了自我效能感动态监控对预测性能的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我效能感影响学习者的表现、学业选择与未来职业发展；TEL环境在支持表现与动机方面潜力显著，若能尽早识别低自我效能感学习者可及时实施补救干预。然而，利用TEL系统使用数据检测自我效能感的研究尚属空白，故本文开展探索性工作。

Method: 在法国/多所高校（两所大学）开展实验，收集学习者解题过程中的交互痕迹与自我效能感自陈量表数据；采用数据挖掘与机器学习技术识别指示低自我效能感的特征，构建包含多个分类模型的机器学习流水线进行检测。

Result: 初步结果表明，自我效能感在学习过程中具有高度动态性，对其进行密切、细粒度的持续监控可显著提升低自我效能感的预测性能。

Conclusion: 基于交互痕迹的机器学习方法可有效检测低自我效能感；未来将进一步完善检测系统并推广补救实践，强调自我效能感动态监测在TEL环境中的关键作用。

Abstract: Self-efficacy beliefs play a critical role in learners' performance, academic choices, and, more broadly, in their future career trajectories. As Technology-Enhanced Learning (TEL) environments have demonstrated strong potential for supporting both performance and motivation, investigating self-efficacy within such environments is of particular interest, especially for implementing remedial practices as early as possible to support learners' self-efficacy beliefs. Our research aims to develop a system capable of detecting low self-efficacy based on learners' interaction traces while they solve exercises in a TEL system. In this paper, we present initial results and future perspectives on a detection approach based on data mining and machine learning techniques to identify features indicative of low self-efficacy beliefs. Because the detection of learners' self-efficacy beliefs through their use of TEL systems remains largely unexplored, this work is exploratory in nature. To this end, we conducted an experiment across two universities to collect interaction traces and self-reported measures of self-efficacy. We then developed a machine learning pipeline comprising multiple classification models, enabling us to highlight the importance of closely monitoring of learners' self-efficacy beliefs for improved prediction performance, given their highly dynamic nature during learning.

</details>


### [219] [Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865)
*Yingzhao Jian,Zhongan Wang,Yuxuan Hou,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LIT框架通过元智能体、模拟用户与导演智能体构成协同进化闭环，实现视频导演智能体交互能力与制作能力的互相强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频导演智能体自进化研究主要提升剧本规划能力，却忽视了"导演执行"与"用户交互"之间的相互促进：交互改善可澄清用户意图并优化工作流，执行可靠则使协作从排障转向创意决策。而先前的"模拟用户+元工程师"范式依赖元智能体的被动归因，难以适应更广泛的交互模式与制作技术。

Method: 提出LIT（Learning to Interact and DirecT）框架，包含导演智能体、模拟用户和元工程师三部分。模拟用户分配创意任务以探测导演弱点，元工程师基于任务轨迹修订交互与制作策略；并引入协同进化范式，使元智能体主动学习外部知识以提升问题发现与方案提出能力，形成元智能体—模拟用户—导演的进化闭环。

Result: 在Test与OOD Test划分上，相较初始流水线，LIT的制作得分与交互得分分别提升19.1%与32.0%。

Conclusion: 控制消融实验揭示了制作改进与交互改进之间的相关性，协同进化机制进一步促进了导演技能与交互模式的探索。

Abstract: Video director agents orchestrate multistage production to realize users' creative visions. Emerging research on their self-evolution advances screenplay planning effectively. However, the mutual reinforcement between directing and user interaction remains largely overlooked. Improved interaction clarifies user intent and informs workflow refinement, while more reliable execution shifts collaboration from troubleshooting to creative decisions. To this end, we introduce LIT (Learning to Interact and DirecT), a framework comprises a director agent, a simulated user, and a meta engineer. Simulated user assigns creative tasks to probe the director's weaknesses, while the meta engineer uses the task traces to revise the director's interaction and production strategy. Although this approach can address discovered interaction and production issues, it still relies on passive attribution by the meta agent. This limits its ability to adapt to broader user interaction patterns and production techniques. To solve this problem, we equip LIT with a co-evolution paradigm. The meta agent proactively learns external knowledge, thereby improving its ability to discover problems and propose solutions. The meta agent, simulated user, and director together form an evolutionary closed loop, collaboratively enhances the system's directing and interaction capabilities. Across the Test and OOD Test splits, LIT improves production and Interaction scores by 19.1% and 32.0%, respectively, relative to the initial pipeline. Controlled ablations reveal the correlation between improvements in production and interaction, where the co-evolution mechanism further encourage directing skill and interaction mode exploration.

</details>


### [220] [Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147)
*Ales Kubicek,Andrea Caciolai,Liangzu Peng,Sukrit Sumant,Benjamin Hoover,Fraser Greenlee,Ruairidh M. Battleday,Radim Urban,Torsten Hoefler,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Sparse Attention (LSA)：在学习到的隐空间中对键做层次聚类，查询只对簇层次打分而非逐键打分，从而在超长上下文（>1M tokens）下兼顾精度与效率；含蒸馏版 D-LSA 与原生训练版 N-LSA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 agentic 工作流需要超长上下文（>1M tokens），但全注意力代价过高；现有稀疏注意力索引器要么索引本身仍是上下文长度的二次复杂度，要么在超长上下文下精度下降。

Method: LSA 将 key 在学习到的隐空间中层次聚类，query 对簇层次进行打分以选取 key 子集，替代逐 key 打分，从而加速 prefill、decode 与训练。两个变体：Distilled-LSA（D-LSA）通过蒸馏迁移到预训练模型；Native-LSA（N-LSA）仅用下一 token 损失原生训练。

Result: 在 GLM-5.3-FLASH（QWEN-3.8-27B）上、10M tokens 时，D-LSA 相对原始稀疏注意力（全注意力）单层 prefill 加速 22.7x（72.4x）、decode 加速 4.6x（42.5x），多任务精度基本持平；N-LSA 在 128k tokens 下精度匹配全注意力，单层训练加速 1.6x。

Conclusion: LSA 为超长上下文提供了实用高效的注意力方案，使模型能以可接受成本受益于超长上下文；隐空间层次索引兼具索引效率与超长上下文精度。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are increasingly shifting towards long-horizon tasks, such as open-ended scientific work, where models benefit from long context windows. However, at ultra-long context (above 1M tokens), full attention is prohibitively expensive. A promising approach is sparse attention, in which a dedicated indexer selects a subset of keys for the attention. Yet current methods of indexing either still have quadratic cost in context size or lose accuracy at ultra-long context scale. We address this with Latent Sparse Attention (LSA), an efficient attention method that maintains accuracy at ultra-long context. In LSA, keys are hierarchically clustered in a learned latent space, and queries score the hierarchy of clusters instead of scoring every key, making prefill, decode and training faster at long context. We propose two variants, Distilled-LSA (D-LSA), which can be applied to pre-trained models via distillation, and Native-LSA (N-LSA), which is trained natively on the next-token loss alone. On GLM-5.3-FLASH (QWEN-3.8-27B) at 10M tokens, D-LSA speeds up one attention layer by 22.7x (72.4x) in prefill and 4.6x (42.5x) in decode over its original sparse attention (full attention), while keeping similar accuracy across a variety of tasks. N-LSA matches the accuracy of full attention with a training speedup of 1.6x per layer at 128k tokens. LSA offers practical and efficient attention for models to benefit from ultra-long context.

</details>
